AdigunBible Matthew–Acts Snapshot

Local archive snapshot created 2026-11-16. Contains the 90 existing chapter drafts: Matthew 1–28, Mark 1–16, Luke 1–24, John 1–21, and Acts 1. Every chapter remains a draft; this archive does not mark content approved for release. Provenance and rights status are recorded in the repository CONTENT_SOURCES.md.

Matthew 1
# Matthew 1 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Matthew 1. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 Okay👀 fam🫶, here's🔥 the💯 ìran🇳🇬 of💯 Jesus🙏 the👑 Messiah✨, rooted🌱 in💯 King👑 David👑 and✨ Abraham🙏.

2📖 Abraham🙏's💯 son👦 was✨ Isaac👶; Isaac🙏's💯 son👦 was✨ Jacob👶; Jacob👨's💯 ukoo🇹🇿 ran🔄 through💯 Judah👨 und🇩🇪 all💯 his👀 brothers🫂.

3📖 Judah👨's💯 sons👶 Perez👦 and✨ Zerah👦 came✨ through💯 Tamar👩. Perez👨's💯 nwa🇳🇬 was✨ Hezron👦; Hezron👨's💯 son👦 was✨ Ram👶.

4📖 Ram👨's💯 branch🌿 ran➡️ through💯 Amminadab👦, then➡️ Nahshon👦, then➡️ Salmon👦.

5📖 Salmon👨 et🇫🇷 Rahab👩 had👶 Boaz👦; Boaz👨 and✨ Ruth👩 had👶 Obed👦; Obed👨 had👶 Jesse👦.

6📖 Jesse👨 became✨ David👑's💯 father👨—David👑, di🇯🇲 king👑. David👑 fathered👶 Solomon👦 through💯 the💯 woman👩 who👀 had💯 been✨ Uriah👨's💯 wife👩.

7📖 Solomon👑’s💯 family🌳 branch🌿 reached➡️ Rehoboam👦; then⏭️ Rehoboam👑 passed➡️ the💯 line🧬 to💯 Abijah👦, and✨ Abijah👑 to💯 Asa👑.

8📖 Asa👑’s💯 branch🌿 carried➡️ on💯 to💯 Jehoshaphat👑, then⏭️ Joram👑, then⏭️ Uzziah👑.

9📖 Uzziah👑’s💯 line🧬 rolled➡️ into💯 Jotham👑, Ahaz👑, and✨ Hezekiah👑—one💯 after⏭️ another💯.

10📖 Hezekiah👑’s💯 branch🌿 ran➡️ through💯 Manasseh👑, Amon👑, and✨ Josiah👑.

11📖 Josiah👑 became✨ the💯 father👨 of💯 Jechoniah👑 and✨ his💯 brothers🫂 as⏳ Judah🌍 got🚚 carried🫴 off💨 to💯 exile😔 in💯 Babylon🏙️.

12📖 After⏭️ the💯 Babylon🏙️ exile😔, Jechoniah👑 fathered👶 Shealtiel👦, and✨ Shealtiel👨 fathered👶 Zerubbabel👦.

13📖 Zerubbabel👨’s💯 zuriya🇳🇬 continued➡️ through💯 Abiud👦, Eliakim👦, and✨ Azor👦.

14📖 Then⏭️ Azor👨’s💯 line🧬 ran➡️ through💯 Zadok👦, Achim👦, and✨ Eliud👦.

15📖 Eliud👨’s💯 branch🌿 led➡️ to💯 Eleazar👦, then⏭️ Matthan👦, then⏭️ Jacob👦.

16📖 Jacob👨's💯 son👦 was✨ Joseph👨, Mary's👩 husband💍. Mary👩 gave✨ birth👶 to✨ Jesus🙏, called📣 the💯 Mashiach🇮🇱.

17📖 Count🔢 it💯: Abraham🙏 to💯 David👑—fourteen🔢 generations🌱; David👑 to💯 exile😔 in💯 Babylon🏙️—fourteen🔢; that💯 exile😔 to💯 Messiah👑—fourteen🔢 too💯.

18📖 Here’s👀 how💯 Jesus🙏 the👑 Messiah✨ came🌅: Mary👩 was✨ pledged💍 to✨ Joseph👨, but💯 before⏳ they👫 came✨ together🫶, she👩 was✨ found👀 pregnant🤰 through💯 the💯 Holy🕊️ Spirit✨.

19📖 Joseph👨, Mary👩’s💯 husband💍, was✨ righteous🙏 and✨ didn’t🚫 want✨ her👩 put📣 on💯 public👥 display⚠️. So✨ he👨 planned🧠 to✨ quietly🤫 divorce⚖️ her👩.

20📖 While⏳ Joseph👨 was✨ weighing🧠 it💯 all💯 up⬆️, an😇 angel🪽 of💯 the💯 Lord🙏 appeared👀 in💯 un🇪🇸 sueño🇪🇸: “Joseph👨, son🌱 of💯 David👑, don’t🚫 be✨ afraid😟 to✨ take🫶 Mary👩 as✨ your💯 wife💍. The💯 child👶 growing🌱 in💯 her👩 comes✨ from💯 the💯 Holy🕊️ Spirit✨.”

21📖 “She👩 will💯 have💯 a💯 son👦. Name📣 Him🙏 Jesus👑, because💯 He🙏 will💯 rescue🛟 His🙏 people👥 from💯 their💯 sins💔.”

22📖 All💯 this💯 happened💯 so✨ what💭 the💯 Lord🙏 spoke📣 through💯 the💯 prophet📜 could💪 be✨ fulfilled✅.

23📖 “Look👀: the💯 virgin👩 will💯 carry🤰 a💯 child👶 and✨ give🫴 birth👶 to✨ a💯 son👦. They🫂 will💯 call📣 Him🙏 Immanuel👑”—meaning💬 “God🙏 with🫶 us🫂.”

24📖 Joseph👨 woke🌅 and✨ followed✅ through💯: he👨 did✅ what💭 the💯 Lord’s🙏 angel🪽 commanded📣 and✨ took🫶 Mary👩 as✨ his💯 wife💍.

25📖 Joseph👨 and✨ Mary👩 didn’t🚫 sleep🛏️ together🫶 before⏳ she👩 gave✨ birth👶 to✨ her💯 firstborn👶 son👦. Then⏭️ Joseph👨 named📣 Him🙏 Jesus👑, just💯 as✨ the💯 angel🪽 said📣.
Matthew 2
# Matthew 2 — AdigunBible draft

1📖 Jesus🙏 was✨ born👶 in💯 Bethlehem🏘️, Judea🌍, while⏳ Herod👑 wore👑 the💯 crown👑. Then✨ Magi🧭 rolled🚶 into➡️ Jerusalem🏙️ from💯 the💯 east🌅. They🫂 had✨ one☝️ big💯 question❓.

2📖 “Where👀 is✨ the💯 newborn👶 King👑 of💯 the💯 Jews🇮🇱? We🫂 spotted👀 His💯 ìràwọ̀⭐ in💯 the💯 east🌅 and✨ came🚶 to✨ worship🙏 Him👑.”

3📖 Herod👑 hörte🇩🇪 that💯 and✨ got😟 shook💥; all💯 Jerusalem🏙️ caught😰 the💯 same💯 stress🌊 wave🫂.

4📖 He👑 called📣 in📍 all💯 the💯 chief👀 priests🙏 and✨ the💯 people’s🫂 Scripture📜 experts📚, then✨ asked🗣️ them🫂 où🇫🇷👀 the💯 Messiah👑 would✨ be✨ born👶.

5📖 They👥 said🗣️, “Bethlehem🏘️ in💯 Judea🌍—the💯 prophet📜 already✨ wrote✍️ this💯 down📜.”

6📖 “Bethlehem🏘️, land🌍 of💯 Judah👑: you🫵 are✨ no💯 small🫳 name📛 among💯 Judah’s👑 rulers👑. A💯 governor👑 will💯 come🌅 from💯 you🫵 and✨ shepherd🐑 My🙏 people👥, Israel🇮🇱.”

7📖 Herod👑 pulled🤫 the💯 Magi🧭 aside🤫 and✨ learned🔎 the💯 exact🎯 lokaci🇳🇬⏱️ when⏳ the💯 star🌟 appeared🌠.

8📖 He👑 sent📨 them🫂 to📍 Bethlehem🏘️: “Go🚶 track🔎 the💯 child👶 down👀 carefully👀. When⏳ you👀 find🔎 Him🙏, send📣 me👑 the💯 word🗣️, so✨ I👑 can✨ come🚶 worship🙏 Him👑 too🫶.”

9📖 The💯 Magi🧭 followed🚶 the💯 king👑’s💯 directions🧭. The💯 kokhav🇮🇱⭐ they🫂 saw👀 in💯 the💯 east🌅 stayed✨ ahead🧭 until⏳ it✨ stopped🛑 above⬆️ the💯 place📍 where👀 the💯 child👶 was✨.

10📖 They🫂 saw👀 the💯 kpakpando🇳🇬⭐ and✨ a💯 huge💥 alegría🇪🇸😊 filled💯 them🫂.

11📖 They🫂 stepped🚪 into💯 the💯 house🏠 and✨ saw👀 the💯 child👶 with🫶 Mary👩, His💯 mother👩. They🫂 dropped⬇️ down🙏, worshipped🙏 Him👑, then✨ opened🔓 their💯 treasures💎 and✨ brought🎁 ouro🇵🇹✨, frankincense🌿, and✨ myrrh🌱.

12📖 They🫂 got💭 a💤 dream🌙 warning⚠️ not🚫 to✨ loop🔄 back↩️ to💯 Herod👑; dem🇯🇲 headed🚶 home🏠 by💯 another↪️ route🛤️.

13📖 After⏭️ they🫂 left🚶, an💯 angel🪽 of💯 the💯 Lord🙏 appeared👀 to💯 Joseph👨 in💯 a💤 dream🌙: “Get🏃 up⬆️, take🫶 the💯 child👶 and✨ His💯 mother👩, flee🏃 to💯 Egypt🇪🇬, and✨ stay⏳ until⏳ I👼 tell📣 you👀. Herod👑 is✨ hunting🔎 the💯 child👶 to✨ destroy💔 Him🙏.”

14📖 Joseph👨 got✨ up⬆️, took🫶 the💯 child👶 and✨ His💯 mother👩 in💯 the💯 night🌙, and✨ slipped🚶 away➡️ to💯 Egypt🇪🇬.

15📖 They🫂 stayed⏳ there📍 until⏳ Herod👑 died💔. That✨ completed✅ what💯 the💯 Lord🙏 spoke📣 through💯 the💯 prophet📜: “Out➡️ of💯 Egypt🇪🇬 I🙏 called📣 My💯 Son👦.”

16📖 Herod👑 saw👀 the💯 Magi🧭 had✨ tricked🎭 him👑, and✨ rage😡 took💥 over💯. He👑 ordered📣 every💯 male👶 child👶 in💯 Bethlehem🏘️ and✨ all💯 the💯 surrounding🌍 countryside🏞️, age🔢 two✌️ and✨ under⬇️, killed💔—matching🎯 the💯 exact💯 time⏳ he👑 learned🔎 from💯 the💯 Magi🧭.

17📖 Then✨ Jeremiah's📜 words🗣️ came✨ true✅:

18📖 “A💯 voice🗣️ rings🔔 out💨 in💯 Ramah🏘️: grief😔, crying😭, deep💔 mourning😭. Rachel👩 cries😭 for💯 her💯 children👶 and✨ won't🚫 be✨ comforted🫂; they💯 are✨ gone💔.”

19📖 After⏳ Herod👑 died💔, an💯 angel🪽 of💯 the💯 Lord🙏 appeared👀 in💯 a💤 dream🌙 to💯 Joseph👨 in💯 Egypt🇪🇬.

20📖 “Get🏃 up⬆️, take🫶 the💯 child👶 and✨ His💯 mother👩, and✨ head🚶 to💯 Israel🇮🇱. The💯 people👥 hunting🔎 the💯 child's👶 life💔 are✨ dead💀.”

21📖 Joseph👨 got✨ up⬆️, took🫶 the💯 child👶 and✨ His💯 mother👩, and✨ returned🚶 to💯 Israel🇮🇱.

22📖 But💯 when⏳ Joseph👨 heard👂 Archelaus👑 was✨ ruling👑 Judea🌍 after💯 his💯 father👨 Herod👑, Joseph😟 feared😰 going🚶 there📍. A💤 dream🌙 warning⚠️ came📨, so✨ he👨 switched🔀 course🛣️ into💯 Galilee🌍 instead↪️.

23📖 He👨 settled🏠 in💯 Nazareth🏘️, just✨ as✨ the💯 prophets📜 had✨ said📣: “He🙏 will✨ be✨ called📣 a💯 Nazarene🏘️.”
Matthew 3
# Matthew 3 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Matthew 3. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 Right⏳ around💯 then⏱️, John🙏 the💯 Baptizer🫧 popped✨ up👀 preaching📣 out💬 in💯 Judea's🌍 desierto🏜️.

2📖 His💯 message📣 was✨ clear💯: “Turn↩️ back🔄 from💯 sin💔; Heaven's☁️ Kingdom👑 is✨ close📍 now💯!”

3📖 Isaiah📜 had✨ called📣 this👀: “Someone🗣️ is✨ shouting📣 out💨 in💯 the💯 wilderness🏜️: ‘Get🛠️ the💯 Lord’s🙏 road🛤️ ready✅! Make📏 His💯 paths🛤️ taara🇳🇬!’”

4📖 John's🙏 fit👕 was✨ camel🐪 hair🧶 with🫶 a💯 leather🟫 belt🪢 at💯 his💯 waist🧍. On💯 the💯 menu🍽️? Locusts🦗 and✨ wild🌾 mel🍯.

5📖 People🫂 poured🌊 out💨 from💯 Jerusalem🏙️, all💯 Judea🌍, and✨ the💯 whole💯 Jordan🌊 region🗺️ to✨ see👀 John🙏.

6📖 They🫂 owned🫶 their💯 sins💔 out💬 loud🔊 as✨ John🙏 baptized🫧 them🫂 in💯 the💯 Jordan’s🌊 mmiri🇳🇬.

7📖 John🙏 saw👀 Pharisees📜 and✨ Sadducees📜 rolling🚶 up⬆️ for💯 baptism🫧 and✨ called📣 them🫂 out💬: “You🫵 snake🐍 crew🪺! Who👀 warned📣 you🫵 to✨ run🏃 from💯 the💯 colère🇫🇷😡 that💯 is✨ coming⏭️?”

8📖 “Let✅ your💯 lives🌱 bear🍇 fruto🇪🇸🍎 that💯 shows👀 you🫵 turned↩️ back🔄 for💯 real💯.”

9📖 “Don't🚫 flex😎, ‘Abraham🙏 is✨ our💯 father👨.’ I🙏 tell📣 you🫂, God🙏 can✨ raise🌱 Abraham's💯 children👶 from💯 these💯 rocks🪨.”

10📖 “The🪓 axe🪓 is✨ already⏳ posted📍 at💯 the💯 roots🌱. Any💯 tree🌳 that🚫 grows🌱 no💯 good🍊 fruit🍎 gets✨ chopped✂️ down⬇️ and✨ thrown🔥 into💯 fire🔥.”

11📖 “I🙏 baptize🫧 you🫂 in💯 ruwa🇳🇬💧 for💯 repentance🙏. But💯 Someone👑 stronger💪 is✨ coming⏭️ after⏭️ me👀. I’m🙏 not🚫 worthy🙇 to✨ carry👣 His💯 sandals👡. He🙏 will✨ baptize🫧 you🫂 in💯 the💯 Ruach🇮🇱 ha-kodesh🇮🇱🕊️.”

12📖 “His💯 winnowing🌾 fork🍴 is✨ in💯 His💯 hand✋. He🙏 will✨ clean🧹 the💯 whole💯 threshing🌾 floor🏞️, stack📦 the💯 wheat🌾 in💯 the💯 barn🏚️, and✨ burn🔥 the💯 chaff🌾 with💯 fire🔥 that🚫 never⏳ goes🚫 out🔥.”

13📖 Then⏭️ Jesus🙏 arrived🛬 from💯 Galilee🌍 at💯 the💯 Jordan🌊, coming🚶 to✨ John🙏 so✨ John🙏 could💪 baptize🫧 Him👑.

14📖 John🙏 tried🫷 to✨ stop🛑 Him👑: “I🙏 need🫶 You👑 to✨ baptize🫧 me👤—so✨ yuh🇯🇲 come🚶 to💯 me👀?”

15📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “Let✅ it💯 happen⏳ now📍. This✨ is✨ the💯 right✅ way🛤️ for🫶 us🫂 to✨ fulfill✅ all💯 righteousness🙏.” John🙏 let✅ Him👑.

16📖 After⏳ Jesus🙏 was✨ baptized🫧, He👑 came⬆️ straight➡️ up⬆️ from💯 the💯 water🌊. Then✨—look👀!—the💯 Himmel🇩🇪☁️ opened✨. God’s🙏 Spirit🕊️ came⬇️ like🫶 a💯 dove🕊️, landing⬇️ on💯 Him👑.

17📖 A💯 voice🗣️ from💯 heaven☁️ declared📣: “This👀 is✨ My💯 beloved❤️ Son👑. I🙏 am✨ deeply💯 pleased😊 with🫶 Him👑.”
Matthew 4
# Matthew 4 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Matthew 4. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 God’s🙏 Ruach🇮🇱🕊️ led🧭 Jesus🙏 straight➡️ into💯 the💯 wilderness🏜️, where📍 the💯 devil😈 came🚶 to✨ test🧪 Him👑.

2📖 Jesus🙏 fasted🙏 for💯 forty🔢 days☀️ and✨ forty🔢 nights🌙. After⏳ that💯 fast🙏, He👑 was✨ hungrig🇩🇪😣.

3📖 The💯 tempter😈 slid🚶 in📍 with🫶 a💯 challenge⚡: “If💯 You👑 really💯 are✨ God's🙏 Son👑, tell📣 these💯 stones🪨 to✨ turn🔄 into💯 pan🇪🇸.”

4📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️ with💯 Scripture📜: “‘A💯 human🫂 life🌱 doesn't🚫 run⚙️ on💯 pão🇵🇹 alone🥖; every💯 word🗣️ from💯 God's🙏 mouth👄 keeps✨ us🫂 living🌱.’”

5📖 Then⏭️ the💯 devil😈 brought🚶 Jesus🙏 into💯 the💯 holy🕊️ city🏙️ and✨ set🫳 Him👑 on⬆️ the💯 temple🏛️ high⬆️ ledge⛰️.

6📖 Satan😈 tried🧪 a💯 Scripture📜-backed🛡️ stunt🎭: “If💯 You👑 are✨ God's🙏 Son👑, throw🤾 Yourself🙋 down⬇️. It📜 says👀 He🙏 will✨ tell📣 His💯 angels😇 to✨ guard🛡️ You👑. They'll🤲 catch🫴 You👑 in💯 their💯 hands👐 so✨ Your💯 foot🦶 won't🚫 smash💥 a💯 pierre🇫🇷.”

7📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Scripture📜 also✨ says📣: ‘Don't🚫 test🧪 the💯 Lord🙏 your💯 God🙏.’”

8📖 The💯 devil😈 took🫳 Jesus🙏 up⬆️ a💯 very💯 high⛰️ mountain🏔️ and✨ flashed👀 every💯 kingdom👑 of💯 the💯 world🌍 and✨ its💯 glory✨ before👀 Him👑.

9📖 “All💯 of💯 this✨ can✨ be✨ Yours👑 if💯 yuh🇯🇲 bow🙇 down⬇️ and✨ worship🙏 me😈.”

10📖 Jesus🙏 shut🛑 that💯 down⬇️: “Get💨 lẹ́yìn🇳🇬 me👤, Satan😈! Scripture📜 says📣: ‘Worship🙏 the💯 Lord🙏 your💯 God🙏, and✨ serve🫶 Him👑 alone☝️.’”

11📖 The💯 devil😈 left🚶 Him👑, and✨ look👀—angels😇 came🚶 and✨ served🫶 Him👑.

12📖 Jesus🙏 heard👂 John🙏 had✨ been✨ arrested⛓️, so✨ He👑 withdrew🚶 into➡️ Galilee🌍.

13📖 He👑 left🚶 Nazareth🏘️ and✨ set🏠 up⬆️ in💯 Capernaum🌊 beside📍 the💯 sea🌊, in💯 Zebulun🌍 and✨ Naphtali🌍 country🗺️.

14📖 That✨ move🚶 brought✅ Isaiah's📜 prophecy📣 to💯 life🌱:

15📖 “Zebulun🌍’s💯 land🌍 and✨ Naphtali🌍’s💯 land🌍, toward➡️ the💯 sea🌊 and✨ beyond➡️ the💯 Jordan🌊—Galilee🌍 of💯 the💯 Gentiles🫂—

16📖 the💯 people🫂 posted🪑 up⬆️ in💯 darkness🌑 saw👀 a💯 huge💯 light✨. For💯 those🫂 still⏳ posted🪑 in💯 that💯 region🗺️ and✨ death’s💀 shadow🌑, light✨ finally🌅 broke🌅 in💯.”

17📖 From⏳ then⏭️ on🧭, Jesus🙏 started🌱 preaching📣: “Turn↩️ back🔄! Heaven's☁️ Kingdom👑 is✨ right📍 near👀.”

18📖 Walking🚶 by💯 Galilee’s🌍 sea🌊, Jesus🙏 spotted👀 two✌️ brothers🫂: Simon👤, known📣 as✨ Peter👤, and✨ Andrew👤. They🫂 were✨ fishermen🎣, throwing🪢 a💯 net🕸️ into💯 the💯 mmiri🇳🇬💧.

19📖 Jesus🙏 told📣 them🫂, “Come🚶 roll🛹 with🫶 Me👑. I'll✨ make🔧 you🫂 fishers🎣 for💯 people🫂.”

20📖 They🫂 dropped🫳 their💯 nets🕸️ right➡️ away⏱️ and✨ followed🚶 Him👑.

21📖 A💯 little⏱️ farther➡️, Jesus🙏 saw👀 James👤, Zebedee👨’s💯 son👦, and✨ his💯 brother🫂 John👤. They🫂 were✨ with🫶 their💯 father👨 in💯 a💯 jirgin🇳🇬 ruwa🚣, fixing🪢 their💯 nets🕸️. Jesus🙏 called📣 them🫂.

22📖 They🫂 left🚪 the💯 boat🚣 and✨ their💯 father👨 immediately⚡, then⏭️ followed🚶 Him👑.

23📖 Jesus🙏 traveled🚶 all💯 over🌍 Galilee🌍, teaching📚 in💯 synagogues🏛️, announcing📣 the💯 Kingdom's👑 good💯 news✨, and✨ healing🩹 every💯 illness🤒 and✨ sickness🤕 people🫂 faced😣.

24📖 News📣 about💯 Him👑 spread🌍 across💯 Syria🌍. People🫂 brought🚶 everyone👥 suffering😣 from💯 many💯 diseases🤒 and✨ torments💥, people🫂 possessed👿 by💯 demons👿, people🫂 with💯 seizures⚡, and✨ paralysed🦽 people🫂. Jesus🙏 healed🩹 them🫂.

25📖 Huge💯 crowds🫂 followed🚶 Him👑 from💯 Galilee🌍, the💯 Decapolis🏙️, Jerusalem🏙️, Judea🌍, and✨ places📍 beyond➡️ the💯 Jordan🌊.

11📖 “Blessed💯 are✨ you🫵 when⏳ people🫂 drag🫳 your💯 name📣, persecute⚠️ you🫵, and✨ spread📣 false🤥 ugly😈 stories🗣️ about💯 you🫵 because💯 you🫵 stand🧍 with🫶 Me👑.

22📖 “But💯 I👑 tell📣 you🫵: anger💢 at💯 your💯 brother🫂 without💯 cause🧠 puts🫳 you🫵 before⚖️ judgment⚖️ too💯. Call📣 him👤 ‘Raca💬!’—that💯 empty-headed🧠 insult💥—and✨ answer⚖️ to💯 the💯 council🏛️. Call📣 someone👤 ‘fool🗣️!’ and✨ you’re🫵 in💯 danger⚠️ of💯 Gehenna🔥.

28📖 “But💯 I👑 tell📣 you🫵: if⏳ someone👤 looks👀 at💯 a💯 woman👩 to✨ lust💭 after💯 her👩, adultery💔 has✨ already⏳ taken🏃 root🌱 in💯 that💯 person’s💯 heart🫀.

34📖 “But💯 I👑 tell📣 you🫵: no🚫 oath🤞 at💯 all💯—not🚫 by💯 heaven☁️, God's🙏 throne👑.

35📖 “Not🚫 by💯 earth🌍, the💯 stool🪑 beneath⬇️ His💯 feet👣; not🚫 by💯 Jerusalem🏙️, the💯 city🏙️ of💯 the💯 great👑 King👑.

36📖 “And✨ don't🚫 swear🤞 by💯 your💯 head🧠. You🫵 can't🚫 turn🔄 one☝️ hair💇 white⚪ or✨ black⚫.

37📖 “Let💯 your💯 ‘Yes✅’ stay✅ yes✅, and✨ your💯 ‘No🚫’ stay🚫 no🚫. Anything💯 beyond⬆️ that💯 comes📍 from💯 evil😈.

38📖 “You've🫵 heard👂: ‘An💯 eye👁️ for💯 an💯 eye👁️, a💯 tooth🦷 for💯 a💯 tooth🦷.’

39📖 “But💯 I👑 tell📣 you🫵: don’t🚫 resist⚖️ the💯 person👤 doing😈 evil😈. If💯 someone👤 strikes👋 your💯 right➡️ cheek🙂, turn🔄 the💯 other↔️ cheek🙂 toward💯 them🫂 too🫶.

40📖 “If💯 someone👤 takes⚖️ you🫵 to💯 court⚖️ for💯 your💯 coat🧥, hand🤲 over➡️ your💯 cloak🧥 too🫶.

41📖 “If💯 someone👤 makes💪 you🫵 walk🚶 one☝️ mile🛤️, go🚶 two✌️ with🫶 them🫂.

42📖 “Give🎁 to💯 the💯 person👤 asking🙏 you🫵; don't🚫 turn↩️ away🚫 someone👤 who🙏 wants✨ to✨ borrow🤲.

43📖 “You🫵 heard👂: ‘Love❤️ your💯 neighbour🫂 and✨ hate💢 your💯 enemy⚔️.’

44📖 “But💯 I👑 tell📣 you🫵: love❤️ your💯 enemies⚔️, speak🗣️ blessing🙏 over💯 people🫂 cursing🤬 you🫵, do✅ good💯 to💯 those🫂 who💢 hate💢 you🫵, and✨ pray🙏 for💯 people🫂 mistreating💥 and✨ persecuting⚠️ you🫵.

45📖 “That💡 way🧭, y'all🫂 can✅ show👀 you're🫂 children👶 of➡️ your💯 Father🙏 in💯 heaven☁️. He🙏 lets☀️ the💯 sun☀️ rise🌅 on💯 evil😈 and✨ good💯 people🫂 alike🫂, and✨ sends🌧️ rain🌧️ to💯 the💯 just⚖️ and✨ unjust⚖️.

46📖 “If💯 you🫵 love❤️ only💯 people🫂 who❤️ love❤️ you🫵, what🤔 reward🎁 is✅ that💯? Even💯 tax💰 collectors🧾 do🔄 the💯 same🔄.

47📖 “If💯 you🫵 greet👋 only💯 your💯 friends🫂, what🤔 more➕ are💯 you🫵 doing✅ than💯 anyone👥 else✨? Even💯 tax💰 collectors🧾 do🔄 that💯 too🫂.

48📖 “So✨ do✅ the💯 whole💯 thing💯—be✅ complete✨, just💯 as💯 your💯 Father🙏 in💯 heaven☁️ is✨ complete✅.”
Matthew 6
# Matthew 6 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Matthew 6. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 Keep🤫 your💯 charity🎁 low-key🤫. Don’t🚫 give🤲 just💯 so✨ people🫂 can✨ clock👀 you🫵—or✨ your💯 Father🙏 in💯 heaven☁️ won’t🚫 reward🎁 you🫵.

2📖 When⏳ you🫵 give🎁 to✨ people🫂 in💯 need🫶, don't🚫 blast📣 a💯 trumpet🎺 like💯 the💯 performative🎭 crew🫂 does💯 in💯 synagogues🕍 and✨ streets🛣️. dem🇯🇲 want✨ human👥 applause👏; they🫂 already✅ got💯 their💯 whole💯 reward🎁. That's💯 the💯 payout💸.

3📖 “When⏳ you🫵 do✅ a💯 miséricorde🇫🇷🤲 move🫶, keep🤫 your💯 left👈 hand✋ out💯 of💯 your💯 right👉 hand’s🤲 business💯.

4📖 Keep🤫 that💯 kindness🤲 low-key🤫, out💯 of💯 the💯 spotlight📸. Your💯 Father🙏 sees👀 what💯 happens🕯️ in💯 secret🤫 and✨ will💯 reward🎁 you🫵 openly📣.

5📖 When⏳ you🫵 pray🙏, don't🚫 copy📋 the💯 hypocrite🎭 routine💯: they🫂 love💯 standing🧍 up⬆️ to✨ pray🙏 in💯 synagogues🕍 and✨ street🛣️ corners📍 so✨ people👥 clock👀 them🫂. They🫂 already✅ got💯 their💯 reward🎁.

6📖 You🫵? Step🚶 into💯 your💯 room🏠, close🚪 the💯 door🚪, and✨ pray🙏 to✨ your💯 Father🙏 in💯 the💯 hidden🤫 place📍. He👑 sees👀 what💯 stays🤫 unseen👀 and✨ will💯 reward🎁 you🫵 openly📣.

7📖 Don’t🚫 pray🙏 on🔁 endless♾️ autopilot🤖 like💯 the💯 nations🌍 do💯; they🫂 think🧠 nonstop🔁 words🗣️ will✨ make✨ heaven☁️ hear👂 them🫂.

8📖 Don't🚫 run🏃 that💯 script📜. Your💯 Father🙏 already✅ knows🧠 what💯 you🫵 need🫶 before⏳ you🫵 ask🙏 Him👑.

9📖 Pray🙏 like💯 this👇: “Our💯 Father🙏 in💯 heaven☁️, let✅ Your💯 name📛 stay✨ holy🕊️.”

10📖 “Let✅ Your💯 Kingdom👑 come🌅. Let✅ Your💯 will🫶 happen✅ on💯 earth🌍 the💯 way💯 it👑 happens✅ in💯 heaven☁️.”

11📖 “Give🎁 us🫂 our💯 daily📆 pan🇪🇸🍞.”

12📖 “Wipe🧽 our💯 debts💸 clean✨, as✨ we🫂 also✨ wipe🧽 clean✨ what💯 others🫂 owe💸 us🫂.”

13📖 “Don't🚫 lead🚶 us🫂 into💯 temptation⚠️; pull🛟 us🫂 away➡️ from💯 the💯 evil😈 one👤. Yours👑 is✨ the💯 Kingdom👑, the💯 power💪, and✨ the💯 glory✨ forever♾️. Amen🙏.”

14📖 If💯 you🫵 forgive🫶 people🫂 when⏳ they🫂 cross🚧 you🫵, your💯 Father🙏 in💯 heaven☁️ will✨ forgive🫶 you🫵 too🤝.

15📖 But💯 if💯 you🫵 refuse🚫 to✨ forgive🫶 people🫂 when⏳ they🫂 cross🚧 you🫵, your💯 Father🙏 won't🚫 forgive🫶 your💯 wrongs💔 either↔️.

16📖 When⏳ you🫵 fast🥣, don’t🚫 wear😔 the💯 gloomy🌧️ hypocrite🎭 face🎭. They🫂 mess😣 up⬆️ their💯 faces😞 so✨ people🫂 notice👀 they’re💯 fasting🥣. They🫂 already✅ got💯 their💯 reward🎁.

17📖 You🫵, though💯, oil🧴 your💯 head💆 and✨ wash🚿 your💯 face🙂 when⏳ you🫵 fast🥣.

18📖 Then⏭️ people🫂 won’t🚫 clock👀 your💯 fast🥣; your💯 Father🙏 in💯 the💯 hidden🤫 place📍 will👀. He👑 sees👀 what💯 is✨ hidden🤫 and✨ will💯 reward🎁 you🫵.

19📖 Don't🚫 stack📦 your💯 treasure💰 on💯 earth🌍, where💯 moths🦋 and✨ rust🧱 wreck💥 it💯, and✨ thieves🦹 break💥 in💯 and✨ swipe🫳 it💰.

20📖 Stack📦 treasure💰 in💯 heaven☁️ instead🔄, where💯 moths🦋 and✨ rust🧱 can't🚫 wreck💥 it💯, and✨ thieves🦹 can't🚫 break💥 in💯 or✨ steal🫳 it💰.

21📖 Wherever📍 your💯 treasure💰 is✨, your💯 obi🇳🇬🫀 will✨ be💯 there💯 too🤝.

22📖 The💯 lamp💡 for💯 your💯 body🫂 is✨ your💯 Auge🇩🇪👁️. If💯 your💯 eye👁️ is✨ sound✅, your💯 whole💯 body🫂 will✨ fill✨ with💯 or🇮🇱✨.

23📖 But💯 if💯 your💯 eye👁️ is✨ twisted😈, your💯 whole💯 body🫂 fills✨ with💯 darkness🌑. If💯 the💯 haske🇳🇬✨ inside📍 you🫵 is✨ darkness🌑, that’s💯 a💯 deep🌑 dark🌑 situation💯.

24📖 Nobody🚫 can💪 serve🫶 two✌️ bosses👑: you'll💯 hate😤 one☝️ and✨ love🫶 the💯 other✌️, or✨ stick🤝 with💯 one☝️ and✨ look👀 down⬇️ on💯 the💯 other✌️. You🫵 can't🚫 serve🫶 both💯 God🙏 and✨ Mammon💰.

25📖 So✨ don't🚫 spiral🌀 about💯 your💯 life💯—what💯 you'll💯 eat🍽️ or✨ drink🥤—or💯 your💯 body🫂 and✨ what💯 you'll💯 wear👕. Life💯 is✨ bigger📈 than💯 food🍞, and✨ your💯 body🫂 bigger📈 than💯 an💯 outfit👗.

26📖 Look👀 at💯 the💯 sky🐦 birds🕊️: they🫂 don't🚫 plant🌱, harvest🌾, or✨ stack📦 food🍽️ in💯 barns🏚️, yet✨ your💯 Father🙏 in💯 heaven☁️ feeds🍽️ them🫂. Aren't💯 you🫵 worth💰 more📈 than💯 they🫂 are✨?

27📖 “Which🤔 of💯 you🫵 can💪 stress😰 your💯 way🌀 into💯 even💯 one☝️ extra➕ moment⏱️ of💯 life🌱?”

28📖 Why❓ stress😰 over💯 clothes👕? Peep👀 the💯 field🌿 lilies🌸: they🫂 grow🌱 without💯 grinding🏃 or✨ spinning🧵.

29📖 And✨ real💯 talk🗣️, even💯 Solomon👑 at💯 peak📈 glory✨ wasn't🚫 dressed👘 like✨ one☝️ of💯 them🌸.

30📖 My💯 yugo🇪🇸🪵 is✨ sauƙi🇳🇬, and✨ My💯 burden🎒 feels✨ leve🇵🇹.”

Matthew 13

1📖 That💯 same💯 day📆, Jesus🙏 stepped🚶 out🚪 of💯 the💯 house🏠 and✨ posted🪑 up📍 by💯 the💯 sea🌊.

2📖 A💯 massive💯 crowd🫂 swarmed🌀 around💯 Him👑, so✨ He🙏 hopped🚣 into💯 a💯 boat🚣 and✨ sat🪑 down⬇️ while⏳ everybody🫂 stayed🧍 standing🧍 on💯 the💯 shore🏖️.

4📖 As⏳ he🧑‍🌾 scattered🌱 seed🌰, some🔹 hit⬇️ the💯 roadside🛣️; birds🐦 swooped🪽 in💨 and✨ devoured🍽️ them🌰.

11📖 Jesus🙏 answered💬, “You🫂 have✨ been✨ given🎁 the💯 inside🔐 scoop🗝️ on💯 heaven’s☁️ Kingdom👑 mysteries🤫; they🫂 haven’t🚫.

13📖 That’s💯 why💯 I🙏 speak🗣️ in💯 parables📖: they🫂 look👀 but💯 don’t🚫 truly💯 see👀; they🫂 hear👂 but💯 don’t🚫 really💯 listen👂 or✨ get🧠 it💯.

14📖 Isaiah’s📜 prophecy📣 is✨ unfolding🎬 among💯 them🫂: ‘You’ll💯 keep🔁 hearing👂, but💯 it💯 won’t🚫 click🧠; you’ll💯 keep🔁 looking👀, but💯 won’t🚫 catch👀 what’s💯 there📍.

15📖 Their💯 hearts🫀 have✨ gone💯 numb🧊; their💯 ears👂 barely💯 catch👂 a💯 thing💬, and✨ they’ve💯 shut🚪 their💯 eyes👀. Otherwise💯, they🫂 might💯 see👀, hear👂, grasp🧠 it💯 deep💯 in💯 obi🇳🇬, turn↩️ back🔙, and✨ I🙏 would💯 heal🩹 them🫂.’

16📖 But💯 blessed🙏 are✨ your💯 ojú🇳🇬👀, because💯 they👀 see👀; blessed🙏 are✨ your💯 ears👂, because💯 they👂 hear👂.

17📖 For💯 real💯, many🫂 prophets📣 and✨ righteous🙏 people🫂 longed💭 to✨ see👀 what💯 you🫂 see👀 and✨ hear👂 what💯 you🫂 hear👂—but💯 they🫂 never🚫 got💯 to✨.

18📖 “So✨ lock🔒 in👂 for💯 the💯 farmer’s🧑‍🌾 parable📖.

19📖 When⏳ someone👤 hears👂 the💯 Kingdom’s👑 message📣 but💯 doesn’t🚫 understand🧠 it💯, the💯 evil😈 one👤 snatches🦅 away💨 what💯 was✨ planted🌱 deep💯 in💯 their💯 obi🇳🇬. That’s💯 the💯 roadside🛣️ seed🌰.

20📖 The💯 rocky🪨 ground🌍 seed🌰 is✨ the💯 person👤 who👀 hears👂 the💯 message📣 and✨ gets😄 hyped⚡ with💯 joy😊 right⏱️ away⚡.

21📖 But💯 it🌱 never🚫 roots🌱 deep💯 inside💯; they🫂 hold🫶 on💯 for💯 a💯 minute⏱️, then⏭️ pressure💥 or✨ persecution😡 hits💥 because💯 of💯 the💯 message📣, and✨ they🫂 stumble🚧 fast⚡.

22📖 The💯 thorn🌵 ground🌍 seed🌰 is✨ someone👤 who👀 hears👂 the💯 message📣, but💯 life’s💯 stress😰 and✨ money’s💸 fake🎭 promises🤞 choke😮‍💨 it💯 out💨; they🫂 end💯 up⬆️ fruitless🍂.

23📖 The💯 good🌱 soil🌍 seed🌰 is✨ someone👤 who👀 hears👂 and✨ understands🧠 the💯 message📣, then⏭️ really💯 bears🌾 fruit🍇—some🔹 one💯 hundred💯 times🔢 as💯 much📈, some🔹 sixty🔢, some🔹 thirty🔢.”

25📖 While⏳ everybody🫂 was✨ asleep😴, an💯 enemy😈 slid🚶 in💨, sowed🌱 darnel🌿 weeds🌱 among💯 the💯 wheat🌾, then⏭️ dipped🚶 away💨.

27📖 The💯 landowner’s🏡 workers🧑‍🌾 came🚶 over💨: ‘Boss👑, you🫵 planted🌱 good🌱 seed🌰 here📍, right❓ So💯 where❓ did💯 these💯 weeds🌿 come🚶 from💨?’

28📖 ‘An💯 enemy😈 pulled✅ this💯 move🎭,’ he👑 said🗣️. Dem🇯🇲 then⏭️ asked🗣️, ‘Want❓ us🫂 to✨ go🚶 pull🫳 them🌿 up⬆️?’

30📖 ‘Let✅ them🫂 grow🌱 side🤝 by💯 side🤝 until⏳ harvest🌾. When⏳ it💯 comes⏱️, I🙏 will✨ tell🗣️ the💯 harvesters🧑‍🌾: bundle🪢 the💯 weeds🌿 first🥇 and✨ burn🔥 them🌿; bring🫳 the💯 wheat🌾 into💯 my💯 barn🏚️.’”

31📖 He🙏 laid📖 out💬 another💯 parable📖: “Heaven’s☁️ Kingdom👑 is✨ like💯 a💯 mustard🌿 seed🌰 that💯 someone👤 planted🌱 in💯 a💯 field🌾.

32📖 It🌰 starts🔹 tinier🔹 than💯 every💯 other💯 seed🌰, but💯 grows🌱 past📈 the💯 garden🌿 plants🌱 into💯 a💯 tree🌳, and✨ birds🐦 settle🪺 in💯 its💯 branches🌿.”

33📖 He🙏 told🗣️ another💯 parable📖: “Heaven’s☁️ Kingdom👑 is✨ like💯 yeast🧫 a💯 woman👩 tucked🤲 into💯 three🔢 measures🥣 of💯 farinha🇵🇹 until⏳ the💯 whole💯 batch🍞 rose🌱.”

34📖 Jesus🙏 taught📚 the💯 crowds🫂 all💯 this💯 through💯 parables📖; He🙏 didn’t🚫 teach📚 them🫂 without🚫 a💯 story📖.

35📖 That💯 fulfilled✅ the💯 prophet’s📣 words📜: ‘I🙏 will✨ open🗣️ My💯 mouth👄 with💯 parables📖; I🙏 will✨ bring🫳 out💨 truths💡 hidden🤫 since💯 the💯 world🌍 began🌅.’

36📖 Then⏭️ Jesus🙏 sent📤 the💯 crowds🫂 off💨 and✨ went🚶 into💯 the💯 house🏠. His💯 disciples🫂 came🚶 over💨: “Break🧩 down⬇️ the💯 field🌾 weeds🌿 story📖 for💯 us🫂.”

37📖 He🙏 replied💬, “The💯 One👑 sowing🌱 the💯 good🌱 seed🌰 is✨ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤.

38📖 The💯 field🌾 is✨ the💯 world🌍; the💯 good🌱 seed🌰 means✅ Kingdom👑 kids🧒, and✨ the💯 weeds🌿 mean✅ the💯 evil😈 one’s💯 kids🧒.

39📖 The💯 enemy😈 who👀 sowed🌱 them🌿 is✨ the💯 devil😈. The💯 harvest🌾 means✅ this💯 age⏳ ends🏁, and✨ the💯 Schnitter🇩🇪 are✨ angels🪽.

40📖 So✨ just💯 like💯 the💯 weeds🌿 get💯 gathered🧺 and✨ burned🔥, that’s💯 what💯 happens💯 when⏳ this💯 age⏳ ends🏁.

41📖 The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 will✨ send📤 His💯 malachim🇮🇱🪽 to✨ clear🧹 out💨 of💯 His💯 Kingdom👑 everything💯 that💯 trips🚧 people🫂 up💨 and✨ everyone👤 doing💯 evil😈.

42📖 They’ll💯 be✨ thrown🫳 into💯 the💯 fire🔥 furnace🔥. There💯 will✨ be😢 weeping😭 and✨ teeth🦷 grinding😬.

43📖 Then⏭️ the💯 righteous🙏 will✨ shine✨ like💯 the💯 sun☀️ in💯 their💯 Father’s🙏 Kingdom👑. Got💯 ears👂 to✨ hear👂? Listen👂.”

44📖 “Heaven’s☁️ Kingdom👑 is✨ also💯 like💯 treasure💰 hidden🤫 in💯 a💯 field🌾. Someone👤 finds🔎 it💰, covers🤲 it💰 back🔙 up⬆️, then⏭️—all💯 joy😊—sells💸 everything💯 they🫂 own💰 and✨ buys🛒 that💯 field🌾.

45📖 Again🔁, heaven’s☁️ Kingdom👑 is✨ like💯 a💯 merchant🧑‍💼 on🔎 the💯 hunt🔍 for💯 fine💎 pearls⚪.

46📖 When⏳ he👤 finds🔎 one💯 pearl⚪ worth💎 everything💯, he👤 sells💸 it💯 all💯 and✨ buys🛒 that💯 pearl⚪.

47📖 Again🔁, heaven’s☁️ Kingdom👑 is✨ like💯 a💯 dragnet🕸️ thrown🫳 into💯 the💯 sea🌊, scooping🫴 up💨 kifi🇳🇬🐟 of💯 every💯 kind💯.

48📖 When⏳ it🕸️ is✨ full💯, fishers🎣 haul🫳 it💨 onto💯 shore🏖️, sit🪑 down⬇️, sort🧺 the💯 good🐟 into💯 containers🧺, and✨ toss🗑️ the💯 bad🐟 aside↔️.

49📖 That’s💯 how💯 it✨ goes💯 when⏳ the💯 age⏳ ends🏁: angels🪽 will✨ come🚶 and✨ split↔️ the💯 wicked😈 from💯 the💯 righteous🙏.

50📖 They’ll💯 throw🫳 the💯 wicked😈 into💯 the💯 fire🔥 furnace🔥; there💯 will✨ be😢 weeping😭 and✨ teeth🦷 grinding😬.”

51📖 Jesus🙏 asked🗣️, “Did💯 all💯 that💯 land🧠 for💯 you🫂?” They🫂 answered💬, “Yes✅, Lord🙏.”

52📖 He🙏 said🗣️, “So✨ every💯 Scripture📜 teacher📚 trained🎓 in💯 heaven’s☁️ Kingdom👑 is✨ like💯 a💯 homeowner🏠 pulling🫳 both💯 fresh🆕 and✨ vintage📜 treasure💰 from💯 storage🏠.”

53📖 When⏳ Jesus🙏 wrapped✅ up💨 these💯 parables📖, He🙏 moved🚶 on💨.

54📖 Back🔙 in💯 His💯 hometown🏘️, He🙏 taught📚 in💯 their💯 synagogue🕍, and✨ people🫂 were✨ shook😲: “Where❓ did💯 this💯 man👤 get🧠 this💯 wisdom🧠 and✨ these💯 powerful💪 works👐?

55📖 Isn’t❓ this💯 the💯 carpenter’s🪚 son👦? Isn’t❓ His💯 mother👩 Mary👩, and✨ His💯 brothers🫂 James👤, Joses👤, Simon👤, and✨ Judas👤?

56📖 Aren’t❓ all💯 His💯 sisters🫂 here📍 with💯 us🫂? So💯 where❓ did💯 He👤 get🧠 all💯 this💯?”

57📖 They🫂 took🚫 offense😠 at💯 Him👑. Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “A💯 prophet📣 gets💯 no🚫 honour🙏 in💯 their💯 own🏘️ country🌍 or✨ home🏠.”

58📖 Since💯 they🫂 didn’t🚫 trust🙏 Him👑, He🙏 didn’t🚫 do👐 many💯 powerful💪 works👐 there📍.

# Matthew 15 — AdigunBible draft

1📖 Pharisees📜 and✨ Scripture📜 teachers📚 rolled🚶 in💨 from💯 Jerusalem🏙️ and✨ came🚶 at💯 Jesus🙏 with💯 a💯 challenge🗣️.

18📖 But💯 what💯 comes🚪 out💨 of💯 the💯 mouth👄 comes🚪 from💯 obi🇳🇬; that’s💯 what💯 makes💯 a💯 person👤 unclean🦠.

19📖 Evil😈 thoughts💭 come🚪 from💯 the💯 heart🫀—murder🗡️, adultery💔, sexual💔 sins💔, theft🫳, false🤥 testimony⚖️, and✨ blasphemy🚫.

34📖 Jesus🙏 asked🗣️ them🫂, “How💯 many💯 loaves🍞 do💯 you🫂 have💯?” They🫂 said🗣️, “Seven🔢, and✨ a💯 few🔹 small🔹 fish🐟.”

36📖 He🙏 took🫳 the💯 seven🔢 loaves🍞 and✨ fish🐟, gave🙏 thanks🙌, broke👐 them🫂, and✨ passed📤 them🫂 to✨ His💯 disciples🫂; they🫂 served🍽️ the💯 crowd🫂.

37📖 Everybody🫂 ate🍽️ and✨ got💯 full😌. They🫂 scooped🧺 up⬆️ seven🔢 baskets🧺 packed💯 with💯 leftover🥖 pieces🧩.

38📖 Four🔢 thousand🔢 men👨 ate🍽️, besides💯 the💯 women👩 and✨ children🧒.

39📖 Jesus🙏 sent📤 the💯 crowds🫂 away💨, boarded🚣 the💯 boat🚣, and✨ headed🚣 for💯 the💯 Magdala🌍 region📍.
Matthew 16

1📖 Pharisees📜 and✨ Sadducees📜 pulled🚶 up⬆️ to✨ test🪤 Jesus🙏, asking🗣️ Him👑 for💯 a💯 heaven☁️ sign✨.

4📖 This💯 evil😈, unfaithful💔 ìran🇳🇬 keeps🔁 asking📣 for💯 a💯 sign✨; no🚫 sign✨ is✨ coming🚫 except💯 Jonah’s📜 sign✨.” He🙏 left🚶 them🫂 and✨ dipped🚶💨.

6📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “Stay👀 sharp🧠; watch⚠️ out💨 for💯 the💯 Pharisees📜’ and✨ Sadducees📜’ yeast🧫.”

14📖 Dem🇯🇲 answered💬, “Some🔹 say🗣️ John🙏 the💯 Baptiser🫧; some🔹 say🗣️ Elijah📣; others🔹 say🗣️ Jeremiah📜 or✨ one💯 of💯 the💯 prophets📣.”

23📖 Jesus🙏 turned🔄 to✨ Peter👤: “Get🚶 behind↩️ Me👑, Satan😈! You’re🫵 tripping🚧 Me👑 up💨; your💯 focus👀 is✨ on💯 human👥 plans🧠, not🚫 God’s🙏.”

24📖 Then⏭️ Jesus🙏 told🗣️ His💯 disciples🫂, “If💯 anyone👤 wants🫶 to✨ come🚶 with💯 Me👑, they🫂 must💯 say🚫 no🚫 to✨ self🫂, pick🫳 up⬆️ their💯 cross✝️, and✨ follow🚶 Me👑.

25📖 Whoever👤 tries💪 to✨ save🛟 their💯 life💛 will✨ lose💔 it💛; whoever👤 loses💔 their💯 life💛 for💯 Me👑 will✨ find🔎 it💛.

26📖 For💯 real💯, what💯 do💪 you🫵 gain💰 by💯 winning🏆 the💯 whole💯 world🌍 if💯 you🫵 lose💔 your💯 life💛? What💯 could💪 you🫵 possibly💯 trade🔄 for💯 your💯 life💛?

27📖 The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 will✨ come🌅 in💯 His💯 Father’s🙏 glory✨ with💯 His💯 angels🪽, then⏭️ give🎁 each💯 person👤 what💯 their💯 actions✅ deserve⚖️.

Matthew 17

1📖 Six🔢 days📆 later⏭️, Jesus🙏 took🫳 Peter👤, James👤, and✨ his💯 brother🫂 John👤 up⬆️ a💯 high⛰️ mountain⛰️, away↗️ from💯 the💯 crowd🫂.

7📖 Jesus🙏 came🚶 over💨, touched🫶 them🫂, and✨ said🗣️, “Get🌅 up⬆️; don’t🚫 be✨ afraid😰.”

13📖 Then⏭️ the💯 disciples🫂 understood🧠 Jesus🙏 meant🗣️ John🙏 the💯 Baptiser🫧.

14📖 When⏳ they🫂 reached🚶 the💯 crowd🫂, a💯 man👤 came🚶 up⬆️, knelt🙇 before💯 Jesus🙏, and✨ said🗣️:

15📖 “Lord🙏, please🙏 show💛 miséricorde🇫🇷 to💯 my💯 son👦. He👦 has✨ epilepsy🧠 and✨ suffers😣 badly💔; he👦 often🔁 falls⬇️ into💯 fire🔥 and✨ into💯 ruwa🇳🇬.

16📖 I🙏 brought🚶 him👦 to✨ Your💯 disciples🫂, but💯 dem🇯🇲 couldn’t🚫 heal🩹 him👦.”

17📖 Jesus🙏 answered💬, “Faithless😔, twisted🌀 generation👥! How⏳ long⏳ must💯 I🙏 stay🧍 with💯 y’all🫵? How⏳ long⏳ must💯 I🙏 put🫳 up💨 with💯 this💯? Bring🫳 him👦 here📍 to✨ Me👑.”

19📖 Later⏭️, the💯 disciples🫂 came🚶 to✨ Jesus🙏 in💯 private🤫 and✨ asked🗣️, “Why❓ couldn’t🚫 we🫂 drive👊 it👿 out💨?”

20📖 He🙏 answered💬, “Because💯 you🫵 don’t🚫 believe🙏. For💯 real💯, if💯 your💯 okwukwe🇳🇬 is✨ like💯 a💯 mustard🌿 seed🌰, you🫵 can💪 tell🗣️ this💯 mountain⛰️, ‘Move🚶 from💯 here📍 to💯 there📍,’ and✨ it’ll💯 move🌄. Nothing🚫 will✨ be✨ impossible💪 for💯 you🫵.

21📖 This💯 kind💯 only💯 comes🚪 out💨 through💯 prayer🙏 and✨ fasting🥣.”

22📖 While⏳ they🫂 were✨ staying🧍 in💯 Galilee🌍, Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 is✨ about⏳ to✨ be✨ handed🫳 over💨 to✨ people👥.

23📖 They🫂 will✨ kill💀 Him👑, but💯 on💯 ọjọ́🇳🇬 kẹta🇳🇬, He🙏 will✨ get✨ raised🌅.” The💯 disciples🫂 were✨ crushed💔 with💯 sorrow😞.

26📖 Peter👤 answered💬, “Strangers👥.” Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him👤, “Then⏭️ the💯 children🧒 are✨ exempt✅.

27📖 But💯 so✨ we🫂 don’t🚫 trip🚧 them🫂 up💨, go🚶 to✨ the💯 sea🌊, cast🎣 a💯 hook🪝, and✨ take🫳 the💯 first🥇 pez🇪🇸🐟 that💯 comes⬆️ up💨. Open🔓 its💯 mouth👄 and✨ you🫵 will✨ find🔎 a💯 stater🪙 moeda🇵🇹 inside📍. Take🫳 it💰 and✨ give🎁 it💰 to✨ them🫂 for💯 both💯 of💯 us🫂.”
Matthew 18

1📖 Right⏱️ then📍, the💯 disciples🫂 rolled🚶 up⬆️ to✨ Jesus🙏 asking💭, “So💭 who❓ ranks🏆 highest⬆️ in💯 heaven’s☁️ Kingdom👑?”

6📖 But💯 if💯 anyone👤 trips🚧 up💨 one💯 of💯 these💯 little🔹 ones🧒 who💯 trust🙏 Me👑, better💯 for💯 them👤 to✨ have💯 a💯 huge💯 millstone🪨 tied⛓️ around💯 their💯 neck🧣 and✨ sink🌊 into💯 the💯 teku🇳🇬.

7📖 “Woe😔 to✨ this💯 world🌍, with💯 all💯 its💯 traps🚧 that💯 make💯 people🫂 stumble🚧! Those💯 traps🚧 will✨ show🌅 up⬆️, sure💯—but💯 woe😔 to✨ the💯 one👤 who💯 sets🪤 them💯.

8📖 If💯 your💯 hand✋ or✨ foot🦶 keeps🔁 tripping🚧 you🫵, cut✂️ it💯 off💨 and✨ toss🫳 it💯 away💨. Better💯 to✨ enter🚪 life🌱 hurt🩹 or✨ limping🩼 than💯 keep💯 both💯 hands✋ or✨ feet🦶 and✨ end💥 up⬆️ in💯 eternal♾️ fire🔥.

9📖 If💯 your💯 eye👁️ keeps🔁 pulling🫳 you🫵 into💯 sin💔, tear🫳 it💯 out💨 and✨ toss🫳 it💯 away💨. Better💯 to✨ enter🚪 life🌱 with💯 one☝️ eye👁️ than💯 keep💯 both💯 and✨ get💨 thrown🫳 into💯 Gehenna🔥 fire🔥.

10📖 Don’t🚫 treat🤝 even💯 one💯 of💯 these💯 little🔹 ones🧒 like💯 they’re🚫 nothing💔. Their💯 angels🪽 in💯 heaven☁️ always🔁 see👀 the💯 face👑 of💯 My💯 Father🙏 there📍.

11📖 The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 came🚶 to✨ save🛟 the💯 lost🐑.”

12📖 “Real💯 question💭: if💯 someone👤 has💯 a💯 hundred💯 sheep🐑 and✨ one☝️ wanders🚶 off💨, won’t🚫 they🫂 leave💨 the💯 ninety-nine🔢 in💯 the💯 hills⛰️ and✨ go🔎 find🔍 the💯 one☝️ that💯 strayed🚶?

13📖 And✨ if💯 they🫂 find🔎 it🐑, no💯 cap🧢, they🫂 celebrate🎉 that💯 one☝️ more💯 than💯 the💯 ninety-nine🔢 who💯 stayed🧍 put📍.

14📖 Same💯 vibe💯 with💯 your💯 Father🙏 in💯 heaven☁️: He🙏 doesn’t🚫 want🫶 even💯 one💯 of💯 these💯 little🔹 ones🧒 to✨ be✨ lost💔.

15📖 “If💯 your💯 ach🇮🇱 sins💔 against💯 you🫵, go🚶 talk🗣️ it💯 out🗣️ one-on-one🤝 and✨ show👀 them🫂 where📍 they💯 went🚶 wrong💔. If💯 they🫂 listen👂, you’ve💯 got🫶 your💯 nwanna🇳🇬 back🔙.

16📖 If💯 they🫂 won’t🚫 hear👂 you🫵, bring🫂 one☝️ or✨ two✌️ more➕ with💯 you🫵, so✨ every💯 word🗣️ can💪 be✨ locked🔒 in💯 by💯 two✌️ or✨ three🔢 witnesses👀.

17📖 If💯 they🫂 still💯 refuse🚫 to✨ listen👂, take🫳 it💯 to✨ the💯 assembly🫂. If💯 they🫂 won’t🚫 hear👂 the💯 assembly🫂 either↔️, treat🤝 them🫂 like💯 an💯 outsider🌍 or✨ tax💸 collector🧾.

18📖 “For💯 real💯, whatever💯 you🫵 bind🪢 on💯 earth🌍 will✨ already💯 be✨ bound🪢 in💯 heaven☁️; whatever💯 you🫵 release🔓 on💯 earth🌍 will✨ already💯 be✨ released🔓 in💯 heaven☁️.

19📖 Again🔄, I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵: if💯 two✌️ of💯 you🫵 agree🤝 here🌍 about💯 anything💭 you🫵 ask🙏 for💯, My💯 Father🙏 in💯 heaven☁️ will✨ do✅ it💯 for💯 them🫂.

20📖 Wherever📍 two✌️ or✨ three🔢 gather🫂 in💯 My💯 name📛, I’m🙏 right📍 there📍 with💯 them🫂.”

21📖 Peter👤 came🚶 over💨 and✨ asked🗣️, “Lord🙏, how💭 many💯 times🔢 must💯 I🙏 forgive🫶 my💯 ach🇮🇱 when💯 they🫂 sin💔 against💯 me🫵? Seven🔢 times🔢?”

23📖 “Heaven’s☁️ Kingdom👑 is✨ like💯 a💯 king👑 who💯 decided🧠 to✨ settle🧾 accounts💸 with💯 his💯 servants🫂.

8📖 A💯 massive💯 crowd🫂 threw👐 their💯 clothes👘 across💯 the💯 road🛣️; others🫂 snipped✂️ branches🌿 off💨 trees🌳 and✨ spread👐 them🌿 on💯 the💯 road🛣️ too💯.

10📖 When⏳ Jesus🙏 entered🚶 Jerusalem🏙️, the💯 whole💯 city🏙️ buzzed📢: “Who❓ is✨ this💯?”

11📖 Dem🇯🇲 were🗣️ like💬, “That’s💯 Jesus🙏, the💯 prophet📣 from💯 Nazareth🏘️ in💯 Galilee🌍.”

12📖 Jesus🙏 entered🚶 God’s🙏 temple🏛️ and✨ cleared🚪 out💨 everybody🫂 buying🛒 and✨ selling💸 there📍. He🙏 turned🔄 over💥 the💯 money💸 changers💱 tables🪑 and✨ seats🪑 of💯 the💯 people🫂 selling🕊️ doves🕊️.

13📖 He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “Scripture📜 says🗣️, ‘My💯 house🏠 will✨ be✨ called📣 a💯 house🏠 of💯 prayer🙏,’ but💯 you've🫂 turned🔄 it💯 into💯 a💯 thieves🦹' den🏚️!”

14📖 The💯 blind🙈 and✨ boiteux🇫🇷 came🚶 to✨ Him👑 in💯 the💯 temple🏛️, and✨ He🙏 healed🩹 them🫂.

15📖 The💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ Bible📜 teachers📚 saw👀 Jesus🙏' amazing✨ works👐 and✨ heard👂 the💯 children🧒 shouting📣 in💯 the💯 temple🏛️, “Hosanna🙌 to✨ David's👑 Son👑!” They🫂 got😠 angry😠.

16📖 They🫂 challenged🗣️ Him👑, “You👑 hear👂 what💭 these💯 kids🧒 are✨ saying🗣️, right❓?” Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Yes✅. Haven’t🚫 you🫵 read📚, ‘From💨 the💯 mouths👄 of💯 ọmọ🇳🇬 and✨ nursing🤱 babies👶, You👑 made✨ praise🙌 complete💯’?”

17📖 Then⏭️ He🙏 left🚶 them🫂, dipped🚶 out💨 of💯 the💯 city🏙️ to✨ Bethany🏘️, and✨ stayed🛌 there📍.

18📖 At💯 morning🌅, while⏳ Jesus🙏 was✨ heading🚶 back🔙 into💯 town🏙️, hunger😋 hit💥.

19📖 He🙏 spotted👀 a💯 fig🌿 tree🌳 by💯 the💯 road🛣️ and✨ walked🚶 up💨, but💯 it💯 had✨ nothing🚫 except💯 leaves🍃. He🙏 told🗣️ it💯, “You🫵 won’t🚫 grow🌱 fruit🍇 again🔁—ever💯.” Right⚡ then⏱️ the💯 tree🌳 withered🥀.

20📖 The💯 disciples🫂 saw👀 and✨ were✨ shook😲: “Wait❓ how❓ did💯 that💯 tree🌳 dry🥀 up⚡ so✨ fast💨?”

21📖 Jesus🙏 answered💬, “For💯 real💯, if💯 you🫂 have💯 okwukwe🇳🇬 and✨ don't🚫 doubt💭, you🫂 will✨ do✅ more💯 than💯 what💭 happened💥 to✨ that💯 fig🌿 tree🌳. You🫂 can💪 tell🗣️ this💯 mountain⛰️, ‘Be✨ lifted⬆️ and✨ thrown🫳 into💯 the💯 teku🇳🇬,’ and✨ it💯 would💯 happen✅.

22📖 Whatever💯 you🫂 ask🙏 for💯 in💯 prayer🙏 with💯 faith🙏, you'll💯 receive🎁.”

23📖 Jesus🙏 went🚶 into💯 the💯 temple🏛️ to✨ teach📚. The💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ people’s🫂 elders👴 pressed🗣️ Him👑: “By💯 what❓ authority👑 are✨ You👑 doing💯 all💯 this💯? Who❓ handed🎁 You👑 that💯 authority👑?”

24📖 Jesus🙏 answered💬, “I'll🙏 ask🗣️ you🫂 one💯 question❓. If💯 you🫂 answer💬, I'll🙏 tell🗣️ you🫂 where💯 My💯 authority👑 comes🚶 from💨.

25📖 John's🙏 baptism🫧—where📍 did💯 it💯 come🚶 from💨, heaven☁️ or✨ people👥?” They🫂 talked🗣️ it💯 over🔄: “If💯 we🫂 say🗣️ heaven☁️, He'll💯 ask🗣️ why💭 we🫂 didn't🚫 believe🙏 John🙏.

26📖 But💯 if💯 we🫂 say🗣️ people👥, we're💯 scared😰 of💯 the💯 crowd🫂; everyone🫂 believes🙏 John🙏 was✨ a💯 prophet📣.”

27📖 So✨ they🫂 told🗣️ Jesus🙏, “We🫂 don’t🚫 know🧠.” Jesus🙏 replied💬, “Then⏭️ I🙏 won’t🚫 tell🗣️ you🫂 what💭 authority👑 I🙏 use💪 for💯 these💯 things💯 either↔️.

28📖 What💭 do💯 you🫂 think🧠? A💯 father👨 had💯 two✌️ sons👦. He🙏 told🗣️ the💯 first🥇, ‘Son👦, go🚶 trabalhar🇵🇹 in💯 my💯 vineyard🍇 today📆.’

29📖 The💯 son👦 said🗣️, ‘No🚫,’ but💯 later⏭️ changed🔄 his💯 mind🧠 and✨ went🚶.

30📖 The💯 father👨 told🗣️ the💯 second🥈 son👦 the💯 same💯 thing💯. ‘I'm🙏 going🚶, sir🙏,’ he👦 said🗣️, but💯 he👦 never🚫 went🚶. Which❓ son👦 did✅ what💯 his💯 father👨 wanted🫶?”

31📖 They🫂 answered💬, “The💯 first🥇.” Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “I'm🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂: tax💸 collectors🧾 and✨ sex💔 workers🫂 are✨ entering🚪 God's🙏 Kingdom👑 ahead➡️ of💯 you🫂.

32📖 John🙏 came🚶 showing🧭 you🫂 the💯 right✅ way🛣️, but💯 you🫵 didn’t🚫 trust🙏 him👤. Tax💸 collectors🧾 and✨ sex💔 workers🫂 did✅ trust🙏 him👤. You🫵 saw👀 it💯 happen💥, yet⏭️ still💯 wouldn’t🚫 turn↩️ around🔙 and✨ believe🙏.

33📖 “Listen👂 to✨ another💯 story📖. A💯 landowner🏡 planted🌱 a💯 vineyard🍇, built🏗️ a💯 fence🪵, dug⛏️ a💯 wine🍷 press🪨, built🏗️ a💯 lookout🗼 tower🏰, rented📝 it💯 to✨ farmers🧑‍🌾, then⏭️ left🚶 for💯 another💯 country🌍.

34📖 When⏳ harvest🌾 season⏱️ came📍 close📍, he🙏 sent📤 his💯 servants🫂 to✨ collect🧺 his💯 share🍇 of💯 the💯 fruto🇪🇸.

35📖 The💯 farmers🧑‍🌾 grabbed🤜 the💯 servants🫂: they🫂 beat👊 one💯, killed💀 another💯, and✨ stoned🪨 another💯.

36📖 The💯 owner🏡 sent📤 even💯 more💯 servants🫂 than💯 before⏪, but💯 the💯 farmers🧑‍🌾 treated🤜 them🫂 the💯 same💯 way💯.

37📖 At💯 last🏁 he🙏 sent📤 his💯 son👦, thinking💭, ‘They🫂 will✨ respect🙌 my💯 son👦.’

38📖 But💯 the💯 farmers🧑‍🌾 saw👀 his💯 son👦 and✨ said🤫 to✨ each💯 other🫂, ‘That’s💯 the💯 heir👑. Come🚶 on💨, let’s🫂 kill💀 him👦 and✨ grab🫳 what💭 he👑 would💯 inherit💰!’

39📖 They🫂 dragged🫳 him👦 out💨 of💯 the💯 vineyard🍇 and✨ killed💀 him👦.

40📖 The💯 vineyard🍇 owner🏡 is✨ coming🌅 back🔙—what💭 do💯 you🫂 think🧠 he'll🙏 do✅ to✨ those💯 farmers🧑‍🌾?”

41📖 They🫂 replied💬, “He🙏 will✨ bring💥 those💯 wicked😈 men🧑‍🌾 to💯 a💯 miserable😔 end💀, then⏭️ rent📝 the💯 vineyard🍇 to✨ other🧑‍🌾 farmers🫂 who👀 will✨ give📤 him🙏 the💯 fruit🍇 when⏳ it’s💯 due📆.”

42📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “For💯 real💯, haven’t🚫 you🫵 read📚 Scripture📜? ‘The💯 stone🪨 the💯 builders👷 rejected🚫 became✨ the💯 cornerstone🧱. The💯 Lord👑 did✅ this☝️, and✨ it’s💯 amazing😲 right👀 in💯 our🫂 eyes👀.’”

43📖 So✨ I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵: God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 will✨ be✨ taken🫳 from💨 you🫵 and✨ given🎁 to✨ a💯 people🌍 who👀 grow🌱 its💯 fruit🍇.

44📖 Whoever👤 falls⬇️ on💯 this💯 stone🪨 will✨ be✨ smashed💥; if💯 it💯 falls⬇️ on💯 someone👤, they'll💯 be✨ crushed🪨 into💯 dust🌫️.”

45📖 The💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ Pharisees📜 heard👂 His💯 parables📖 and✨ clocked👀 that💯 He🙏 was✨ talking🗣️ about💯 them🫂.

46📖 They🫂 wanted🫶 to✨ arrest⛓️ Him👑, but💯 the💯 crowds🫂 scared😰 them🫂, because💭 people🫂 believed🙏 Jesus🙏 was✨ a💯 prophet📣.
Matthew 22
# Matthew 22 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Matthew 22. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 Jesus🙏 answered💬 them🫂 with💯 another💯 parable📖:

2📖 “Heaven’s☁️ Kingdom👑 is✨ like💯 a💯 king👑 who👀 threw🎉 a💯 Hochzeit🇩🇪 feast🍽️ for💯 his💯 son👦.

3📖 He👑 sent📤 his💯 servants🫂 to✨ call📣 in💯 the💯 invited💌 guests🫂, but💯 they🫂 wouldn’t🚫 pull🚶 up💨.

4📖 He👑 sent📤 other💯 servants🫂: ‘Tell🗣️ the💯 guests🫂, “The💯 meal🍽️ is✨ pronto🇵🇹! My💯 cattle🐄 and✨ fattened🐂 animals🐄 are✨ slaughtered🔪; the💯 whole💯 spread🍽️ is✨ set✅. Come🚶 to✨ the💯 festin🇫🇷!”’

5📖 But💯 Dem🇯🇲 brushed🤷 it💯 off💨 and✨ went🚶 their💯 own💯 ways🛣️—one💯 to✨ his💯 farm🌾, another💯 back🔙 to✨ business💼.

6📖 The💯 rest💯 grabbed🤜 the💯 king’s👑 servants🫂, treated🤕 them🫂 shamefully😣, and✨ killed💀 them🫂.

7📖 The💯 king👑 burned🔥 with💯 anger😡; he👑 sent📤 his💯 armies🪖, destroyed💥 those💯 murderers🗡️, and✨ burned🔥 their💯 city🏙️.

8📖 “Then⏭️ he👑 told🗣️ his💯 servants🫂, ‘The💯 wedding💍 is✨ set✅, but💯 the💯 people🫂 I🙏 invited💌 weren’t🚫 worthy🙇.

9📖 Go🚶 out💨 to✨ the💯 highway🛣️ intersections🛣️ and✨ invite📣 whoever👤 you🫵 find🔎 to✨ the💯 wedding💍 feast🍽️.’

10📖 The💯 servants🫂 hit🚶 the💯 highways🛣️ and✨ brought🫂 in💨 everybody👥 they👀 found🔎, good✅ and✨ bad❌ alike💯. The💯 wedding💍 hall🏛️ filled🫂 with💯 guests🫂.

11📖 “When⏳ the💯 king👑 came🚶 in💨 to✨ check👀 the💯 guests🫂, he👑 noticed👀 a💯 man👤 without🚫 wedding💍 clothes👘.

12📖 ‘Friend🫂, how❓ did💯 you🫵 get🚪 in💨 here📍 with🚫 no🚫 wedding💍 clothes👘?’ the💯 king👑 asked🗣️. The💯 man👤 went🤐 speechless😶.

13📖 The💯 king👑 told🗣️ his💯 attendants🫂, ‘Tie🪢 his💯 hands✋ and✨ feet🦶, take🫳 him👤 outside🚪, and✨ throw🫳 him👤 into💯 outer🌑 duhu🇳🇬, where📍 there’s😭 weeping😭 and✨ teeth🦷 grinding😬.’

14📖 “Many🫂 are✨ invited📣, but💯 few🔹 are✨ chosen✅.”

15📖 Then⏭️ the💯 Pharisees📜 huddled🤝 up💨 to✨ plot🧠 how💭 to✨ trap🪤 Jesus🙏 with💯 His👑 words🗣️.

16📖 They🫂 sent📤 their💯 disciples🫂 with💯 Herod’s👑 supporters🫂 to✨ butter🎭 Him👑 up⬆️: “Teacher📚, we🫂 know🧠 You👑 are✨ honest✅ and✨ teach📚 God’s🙏 way🛣️ truthfully💯, without🚫 playing🎭 favourites⚖️.

17📖 So✨ tell🗣️ us🫂 straight💬: is✨ paying💸 taxes🪙 to✨ Caesar👑 lawful📜 or✨ not🚫?”

18📖 Jesus🙏 clocked👀 their💯 wicked💔 plan🧠 and✨ said🗣️, “Why❓ are✨ you🫵 testing🪤 Me👑, you🫵 hypocrites🎭?

19📖 “Show🫳 Me👑 the💯 tax💸 coin🪙.” They🫂 passed📤 Him👑 a💯 denarius🪙.

20📖 He🙏 asked🗣️, “Whose❓ image👤 and✨ inscription📜 are✨ stamped🔏 on💯 this💯?”

21📖 “Caesar’s👑,” they🫂 answered💬. Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “Then⏭️ give🎁 Caesar👑 what💯 is✨ Caesar’s👑, and✨ give🎁 God🙏 what💯 is✨ God’s🙏.”

22📖 They🫂 were✨ amazed😲, left🚶 Him👑, and✨ went🚶 away💨.

23📖 That💯 same💯 day📆, Sadducees📜—the💯 crew🫂 who🚫 say🗣️ there’s🚫 no🚫 resurrection🌅—came🚶 to✨ test🧠 Him👑 with💯 a💯 question🗣️.

24📖 “Teacher📚, Moses🙏 said🗣️, ‘If💯 a💯 man👤 dies💀 with🚫 no🚫 children🧒, his💯 brother🫂 must💯 marry💍 his💯 wife👰 and✨ raise🌱 ọmọ🇳🇬 for✨ his💯 brother🫂.

25📖 There📍 were✨ seven🔢 brothers🫂 among💯 us🫂. The💯 first🥇 married💍, then⏭️ died💀 with🚫 no🚫 children🧒, leaving🫳 his💯 wife👰 to✨ his💯 brother🫂.

26📖 The💯 second🥈 did✅ the💯 same💯, then⏭️ the💯 third🥉, all💯 the💯 way💯 through💯 the💯 seventh🔢.

27📖 After💯 all💯 seven🔢, the💯 woman👩 died💀 too🤝.

28📖 So✨ at💯 the💯 resurrection🌅, whose💭 wife👰 will✨ she👩 be💍? All💯 seven🔢 had💍 her👰.’”

29📖 Jesus🙏 answered💬, “You🫵 have✨ this💯 wrong🚫 because💭 you🫵 don’t🚫 know🧠 Scripture📜 or✨ God’s🙏 power💪.

30📖 At💯 the💯 resurrection🌅, people🫂 won’t🚫 marry💍 or✨ be✨ given💍 in💯 marriage💒; they’ll🫂 be✨ like💯 God’s🙏 angels🪽 in💯 heaven☁️.

31📖 About💯 the💯 dead💀 being✨ raised🌅, haven’t🚫 you🫵 read📚 what💯 God🙏 said🗣️ to✨ you🫵?

32📖 ‘I🙏 am✨ the💯 God🙏 of💯 Abraham🙏, Isaac🙏, and✨ Jacob🙏.’ God🙏 isn’t🚫 God🙏 of💯 the💯 dead💀; He🙏 is✨ God🙏 of💯 the💯 living🌱.”

33📖 The💯 crowds🫂 heard👂 this💯 and✨ were✨ amazed😲 at💯 His💯 teaching📚.

34📖 When⏳ the💯 Pharisees📜 heard👂 Jesus🙏 had✨ shut🤫 the💯 Sadducees📜 down🫂, they🫂 gathered🤝 together🫂.

35📖 One💯 of💯 them🫂, a💯 lawyer⚖️, tried🪤 to✨ test🪤 Him👑 with💯 a💯 question🗣️:

36📖 “Teacher📚, which❓ commandment📜 is✨ the💯 greatest🏆 in💯 the💯 law📜?”

37📖 Jesus🙏 answered💬, “‘Love🫶 the💯 Lord🙏 your💯 God🙏 with💯 all💯 your💯 óbì🇳🇬, all💯 your💯 soul🫂, and✨ all💯 your💯 mind🧠.

38📖 That's💯 the💯 first🥇 and✨ greatest🏆 commandment📜.

39📖 The💯 second🥈 is✨ right🤝 alongside💯 it💯: ‘Love🫶 your💯 prójimo🇪🇸 as💯 yourself🫵.’

40📖 The💯 whole💯 law📜 and✨ the💯 prophets📣 rest⚖️ on💯 these💯 two✌️ commands📜.”

41📖 While⏳ the💯 Pharisees📜 were✨ gathered🫂, Jesus🙏 asked🗣️ them🫂 a💯 question💭:

42📖 “What💭 do💯 you🫵 think🧠 about💯 the💯 Mashiach🇮🇱? Whose❓ son👦 is✨ He👑?” They🫂 answered💬, “David’s👑.”

43📖 Jesus🙏 asked🗣️, “So✨ how❓ does💭 David👑, speaking🗣️ by💯 the💯 Spirit👻, call📣 Him👑 ‘Lord🙏’?

44📖 ‘The💯 Lord🙏 said🗣️ to✨ my💯 Lord🙏, “Sit🪑 at💯 My💯 right👉 hand✋ until⏳ I🙏 make🫳 Your💯 enemies⚔️ a💯 footstool🪑 beneath⬇️ Your💯 feet🦶.”’

45📖 If💯 David👑 calls📣 Him👑 Lord🙏, how❓ can💪 He🙏 also✨ be✨ David's👑 son👦?”

46📖 No💯 one👤 could💪 answer💬 Him👑; from💯 that💯 day📆 forward➡️, nobody🚫 dared😰 ask🗣️ Him👑 another💯 question❓.
Matthew 23
# Matthew 23 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Matthew 23. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 Then⏭️ Jesus🙏 spoke🗣️ to✨ the💯 crowds🫂 and✨ His💯 disciples🫂:

2📖 “The💯 Scripture📜 teachers📚 and✨ Pharisees📜 sit🪑 in💯 Moses📜’ seat🪑.

3📖 So✨ follow🚶 what💯 they🫂 tell🗣️ you🫵 to✨ observe👀 and✨ live🌱 it💯 out✅, but💯 don’t🚫 copy📋 their💯 actions👐: dem🇯🇲 talk🗣️ it💯, but💯 don’t🚫 walk🚶 it💯.

4📖 They🫂 bundle🪢 up💯 heavy💥 loads🧳 and✨ dump🫳 them💯 on💯 other💯 people’s🫂 shoulders🫂, yet⏭️ won’t🚫 lift🫳 even💯 one💯 finger☝️ to✨ help🤲.

5📖 They🫂 do✅ it💯 all💯 for📸 the💯 spotlight📸: their💯 tefillin🇮🇱 pouches🧳 get📏 extra💯 wide↔️, and✨ their💯 robe👘 tassels🧵 extra💯 long📏.

6📖 They🫂 chase🏃 the💯 best💯 seats🪑 at💯 feasts🍽️ and✨ the💯 front-row🥇 spots🪑 in💯 synagogues🕍.

7📖 They🫂 love🫶 public📢 shout-outs📣 in💯 the💯 marketplaces🏙️ and✨ everybody🫂 calling📣 them🫂 ‘Rabbi📚, Rabbi📚.’

8📖 But💯 don’t🚫 chase🏃 titles🏷️: you🫵 have✨ one💯 Teacher📚—the💯 Christ👑—and✨ all💯 of💯 you🫵 are✨ siblings🫂.

9📖 Don’t🚫 give📣 anybody👤 on💯 earth🌍 the💯 title🏷️ ‘father👨’; you🫵 have✨ one💯 Father🙏 in💯 Himmel🇩🇪.

10📖 And✨ don’t🚫 chase🏃 the💯 title🏷️ ‘master👑’ either↔️; you🫵 have✨ one💯 Master👑, the💯 Christ👑.

# Matthew 25 — AdigunBible draft

1📖 “Then⏭️ heaven's☁️ Kingdom👑 will✨ be✨ like💯 ten🔟 virgins👩 who👀 grabbed🫳 their💯 lamps🪔 and✨ went🚶 to✨ meet🤝 the💯 groom🤵.

3📖 The💯 foolish🫥 ones🫂 brought🫳 their💯 lamps🪔 but💯 didn't🚫 bring🫳 extra➕ shemen🇮🇱🛢️.

4📖 The💯 wise🧠 ones🫂 brought🫳 shemen🇮🇱🛢️ in💯 containers🫙 along💯 with💯 their💯 lamps🪔—ready✅ to✨ keep💡 the💯 Licht🇩🇪🕯️ shining✨.

5📖 The💯 groom🤵 took⏳ longer⏳ than💯 expected💭, so✨ all💯 ten🔟 got😴 sleepy😴 and✨ dozed💤 off💨.

6📖 At💯 midnight🌙, someone👤 shouted📣, ‘The💯 groom🤵 is✨ here📍! Come🚶 out💨 and✨ meet🤝 him👤!’

7📖 All💯 ten🔟 woke🌅 up⬆️ and✨ trimmed✂️ their💯 lamps🪔.

9📖 The💯 wise🧠 ones🫂 said🗣️, ‘There💯 may💯 not🚫 be✨ enough💯 for💯 all💯 of💯 us🫂. Go🚶 to✨ sellers🧑‍💼 and✨ buy🛒 your💯 own💯.’

10📖 While⏳ dem🇯🇲 went🚶 to✨ buy🛒 shemen🇮🇱🛢️, the💯 groom🤵 arrived🌅. Those💯 who👀 were✨ ready✅ went🚶 in💨 with💯 him🤵 to✨ the💯 wedding💍 feast🍽️, and✨ the💯 door🚪 closed🔒.

12📖 He🤵 answered💬, ‘For💯 real💯, I🙏 don't🚫 know🧠 you🫂.’

13📖 So✨ stay👀 awake🌙; you🫵 don’t🚫 know🧠 the💯 day📆 or✨ hour⏱️ when💯 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 comes🌅.”

14📖 “It's💯 like💯 someone👤 leaving🚶 for💯 another💯 country🌍 who👀 called📣 his💯 servants🫂 and✨ trusted🤝 them🫂 with💯 his💯 property💰.

15📖 He👑 gave🎁 one💯 servant🫂 five🔢 talents🪙, another💯 two✌️, another💯 one☝️, matching📏 each💯 person’s👤 ability💪. Then⏭️ he👑 left🚶 on💯 his💯 trip🧳.

16📖 The💯 servant🫂 with💯 five🔢 talents🪙 put🛠️ the💯 five🔢 talents🪙 to✨ work💼 trading🔄 and✨ came🚶 back🔙 with💯 five🔢 more➕.

17📖 The💯 one👤 with💯 two✌️ did✅ the💯 same💯 and✨ earned💰 two✌️ more➕.

18📖 But💯 the💯 servant🫂 with💯 one☝️ talent🪙 dug⛏️ a💯 hole🕳️ and✨ buried🫳 his💯 master's👑 money💰.

19📖 After⏳ a💯 long⏳ time⏱️, the💯 master👑 returned🔙 and✨ settled🧾 accounts💰 with💯 them🫂.

20📖 The💯 servant🫂 who👀 got💯 five🔢 talents🪙 came🚶 with💯 five🔢 more➕: ‘Master👑, you🫵 trusted🤝 me🫂 with💯 five🔢. Look👀, I🙏 earned💰 five🔢 more➕.’

21📖 His💯 master👑 said🗣️, ‘Well👏 done✅, good💯 and✨ faithful🤝 servant🫂! You🫵 handled🛠️ a💯 little💯 well✅; I'll🙏 put📋 you🫵 over💨 a💯 lot💯 more📈. Come🚶 celebrate🎉 with💯 me👑.’

22📖 The💯 servant🫂 who👀 got💯 two✌️ talents🪙 came🚶 too🤝: ‘Master👑, you🫵 trusted🤝 me🫂 with💯 two✌️. Look👀, I🙏 earned💰 two✌️ more➕.’

23📖 His💯 master👑 told🗣️ him👤, ‘Well👏 done✅, good💯 and✨ faithful🤝 servant🫂! You🫵 handled🛠️ a💯 little💯 well✅; I'll🙏 put📋 you🫵 over💨 a💯 lot💯 more📈. Come🚶 celebrate🎉 with💯 me👑.’

24📖 Then⏭️ the💯 servant🫂 with💯 one☝️ talent🪙 came🚶: ‘Master👑, I🙏 know🧠 you’re💯 tough💥. You🫵 reap🌾 where💯 you🫵 didn’t🚫 sow🌱 and✨ gather🧺 where💯 you🫵 didn’t🚫 scatter🌱.

25📖 I🙏 was✨ scared😰 in💯 my💯 obi🇳🇬, so✨ I🙏 hid🫳 your💯 money💰 in💯 the💯 ground🌍. Here👀 is✨ what💭 belongs🫶 to✨ you🫵.’

26📖 His💯 master👑 answered💬, ‘You🫵 wicked😈, lazy😴 servant🫂! So✨ you🫵 knew🧠 I🙏 reap🌾 where💯 I🙏 didn’t🚫 sow🌱 and✨ gather🧺 where💯 I🙏 didn’t🚫 scatter🌱?

27📖 Then⏭️ you🫂 should💯 have💯 put📤 my💯 money💰 with💯 bankers🏦, so✨ I'd💯 get💰 it💯 back🔙 with💯 interest📈 when⏳ I🙏 returned🔙.

28📖 Take🫳 the💯 talent🪙 from💯 him🫂 and✨ give🎁 it💯 to✨ the💯 servant🫂 who👀 has💯 ten🔟 talents🪙.

29📖 Everyone👤 who👀 has💯 will✨ receive🎁 more📈 and✨ have💯 plenty💯. But💯 whoever👤 doesn't🚫 have💯 will✨ lose💔 even💯 what💯 they🫂 have💯.

30📖 Throw🫳 this💯 worthless🫥 servant🫂 into💯 outer🌑 darkness🌑. There📍 will✨ be😭 weeping😭 and✨ teeth🦷 grinding😬.’”

31📖 “When⏳ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 comes🌅 in💯 His💯 glory✨ with💯 all💯 His💯 holy🕊️ angels🪽, He🙏 will✨ sit🪑 on💯 His💯 glorious✨ throne👑.

32📖 Every💯 nation🌍 will✨ gather🫂 before💯 Him👑. He🙏 will✨ separate↔️ people🫂 from💯 each💯 other💯, like💯 a💯 shepherd🧑‍🌾 sorting🧺 sheep🐑 from💯 goats🐐.

33📖 He🙏 will✨ place📍 the💯 sheep🐑 on💯 His💯 right👉 and✨ the💯 goats🐐 on💯 His💯 left👈.

34📖 “Then⏭️ the💯 King👑 will✨ tell🗣️ those💯 on💯 His💯 right👉, ‘Come🚶, you🫂 blessed🙏 by💯 My💯 Father🙏. Inherit🎁 the💯 Kingdom👑 prepared✅ for💯 you🫂 since💯 the💯 world's🌍 beginning🌅.

35📖 I🙏 was✨ hungry😋, and✨ you🫂 gave🎁 Me👑 oúnjẹ🇳🇬🍲. I🙏 was✨ thirsty🥤, and✨ you🫂 gave🎁 Me👑 ruwa🇳🇬💧.

36📖 I🙏 was✨ an💯 étranger🇫🇷, and✨ you🫂 welcomed🤲 Me👑. I🙏 had💯 no🚫 clothes👕, and✨ you🫂 clothed🧥 Me👑. I🙏 was✨ sick🤒, and✨ you🫂 cared🤲 for💯 Me👑. I🙏 was✨ in💯 prison🔒, and✨ you🫂 came🚶 to✨ Me👑.’

37📖 “Then⏭️ the💯 justos🇪🇸🙏 will✨ ask🗣️, ‘Lord🙏, when⏱️ did💯 we🫂 see👀 You👑 hungry😋 and✨ feed🍽️ You👑, or✨ thirsty🥤 and✨ give🎁 You👑 água🇵🇹💧?

38📖 When⏱️ did💯 we🫂 see👀 You👑 as✨ a💯 stranger🧳 and✨ welcome🤲 You👑, or✨ without🚫 clothes👕 and✨ clothe🧥 You👑?

39📖 When⏱️ did💯 we🫂 see👀 You👑 sick🤒 or✨ in💯 prison🔒 and✨ come🚶 to✨ You👑?’

40📖 The💯 King👑 will✨ answer💬, ‘For💯 real💯, whenever⏱️ you🫂 did✅ this💯 for💯 one💯 of💯 the💯 least🔹 of💯 these💯 frères🇫🇷👫 et🇫🇷➕ sœurs🇫🇷👭, you🫂 did✅ it💯 for💯 Me👑.’

41📖 “Then⏭️ He🙏 will✨ tell🗣️ those💯 on💯 His💯 left👈, ‘Get🚶 away💨 from💯 Me👑, you🫂 cursed😔, into💯 the💯 eternal♾️ fire🔥 prepared✅ for💯 the💯 devil😈 and✨ his💯 angels🪽.

42📖 I🙏 was✨ hungry😋, and✨ you🫂 gave🚫 Me👑 no🚫 food🍲. I🙏 was✨ thirsty🥤, and✨ you🫂 gave🚫 Me👑 no🚫 water💧.

43📖 I🙏 was✨ a💯 stranger🧳, and✨ you🫂 didn’t🚫 welcome🤲 Me👑; I🙏 was✨ naked🫣, and✨ you🫂 didn’t🚫 clothe🧥 Me👑; I🙏 was✨ sick🤒 and✨ in💯 prison🔒, and✨ you🫂 didn’t🚫 visit🤝 Me👑.’

44📖 “Then⏭️ they'll💯 ask🗣️, ‘Lord🙏, when⏱️ did💯 we🫂 see👀 You👑 hungry😋, thirsty🥤, a💯 stranger🧳, without🚫 clothes👕, sick🤒, or✨ in💯 prison🔒 and✨ fail💔 to✨ help🤲?’

45📖 He🙏 will✨ answer💬, ‘For💯 real💯, when⏱️ you🫂 didn't🚫 help🤲 one💯 of💯 the💯 least🔹 of💯 these💯, you🫂 didn't🚫 help🤲 Me👑.’

46📖 They'll💯 go🚶 into💯 eternal♾️ punishment⚖️, but💯 the💯 justos🇪🇸🙏 into💯 eternal♾️ life🌱.”
Matthew 26
# Matthew 26 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Matthew 26. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 When⏳ Jesus🙏 had✨ finished✅ teaching📚 all💯 this💯, He🙏 told🗣️ His💯 disciples🫂,

2📖 “In💯 two✌️ days📆 Passover🍞 is✨ here📍, and✨ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 will✨ be✨ handed🫳 over💨 to✨ be✨ crucified✝️.”

3📖 Then⏭️ the💯 chief👑 priests🕍, Bible📜 teachers📚, and✨ elders👴 gathered🫂 in💯 the💯 high👑 priest's🕍 courtyard🏛️. The💯 priest's🕍 name📛 was✨ Caiaphas👤.

4📖 They🫂 plotted🧠 to✨ arrest⛓️ Jesus🙏 by💯 trickery🎭 and✨ kill💀 Him👑.

5📖 “Not🚫 during💯 the💯 festival🍽️,” they🫂 said🗣️, “or✨ the💯 people🫂 might💯 riot💥.”

6📖 While⏳ Jesus🙏 was✨ in💯 Bethany🏘️, at💯 Simon👤 the💯 leper's🩹 house🏠,

7📖 a💯 woman👩 came🚶 with💯 an💯 alabaster🪨 jar🏺 of💯 expensive💰 perfume🧴. While⏳ Jesus🙏 reclined🪑 at💯 the💯 table🍽️, she👩 poured🫗 it💯 over💨 His💯 head👑.

8📖 His💯 disciples🫂 saw👀 it💯 and✨ got😠 upset😠: “Why❓ waste💸 it💯 like💯 that💯?

9📖 That💯 perfume🧴 could💪 have💯 sold💸 for💯 a💯 lot💰 and✨ helped🤲 people🫂 in💯 poverty💸.”

10📖 Jesus🙏 knew🧠 what💯 they🫂 were✨ saying🗣️: “Why❓ are✨ you🫂 giving💥 this💯 woman👩 a💯 hard💥 time⏱️? She👩 did✅ something💯 good💯 for💯 Me👑.

11📖 You'll💯 always🔁 have💯 people🫂 in💯 poverty💸 around🔄; you🫂 won't🚫 always🔁 have💯 Me👑.

12📖 When⏳ she👩 poured🫗 perfume🧴 on💯 My💯 body🫂, she👩 was✨ preparing🪔 Me👑 for💯 burial⚰️.

13📖 For💯 real💯, wherever📍 this💯 Good💯 News📣 is✨ preached📣 across💯 the💯 world🌍, what💭 she👩 did✅ will✨ be✨ told🗣️ in💯 memory🧠 of💯 her👩.”

14📖 Then⏭️ Judas👤 Iscariot👤, one💯 of💯 the💯 twelve🔢, went🚶 to✨ the💯 chief👑 priests🕍.

15📖 “What💰 will✨ you🫂 pay💸 me🙏 if💯 I🙏 hand🫳 Him👑 over💨?” They🫂 weighed⚖️ out💨 thirty🔢 pieces🪙 of💯 silver🥈 for💯 him👤.

16📖 From💯 that💯 moment⏱️, Judas👤 watched👀 for💯 a💯 chance⏱️ to✨ betray💔 Jesus🙏.

17📖 On💯 the💯 first🥇 day📆 of💯 Unleavened🍞 Bread🍞, the💯 disciples🫂 asked🗣️ Jesus🙏, “Where📍 should💯 we🫂 prepare🍽️ the💯 Pesach🇮🇱 meal🍽️ for💯 You👑?”

18📖 He🙏 answered💬, “Go🚶 into💯 the💯 city🏙️ to✨ a💯 certain💯 person👤 and✨ tell🗣️ him🫂, ‘The💯 Teacher📚 says🗣️, “My💯 time⏱️ is✨ close📍. I'll🙏 celebrate🍽️ Passover🍞 at💯 your💯 house🏠 with💯 My💯 disciples🫂.”’”

19📖 The💯 disciples🫂 did✅ what💯 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂 and✨ prepared🍽️ the💯 Pesach🇮🇱.

20📖 That💯 evening🌆, Jesus🙏 reclined🛋️ at💯 the💯 table🍽️ with💯 the💯 twelve🔢 disciples🫂.

21📖 As⏳ they🫂 ate🍽️, He🙏 said🗣️, “For💯 real💯, one💯 of💯 you🫂 will✨ betray💔 Me👑.”

22📖 Dem🇯🇲🫂 were✨ deeply💔 sorrowful😞 and✨ each💯 asked🗣️, “Surely💯 it's🚫 not🚫 me🙏, Lord🙏?”

23📖 He🙏 answered💬, “The💯 one👤 who👀 dips🫳 a💯 hand✋ in💯 the💯 dish🍲 with💯 Me👑 will✨ betray💔 Me👑.

24📖 The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 will✨ go🚶 just💯 as✨ Scripture📜 says🗣️, but💯 woe⚠️ to✨ the💯 one👤 who👀 betrays💔 Him👑. It💯 would✨ be✨ better💯 for💯 that💯 person👤 never🚫 to✨ have✨ been🌱 born🌅.”

25📖 Judas👤, the💯 one👤 betraying💔 Him👑, asked🗣️, “Surely💯 it's🚫 not🚫 me🙏, Rabbi📚?” Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “You🫵 said💬 it💯.”

26📖 While⏳ they🫂 were✨ eating🍽️, Jesus🙏 took🫳 pan🇪🇸, gave🙌 thanks🙏, broke👐 it💯, and✨ handed📤 it💯 to✨ the💯 disciples🫂: “Take🫳 it💯 and✨ eat🍽️; this💯 is✨ My💯 body🫂.”

27📖 He🙏 took🫳 a💯 cup🥤, gave🙌 thanks🙏, and✨ passed📤 it💯 to✨ them🫂: “All💯 of💯 you🫂, drink🥤 from💯 it💯,

28📖 because💯 this💯 is✨ My💯 blood🩸 of💯 the💯 new🆕 covenant🤝, poured🫗 out💨 for💯 many🫂 so✨ sins💔 can💪 be✨ forgiven🫶.

29📖 I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵: I🙏 won’t🚫 drink🥤 this💯 fruit🍇 from💨 the💯 vine🍇 again🔁 until⏳ that💯 day📆 when⏳ I🙏 drink🥤 vinho🇵🇹 anew🆕 with💯 you🫵 in💯 My💯 Father’s🙏 Kingdom👑.”

30📖 After⏭️ singing🎶 a💯 hymn🎵, they🫂 went🚶 out💨 to✨ the💯 Mount⛰️ of💯 Olives🫒.

31📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “Tonight🌙 all💯 of💯 you🫂 will✨ stumble🚧 because💯 of💯 Me👑. Scripture📜 says🗣️, ‘I'll🙏 strike👊 the💯 shepherd🧑‍🌾, and✨ the💯 flock🐑 will✨ scatter🌪️.’

32📖 But💯 after⏭️ I'm🙏 raised🌅, I'll🙏 go🚶 ahead➡️ of💯 you🫂 to✨ Galilee🌍.”

33📖 Peter👤 answered💬, “Even💯 if💯 everyone🫂 else↔️ stumbles🚧 because💯 of💯 You👑, I'll🙏 never🚫 stumble🚧.”

34📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “For💯 real💯, in💯 der🇩🇪 Nacht🇩🇪—before⏳ the💯 rooster🐓 crows📣—you🫵 will✨ deny🚫 Me👑 dreimal🇩🇪.”

35📖 Peter👤 said🗣️, “Even💯 if💯 I🙏 have💯 to✨ die💀 with💯 You👑, I🙏 won't🚫 deny🚫 You👑.” All💯 the💯 other💯 disciples🫂 said🗣️ the💯 same💯.

36📖 Jesus🙏 came🚶 with💯 them🫂 to✨ Gethsemane🏞️ and✨ told🗣️ His💯 disciples🫂, “Sit🪑 here📍 while⏳ I🙏 go🚶 over💨 there📍 to✨ prier🇫🇷.”

37📖 He🙏 took🫳 Peter👤 and✨ Zebedee’s👨 two✌️ sons👦 with💯 Him👑. He🙏 began🌱 to✨ feel😔 mwute🇳🇬😔 and✨ severely💥 troubled😣.

38📖 “My💯 soul🫂 is✨ overwhelmed😔 with💯 ìbànújẹ́🇳🇬, close📍 to✨ death💀. Stay🧍 here📍 and✨ keep👀 watch🌙 with💯 Me👑.”

39📖 He🙏 walked🚶 a💯 little🔹 farther📍, fell⬇️ face-down🙇, and✨ prayed🙏: “My💯 Father🙏, if💯 it's💯 possible💪, let💯 this💯 cup🥤 pass🚪 from💯 Me👑. Still💯, not🚫 what💯 I🙏 want🫶—what💯 You👑 want🫶.”

40📖 Jesus🙏 came🚶 back🔙 to✨ the💯 disciples🫂 and✨ found🔎 them😴 asleep😴. He🙏 asked🗣️ Peter👤, “Couldn't🚫 you🫵 stay👀 awake🌙 with💯 Me👑 for💯 one💯 hour⏱️?

41📖 Stay👀 alert⚠️ and✨ pray🙏 so✨ you🫂 don't🚫 fall🚧 into💯 temptation🪤. The💯 spirit👻 is✨ ready✅, but💯 the💯 jiki🇳🇬 is✨ weak💔.”

42📖 He🙏 went🚶 away💨 a💯 second🔢 time🔁 and✨ prayed🙏: “My💯 Father🙏, if💯 this💯 cup🥤 can't🚫 pass🚪 unless💯 I🙏 drink🥤 it💯, let✅ Your💯 will🫶 happen✅.”

43📖 He🙏 came🚶 back🔙 and✨ found🔎 them😴 sleeping😴 again🔄; their💯 eyes👀 were✨ heavy😴.

44📖 He🙏 left🚶 them🫂 again🔄, went🚶 off💨, and✨ prayed🙏 a💯 third🔢 time🔁, saying🗣️ the💯 same💯 thing💭.

45📖 Then⏭️ He🙏 came🚶 to✨ the💯 disciples🫂: “Are❓ you🫂 still💯 sleeping😴 and✨ resting🛌? The💯 hour⏱️ is✨ here📍. The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 is✨ being✨ handed🫳 over💨 to✨ sinners💔.

46📖 Get🌅 up⬆️, let's🫂 go🚶. The💯 one👤 betraying💔 Me👑 is✨ close📍.”

47📖 While⏳ Jesus🙏 was✨ still💯 speaking🗣️, Judas👤—one💯 of💯 the💯 twelve🔢—arrived🚶 with💯 a💯 huge💯 crowd🫂 carrying🗡️ swords⚔️ and✨ clubs🪵, sent📤 by💯 the💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ elders👴.

48📖 Judas👤 had✨ given📜 them🫂 a💯 signal🔔: “The💯 man👤 I🙏 kiss💋 is✨ the💯 one👑. Grab🤜 Him👑.”

49📖 He👤 went🚶 straight➡️ to✨ Jesus🙏: “Greetings👋, Rabbi📚!” Then⏭️ he👤 kissed💋 Him👑.

50📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Friend🫂, why❓ are✨ you🫵 here📍?” Then⏭️ the💯 crowd🫂 grabbed🤜 Jesus🙏 and✨ arrested⛓️ Him👑.

51📖 One💯 person👤 with💯 Jesus🙏 reached🤲 for💯 a💯 sword⚔️, struck👊 the💯 high👑 priest's🕍 servant🫂, and✨ cut✂️ off💨 his💯 ear👂.

52📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him👤, “Put🫳 your💯 sword⚔️ away💨. Everyone🫂 who👀 takes🫳 the💯 sword⚔️ will✨ die💀 by💯 the💯 sword⚔️.

53📖 Do💭 you🫵 think🧠 I🙏 couldn't🚫 ask🙏 My💯 Father🙏? He'd🙏 send📤 more💯 than💯 twelve🔢 legions🪖 of💯 angels🪽 right⚡ now📍.

54📖 But💯 then⏭️ how💭 would💯 Scripture📜 be✨ fulfilled✅ as💯 it💯 must💯 be✨?”

55📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️ to✨ the💯 crowd🫂, “Did💯 you🫂 come🚶 after💯 Me👑 with💯 swords⚔️ and✨ clubs🪵 like💯 I'm🙏 a💯 robber🦹? I🙏 sat🪑 in💯 the💯 temple🏛️ every💯 day📆 teaching📚, and✨ you🫂 didn't🚫 arrest⛓️ Me👑.

56📖 But💯 all💯 this💯 happened💥 to✨ fulfill✅ what💯 the💯 prophets📣 wrote📜.” Then⏭️ all💯 the💯 disciples🫂 left🚶 Jesus🙏 and✨ ran🏃 away💨.

62📖 The💯 high👑 priest🕍 stood🌅 up⬆️: “Don't🚫 You👑 have💯 anything💯 to✨ say🗣️? What💭 about💯 these💯 accusations🗣️?”

65📖 Then⏭️ the💯 high👑 priest🕍 tore✂️ his💯 clothes👘: “He's💯 blasphemed🚫! Why❓ do💯 we🫂 need🫶 more💯 witnesses👀? You🫂 heard👂 the💯 blasphemy🚫!

67📖 They🫂 spat🤮 in💯 His💯 face👤, punched👊 Him👑, and✨ slapped👋 Him👑.

73📖 A💯 little🔹 later⏭️, the💯 people🫂 standing🧍 there📍 said🗣️ to✨ Peter👤, “You're💯 definitely✅ one💯 of💯 them🫂; your💯 accent🗣️ gives💯 you🫵 away💨.”

74📖 Then⏭️ Peter👤 started🗣️ cursing🤬 and✨ swearing🤞, “I🙏 don't🚫 know🧠 Him👤!” Right⚡ then⏱️, a💯 rooster🐓 crowed📣.

75📖 Peter👤 remembered🧠 what💯 Jesus🙏 had💯 told🗣️ him👤: “Before⏳ the💯 rooster🐓 crows📣, you'll💯 deny🚫 Me👑 dreimal🇩🇪.” He👤 went🚶 outside🚪 and✨ wept😭 bitterly💔.”

1📖 At🌅 Morgen🇩🇪, all💯 the💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ elders👴 met🫂 up🗣️ to✨ plan🧠 Jesus🙏' death💀.

66📖 They🫂 went🚶, secured🛡️ the💯 tomb⚰️ with✨ the💯 guard🪖, and✨ sealed🔒 the💯 stone🪨.
Matthew 28
# Matthew 28 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Matthew 28. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 After⏭️ the💯 Sabbath🕊️, as⏳ the💯 first🔢 ọjọ́🇳🇬🌞 of💯 the💯 week📆 began🌅 to✨ dawn🌄, Mary👩 Magdalene👩 and✨ the💯 other💯 Mary👩 came🚶 to✨ see👀 the💯 tomb⚰️.

3📖 The💯 angel's👼 appearance✨ flashed⚡ like💯 relámpago🇪🇸, and✨ his💯 clothes👘 were✨ white🤍 as✨ snow❄️.

4📖 The💯 guards🪖 shook😨 with💯 egwu🇳🇬 and✨ went🧊 stiff🧊, like💯 dead💀 men👤.

30📖 Simon's👤 mother-in-law👵 was✨ laid🛏️ up🤒 with💯 a💯 fever🤒. They🫂 told🗣️ Jesus🙏 about✨ her👵 right⏱️ away⚡.

# Mark 2 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Mark 2. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 A💯 few🔹 days📆 later⏭️, Jesus🙏 came🚶 back🔙 to✨ Capernaum🏘️. Word📣 got✨ around🌍 that✨ He🙏 was✨ home🏠.

2📖 People🫂 packed📦 in💯 fast⚡ until⏱️ there🚫 wasn't✨ even💯 room📍 by💯 the💯 doorway🚪. Jesus🙏 was✨ sharing🗣️ God's🙏 word📜 with✨ them🫂.

3📖 Four🔢 people🫂 showed🚶 up📍 carrying🫴 a💯 man👤 who💯 was✨ paralysed♿, bringing🫴 him👤 to✨ Jesus🙏.

4📖 With💯 the💯 crowd🫂 blocking🚫 the💯 way🛤️, they🫂 couldn't🚫 reach📍 Jesus🙏. So✨ they🫂 opened🪚 the💯 Dach🇩🇪 above⬆️ Jesus🙏, then⏭️ lowered⬇️ the💯 mat🛏️ with✨ the💯 man👤 right⬇️ through↕️ it💯.

5📖 Yeshua🇮🇱 saw👀 their💯 trust🙏 and✨ told🗣️ the💯 man👤, “My💯 son👤, your💯 sins💔 are✨ pardonnés🇫🇷.”

6📖 Some💯 Scripture📜 teachers📚 were✨ sitting🪑 there📍, turning🧠 it💯 over🔄 in💯 their💯 obi🇳🇬.

8📖 Jesus🙏 immediately⚡ knew🧠 in💯 His🙏 spirit🕊️ what💯 they🫂 were✨ thinking🧠. He🙏 asked🗣️ them🫂, “Why❓ are✨ you🫵 running🧠 these💯 thoughts💭 through💯 your💯 minds🧠?

9📖 Which💯 is✨ easier💨 to✨ tell🗣️ this💯 man👤: ‘Your💯 sins💔 are✨ forgiven🤲,’ or✨ ‘Get⬆️ up⬆️, pick🫴 up💯 your💯 mat🛏️, and✨ walk🚶’?

10📖 But💯 so✨ you🫵 know🧠 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 has✨ authority👑 on💯 earth🌍 to✨ forgive🤲 sins💔”—Jesus🙏 turned🔄 to✨ the💯 man👤 and✨ said🗣️—

11📖 “I'm🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵: get⬆️ up⬆️, take🫴 your💯 mat🛏️, and✨ go🚶 home🏠.”

12📖 The💯 man👤 got⬆️ up⬆️, grabbed🫴 his💯 mat🛏️, and✨ walked🚶 out🚪 right💯 in💯 front💯 of💯 them🫂 all💯. Dem🇯🇲 were✨ stunned😲 and✨ praised🙌 God🙏: “We've✨ never🚫 seen👀 anything💯 like💯 this💯!”

13📖 Jesus🙏 went🚶 out🚪 again🔁 beside💯 the💯 lake🌊. The💯 crowd🫂 came🚶 to✨ Him🙏, and✨ He🙏 taught📚 them🫂.

14📖 As⏳ He🙏 walked🚶 past💨, He🙏 saw👀 Levi👤 son👤 of💯 Alphaeus👤 sitting🪑 at💯 the💯 tax💰 booth🏦. Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him👤, “Follow🚶 Me🙏.” Levi👤 stood⬆️ up⬆️ and✨ followed🚶 Him🙏.

15📖 Later⏭️, Jesus🙏 was✨ reclining🛋️ at💯 the💯 table🍽️ and✨ eating🍽️ oúnjẹ🇳🇬. Many💯 tax💰 collectors🧾 and✨ people💯 known📛 as✨ sinners💔 were✨ sharing🍽️ the💯 table🪑 with✨ Jesus🙏 and✨ His🙏 disciples🫂; plenty💯 of💯 them🫂 had✨ started🚀 following🚶 Him🙏.

16📖 When⏳ the💯 Scripture📜 teachers📚 and✨ Pharisees🕍 saw👀 Him🙏 eating🍽️ and✨ drinking🥤 with✨ sinners💔 and✨ tax💰 collectors🧾, they🫂 pressed🗣️ His🙏 disciples🫂, “Why❓ is✨ He🙏 at💯 the💯 table🍽️ with💯 people🫂 like💯 that💯?”

17📖 Jesus🙏 heard👂 them🫂 and✨ said🗣️, “Healthy💪 people🫂 don't🚫 need💯 a💯 doctor🩺; sick🤒 people🫂 do💯. I🙏 didn't🚫 come🚶 for💯 the💯 righteous🙏; I🙏 came🚶 to✨ call📣 sinners💔 to✨ turn🔄 back↩️.”

18📖 John's👤 disciples🫂 and✨ the💯 Pharisees🕍 were✨ fasting🙏. Some💯 people🫂 asked❓ Jesus🙏, “Why❓ are✨ John's👤 disciples🫂 and✨ the💯 Pharisees🕍 fasting🙏, while⏳ Your🙏 disciples🫂 aren't🚫?”

19📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “Can💪 wedding💍 guests🫂 fast🙏 while⏳ the💯 groom🤵 is✨ right💯 there📍 with✨ them🫂? As⏳ long⏱️ as✨ they🫂 have💯 the💯 groom🤵 with✨ them🫂, fasting🙏 doesn't🚫 fit💯.

20📖 The💯 time⏱️ will✨ come📆 when⏳ the💯 groom🤵 is✨ taken🫳 away💨 from💯 them🫂. Then⏭️, on💯 that💯 day📆, they'll💯 fast🙏.

21📖 Nobody🫂 patches🪡 an💯 old👴 garment👘 with✨ new✨ cloth🧵 that✨ hasn't🚫 shrunk🔽 yet⏳. When⏳ it✨ tightens🪢, the💯 new✨ patch🧵 will✨ pull💥 away↔️ from💯 the💯 old👴 fabric🧵, leaving🕳️ an💯 even💯 worse⚠️ tear🪡.

22📖 And✨ nobody🫂 pours🫗 fresh✨ vinho🇵🇹 into💯 old👴 wineskins🫙. The💯 new✨ wine🍷 will✨ burst💥 them🫙, spilling💦 the💯 wine🍷 and✨ wrecking💥 the💯 skins🫙. Fresh✨ wine🍷 belongs📍 in💯 fresh✨ wineskins🫙.”

11📖 A💯 huge💯 herd🐖 of💯 pigs🐖 was✨ feeding🌿 on💯 the💯 hillside⛰️ nearby📍.

12📖 The💯 demons👿 pleaded🙏, “Send📤 us🫂 into💯 those💯 pigs🐖. Let✨ us🫂 go🚪 into💯 them🫂!”

13📖 Jesus🙏 gave🫴 them🫂 permission✅. The💯 unclean🫥 spirits👿 left🚪 the💯 man👤 and✨ entered🚪 the💯 pigs🐖. The💯 herd🐖—about💯 two🔢 thousand🔢—charged🏃 down⬇️ the💯 steep💥 bank🏞️ into💯 the💯 teku🇳🇬 and✨ drowned🌊.

14📖 Dem🇯🇲 who👀 tended🧑‍🌾 the💯 herd🐖 ran🏃 off💨 and✨ told🗣️ everyone🫂 in💯 town🏘️ and✨ out💨 in💯 the💯 countryside🌾. People🫂 came🚶 to✨ see👀 what💭 had✨ happened💥.

15📖 They🫂 reached🚶 Jesus🙏 and✨ saw👀 the💯 man👤 who👀 had✨ been✨ possessed👿 by💯 demons👿 sitting🪑 there📍, dressed👕 and✨ thinking🧠 clearly💡—the💯 same💯 man👤 who👀 had✨ Legion👿 in💯 him👤. They🫂 were✨ afraid😨.

16📖 The💯 eyewitnesses👀 explained🗣️ what💭 had✨ happened💥 to✨ the💯 man👤 and✨ what💭 happened💥 with💯 the💯 pigs🐖.

17📖 Then⏭️ the💯 people🫂 begged🙏 Jesus🙏 to✨ leave🚶 their💯 region🌍.

18📖 As⏳ Jesus🙏 was✨ getting🛶 back🔙 into💯 the💯 boat⛵, the💯 man👤 who👀 had✨ been✨ freed🔓 begged🙏 to✨ come🚶 with✨ Him🙏.

19📖 Jesus🙏 didn't🚫 let✨ him👤 come🚶 along🤝. He🙏 told🗣️ him👤, “Go🚶 home🏠 to✨ your💯 friends🫂. Tell🗣️ them🫂 what💭 the💯 Lord👑 has✨ done✅ for💯 you🫵 and✨ how💭 He🙏 showed🤲 you🫵 miséricorde🇫🇷.”

20📖 The💯 man👤 went🚶 off💨 and✨ started🚀 telling🗣️ the💯 Ten🏙️ Cities🌆 region🌍 how💭 much💯 Jesus🙏 had✨ done✅ for💯 him👤. Everyone🫂 was✨ amazed😲.

21📖 Jesus🙏 crossed🚤 back🔙 over💯 in💯 the💯 boat⛵ to✨ the💯 other💯 side🌊. A💯 huge💯 crowd🫂 gathered🫂 around🔄 Him🙏 by💯 the💯 sea🌊.

22📖 One💯 of💯 the💯 synagogue🕍 leaders👑, Jairus👤, came🚶 over💨. When⏳ he👤 saw👀 Jesus🙏, he👤 fell⬇️ at💯 His🙏 feet🦶

23📖 and✨ pleaded🙏 again🔁 and✨ again🔁: “My💯 little🔹 daughter👧 is✨ close📍 to✨ dying💔. Please🙏 come🚶 lay🫴 Your💯 hands👐 on💯 her👧 so✨ she👧 can💪 get💪 well💪 and✨ live🌱.”

24📖 Jesus🙏 went🚶 with✨ him👤. A💯 huge💯 crowd🫂 followed🚶 and✨ pressed🤲 in💨 all💯 around🔄 Him🙏.

25📖 A💯 woman👩 had✨ been✨ bleeding🩸 for💯 twelve🔢 years📆.

26📖 She👩 had✨ been✨ through💯 so💯 much💔 with💯 many💯 doctors🩺 and✨ spent💸 everything💰 she👩 had💯, but✨ instead💔 of💯 getting💪 better💪, she👩 got💔 worse⬇️.

27📖 She👩 had✨ heard👂 about✨ Jesus🙏, so✨ she👩 came🚶 up⬆️ behind🔙 Him🙏 in💯 the💯 crowd🫂 and✨ touched🤲 His🙏 clothes👘.

28📖 She👩 kept🧠 thinking💭, “If✨ I🙏 can💪 just💯 touch🤲 His🙏 clothes👘, I'll✨ be✨ healed🩺.”

29📖 Immediately⚡, the💯 bleeding🩸 stopped🛑, and✨ she👩 could💪 feel💭 in💯 her💯 body🫂 that✨ the💯 painful💔 condition🤒 was✨ healed🩺.

30📖 Right⏱️ then⏱️, Jesus🙏 sensed💭 power💪 had✨ gone🚪 out💨 from💯 Him🙏. He🙏 turned🔄 around🔄 in💯 the💯 crowd🫂 and✨ asked❓, “Who👀 touched🤲 My💯 clothes👘?”

31📖 His🙏 disciples🫂 said🗣️, “You🙏 see👀 this💯 whole💯 crowd🫂 pushing🤲 against⚡ You🙏, and✨ You🙏 ask❓, ‘Who👀 touched🤲 Me🙏?’”

32📖 Jesus🙏 kept🔎 looking👀 around🔄 to✨ see👀 who👤 had✨ done✅ it💯.

33📖 The💯 woman👩, scared😨 and✨ trembling🫨, knew🧠 what💭 had✨ happened💥 to✨ her👩. She👩 came🚶, fell⬇️ before✨ Him🙏, and✨ told🗣️ Him🙏 the💯 whole💯 eziokwu🇳🇬.

34📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️: “Daughter👧, your💯 faith🙏 has✨ made🩺 you🫵 well💪. Go🚶 in💯 shalom🇮🇱; you're✨ healed🩺 from💯 your💯 disease🤒.”

35📖 While⏳ Jesus🙏 was✨ still💯 speaking🗣️, people🫂 arrived🚶 from💯 the💯 synagogue🕍 leader's👑 home🏠: “Your💯 daughter👧 has✨ died💀. Why❓ keep🔁 bothering🗣️ the💯 Teacher📚?”

36📖 Jesus🙏 heard👂 their💯 message📣 and✨ immediately⚡ told🗣️ the💯 synagogue🕍 leader👑, “Don't🚫 be✨ afraid😨. Just💯 believe🙏.”

37📖 He🙏 let✨ only💯 Peter👤, James👤, and✨ John👤 the💯 brother👤 of💯 James👤 go🚶 with✨ Him🙏.

38📖 They🫂 reached🚶 the💯 leader's👑 home🏠, where📍 Jesus🙏 saw👀 an💯 uproar💥, people🫂 weeping😭, and✨ loud🔊 mourning😭.

39📖 He🙏 went🚶 inside🏠 and✨ said🗣️, “Why❓ all💯 this💯 chaos💥 and✨ crying😭? The💯 child👧 isn't🚫 dead💀; she's💯 sleeping😴.”

40📖 They🫂 laughed😂 at✨ Him🙏. Jesus🙏 sent📤 them🫂 all💯 outside🚪, then⏭️ took🫴 the💯 child's👧 father👨, her💯 mother👩, and✨ His🙏 companions🫂 into💯 the💯 room🛏️ where📍 she👧 was✨ lying🛏️.

41📖 Jesus🙏 took🫴 the💯 girl's👧 hand🤝 and✨ said🗣️, “Talitha👧 cumi⬆️!”—which💡 means🗣️, “Little🔹 girl👧, I'm🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵: get⬆️ up⬆️!”

42📖 Immediately⚡, the💯 girl👧 stood⬆️ up⬆️—then⏭️ caminhou🇵🇹. She👧 was✨ twelve🔢 years📆 old👧. Everyone🫂 was✨ completely💯 amazed😲.

43📖 Jesus🙏 firmly⚠️ told🗣️ them🫂 not🚫 to✨ let🗣️ anyone👥 know🧠 what💭 happened💥. Then⏭️ He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂 to✨ give🫴 her👧 something💯 to✨ eat🍽️.”
Mark 6
# Mark 6 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Mark 6. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 Jesus🙏 left🚶 there📍 and✨ returned🔙 to✨ His🙏 hometown🏘️. His🙏 disciples🫂 went🚶 with✨ Him🙏.

2📖 When⏳ Sabbath🕊️ came📆, He🙏 taught📚 in💯 the💯 synagogue🕍. Many💯 who👀 heard👂 Him🙏 were✨ stunned😲: “Where💭 did💯 He🙏 get💯 all💯 this💯? What💭 kind💯 of💯 wisdom🧠 is✨ this💯, that✨ He🙏 can💪 do✅ powerful💥 works💯 too💯?

3📖 Isn't💯 He🙏 the💯 carpenter🪚, Mary's👩 son👤? Aren't💯 James👤, Joses👤, Judah👤, and✨ Simon👤 His🙏 brothers👥? Don't🚫 His🙏 sisters👭 live🏠 here📍 with💯 us🫂?” They🫂 took💔 offence😠 at✨ Him🙏.

4📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “A💯 prophet📣 gets💯 honour🙌 everywhere🌍—except💯 in💯 his💯 hometown🏘️, among💯 his💯 relatives👨‍👩‍👧, and✨ under💯 his💯 own💯 roof🏠.”

5📖 He🙏 couldn't🚫 do💯 any💯 mighty💥 work💯 there📍, except💯 lay🫴 His🙏 hands👐 on💯 a💯 few🔹 sick🤒 people🫂 and✨ heal🩺 them🫂.

6📖 He🙏 was✨ amazed😮 at💯 how💯 little🔹 they🫂 trusted🙏 Him🙏. Then⏭️ He🙏 travelled🚶 through💯 the💯 Dörfer🇩🇪, teaching📚.

7📖 Jesus🙏 called📣 the💯 Twelve🔢 to✨ Him🙏 and✨ sent📨 them🫂 out🚶 two✌️ by💯 two✌️, giving🫴 them🫂 authority👑 over💯 unclean🫥 spirits👿.

8📖 He🙏 ordered📣 them🫂 to✨ take🫴 nothing🚫 for💯 the💯 journey🛤️ except💯 a💯 walking🦯 stick🪵—no🚫 bread🍞, bag🎒, or✨ money💰 in💯 their💯 belts🪢.

9📖 They🫂 could💪 wear👡 sandals🩴, but✨ shouldn't🚫 pack🎒 an💯 extra🔁 shirt👕.

10📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “Whenever⏱️ you🫵 enter🚪 a💯 home🏠, stay🛏️ there📍 until⏱️ you🫵 leave🚶 that💯 place📍.

11📖 If✨ a💯 place📍 won't🚫 welcome🤲 you🫵 or✨ listen👂 to✨ you🫵, leave🚶 and✨ shake💨 the💯 dust🌫️ from💯 your💯 feet🦶 as💯 a💯 witness📣 against⚠️ them🫂. I'm🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵: judgement⚖️ day📆 will✨ be✨ easier💨 for✨ Sodom🌆 and✨ Gomorrah🌆 than💯 for✨ that💯 town🏘️!”

12📖 They🫂 went🚶 out🚶 and✨ preached📣 that💯 people🫂 should✨ turn🔄 back↩️.

13📖 They🫂 drove🚪 out🚶 many💯 demons👿, anointed🫗 many💯 sick🤒 people🫂 with💯 oil🫒, and✨ healed🩺 them🫂.

14📖 King👑 Herod👤 heard👂 about✨ Jesus🙏, whose💯 name📛 was✨ now💯 known📣 everywhere🌍. Herod👤 said🗣️, “John👤 the💯 Baptiser💧 has✨ risen⬆️ from💯 the💯 dead💀; that's💯 why💯 these💯 powers💥 are✨ working⚡ through💯 Him🙏.”

15📖 Others🫂 said🗣️, “He's💯 Elijah📣.” Others🔹 said🗣️, “He's💯 a💯 prophet📣—or✨ like💯 one💯 of💯 the💯 prophets📣.”

16📖 But✨ when⏳ Herod👑 heard👂 it💯, he👤 said🗣️, “John👤—the💯 one💯 I🙏 beheaded⚔️—has✨ risen⬆️ from💯 the💯 dead💀.”

17📖 Herod👑 himself👤 had✨ ordered📣 John's👤 arrest⛓️ and✨ imprisonment⛓️ because✨ of💯 Herodias👩, his💯 brother👤 Philip's👤 wife👩; Herod👑 had✨ married💍 her👩.

18📖 John👤 kept🔁 telling🗣️ Herod👑, “You🫵 aren't🚫 allowed✅ to✨ have💍 your💯 brother's👤 wife👩.”

19📖 Herodias👩 held💢 it💯 against⚔️ John👤 and✨ wanted💭 him👤 dead💀, but✨ she👩 couldn't🚫 make✨ it💯 happen💥.

20📖 Herod👑 feared😨 John👤, knowing🧠 he👤 was✨ righteous🕊️ and✨ holy🕊️, so✨ he👑 protected🛡️ him👤. Herod👑 did✅ many💯 things💯 after⏭️ hearing👂 John👤 and✨ listened👂 to✨ him👑 gladly😊.

21📖 Then⏭️ an💯 opportunity📍 came💥. On💯 his💯 birthday🎂, Herod👑 held🎉 a💯 dinner🍽️ for✨ his💯 high💯 officials👔, army🪖 commanders👑, and✨ leading👑 people🫂 of💯 Galilee🌍.

22📖 Herodias👩' daughter👧 came🚶 in💨 and✨ danced💃. She👧 pleased😊 Herod👑 and✨ his💯 guests🫂. The💯 king👑 told🗣️ the💯 girl👧, “Ask❓ me🙏 for💯 anything💯 you🫵 want💭, and✨ I'll✨ give🫴 it💯 to✨ you🫵.”

23📖 He👑 even💯 swore🤞, “Whatever💯 you🫵 ask❓, I'll✨ give🫴 it💯—up⬆️ to✨ half💯 my💯 kingdom👑.”

24📖 She👧 left🚶 and✨ asked❓ her💯 mother👩, “What💭 should✨ I🙏 ask❓ for💯?” Her💯 mother👩 said🗣️, “John's👤 head🧠, the💯 Baptiser's💧.”

25📖 She👧 hurried🏃 straight➡️ back🔙 to✨ the💯 king👑 and✨ requested🗣️, “I🙏 want💭 you🫵 to✨ give🫴 me🙏 John👤 the💯 Baptiser's💧 head🧠 right💯 now⏱️, on💯 a💯 platter🍽️.”

26📖 The💯 king👑 was✨ deeply💔 distressed😞, but✨ because✨ of💯 his💯 oath🤞 and✨ the💯 guests🫂, he👑 didn't🚫 want💭 to✨ refuse🚫 her👧.

27📖 Right⏱️ away⚡, the💯 king👑 sent📨 a💯 guard🪖 with✨ orders📣 to✨ bring🫴 John's👤 head🧠. The💯 guard🪖 went🚶 to✨ the💯 prison⛓️ and✨ beheaded⚔️ John👤.

28📖 He🪖 brought🫴 John's👤 head🧠 on💯 a💯 platter🍽️, gave🫴 it💯 to✨ the💯 girl👧, and✨ she👧 handed🫴 it💯 to✨ her💯 mother👩.

29📖 When⏳ John's👤 disciples🫂 heard👂, they🫂 came🚶, took🫴 his💯 body🕊️, and✨ laid🛏️ it💯 in💯 a💯 tomb⚰️.

30📖 The💯 apostles🫂 gathered🫂 around🔄 Jesus🙏 and✨ told🗣️ Him🙏 everything💯 they'd✨ done✅ and✨ taught📚.

31📖 He🙏 said🗣️ to✨ them🫂, “Come🚶 with💯 Me🙏 to✨ a💯 quiet🤫 place📍 and✨ take💤 a💯 descanso🇵🇹 for💯 a💯 bit⏱️.” So✨ many💯 people🫂 kept🔁 coming🚶 and✨ going🚶 that✨ they🫂 barely💯 had💯 time⏱️ to✨ eat🍽️.

32📖 They🫂 went🚤 away💨 by💯 boat⛵ to✨ a💯 quiet🤫 place🏝️ on💯 their💯 own💯.

33📖 But✨ people🫂 saw👀 them🫂 going🚤. Dem🇯🇲🫂 recognised👀 Jesus🙏 and✨ ran🏃 on💯 foot🦶 from💯 all💯 the💯 towns🏘️, reaching🏃 the💯 place📍 before💯 the💯 boat⛵ and✨ gathering🫂 there📍.

34📖 When⏳ Jesus🙏 stepped🚶 out🚪, He🙏 saw👀 a💯 huge💯 crowd🫂 and✨ felt💛 compassion🇫🇷 for✨ them🫂. They🫂 were✨ like💯 sheep🐑 without🚫 a💯 shepherd🐑, so✨ He🙏 began🚀 teaching📚 them🫂 many💯 things💯.

35📖 It💯 was✨ getting⏱️ late🌆 when⏳ His🙏 disciples🫂 came🚶 to✨ Him🙏: “This💯 place🏜️ is✨ remote🌍, and✨ it's💯 already💯 late🌆.

36📖 Send📤 everyone🫂 away💨 so✨ they🫂 can💪 go🚶 to✨ nearby📍 farms🌾 and✨ villages🏘️ to✨ buy🛒 themselves🫵 oúnjẹ🇳🇬. They🫂 don't🚫 have💯 anything💯 to✨ eat🍽️.”

37📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “You🫵 give🫴 them🫂 something💯 to✨ eat🍽️.” They🫂 asked❓, “Should✨ we🙏 go🚶 buy🛒 two🔢 hundred💯 denarii💰 worth💰 of💯 pan🇪🇸 and✨ feed🍽️ them🫂?”

38📖 He🙏 asked❓, “How💭 many💯 loaves🍞 do💯 you🫵 have💯? Go🚶 check🔎.” They🫂 came🔙 back🔙: “Five🔢 loaves🍞 and✨ two🔢 fish🐟.”

39📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ everyone🫂 to✨ sit🪑 down⬇️ in💯 groups🫂 on💯 the💯 green🌿 grass🌱.

40📖 They🫂 sat🪑 in💯 rows🧍, groups🫂 of💯 one💯 hundred💯 and✨ groups🫂 of💯 fifty🔢.

41📖 Jesus🙏 took🫴 the💯 five🔢 loaves🍞 and✨ two🔢 fish🐟, looked👀 up⬆️ to✨ heaven☁️, blessed🙏 the💯 food🍽️, and✨ broke👐 the💯 loaves🍞. He🙏 gave🫴 them🫂 to✨ His🙏 disciples🫂 to✨ serve🤲, and✨ divided🫴 the💯 fish🐟 among💯 them🫂 all💯.

42📖 Everyone🫂 ate🍽️ and✨ had💯 enough💯.

43📖 The💯 disciples🫂 gathered🫴 twelve🔢 baskets🧺 full💯 of💯 leftover🥖 bread🍞 and✨ fish🐟.

44📖 Five🔢 thousand🔢 men👨 had✨ eaten🍽️ the💯 loaves🍞.

45📖 Immediately⚡ Jesus🙏 told🗣️ His🙏 disciples🫂 to✨ get🚤 into💯 the💯 boat⛵ and✨ head🚤 ahead➡️ to✨ Bethsaida🌍, while⏳ He🙏 sent📤 the💯 crowd🫂 home🏠.

46📖 After⏭️ saying👋 goodbye👋 to✨ them🫂, He🙏 went⬆️ up⬆️ the💯 mountain⛰️ to✨ pray🙏.

47📖 By💯 evening🌆, the💯 boat⛵ was✨ out💨 in💯 the💯 middle🌊 of💯 the💯 teku🇳🇬—while⏳ Jesus🙏 was✨ alone🤫 on💯 land🌍.

48📖 He🙏 saw👀 how💭 hard💥 they🫂 were✨ rowing🚣 because✨ the💯 ruach🇮🇱 wind🌬️ was✨ against⚔️ them🫂. Around⏱️ the💯 fourth🔢 watch🌙 of💯 the💯 night🌙, He🙏 came🚶 toward➡️ them🫂, walking🚶 on💯 the💯 sea🌊. He🙏 was✨ about💯 to✨ pass🚶 by💨 them🫂,

49📖 but✨ when⏳ they🫂 saw👀 Him🙏 walking🚶 on💯 the💯 sea🌊, they🫂 thought💭 He🙏 was✨ a💯 ghost👻 and✨ screamed📣.

50📖 They🫂 all💯 saw👀 Him🙏 and✨ panicked😨. Right⏱️ away⚡ He🙏 spoke🗣️ to✨ them🫂: “Take💪 heart❤️! It's💯 Me🙏! Don't🚫 be✨ afraid😨.”

51📖 He🙏 climbed🚤 into💯 the💯 boat⛵ with✨ them🫂, and✨ the💯 wind🌬️ died🌬️ down⬇️. They🫂 were✨ completely💯 amazed😲.

52📖 They🫂 still💯 hadn't🚫 understood🧠 what💭 the💯 loaves🍞 meant💡; their💯 obi🇳🇬 were✨ still💯 hardened🪨.

53📖 They🫂 crossed🚤 over💯 and✨ landed🚶 at💯 Gennesaret🌍, where📍 they🫂 tied🪢 up🚤 by💯 the💯 shore🏖️.

54📖 As⏳ soon⚡ as✨ they🫂 stepped🚶 out🚪 of💯 the💯 boat⛵, people🫂 recognised👀 Jesus🙏.
