9📖 As⏳ they🫂 came⬇️ down⬇️ the💯 mountain⛰️, Jesus🙏 warned⚠️ them🫂 not🚫 to✨ tell🗣️ anyone👥 what💭 they🫂 had✨ seen👀 until⏱️ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 had✨ risen⬆️ from💯 the💯 dead💀.

10📖 Dem🇯🇲🫂 kept🔒 it💯 to✨ themselves🫵, still💭 trying🧠 to✨ figure🔎 out💭 what💭 “rising⬆️ from💯 the💯 dead💀” meant💡.

11📖 They🫂 asked❓ Him🙏, “Why❓ do💯 the💯 Scripture📜 teachers📚 say🗣️ Elijah👤 has✨ to✨ come🚶 first⏮️?”

12📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Elijah👤 does✨ come🚶 first⏮️ to✨ restore🔧 everything💯. How❓ does✨ Scripture📜 say🗣️ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 must💯 suffer💔 many💯 things💥 and✨ be✨ treated💔 with💯 contempt😔?

13📖 I'm🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵, Elijah👤 already💯 came🚶, and✨ people🫂 did✅ whatever💯 they🫂 wanted💭 to✨ him👤, just💯 as✨ Scripture📜 said🗣️.”

14📖 When⏳ they🫂 returned🚶 to✨ the💯 other💯 disciples🫂, a💯 huge💯 crowd🫂 surrounded🔄 them🫂, and✨ Scripture📜 teachers📚 were✨ arguing🗣️ with💯 them🫂.

15📖 As⏳ soon⚡ as✨ all💯 the💯 crowd🫂 saw👀 Jesus🙏, they🫂 were✨ amazed😲 and✨ rushed🏃 over💨 to✨ greet👋 Him🙏.

16📖 Jesus🙏 asked❓ the💯 Scripture📜 teachers📚, “What💭 are✨ you🫵 debating🗣️ with💯 them🫂?”

17📖 Someone🫂 in💯 the💯 crowd🫂 answered🗣️, “Teacher📚, I🙏 brought🫴 my💯 son👦 to✨ You🙏. An💯 unclean🫥 spirit👿 makes✨ him👦 unable🤐 to✨ speak🗣️.

18📖 Whenever⏱️ it👿 grabs🫳 him👦, it👿 throws🫳 him👦 down⬇️; he👦 foams🫧 at💯 the💯 mouth👄, grinds🦷 his💯 teeth🦷, and✨ goes🧊 rigid🧊. I🙏 asked❓ Your🙏 disciples🫂 to✨ drive🚪 it👿 out🚶, but✨ they🫂 couldn't🚫.”

19📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “Unbelieving💔 generation👥, how⏱️ long💯 do💯 I🙏 have✨ to✨ stay🧍 with💯 you🫵? How⏱️ long💯 must💯 I🙏 bear🫴 with💯 this💯? Bring🫴 the💯 boy👦 to✨ Me🙏.”

20📖 They🫂 brought🫴 him👦 to✨ Jesus🙏. When⏳ the💯 spirit👿 saw👀 Him🙏, it👿 immediately⚡ shook💥 the💯 boy👦 violently💥. He👦 fell⬇️ to✨ the💯 ground🌍, rolling🔄 and✨ foaming🫧 at💯 the💯 mouth👄.

21📖 Jesus🙏 asked❓ his💯 father👨, “How⏱️ long💯 has✨ this💯 been✨ happening💭?” The💯 father👨 said🗣️, “Since⏳ he👦 was✨ little👶.

22📖 The💯 spirit👿 often🔁 throws🫳 him👦 into💯 fuego🇪🇸 and✨ água🇵🇹 to✨ destroy💥 him👦. If✨ You🙏 can💪 do💯 anything💯, show💛 racham🇮🇱 to✨ us🫂 and✨ help🛟 us🫂!”

23📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “‘If✨ You🙏 can💪’? Everything💯 is✨ possible💪 for✨ the💯 one💯 who👀 believes🙏.”

24📖 Immediately⚡ the💯 boy's👦 father👨 cried📣 out🔊 through💯 tears😭, “I🙏 believe🙏! But💯 doubt🫤 is✨ still💯 loud🔊 in💯 my💯 obi🇳🇬; help🛟 me🫵!”

25📖 When⏳ Jesus🙏 saw👀 the💯 crowd🫂 running🏃 over💨, He🙏 rebuked⚠️ the💯 unclean🫥 spirit👿: “You🤐 mute🤐 and✨ deaf🦻 spirit👿, I🙏 command📣 you🫵: come🚪 out🚶 of💯 him👦 and✨ never🚫 enter🚪 him👦 again🔁!”

26📖 The💯 spirit👿 screamed📣 and✨ shook💥 him👦 violently💥, then⏭️ came🚪 out🚶. The💯 boy👦 looked💀 like💯 he👦 was✨ dead💀, and✨ most💯 of💯 them🫂 said🗣️, “He's💯 dead💀.”

27📖 But✨ Jesus🙏 took🫴 him👦 by💯 the💯 hand🤝 and✨ lifted⬆️ him👦 up⬆️. He👦 stood🧍 up⬆️.

28📖 After⏭️ Jesus🙏 went🚶 inside🏠, His🙏 disciples🫂 asked❓ Him🙏 privately🤫, “Why❓ couldn't🚫 we🙏 drive🚪 it👿 out🚶?”

29📖 He🙏 said🗣️, “This💯 kind💯 can💪 come🚪 out🚶 only💯 through💯 prayer🙏 and✨ fasting🙏.”

30📖 They🫂 left🚶 that💯 place📍 and✨ travelled🚶 through💯 Galilee🌍. Jesus🙏 didn't🚫 want💭 anyone👥 to✨ know🧠 where📍 they🫂 were✨.

31📖 He🙏 was✨ teaching📚 His🙏 disciples🫂: “The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 will✨ be✨ handed🫳 over💨 to✨ people🫂. They'll✨ kill💀 Him🙏, and✨ after⏭️ He's💯 killed💀, He'll✨ rise⬆️ again🔁 on💯 the💯 third🔢 day📆.”

32📖 They🫂 didn't🚫 understand🧠 what💭 He🙏 meant💡, but✨ they🫂 were✨ afraid😨 to✨ ask❓ Him🙏.

33📖 They🫂 arrived🚶 in💯 Capernaum🏘️. Once⏭️ they🫂 were✨ inside🏠, Jesus🙏 asked❓ them🫂, “What💭 were✨ you🫵 arguing🗣️ about💯 on💯 the💯 way🛤️?”

34📖 They🫂 went🤐 quiet🤐, because✨ on💯 the💯 way🛤️ they'd✨ been✨ debating🗣️ who👀 among💯 them🫂 was✨ the💯 greatest👑.

35📖 Jesus🙏 sat🪑 down⬇️, called📣 the💯 Twelve🔢 over💨, and✨ said🗣️, “If✨ anyone👤 wants💭 to✨ be✨ first⏮️, they🫂 must💯 be✨ last🔚 of💯 all💯 and✨ servant🤲 of💯 all💯.”

36📖 He🙏 took🫴 a💯 little🔹 ọmọ🇳🇬 and✨ stood🧍 the💯 child👧 among💯 them🫂. Then⏭️ He🙏 gathered🤗 the💯 child👧 into💯 His🙏 arms🤲 and✨ said🗣️,

37📖 “Whoever👤 welcomes🤗 one🔢 little🔹 child👧 like💯 this💯 in💯 My🙏 name📛 welcomes🤗 Me🙏. And✨ whoever👤 welcomes🤗 Me🙏 isn't🚫 only💯 welcoming🤗 Me🙏—they🫂 welcome🤗 the💯 One👑 who👀 sent📨 Me🙏.”

38📖 John👤 said🗣️, “Teacher📚, we🙏 saw👀 someone👤 driving🚪 out🚶 demons👿 in💯 Your🙏 name📛. We🙏 tried🚫 to✨ stop🛑 him👤 because✨ he👤 wasn't🚫 part💯 of💯 our💯 group🫂.”

39📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Don't🚫 stop🛑 him👤. Nobody🫂 who👀 does✅ a💯 powerful💥 work💯 in💯 My🙏 name📛 will✨ quickly⚡ speak🗣️ evil💔 of💯 Me🙏.

40📖 Anyone👤 who👀 isn't🚫 against⚔️ us🫂 is✨ on💯 our💯 side🤝.

41📖 For💯 real💯, anyone👤 who👀 gives🫴 you🫵 a💯 cup🥤 of💯 ruwa🇳🇬 because✨ you🫵 belong🤝 to✨ Christ👑 won't🚫 lose💔 their💯 reward🎁.”

42📖 “Whoever👤 trips🫳 up💥 one🔢 of💯 these💯 little🔹 ones👧 who👀 trust🙏 Me🙏 would💯 be✨ better💯 off💯 with💯 a💯 heavy⚙️ millstone🪨 tied⛓️ around🔄 their💯 neck🧣 and✨ thrown🌊 into💯 the💯 sea🌊.

43📖 If✨ your💯 hand🖐️ pulls🫳 you🫵 into💯 sin💔, cut✂️ it💯 off🚫. It's💯 better💯 to✨ enter🚪 life🌱 injured🩹 than💯 to✨ keep💯 both💯 hands👐 and✨ be✨ thrown🫳 into💯 Gehenna🔥, into💯 fire🔥 that✨ can't🚫 be✨ put💧 out🚫,

44📖 where📍 the💯 worm🐛 doesn't🚫 die💀 and✨ the💯 fire🔥 doesn't🚫 go🚫 out💨.

45📖 If✨ your💯 foot🦶 pulls🫳 you🫵 into💯 sin💔, cut✂️ it💯 off🚫. It's💯 better💯 to✨ enter🚪 life🌱 limping🦯 than💯 to✨ keep💯 both💯 feet🦶 and✨ be✨ thrown🫳 into💯 Gehenna🔥, into💯 fire🔥 that✨ can't🚫 be✨ put💧 out🚫—

46📖 where📍 the💯 worm🐛 doesn't🚫 die💀 and✨ the💯 fire🔥 doesn't🚫 go🚫 out💨.

47📖 If✨ your💯 eye👁️ pulls🫳 you🫵 into💯 sin💔, tear🚫 it💯 out🚪. It's💯 better💯 to✨ enter🚪 God's🙏 Kingdom👑 with💯 one🔢 eye👁️ than💯 to✨ keep💯 both💯 eyes👀 and✨ be✨ thrown🫳 into💯 Gehenna🔥,

48📖 where📍 the💯 worm🐛 doesn't🚫 die💀 and✨ the💯 fire🔥 doesn't🚫 go🚫 out💨.

49📖 Everyone🫂 will✨ be✨ salted🧂 with💯 fire🔥, and✨ every💯 sacrifice🎁 will✨ be✨ seasoned🧂 with💯 salt🧂.

50📖 Salt🧂 is✨ good💛, but✨ if✨ it💯 loses💔 its💯 saltiness🧂, what💭 will✨ make🧂 it💯 salty🧂 again🔁? Keep🫴 salt🧂 within💯 yourselves🫵, and✨ live🤝 in💯 shalom🇮🇱 with💯 one💯 another🫂.”
Mark 10
# Mark 10 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Mark 10. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 Jesus🙏 left🚶 there📍 and✨ headed🚶 into💯 Judea🌍 and✨ across💯 the💯 Jordan🌊. Crowds🫂 gathered🫂 around🔄 Him🙏 again🔁, and✨ as✨ usual💯, He🙏 taught📚 them🫂.

2📖 Pharisees🕍 came🚶 to✨ test🧪 Him🙏: “Is✨ it💯 lawful⚖️ for💯 a💯 man👨 to✨ divorce📜 his💯 wife👩?”

3📖 Jesus🙏 asked❓ them🫂, “What💭 did💯 Moses📜 command📣 you🫵?”

4📖 They🫂 replied🗣️, “Moses📜 allowed✅ a💯 man👨 to✨ write✍️ a💯 divorce📜 certificate📄 and✨ send📤 his💯 wife👩 away🚶.”

5📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Moses📜 wrote✍️ that💯 rule📜 because✨ your💯 cœur🇫🇷 had✨ hardened🪨.

6📖 But✨ from💯 the💯 very💯 start🌅 of💯 creation🌍, God🙏 made🛠️ people🫂 male👨 and✨ female👩.

7📖 That's💯 why💯 a💯 man👨 leaves🚶 his💯 father👨 and✨ mother👩 and✨ joins🤝 his💯 wife👩,

8📖 and✨ the💯 two✌️ become🤝 one🔢 flesh🫂. They're💯 no🚫 longer⏱️ two✌️, but✨ one🔢.

9📖 So💯 what💯 God🙏 has✨ joined🤝 together🫂, no🚫 person👤 should✨ split💔 apart↔️.”

10📖 Back🔙 inside🏠, the💯 disciples🫂 asked❓ Jesus🙏 about✨ this💯 again🔁.

11📖 He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “Whoever👤 divorces📜 his💯 wife👩 and✨ marries💍 someone👩 else💯 commits💔 adultery💔 against⚔️ her👩.

12📖 And✨ if💯 a💯 woman👩 divorces📜 her💯 husband👨 and✨ marries💍 someone👨 else💯, she👩 commits💔 adultery💔.”

13📖 People🫂 brought🫴 little🔹 children👧 to✨ Jesus🙏, hoping💭 He🙏 would✨ touch🤲 them🫂, but✨ the💯 disciples🫂 told🗣️ off⚠️ the💯 people🫂 bringing🫴 them🧒.

14📖 Jesus🙏 saw👀 that💯 and✨ it💯 got💯 Him🙏 heated🔥. He🙏 said🗣️, “Let✨ the💯 children👧 come🚶 to✨ Me🙏! Don't🚫 stop🛑 them🫂. God's🙏 Kingdom👑 belongs🤲 to✨ people💯 like💯 these💯.

15📖 I'm🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵 for💯 real💯: whoever👤 doesn't🚫 receive🤲 God's🙏 Kingdom👑 like💯 a💯 little🔹 ọmọ🇳🇬 will✨ never🚫 enter🚪 it💯.”

16📖 Jesus🙏 gathered🤗 the💯 children👧 into💯 His🙏 arms🤲, laid🫴 His🙏 hands👐 on💯 them🫂, and✨ blessed🙏 them🫂.

17📖 As⏳ Jesus🙏 was✨ setting🚶 out🚶 again🔁, a💯 Mann🇩🇪 ran🏃 up⬆️, knelt🙇 before✨ Him🙏, and✨ asked❓, “Good💛 Teacher📚, what💭 should✨ I🙏 do✅ to✨ inherit🎁 eternal♾️ life🌱?”

18📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “Why❓ do💯 you🫵 call📣 Me🙏 good💛? No🚫 one👤 is✨ good💛 except💯 God🙏 alone👑.

19📖 You🫵 know🧠 the💯 mitzvot🇮🇱: ‘Don't🚫 murder💀. Don't🚫 commit💔 adultery💔. Don't🚫 steal🫳. Don't🚫 give🗣️ false🎭 testimony🧾. Don't🚫 cheat💰 anyone👤. Honour🙌 your💯 father👨 and✨ mother👩.’”

20📖 The💯 man👨 answered🗣️, “Teacher📚, I've🙏 kept🔒 all💯 of💯 those💯 since⏳ I🙏 was✨ young👦.”

21📖 Jesus🙏 looked👀 at✨ him👨 with💯 love❤️ and✨ said🗣️, “One🔢 thing💯 is✨ missing🔎. Go🚶 sell🛒 what💯 you🫵 own💰, give🤲 the💯 money💰 to✨ the💯 poor🫂, and✨ you'll💯 have💯 treasure💎 in💯 heaven☁️. Then⏭️ come🚶 follow🚶 Me🙏, taking🫴 up⬆️ the💯 cross✝️.”

22📖 The💯 man's👨 face😔 fell⬇️. Sorrow😢 sank⬇️ deep💯 in💯 his💯 obi🇳🇬; he👨 walked🚶 away💨 grieving😢, because✨ he👨 owned💰 a💯 lot💯.

23📖 Jesus🙏 looked👀 around🔄 and✨ said🗣️ to✨ His🙏 disciples🫂, “It's💯 so💯 hard💥 for💯 people🫂 with💰 wealth💰 to✨ enter🚪 God's🙏 Kingdom👑.”

24📖 The💯 disciples🫂 were✨ stunned😲. Jesus🙏 continued🔄, “Children👧, how💭 hard💥 it💯 is✨ for💯 people🫂 who👀 put💯 their💯 zuciya🇳🇬 in💰 riches💰 to✨ enter🚪 God's🙏 Kingdom👑!

25📖 It's💯 easier💨 for💯 a💯 camello🇪🇸🐪 to✨ squeeze🤏 through💯 the💯 eye👁️ of💯 a💯 needle🪡 than💯 for💯 a💯 rich💰 person👤 to✨ enter🚪 God's🙏 Kingdom👑.”

26📖 They🫂 were✨ completely💯 shocked😲 and✨ asked❓ Him🙏, “Then⏭️ who👀 can💪 be✨ saved🛟?”

27📖 Jesus🙏 looked👀 at✨ them🫂: “People🫂 can't🚫 do💪 this💯 on💯 their💯 own💯, but✨ God🙏 can💪. With💯 God🙏, everything💯 is✨ possible💪.”

28📖 Peter👤 said🗣️, “Look👀, we🙏 left🚶 everything💯 and✨ followed🚶 You🙏.”

29📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “For💯 real💯, everyone🫂 who👀 left🚶 home🏠, brothers👥, sisters👭, father👨, mother👩, wife👩, children👧, or✨ land🌾 because✨ of💯 Me🙏 and✨ the💯 Good💯 News📣

30📖 will✨ receive🤲 a💯 hundred💯 times🔁 more💯 in💯 this💯 life🌍: homes🏠, brothers👥, sisters👭, mothers👩, children👧, and✨ land🌾—along🤝 with✨ persecutions⚠️. And✨ in💯 the💯 age⏳ to✨ come🚶, they'll💯 receive🤲 eternal♾️ life🌱.

31📖 But✨ many💯 who👀 are✨ first🥇 will✨ be✨ last🔚, and✨ the💯 last🔚 will✨ be✨ first🥇.”

32📖 They🫂 were✨ on💯 the💯 road🛤️ going🚶 up⬆️ to✨ Jerusalem🏙️. Jesus🙏 walked🚶 ahead➡️; the💯 disciples🫂 were✨ amazed😲, and✨ those💯 following🚶 felt😨 afraid😨. Jesus🙏 took🫴 the💯 Twelve🔢 aside🤫 again🔁 and✨ explained🗣️ what💭 was✨ about💯 to✨ happen💥 to✨ Him🙏:

33📖 “We're🙏 going🚶 up⬆️ to✨ Jerusalem🏙️. The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 will✨ be✨ handed🫳 over💨 to✨ the💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ Scripture📜 teachers📚. They'll💯 sentence⚖️ Him🙏 to✨ death💀 and✨ hand📤 Him🙏 over💨 to✨ the💯 Gentiles🌍.

34📖 They'll💯 mock🎭 Him🙏, spit💦 on💯 Him🙏, whip🩸 Him🙏, and✨ kill💀 Him🙏. Then⏭️ after💯 three🔢 days📆, He'll✨ rise⬆️ again🔁.”

35📖 James👤 and✨ John👤, the💯 sons👥 of💯 Zebedee👤, came🚶 over💨: “Teacher📚, we🙏 want💭 You🙏 to✨ do✅ whatever💯 we🙏 ask❓.”

36📖 Jesus🙏 asked❓ them🫂, “What💭 do💯 you🫵 want💭 Me🙏 to✨ do✅ for✨ you🫵?”

37📖 They🫂 said🗣️, “Let✨ us🫂 sit🪑 beside💯 You🙏 in💯 Your🙏 glory✨—one🔢 on💯 Your🙏 right👉 and✨ one🔢 on💯 Your🙏 left👈.”

38📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “You🫵 don't🚫 understand🧠 what💭 you're🙏 asking❓. Can💪 you🫵 drink🥤 the💯 cup🥤 I'm🙏 going🚶 to✨ drink🥤 and✨ go💧 through💯 the💯 baptism💧 I'm🙏 going🚶 to✨ face💯?”

39📖 They🫂 answered🗣️, “We🙏 can💪.” Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “You🫵 will✨ drink🥤 My💯 cup🥤 and✨ go💧 through💯 My💯 baptism💧.

40📖 But✨ sitting🪑 at💯 My💯 right👉 or✨ left👈 isn't🚫 Mine🙏 to✨ assign📜. Those💯 places📍 belong🤲 to✨ the💯 people🫂 they're✨ prepared✅ for💯.”

41📖 When⏳ the💯 other🔟 ten🔟 disciples🫂 heard👂, Dem🇯🇲🫂 got😠 upset😠 with✨ James👤 and✨ John👤.

42📖 Jesus🙏 called📣 them🫂 together🤝: “You🫵 know🧠 how💭 rulers👑 of💯 the💯 nations🌍 push💥 people🫂 around🔄 and✨ their💯 big💯 names📛 throw🫴 their💯 weight💪 around🔄.

43📖 That's💯 not🚫 how💭 it💯 works⚙️ among💯 you🫂. Whoever👤 wants💭 to✨ be✨ great👑 among💯 you🫂 must💯 become🤝 your💯 servant🤲.

44📖 Whoever👤 wants💭 to✨ be✨ first🥇 among💯 you🫂 must💯 become🤲 everyone's💯 servo🇵🇹🫶.

45📖 Even💯 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 came🚶 not🚫 to✨ be✨ served🤲 but✨ to✨ serve🤲, and✨ to✨ give🫴 His🙏 life🌱 as✨ a💯 ransom💰 for✨ many💯.”

49📖 I🙏 was✨ teaching📚 in💯 the💯 Temple🕍 with💯 you🫂 every💯 day📆, and✨ you🫵 didn't🚫 arrest⛓️ Me🙏. But✨ this💯 is✨ happening💥 so✨ the💯 Scriptures📜 will✨ be✨ fulfilled✅.”

53📖 They🫂 led🚶 Jesus🙏 to✨ the💯 high👑 priest🕍. All💯 the💯 chief👑 priests🕍, elders👴, and✨ Scripture📜 teachers📚 gathered🫂 there📍.

56📖 Many💯 people🫂 gave🗣️ false🎭 testimony🗣️ against⚔️ Him🙏, but✨ their💯 stories📣 didn't🚫 match🧩.

57📖 Some💯 stood🧍 up⬆️ and✨ gave🗣️ this💯 false🎭 testimony🗣️:

58📖 “We🙏 heard👂 Him🙏 say🗣️, ‘I'll✨ destroy💥 this💯 Temple🕍 made👐 by💯 human🫂 hands👐, and✨ in💯 three🔢 days📆 I'll✨ build🛠️ another💯 one🔢 not🚫 made👐 by💯 human🫂 hands👐.’”

59📖 Even💯 then⏭️, their💯 testimony🗣️ didn't🚫 line🧩 up⬆️.

60📖 The💯 high👑 priest🕍 stood⬆️ in💯 the💯 middle🧍 and✨ asked❓ Jesus🙏, “Aren't💭 You🙏 going💭 to✨ answer🗣️? What💭 do💯 You🙏 say🗣️ about💯 these💯 accusations⚖️?”

61📖 Jesus🙏 stayed🤐 silent🤐 and✨ said🚫 nothing💬. The💯 high👑 priest🕍 questioned❓ Him🙏 again🔁: “Are💭 You🙏 the💯 Christ👑, the💯 Son👑 of💯 the💯 Blessed🙏 One👑?”

62📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “I🙏 am✨. And✨ you'll💯 see👀 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 seated🪑 at💯 Power's💪 right👉 hand🤚 and✨ coming🚶 with💯 clouds☁️ am🇩🇪 Himmel🇩🇪.”

64📖 You🫵 heard👂 what💭 He🙏 said🗣️! You🫵 heard👂 the💯 blasphemy⚠️! What💭 do💯 you🫵 think🧠?” They🫂 all💯 condemned⚖️ Jesus🙏 as✨ deserving⚖️ death💀.

65📖 Some💯 started🚀 spitting💦 on💯 Him🙏, covering🫥 His🙏 face😶, punching👊 Him🙏, and✨ taunting🎭, “Prophesy📣!” The💯 guards🪖 slapped👋 Him🙏.

Mark 15
# Mark 15 — AdigunBible draft

1📖 Early🌅 in💯 the💯 morning🌄, the💯 chief👑 priests🕍 met🫂 with💯 elders👴, Scripture📜 teachers📚, and✨ the💯 whole💯 council🏛️. They🫂 tied⛓️ Jesus🙏 up⛓️, took🫴 Him🙏 to✨ Pilate👑, and✨ handed📤 Him🙏 over💨.

Mark 16
# Mark 16 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Mark 16, including its long ending (verses 9–20). Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 When⏱️ the💯 Sabbath🕊️ was✨ over⏳, Mary👩 Magdalene👩, Mary👩 the💯 mother👩 of💯 James👤, and✨ Salome👩 bought🛒 spices🌿 to✨ anoint🧴 Jesus🙏.

4📖 Then⏭️ they🫂 looked👀 up⬆️ and✨ saw👀 the💯 very💯 large💯 stone🪨 had✨ already💯 been✨ rolled🪨 back↩️.

5📖 They🫂 went🚶 inside🏠 the💯 tomb⚰️ and✨ saw👀 a💯 young🔹 man👤 sitting🪑 on💯 the💯 right👉 side➡️, wearing👘 a💯 bright🤍 white🤍 robe👘. They🫂 were✨ shocked😲.

6📖 He👤 said🗣️, “Don't🚫 be✨ shocked😲. You're🙏 looking🔎 for💯 Jesus🙏 from💯 Nazareth🌍, the💯 One👤 who👀 was✨ crucified✝️. He's👑 risen⬆️ to✨ ìyè🇳🇬! He's👑 not🚫 here📍. Look👀 at💯 the💯 place📍 where💯 they🫂 laid🛏️ Him🙏.

7📖 Now💯 go🚶 tell🗣️ His🙏 disciples🫂—and✨ Peter👤 too💯: ‘He's👑 going🚶 ahead➡️ of💯 you🫵 into💯 Galilee🌍. You'll💯 see👀 Him🙏 there📍, just💯 as✨ He🙏 told🗣️ you🫵.’”

8📖 They🫂 rushed🏃 out🚪 and✨ ran🏃 from💯 the💯 tomb⚰️, trembling🫨 and✨ overwhelmed😲. They🫂 didn't🚫 tell🗣️ anyone👥, because✨ peur🇫🇷 had✨ taken🫳 hold⛓️ of💯 them🫂.

9📖 After⏭️ Jesus🙏 rose⬆️ early🌅 on💯 the💯 first🔢 day📆 of💯 the💯 week📅, He👑 appeared👀 first⏮️ to✨ Mary👩 Magdalene👩, the💯 woman👩 from💯 whom💯 He🙏 had✨ driven🚪 out🚶 seven🔢 demons👿.

10📖 She👩 went🚶 and✨ told🗣️ the💯 people🫂 who👀 had✨ been✨ with💯 Him🙏, while⏳ they🫂 were✨ mourning😢 and✨ crying😭.

11📖 They🫂 heard👂 Jesus🙏 was✨ alive🌱 and✨ that✨ Mary👩 had✨ seen👀 Him🙏, but✨ they🫂 didn't🚫 believe🙏 it💯.

12📖 Later⏭️, Jesus🙏 appeared👀 in💯 another💯 form✨ to✨ two🔢 of💯 them🫂 as⏳ they🫂 walked🚶 out🚶 into💯 the💯 countryside🌾.

13📖 They🫂 went🚶 back🔙 and✨ told🗣️ the💯 others🫂, but✨ dem🇯🇲🫂 didn't🚫 believe🙏 them🫂 either💯.

14📖 Later⏭️, Jesus🙏 appeared👀 to✨ the💯 eleven🔢 as⏳ they🫂 sat🪑 eating🍽️. He🙏 rebuked🗣️ them🫂 for✨ their💯 unbelief💔 and✨ hard🪨 obi🇳🇬; they🫂 hadn't🚫 believed🙏 the💯 people🫂 who👀 saw👀 Him🙏 after⏭️ He🙏 rose⬆️.

15📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “Go🚶 into💯 every💯 corner📍 of💯 mundo🇪🇸 and✨ announce📣 the💯 Good💯 News📣 to✨ the💯 whole💯 creation🌍.

16📖 Whoever👤 believes🙏 and✨ is✨ baptised💧 will✨ be✨ saved🛟; whoever👤 refuses🚫 to✨ believe🙏 will✨ be✨ condemned⚖️.

17📖 These💯 signs✨ will✨ accompany🤝 believers🙏: in💯 My🙏 name📛 they'll💯 drive🚪 out🚶 demons👿 and✨ speak🗣️ new✨ línguas🇵🇹.

18📖 They'll💯 pick🫴 up⬆️ snakes🐍, and✨ if✨ they🫂 drink🥤 something💯 deadly☠️, it💯 won't🚫 harm💔 them🫂. They'll💯 lay🫴 hands👐 on💯 sick🤒 people🫂, and✨ those💯 people🫂 will✨ recover💪.”

19📖 After⏭️ speaking🗣️ to✨ them🫂, the💯 Lord👑 was✨ taken⬆️ up⬆️ into💯 heaven☁️ and✨ sat🪑 at💯 the💯 right👉 hand🤚 of💯 Elohim🇮🇱.

20📖 The💯 disciples🫂 went🚶 out🚶 and✨ preached📣 everywhere🌍. The💯 Lord👑 worked⚙️ with💯 them🫂 and✨ confirmed✅ His🙏 word📜 through💯 the💯 signs✨ that✨ followed🚶. Amen🙏.”

Luke 1
# Luke 1 — AdigunBible draft

1📖 Fam🫂, plenty💯 people👥 have✨ already💯 tried📝 to✨ lay📚 out📖 the💯 story📖 of💯 what💭 God🙏 brought✅ to✨ pass💯 among💯 us🫂,

3📖 So✨ I🙏 checked🔎 every💯 part🧩 from💯 the💯 start🌅 too💯, honoured🌟 Theophilus👤, then⏱️ chose📝 to✨ lay📜 it💯 out📚 in💯 order📚 for✨ you🫵,

8📖 One💯 day📆, Zechariah👤 was✨ doing🤲 his💯 priestly🕍 service🕍 before💯 God🙏 while⏳ his💯 division🧾 was✨ up📋 on💯 duty📋.

9📖 By💯 priestly🕍 custom📜, they🫂 drew🎲 lots🎲 to✨ choose📍 who👤 would💭 enter🚪 the💯 Lord’s👑 Temple🕍 and✨ burn🔥 incense🌿. Zechariah👤 got✅ picked🎯.

67📖 His💯 father👨 Zechariah👤 filled💯 with💯 the💯 Holy🕊️ Spirit🕊️ began🚀 prophesying📣:

# Luke 2 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Luke 2. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 In💯 those💯 days📆, Caesar👑 Augustus👤 sent📜 out💨 an💯 order📣 for✨ the💯 whole💯 mundo🇪🇸 to✨ sign📝 up💯.

2📖 This💯 first🔢 registration📝 happened⏱️ while✨ Quirinius👤 was✨ governor👑 over💯 Syria🌍.

3📖 Everybody💯 headed🚶 to✨ their💯 own💯 hometown🏘️ to✨ get📝 registered✅.

4📖 Joseph👤 went🚶 from💯 Nazareth🏘️ in💯 Galilee🌍 up🚶 to✨ Judea🌍, to✨ Bethlehem🏘️—David’s👑 town🏘️—because✨ he👤 belonged🤝 to✨ David’s👑 family👨‍👩‍👧.

5📖 He👤 registered📝 with💯 Mary👩, pledged💍 to✨ be✨ his💯 wife👰; she👩 was✨ pregnant🤰.

6📖 While⏳ they🫂 were✨ there📍, her💯 time⏱️ to✨ give🫴 birth🌱 arrived📍.

7📖 She👩 gave🫴 birth🌱 to✨ her💯 firstborn👶 son👑, wrapped🫂 Him🙏 in💯 cloth🧵 bands🧵, and✨ laid🛏️ Him🙏 in💯 a💯 feeding🍽️ trough🪵, because✨ the💯 inn🏠 had🚫 no🚫 room🛏️ for✨ them🫂.

8📖 Out📍 in💯 that💯 same💯 region🌍, shepherds🐑 were✨ posted🏕️ in💯 the💯 fields🌾, keeping👀 night🌙 watch🛡️ over💯 their💯 flock🐑.

9📖 Suddenly⚡ an💯 angel👼 of💯 the💯 Lord👑 stood🧍 beside💯 them🫂; the💯 Lord’s👑 glory✨ lit💡 up🔆 around💯 them🫂, and✨ fear😨 hit💥 hard💯.

10📖 The💯 angel👼 told🗣️ them🫂, “Don’t🚫 fear😨! Look👀, I’m🙏 bringing🫴 good📣 news📣—great💯 joie🇫🇷 for✨ all💯 the💯 people👥.

11📖 Today📆, in💯 David’s👑 town🏘️, a💯 Saviour🛟 has✨ been✨ born🌱 for✨ you🫵: the💯 Mashiach🇮🇱, Christ👑 the💯 Lord👑.

12📖 Here’s👀 your💯 sign✨: you’ll💯 find🔎 a💯 baby👶 wrapped🫂 in💯 cloth🧵 strips🧵, lying🛏️ in💯 a💯 feeding🍽️ trough🪵.”

13📖 Then⚡ a💯 huge💯 heavenly☁️ host👼 showed👀 up💯 with💯 the💯 angel👼, praising🙌 God🙏 and✨ singing🗣️,

14📖 “Glory✨ to✨ God🙏 above⬆️ everything💯! On💯 earth🌍: paz🇵🇹, goodwill💛 toward💯 all💯 people👥!”

15📖 When⏱️ the💯 angels👼 left🚶 them🫂 and✨ went🔙 into💯 heaven☁️, Dem🇯🇲🫂 said🗣️ to✨ one💯 another🫂, “Let’s💯 move🚶 to✨ Bethlehem🏘️ now⏱️ and✨ see👀 this💯 thing💭 the💯 Lord👑 let🗣️ us🫂 know🧠.”

16📖 They🫂 rushed🏃 over🚶 and✨ found🔎 Mary👩, Joseph👤, and✨ the💯 baby👶 lying🛏️ in💯 the💯 trough🪵.

17📖 Once⏱️ they🫂 saw👀 Him🙏, they🫂 spread📣 everywhere💯 what💭 they’d✨ been✨ told🗣️ about💯 this💯 child👶.

18📖 Everyone👥 who✨ heard👂 the💯 shepherds🐑 was✨ stunned😲 by💯 what💭 they🫂 told🗣️ them💯.

19📖 Mary👩 held🔒 every💯 word🗣️ close💯, turning🔄 it💭 over💭 in💯 her💯 obi🇳🇬 heart❤️.

20📖 The💯 shepherds🐑 headed🚶 back🔙, praising🙌 and✨ giving✨ God🙏 glory💯 for✨ everything💯 they’d✨ heard👂 and✨ seen👀—just💯 as✨ they’d✨ been✨ told🗣️.

21📖 On💯 day📆 eight🔢, when⏱️ the💯 baby👶 was✨ circumcised✂️, He🙏 was✨ named📛 Jesus🙏—the💯 name📛 the💯 angel👼 gave🫴 before⏱️ He🙏 was✨ conceived🤰.

22📖 When⏱️ the💯 days📆 of💯 purification🕊️ under💯 the💯 law📜 given🫴 through💯 Moses📜 were✨ complete✅, they🫂 brought🫴 Jesus🙏 up🚶 to✨ Jerusalem🏙️ to✨ present🤲 Him🙏 to✨ the💯 Lord👑,

23📖 just💯 as✨ the💯 Lord’s👑 law📜 says🗣️: “Every💯 male👶 who✨ opens🔓 the💯 womb🤰 is✨ to💭 be✨ called📣 holy🕊️ to✨ the💯 Lord👑.”

24📖 They🫂 also💯 brought🫴 the💯 sacrifice🎁 the💯 Lord’s👑 law📜 called📣 for💯: a💯 pair✌️ of💯 turtledoves🕊️, or✨ two🔢 young🐣 pigeons🕊️.

25📖 In💯 Jerusalem🏙️ lived🏠 Simeon👤, a💯 righteous🕊️, devoted🙏 man👤 waiting⏳ for✨ Israel👥’s🫂 comfort🤲; his💯 zuciya🇳🇬 held🔒 that💯 hope🌱, and✨ the💯 Holy🕊️ Spirit🕊️ rested🕊️ on💯 him👤.

27📖 The💯 Spirit🕊️ led🧭 Simeon👤 into💯 the💯 Temple🕍. As⏱️ Mary👩 and✨ Joseph👤 brought🫴 in💯 baby👶 Jesus🙏 to✨ follow✅ the💯 law📜,

30📖 My💯 eyes👀 have✨ seen👀 Your🙏 rescue🛟,

31📖 the💯 rescue🛟 You🙏 prepared🛠️ for✨ all💯 peoples👥 to✨ see👀,

32📖 luz🇪🇸 for✨ revelation🔦 to✨ the💯 nations🌍, and✨ glory✨ for💯 Your🙏 people👥 Israel👥.”

33📖 Joseph👤 and✨ the💯 mother👩 of💯 Jesus🙏 were✨ amazed😲 at💯 what💭 people🫂 were✨ saying🗣️ about💯 Him🙏.

35📖 A💯 sword⚔️ will💯 pierce🗡️ your💯 soul🫶 too💯; the💯 thoughts💭 of💯 many💯 hearts💖 will💯 come🔦 into💯 the💯 open🔦.”

36📖 Anna👩 the💯 prophetess📣 was✨ there📍 too💯—Phanuel’s👤 daughter👧, from💯 Asher’s👤 tribe👥, and✨ already💯 very💯 old👵. She👩 had✨ lived🏠 with💯 her💯 husband👨 seven🔢 years📆 from💯 her💯 virginity👰,

37📖 then⏭️ stayed✨ a💯 widow👵 about💯 eighty-four🔢 years📆. She👩 never🚫 left🚪 the💯 Temple🕍, serving🙏 God🙏 with💯 fasting🙏 and✨ prayer🙏 night🌙 and✨ day🌞.

38📖 Right⏱️ then⏱️ Anna👩 came🚶 up💯, thanked🙏 the💯 Lord👑, and✨ spoke🗣️ about💯 Jesus🙏 to✨ everyone🫂 waiting⏳ for✨ Jerusalem🏙️’s🫂 redemption🛟.

39📖 After⏱️ Mary👩 and✨ Joseph👤 finished✅ everything💯 the💯 Lord’s👑 law📜 required📜, they🫂 went🚶 back🔙 to✨ Galilee🌍, to✨ their💯 own💯 town🏘️, Nazareth🏘️.

40📖 The💯 child👶 kept🔁 growing📈, becoming💪 strong💪 in💯 spirit🕊️ and✨ full💯 of💯 wisdom🧠; God’s🙏 grace🤲 rested✨ on💯 Him🙏.

43📖 When⏱️ the💯 feast🎉 days📆 were✨ over🔚 and✨ they🫂 headed🚶 home🏠, boy👦 Jesus🙏 stayed🧍 behind🔙 in💯 Jerusalem🏙️—Joseph👤 and✨ Mary👩 didn’t🚫 know🧠.

44📖 Thinking💭 He🙏 was✨ somewhere💯 in💯 the💯 travel🫂 group🫂, they🫂 went🚶 a💯 full💯 day’s📆 journey🛤️, then⏭️ searched🔎 among💯 relatives👨‍👩‍👧 and✨ people🫂 they💯 knew🤝.

45📖 When⏱️ they🫂 couldn’t🚫 find🔎 Him🙏, they🫂 turned🚶 back🔙 to✨ Jerusalem🏙️, looking🔎 for✨ Him🙏.

46📖 After⏭️ three🔢 days📆, they🫂 found🔎 Him🙏 in💯 the💯 Temple🕍, sitting🪑 among💯 the💯 teachers📚, listening👂 close💯 and✨ asking❓ them🫂 questions❓.

47📖 Everyone👥 hearing👂 Him🙏 was✨ amazed😲 at💯 His🙏 insight🧠 and✨ answers🗣️.

48📖 His🙏 parents👨‍👩‍👦 saw👀 Him🙏 and✨ froze😲 in💯 shock😲. His💯 mother👩 said🗣️, “Son👦, why❓ did✨ You🙏 do✅ this💯 to✨ us🫂? Your💯 father👨 and✨ I🙏 have✨ been✨ searching🔎 in💯 serious😟 stress😟.”

49📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Why❓ were✨ you🫵 looking🔎 for✨ Me🙏? Didn’t🚫 you🫵 know🧠 I🙏 must💯 be✨ in💯 My💯 Father’s🙏 house🏠?”

50📖 They🫂 didn’t🚫 understand🧠 what💭 He🙏 was✨ telling💬 them🫂.

51📖 Jesus🙏 went⬇️ back🔙 with💯 them🫂 to✨ Nazareth🏘️ and✨ stayed🤝 subject📜 to✨ them🫂. His💯 mother👩 kept🔒 every💯 one💯 of💯 these💭 things💭 in💯 her💯 heart💖.

52📖 Jesus🙏 kept🔁 growing📈 in💯 wisdom🧠 and✨ stature📏, gaining💛 favour💛 with💯 God🙏 and✨ people🫂.

# Luke 4 — AdigunBible draft

1📖 Jesus🙏 was✨ filled💯 with💯 the💯 Holy🕊️ Spirit🕊️ when⏱️ He🙏 returned🚶 from💯 the💯 Jordan🌊. The💯 Spirit🕊️ led🧭 Him🙏 out➡️ into💯 Wüste🇩🇪🏜️,

2📖 where📍 the💯 devil😈 tested🎭 Him🙏 for💯 forty🔢 days📆. He🙏 ate🚫 no🚫 oúnjẹ🇳🇬🍲 during💯 those💯 days📆; when⏱️ they🫂 ended🔚, He🙏 was✨ hungry🤤.

3📖 The💯 devil😈 said🗣️, “If✨ You🙏 are✨ God’s🙏 Son👑, command🗣️ this💯 stone🪨 to✨ become🔄 pan🇪🇸🍞.”

4📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Scripture📜 says🗣️: ‘A💯 person👤 won’t🚫 live💛 on💯 pão🇵🇹🍞 alone💯, but✨ by💯 every💯 word📜 from💯 God🙏.’”

5📖 Then⏱️ the💯 devil😈 led🧭 Jesus🙏 up⬆️ a💯 high💯 mountain⛰️ and✨ flashed⚡ all💯 the💯 kingdoms👑 of💯 the💯 world🌍 before💯 Him🙏.

6📖 “I🙏 will💯 give🎁 You🙏 authority👑 over💯 all💯 this💯 and✨ all💯 its💯 glory✨,” the💯 devil😈 said🗣️. “It💯 was✨ handed📤 to✨ me🙏, and✨ I🙏 can💪 give🎁 it💯 to✨ anyone👤 I🙏 choose💭.

7📖 So✨ worship🙏 me🙏, and✨ it💯 can💪 all💯 be✨ Yours🫵.”

8📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Get⬅️ behind💯 Me🙏, Satan😈! Scripture📜 says🗣️: ‘Worship🙏 the💯 Lord👑 your💯 God🙏, and✨ serve🤲 Him🙏 alone💯.’”

9📖 The💯 devil😈 led🧭 Jesus🙏 to✨ Jerusalem🏙️ and✨ set🧍 Him🙏 on💯 the💯 Temple’s🕍 highest⬆️ point📍: “If✨ You🙏 are✨ God’s🙏 Son👑, throw🫳 Yourself🙏 down⬇️ from💯 here📍!

10📖 Scripture📜 says🗣️, ‘God🙏 will💯 put🫴 His🙏 angels👼 in💯 charge👑 of💯 You🙏, to✨ guard🛡️ You🙏;

11📖 ‘And✨ they’ll💯 carry🫴 You🙏 in💯 their💯 hands🤲, so✨ You🙏 don’t🚫 hit💥 Your🙏 foot🦶 against⚔️ a💯 stone🪨.’”

12📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Scripture📜 also➕ says🗣️: ‘You🫵 must💯 not🚫 test🧪 the💯 Lord👑 your💯 God🙏.’”

13📖 When⏱️ the💯 devil😈 had💯 tried🧪 every💯 temptation🎭, he👤 left🚶 Jesus🙏 until⏳ another💯 time⏱️.

14📖 Jesus🙏 returned🚶 to✨ Galilee🌍 in💯 the💯 Spirit’s🕊️ power💪. News📣 about💭 Him🙏 spread🏃 through💯 the💯 whole💯 region🌍.

15📖 He🙏 taught📚 in💯 their💯 synagogues🕍, and✨ everyone💯 honoured🙌 Him🙏.

16📖 Jesus🙏 came🚶 to✨ Nazareth🏘️, where📍 He🙏 grew⬆️ up⬆️. On💯 the💯 Sabbath🕊️, He🙏 went🚶 to✨ the💯 synagogue🕍 as⏳ usual💯 and✨ stood⬆️ to✨ read📜.

17📖 Someone👤 handed📤 Him🙏 the💯 scroll📜 of💯 the💯 prophet📣 Isaiah👤. He🙏 opened📖 it💯 and✨ found🔎 the💯 passage📍 that✨ said🗣️:

18📖 “The💯 Spirit🕊️ of💯 the💯 Lord👑 is✨ on💯 Me🙏, because✨ He🙏 anointed🫗 Me🙏 to✨ bring🫴 good💛 news📣 to✨ the💯 poor💸. He🙏 sent📨 Me🙏 to✨ heal💊 the💯 broken💔 obi🇳🇬❤️, announce📣 release🔓 to✨ captives⛓️, sight👀 to✨ the💯 blind🙈, and✨ set🔓 crushed💔 people👥 free🕊️,

19📖 to✨ announce📣 the💯 Lord’s👑 year📆 of💯 favour💛.”

20📖 Jesus🙏 closed🔒 the💯 scroll📜, gave📤 it💯 back↩️ to✨ the💯 attendant🫂, and✨ sat🪑 down⬇️. Everyone💯 in💯 the💯 synagogue🕍 kept👀 their💯 eyes👀 on💯 Him🙏.

21📖 He🙏 began🚀 speaking🗣️: “Today📆, while⏱️ you🫵 hear👂 it💯, this💯 Scripture📜 has✨ been💯 fulfilled✅.”

22📖 Everyone💯 spoke🗣️ well💛 of💯 Him🙏 and✨ marvelled😲 at💯 the💯 gracious🤲 parole🇫🇷💬 coming🚶 from💯 His🙏 mouth🗣️. “Isn’t❓ He🙏 Joseph’s👤 son👦?” they🫂 asked🗣️.

23📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “You’ll💯 probably💭 quote📣 this💯 saying🗣️ to✨ me🙏: ‘Doctor🩺, heal🩺 yourself🫵!’ You’ll✨ also➕ say🗣️, ‘Do✅ here📍 in💯 Your🙏 hometown🏘️ the💯 same💯 things💭 we🫂 heard👂 You🙏 did✅ in💯 Capernaum🏘️.’

24📖 For💯 real💯, no🚫 prophet📣 is✨ welcomed🤗 in💯 his💯 hometown🏘️.

25📖 Think🧠 about💭 it💯: there✨ were✨ many💯 widows👵 in💯 Israel✡️ during💯 Elijah’s👤 time⏱️, when⏱️ the💯 sky☁️ stayed🔒 shut🌑 for💯 three🔢 years📆 and✨ six🔢 months📆, and✨ a💯 severe💥 famine🥀 swept🌪️ across💯 the💯 land🌍.

26📖 But✨ Elijah👤 wasn’t🚫 sent📨 to✨ any💯 of💯 them🫂. He👤 was✨ sent📨 only💯 to✨ a💯 widow👵 in💯 Zarephath🏘️, in💯 the💯 region🌍 of💯 Sidon🏙️.

27📖 There✨ were✨ many💯 people👥 with💯 leprosy🤒 in💯 Israel✡️ during💯 Elisha’s👤 time⏱️, but✨ none🚫 were✨ cleansed✨ except💯 Naaman👤 the💯 Syrian🌍.”

28📖 Everyone💯 in💯 the💯 synagogue🕍 was✨ furious😡 when⏱️ they🫂 heard👂 that💯.

29📖 They🫂 jumped⬆️ up⬆️, drove🚪 Jesus🙏 out💨 of💯 town🏘️, and✨ took🫴 Him🙏 to✨ the💯 brow📍 of💯 the💯 hill⛰️ their💯 town🏘️ stood🏗️ on💯, planning🧠 to✨ throw🫳 Him🙏 off⬇️ the💯 cliff⛰️.

30📖 But✨ Jesus🙏 walked🚶 straight💯 through💨 the💯 middle🫂 of💯 them🫂 and✨ went🚶 on💨 His🙏 way🛤️.

31📖 Jesus🙏 went⬇️ down⬇️ to✨ Capernaum🏘️, a💯 city🏙️ in💯 Galilee🌍. He🙏 taught📚 people👥 on💯 the💯 Sabbath🕊️,

32📖 and✨ they🫂 were✨ amazed😲 because✨ His🙏 word📜 carried💪 authority👑.

33📖 A💯 man👤 in💯 the💯 synagogue🕍 had💯 an💯 unclean🫥 spirit👿. He👤 shouted📣 at💯 the💯 top🔊 of💯 his💯 voice🗣️,

34📖 “Ah😠! What❓ do💭 You🙏 want💭 with💯 us🫂, Jesus🙏 of💯 Nazareth🏘️? Did💭 You🙏 come🚶 to✨ destroy💥 us🫂? I🙏 know🧠 who👀 You🙏 are✨—God’s🙏 Holy🕊️ One👑!”

35📖 Jesus🙏 rebuked⚠️ him👤: “Be🤫 silent🤫 and✨ come🚪 out🚶 of💯 him👤!” The💯 demon👿 threw🫳 the💯 man👤 down⬇️ in💯 front💯 of💯 everyone🫂, then⏭️ came🚪 out🚶 without🚫 hurting💔 him👤.

36📖 Everyone💯 was✨ amazed😲 and✨ asked🗣️ each💯 other👥, “What💭 is✨ this💯 word📜? He🙏 commands👑 unclean🫥 spirits👿 with💯 authority👑 and✨ power💪, and✨ they🫂 come🚪 out🚶!”

37📖 News📣 about💭 Jesus🙏 spread🏃 all💯 through💯 the💯 surrounding🌍 region🌍.

38📖 Jesus🙏 left🚶 the💯 synagogue🕍 and✨ went🚶 into💯 Simon’s👤 gida🇳🇬🏠. Simon’s👤 mother-in-law👵 was✨ suffering🤒 from💯 a💯 severe💥 fever🤒, and✨ they🫂 begged🙏 Him🙏 to✨ help🤲 her👵.

39📖 Jesus🙏 stood🧍 over💯 her👵 and✨ rebuked⚠️ the💯 fever🤒. It💯 left🚪 her👵 immediately⚡. She👵 got⬆️ up⬆️ and✨ started🚀 serving🍽️ them🫂.

40📖 At💯 sunset🌇, people👥 brought🫴 everyone💯 sick🤒 with💯 different💯 diseases🤒 to✨ Jesus🙏. He🙏 laid🫴 His🙏 hands👐 on💯 each💯 person👤 and✨ healed🩺 them🫂.

41📖 Demons👿 came🚪 out🚶 of💯 many💯 people👥; dem🇯🇲 shouted📣, “You🙏 are✨ the💯 Mashiach🇮🇱👑, the💯 Son👑 of💯 God🙏!” Jesus🙏 rebuked⚠️ them🫂 and✨ wouldn’t🚫 let✨ them🫂 speak🗣️, because✨ they🫂 knew🧠 He🙏 was✨ the💯 Mashiach🇮🇱.

42📖 At💯 daybreak🌅, Jesus🙏 left🚶 for💯 an💯 uninhabited🏜️ place📍. The💯 crowds🫂 searched🔎 for✨ Him🙏, came🚶 to✨ Him🙏, and✨ tried🤲 to✨ keep🔒 Him🙏 from💯 leaving🚶.

43📖 But✨ He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “I🙏 must💯 announce📣 the💯 Good✨ News📣 of💯 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 to✨ other💯 cities🏙️ too💯. That’s✨ why💭 I🙏 was✨ sent📨.”

44📖 He🙏 kept🔁 preaching📣 in💯 the💯 synagogues🕍 throughout💯 Galilee🌍.

5📖 Simon👤 replied🗣️, “Master👑, we🫂 worked💪 all💯 night🌙 and✨ caught🚫 nothing🚫. But✨ à🇫🇷 Ta🇫🇷 parole🇫🇷📣, I🙏 will💯 lower⬇️ la🇪🇸 red🇪🇸🎣.”

7📖 They🫂 signalled👋 to✨ their💯 partners🤝 in💯 the💯 other💯 boat🛶 to✨ come🚣 help🤲. The💯 partners🤝 came🚣, and✨ both💯 boats🛶 filled💯 up⬆️ until⏳ they🫂 began⚠️ sinking🌊.

8📖 When⏱️ Simon👤 Peter👤 saw👀 it💯, he👤 fell⬇️ at💯 Jesus🙏’ knees🦵 and✨ said🗣️, “Lord🙏, leave🚪 me👤; I🙏 am✨ a💯 sinful💔 man👤.”

21📖 The💯 scribes📜 and✨ Pharisees👥 started🔄 reasoning🧠, “Who❓ is✨ this💯 speaking🗣️ blasphemy⚠️? Who❓ can💪 forgive🤲 sins💔 except✨ God🙏 alone💯?”

32📖 I🙏 didn’t🚫 come🚶 to✨ call📣 the💯 righteous🙏, but✨ sinners💔 to✨ teshuvah🇮🇱🔄.”

35📖 The💯 time⏱️ will💯 come🚶 when⏱️ the💯 groom🤵 is✨ taken🫴 away↩️ from💯 them🫂. Then⏱️ they’ll💯 fast🚫 in💯 those💯 days📆.”

36📖 He🙏 also➕ told🗣️ them🫂 this💯 story📖: “Nobody👤 cuts✂️ a💯 piece🧵 from💯 a💯 new🆕 outfit👕 to✨ patch🪡 an💯 old🆙 one👕. That✨ would💯 tear🪡 the💯 new🆕 garment👕, and✨ the💯 new🆕 patch🧵 wouldn’t🚫 match🎨 the💯 old🆙 one👕.

37📖 Nobody👤 pours🫗 new🆕 wine🍷 into💯 old🆙 wineskins👜. The💯 new🆕 wine🍷 would💯 burst💥 them👜, spilling🫗 the💯 wine🍷 and✨ destroying💥 the💯 skins👜.

38📖 New🆕 wine🍷 belongs💯 in💯 fresh🆕 wineskins👜, so✨ both💯 stay🛡️ preserved✅.

39📖 And✨ nobody👤 who✨ has💯 tasted👅 old🆙 wine🍷 immediately⏱️ wants💭 the💯 new🆕 kind🍷. They🫂 say🗣️, ‘The💯 old🆙 is✨ better👍.’”
Luke 6
# Luke 6 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Luke 6. Draft only; native-language, rendering, red-letter, and independent human review remain pending.

1📖 On⏱️ the💯 zweiten🇩🇪 Sabbath🕍 after💯 the💯 first🔢, Jesus🙏 walked🚶 through💯 grain🌾 fields🌾. His🙏 disciples👥 plucked🫴 grain🌾 heads🌾, rubbed🤲 them🫂 between💯 their💯 hands👐, and✨ ate🍽️.

2📖 Some💯 Pharisees👥 called🗣️ them🫂 out⚠️: “Why❓ are✨ you🫵 doing💪 something💭 the💯 Sabbath🕍 law📜 doesn’t🚫 allow✅?”

3📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Haven’t❓ you🫵 read📜 what💭 David👑 did💪 when⏱️ he👤 was✨ hungry🤤—him👤 and✨ the💯 people👥 with💯 him👤?

4📖 He👤 went🚪 into💯 God’s🙏 house🏠, took🫴 the💯 showbread🍞, ate🍽️ it💯, and✨ shared🤲 it💯 with💯 his💯 crew👥. The💯 law📜 says🗣️ that✨ bread🍞 was🚫 for💯 priests🕍 alone💯.”

5📖 Then⏱️ Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 is✨ Lord👑 over💯 the💯 Sabbath🕍.”

6📖 On⏱️ another💯 Sabbath🕍, Jesus🙏 entered🚪 the💯 synagogue🕍 and✨ taught📣. A💯 man👤 there📍 had💯 a💯 withered🤕 right➡️ hand🖐️.

7📖 The💯 scribes📜 and✨ Pharisees👥 watched👀 Jesus🙏 closely🔎, waiting⏳ to✨ see👀 if✨ He🙏 would💯 heal🩺 on💯 Sabbath🕍, so✨ they🫂 could💯 accuse⚠️ Him🙏.

8📖 Jesus🙏 knew🧠 what💭 they🫂 were✨ thinking💭. He🙏 told🗣️ the💯 man👤 with💯 the💯 withered🤕 hand🖐️, “Get⬆️ up⬆️ and✨ stand🧍 in💯 the💯 middle🎯.” The💯 man👤 stood⬆️ up⬆️.

9📖 Jesus🙏 asked🗣️ them🫂, “Let✨ me🙏 ask🗣️ you🫵 this💯: on💯 Sabbath🕍, is✨ it💯 right✅ to✨ do💪 good✨ or✨ harm💥? To✨ save🛟 ìyè🇳🇬💛 or✨ kill💀?”

10📖 He🙏 looked👀 around🔄 at💯 everyone💯, then⏱️ told🗣️ the💯 man👤, “Stretch🤲 out➡️ your💯 hand🖐️.” He👤 did✅, and✨ his💯 hand🖐️ was✨ restored🩺, healthy💪 like💯 the💯 other💯 one🔢.

11📖 The💯 religious📜 leaders👥 were✨ furious😡 and✨ started🔄 plotting🧠 together🫂 what💭 they🫂 could💯 do💪 to✨ Jesus🙏.

12📖 Around⏱️ then⏱️, Jesus🙏 went🚶 up⬆️ the💯 mountain⛰️ to✨ pray🙏. He🙏 spent⏳ the💯 whole💯 night🌙 praying🙏 to✨ God🙏.

13📖 When⏱️ morning🌅 came🚶, He🙏 called📣 His🙏 disciples👥 and✨ chose🫴 twelve🔢 of💯 them🫂, naming🏷️ them🫂 apostles📣:

14📖 Simon👤, whom✨ He🙏 also➕ named🏷️ Peter👤; Andrew👤, his💯 brother👤; James👤; John👤; Philip👤; Bartholomew👤;

15📖 Matthew👤; Thomas👤; James👤, son👨‍👦 of💯 Alphaeus👤; Simon👤, called🏷️ the💯 Zealot🔥;

16📖 Judas👤, son👨‍👦 of💯 James👤; and✨ Judas👤 Iscariot👤, who✨ later⏱️ became✨ a💯 traitor⚠️.

17📖 Jesus🙏 came⬇️ down⬇️ with💯 them🫂 and✨ stood🧍 on💯 level🟰 ground🌍. A💯 crowd🫂 of💯 His🙏 disciples👥 and✨ lots💯 of💯 people👥 from💯 Judea🌍, Jerusalem🏙️, and✨ the💯 coast🌊 near💯 Tyre🏘️ and✨ Sidon🏘️ gathered🫂 to✨ hear👂 Him🙏 and✨ get💯 healed🩺.

18📖 People👥 tormented😣 by💯 unclean🕷️ spirits👿 came🚶 too💯; dem🇯🇲 were✨ healed🩺.

19📖 Everyone💯 in💯 the💯 crowd🫂 tried🤲 to✨ touch🤲 Jesus🙏, because✨ power⚡ came🚪 from💯 Him🙏 and✨ healed🩺 them🫂 all💯.

20📖 Jesus🙏 looked👀 at💯 His🙏 disciples👥 and✨ said🗣️, “Blessed✨ are✨ you🫵 who✨ are✨ poor💸, because✨ God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 is✨ yours💛.

21📖 Blessed✨ are✨ you🫵 who✨ are✨ hungry🤤 now⏱️; you🫵 will💯 be✨ filled🍽️. Blessed✨ are✨ you🫵 who✨ weep😢 now⏱️; you🫵 will💯 laugh😄.

22📖 Blessed✨ are✨ you🫵 when⏱️ people👥 hate😡 you🫵, exclude🚪 you🫵, mock🤡 you🫵, and✨ smear🗣️ your💯 name🏷️ as✨ evil😈 because💯 of💯 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤.

23📖 When⏱️ that💯 day📆 comes🚶, rejoice🎉 and✨ leap🤸 for💯 joy😄! Your💯 reward🎁 in💯 heaven🌌 is✨ great💯, because✨ their💯 ancestors👨‍👩‍👧 did✅ the💯 same💯 to✨ the💯 prophets📣.

24📖 “But✨ woe⚠️ to✨ you🫵 who✨ are✨ rich💰; you🫵 already💯 got🎁 your💯 comfort🛋️.

25📖 Woe⚠️ to✨ you🫵 who✨ are✨ full🍽️ now⏱️; you🫵 will💯 be✨ hungry🤤. Woe⚠️ to✨ you🫵 who✨ laugh😄 now⏱️; you🫵 will💯 mourn😢 and✨ weep😭.

26📖 Woe⚠️ when⏱️ everyone💯 speaks🗣️ well💛 of💯 you🫵; their💯 ancestors👨‍👩‍👧 treated💯 the💯 false🚫 prophets📣 like💯 that💯 too💯.

27📖 “But✨ I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵 who✨ hear👂 me🙏: love💛 your💯 enemies⚔️ and✨ do💪 good✨ to✨ people👥 who✨ hate😡 you🫵.

28📖 Bless🙏 people👥 who✨ curse🤬 you🫵, and✨ pray🙏 for💯 those💯 who✨ mistreat💥 you🫵.

29📖 If✨ someone👤 slaps👋 one🔢 cheek😊, offer🫴 the💯 other💯 too💯. If✨ someone👤 takes🫴 your💯 cloak🧥, don’t🚫 hold🔒 back↩️ your💯 tunic👕 either💯.

30📖 Give🎁 to✨ everyone💯 who✨ asks🙏 you🫵. If✨ someone👤 takes🫴 your💯 belongings📦, don’t🚫 demand🗣️ them🫂 back↩️.

31📖 Treat🤝 people👥 exactly💯 the💯 way🛣️ you🫵 want💭 them🫂 to✨ treat🤝 you🫵.

32📖 If✨ you🫵 love💛 only💯 people👥 who✨ love💛 you🫵, what❓ credit🏆 is✨ that💯? Even💯 sinners💔 love💛 those💯 who✨ love💛 them🫂.

33📖 If✨ you🫵 do💪 good✨ only💯 to✨ people👥 who✨ do💪 good✨ to✨ you🫵, what❓ credit🏆 is✨ that💯? Sinners💔 do💪 the💯 same💯.

34📖 If✨ you🫵 lend💸 only💯 to✨ people👥 you🫵 expect💭 to✨ pay💰 you🫵 back↩️, what❓ credit🏆 is✨ that💯? Even💯 sinners💔 lend💸 to✨ sinners💔, expecting💭 the💯 full💯 amount💰 back↩️.

35📖 Love💛 your💯 enemies⚔️, do💪 good✨, and✨ lend💸 without🚫 expecting💭 anything💯 back↩️. Your💯 reward🎁 will💯 be✨ great💯, and✨ you🫵 will💯 be✨ children👨‍👩‍👧 of💯 the💯 Most💯 High👑, who✨ is✨ kind💛 to✨ the💯 ungrateful😒 and✨ wicked😈.

36📖 “Show💛 miséricorde🇫🇷🤲, just💯 as✨ your💯 Father🙏 shows🫶 rachamim🇮🇱🤲.

37📖 Don’t🚫 judge⚖️, and✨ you🫵 won’t🚫 be✨ judged⚖️. Don’t🚫 condemn⚠️, and✨ you🫵 won’t🚫 be✨ condemned⚠️. Liberta🇵🇹🔓, and✨ you🫵 will💯 be✨ set🔓 free🕊️.

38📖 Give🎁, and✨ you🫵 will💯 receive🎁: a💯 good✨ measure⚖️, pressed⬇️ down⬇️, shaken🔄 together🫂, and✨ overflowing🌊 will💯 be✨ poured🫗 into💯 your💯 lap🫴. The💯 measure⚖️ you🫵 use💪 comes🔄 back↩️ to✨ you🫵.

39📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂 a💯 story📖: “Can❓ one👤 blind🙈 person👤 guide🧭 another👤? Won’t🚫 they🫂 both💯 fall⬇️ into💯 a💯 pit🕳️?

40📖 A💯 student🎓 isn’t🚫 above💯 their💯 teacher👨‍🏫. When⏱️ fully💯 trained🎓, each💯 student🎓 will💯 be✨ like💯 their💯 teacher👨‍🏫.

41📖 Why❓ do✨ you🫵 notice👀 the💯 tiny🔎 speck🪵 in💯 your💯 sibling’s👥 eye👁️ but✨ miss🚫 the💯 huge💯 beam🪵 in💯 your💯 own💯 eye👁️?

42📖 How❓ can💭 you🫵 say🗣️, ‘Sibling👥, let✨ me🙏 take🫴 that💯 speck🪵 out🚪 of💯 your💯 eye👁️,’ when⏱️ you🫵 can’t🚫 see👀 the💯 massive💯 beam🪵 in💯 your💯 own💯 eye👁️? Hypocrite🎭! Pull🫴 your💯 own💯 beam🪵 out🚪 first⏱️; then⏱️ you🫵 can💯 see👀 clearly🔎 to✨ take🫴 the💯 speck🪵 out🚪 of💯 your💯 sibling’s👥 eye👁️.

43📖 A💯 good✨ tree🌳 doesn’t🚫 grow🌱 rotten🤢 fruit🍎, and✨ a💯 rotten🤢 tree🌳 doesn’t🚫 grow🌱 good✨ fruit🍇.

44📖 Each💯 tree🌳 is✨ known👀 by💯 its💯 own💯 fruto🇪🇸🍇. People👥 don’t🚫 pick🫴 figs🍈 from💯 thorn🌵 bushes🌿 or✨ grapes🍇 from💯 brambles🌿.

45📖 A💯 good✨ person👤 brings🫴 good✨ things💛 from💯 the💯 treasure🏦 in💯 their💯 heart💛. An💯 evil😈 person👤 brings🫴 evil😈 from💯 their💯 stash🏦. What💭 fills💯 the💯 obi🇳🇬❤️ spills🗣️ out💬 of💯 the💯 mouth🗣️.

46📖 “Why❓ keep🔁 calling📣 me🙏 ‘Lord👑, Lord👑,’ but✨ don’t🚫 do💪 what💭 I🙏 say🗣️?

47📖 Everyone💯 who✨ comes🚶 to✨ me🙏, hears👂 my💯 words📣, and✨ acts💪 on💯 them🫂—I🙏 will💯 show👀 you🫵 what💭 they’re✨ like💯.

48📖 They’re🫂 like💯 someone👤 building🏗️ a💯 gida🇳🇬🏠: they👤 dug⛏️ deep⬇️ and✨ laid🧱 its💯 foundation🧱 on💯 rock🪨. When⏱️ a💯 flood🌊 came🚶 and✨ the💯 river🌊 crashed💥 into💯 that💯 home🏠, it💯 couldn’t🚫 shake🫨 the💯 building🏗️, because✨ it💯 was✨ solidly💪 founded🧱 on💯 rock🪨.

49📖 But✨ whoever💯 hears👂 my💯 words📣 and✨ doesn’t🚫 act💪 is✨ like💯 someone👤 who✨ built🏗️ a💯 home🏠 on💯 bare🟫 ground🌍 without🚫 a💯 foundation🧱. When⏱️ the💯 river🌊 slammed💥 into💯 it💯, the💯 home🏚️ collapsed💥 immediately⏱️, and✨ the💯 wreckage💥 was✨ massive💯.”
Luke 7
# Luke 7 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Luke 7. Draft only; native-language, rendering, red-letter, and independent human review remain pending.

1📖 After⏱️ Jesus🙏 finished✅ speaking🗣️ where📍 the💯 people👥 could💯 hear👂, He🙏 entered🚪 Capernaum🏘️.

2📖 A💯 centurion🪖 had💯 a💯 dear💛 Knecht🇩🇪🧑‍✈️ who✨ was✨ sick🤒 and✨ close⚠️ to✨ death💀.

3📖 When⏱️ he👤 heard👂 about💭 Jesus🙏, he👤 sent📨 some💯 Jewish✡️ elders👴 to✨ ask🙏 Him🙏 to✨ come🚶 and✨ save🛟 his💯 servant🧑‍✈️.

4📖 They🫂 came🚶 to✨ Jesus🙏 and✨ begged🙏 Him🙏 earnestly💯, “He👤 deserves💯 You🙏 doing💪 this💯 for💯 him👤,

5📖 because✨ he👤 loves💛 our💯 people👥 and✨ built🏗️ our💯 synagogue🕍 for💯 us🫂.”

6📖 Jesus🙏 went🚶 with💯 them🫂. When⏱️ He🙏 was✨ getting🚶 close📍 to✨ the💯 house🏠, the💯 centurion🪖 sent📨 friends🤝 with💯 this💯 message📣: “Lord🙏, don’t🚫 trouble😣 Yourself🙏. I🙏 am✨ not🚫 worthy✨ for💯 You🙏 to✨ come🚶 under💯 my💯 roof🏠.

7📖 I🙏 didn’t🚫 even💯 consider🧠 myself🫵 worthy✨ to✨ come🚶 to✨ You🙏. Just💯 say🗣️ the💯 word📣, and✨ my💯 servant🧑‍✈️ will💯 be✨ healed🩺.

8📖 I🙏 know🧠 how💭 authority👑 works⚙️: I🙏 serve🫡 under💯 authority👑, and✨ I🙏 have💯 soldiers🪖 under💯 me👤. I🙏 tell🗣️ one🔢, ‘Go🚶,’ and✨ he👤 goes🚶; another🔢, ‘Come🚶,’ and✨ he👤 comes🚶; my💯 servant🧑‍✈️, ‘Do💪 this💯,’ and✨ he👤 does✅ it💯.”

9📖 Jesus🙏 heard👂 him👤 and✨ marvelled😲. He🙏 turned🔄 to✨ the💯 crowd🫂 following🚶 Him🙏 and✨ said🗣️, “I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵, I🙏 haven’t🚫 found🔎 faith🙏 this💯 strong💪, not🚫 even💯 in💯 Israel✡️.”

10📖 The💯 messengers📨 returned🚶 to✨ the💯 house🏠; dem🇯🇲 found🔎 the💯 servant🧑‍✈️ who✨ had💯 been✨ sick🤒 now⏱️ well💪.

11📖 Soon⏱️ after💯 that💯, Jesus🙏 went🚶 to✨ a💯 town🏘️ called🏷️ Nain🏘️. Many💯 disciples👥 and✨ a💯 huge💯 crowd🫂 went🚶 with💯 Him🙏.

12📖 As⏱️ He🙏 reached🚶 the💯 town🏘️ gate🚪, a💯 dead💀 young👦 man👤 was✨ being✨ carried🫴 out🚪. He👤 was✨ his💯 mother’s👩 only💯 ọmọkùnrin🇳🇬👦, and✨ she👩 was✨ a💯 widow💔. Many💯 people👥 from💯 town🏘️ were✨ with💯 her👩.

13📖 When⏱️ the💯 Lord🙏 saw👀 her👩, He🙏 showed💛 her👩 miséricorde🇫🇷🤲 and✨ said🗣️, “Don’t🚫 cry😭.”

14📖 He🙏 came🚶 close📍 and✨ touched🤲 the💯 coffin⚰️. The💯 people👥 carrying🫴 it💯 stopped🛑. He🙏 said🗣️, “Young👦 man👤, I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵: arise⬆️!”

15📖 The💯 dead💀 man👤 sat⬆️ up⬆️ and✨ began🔄 talking🗣️. Then⏱️ Jesus🙏 gave🫴 him👤 back↩️ to✨ his💯 mother👩.

16📖 Fear😨 hit💥 everyone💯, and✨ they🫂 glorified🙌 God🙏, saying🗣️, “A💯 great💯 prophet📣 has✨ risen⬆️ among💯 us🫂!” and✨ “God🙏 has💯 visited🚶 His🙏 people👥!”

17📖 This💯 report📰 about💭 Jesus🙏 spread📣 through💯 all💯 Judea🌍 and✨ the💯 surrounding🌍 region🌍.

18📖 John’s👤 disciples👥 told🗣️ him👤 everything💯 that✨ had💯 happened✅. John👤 called📣 two🔢 of💯 them🫂 to✨ him👤,

19📖 then⏱️ sent📨 them🫂 to✨ Jesus🙏 with💯 this💯 question❓: “Are✨ You🙏 the💯 One🔢 who✨ is✨ coming🚶, or✨ should💭 we🫂 expect💭 somebody👤 else💯?”

20📖 The💯 men👥 came🚶 to✨ Jesus🙏 and✨ said🗣️, “John👤 the💯 Baptiser💧 sent📨 us🫂 to✨ ask🙏 You🙏: ‘Are✨ You🙏 the💯 One🔢 who✨ is✨ coming🚶, or✨ should💭 we🫂 look🔎 for💯 somebody👤 else💯?’”

21📖 Right⏱️ then⏱️, Jesus🙏 healed🩺 many💯 people👥 from💯 illnesses🤒, plagues🦠, and✨ evil😈 spirits👻. He🙏 also➕ gave🎁 sight👀 to✨ many💯 who✨ were✨ blind🙈.

22📖 He🙏 answered🗣️ them🫂, “Go🚶 tell🗣️ John👤 what💭 you🫵 saw👀 and✨ heard👂: blind🙈 people👥 see👀, lame🦵 people👥 walk🚶, lepers🤒 are✨ cleansed✨, deaf🙉 people👥 hear👂, the💯 dead💀 are✨ raised⬆️, and✨ poor💸 people👥 hear👂 the💯 good✨ news📣.

23📖 Blessed✨ is✨ anyone💯 who✨ doesn’t🚫 stumble⚠️ because💯 of💯 me🙏.”

24📖 After⏱️ John’s👤 messengers📨 left🚶, Jesus🙏 started🔄 speaking🗣️ to✨ the💯 crowd🫂 about💭 John👤: “What❓ did💭 you🫵 go🚶 out🏜️ to✨ see👀 in💯 the💯 wilderness🏜️? A💯 reed🌾 shaking🌬️ in💯 the💯 wind🌬️?

25📖 What❓ did💭 you🫵 go🚶 out🏜️ to✨ see👀? Somebody👤 dressed👘 in💯 soft💛 clothes👕? Look👀, people👥 dressed👘 in💯 luxury💎 and✨ living🏰 comfortably🛋️ stay🏰 in💯 royal👑 courts🏛️.

26📖 So✨ what💭 did💭 you🫵 go🚶 out🏜️ to✨ see👀? A💯 prophet📣? Yes✅—and✨ way💯 more💯 than💯 a💯 prophet📣.

27📖 John👤 is✨ the💯 one🔢 Scripture📜 talks🗣️ about💭: ‘Look👀, I🙏 send📨 my💯 messenger📣 ahead🔜 of💯 You🙏, to✨ prepare🧭 Your💯 way🛣️ before💯 You🙏.’

28📖 I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵, among💯 everyone💯 born👶 of💯 women👩, no🚫 prophet📣 is✨ greater💯 than💯 John👤 the💯 Baptiser💧. Yet✨ even💯 the💯 least💯 in💯 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 is✨ greater💯 than💯 him👤.

29📖 When⏱️ all💯 the💯 people👥 and✨ tax💰 collectors💰 heard👂 this💯, they🫂 declared📣 God🙏 just⚖️; they🫂 had💯 been✨ baptised💧 with💯 John’s👤 baptism💧.

30📖 But✨ the💯 Pharisees👥 and✨ law⚖️ experts👨‍⚖️ rejected🚫 God’s🙏 counsel🧭; they🫂 had💯 not🚫 been✨ baptised💧 by💯 John👤.

31📖 Jesus🙏 continued🔄, “What❓ should💭 I🙏 compare⚖️ this💯 generation📆 to✨? What❓ are✨ they🫂 like💭?

32📖 They’re🫂 like💯 kids🧒 hanging🧍 out🏙️ in💯 the💯 marketplace🏪, calling📣 to✨ each💯 other👥: ‘We🫂 played🎶 music🎼 for💯 you🫵, but✨ you🫵 didn’t🚫 dance💃. We🫂 mourned😢, but✨ you🫵 didn’t🚫 cry😭.’

33📖 John👤 the💯 Baptiser💧 came🚶 neither🚫 eating🍽️ bread🍞 nor🚫 drinking🥤 wine🍷, and✨ you🫵 said🗣️, ‘He👤 has💯 a💯 demon👿.’

34📖 The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 came🚶 eating🍽️ and✨ drinking🥤, and✨ you🫵 say🗣️, ‘Look👀 at💯 him👤—a💯 glutton🍽️ and✨ drunkard🍷, a💯 friend🤝 of💯 tax💰 collectors💰 and✨ sinners💔!’

35📖 But✨ wisdom🧠 is✨ shown👀 right✅ by💯 all💯 her💯 children👨‍👩‍👧.”

36📖 One🔢 Pharisee👤 invited📨 Jesus🙏 to✨ eat🍽️ with💯 him👤. Jesus🙏 went🚶 into💯 his💯 house🏠 and✨ sat🪑 at💯 the💯 table🍽️.

37📖 A💯 woman👩 in💯 that💯 town🏘️, known📣 as✨ a💯 sinner💔, found🔎 out💭 that✨ Jesus🙏 was✨ eating🍽️ in💯 the💯 Pharisee’s👤 gida🇳🇬🏠. She👩 brought🫴 an💯 alabaster🏺 jar🏺 of💯 perfume🌸.

38📖 She👩 stood🧍 behind🔙 Jesus🙏 near📍 His🙏 feet🦶, crying😭. Her💯 tears💧 wet💧 His🙏 feet🦶; she👩 wiped🧼 them🦶 with💯 her💯 hair💇, kissed💋 His🙏 feet🦶, and✨ anointed🫗 them🦶 with💯 perfume🌸.

40📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️ him👤, “Simon👤, I🙏 have💯 something💯 to✨ tell🗣️ you🫵.” Simon👤 said🗣️, “Teacher👨‍🏫, go💬 ahead🔜.”

41📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “A💯 lender💸 had💯 two🔢 people👥 who✨ owed💰 him👤 money💵. One🔢 owed💸 five🔢 hundred💯 denarii💰, and✨ the💯 other🔢 owed💸 fifty🔢.

42📖 Neither👥 could💭 pay💰 him👤 back↩️, so✨ he👤 perdonó🇪🇸🤲 both💯 their💯 debts💸. Which❓ one🔢 will💯 love💛 him👤 more💯?”

43📖 Simon👤 answered🗣️, “I🙏 guess💭 the💯 one🔢 he👤 forgave🤲 more💯.” Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “You🫵 judged⚖️ that💯 correctly✅.”

44📖 Then⏱️ He🙏 turned🔄 toward💯 the💯 woman👩 and✨ said🗣️ to✨ Simon👤, “Do❓ you🫵 see👀 her👩? I🙏 came🚶 into💯 your💯 house🏠, but✨ you🫵 gave🎁 me🙏 no🚫 water💧 for💯 my💯 feet🦶. She👩 wet💧 my💯 feet🦶 with💯 her💯 tears💧 and✨ wiped🧼 them🦶 with💯 her💯 hair💇.

45📖 You🫵 gave🎁 me🙏 no🚫 beijo🇵🇹💋, but✨ she👩 hasn’t🚫 stopped🔁 kissing💋 my💯 feet🦶 since⏱️ I🙏 came🚶 in🚪.

46📖 You🫵 didn’t🚫 put🫗 oil🫒 on💯 my💯 head🧑, but✨ she👩 anointed🫗 my💯 feet🦶 with💯 perfume🌸.

47📖 So✨ I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵: her💯 many💯 sins💔 are✨ forgiven🤲, because✨ she👩 loved💛 much💯. But✨ the💯 person👤 forgiven🤲 little💯 loves💛 little💯.”

48📖 Then⏱️ Jesus🙏 told🗣️ her👩, “Your💯 sins💔 are✨ forgiven🤲.”

49📖 The💯 people👥 sitting🪑 at💯 the💯 table🍽️ began🔄 asking🗣️ each💯 other👥, “Who❓ is✨ this💯, who✨ even💯 forgives🤲 sins💔?”

50📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ the💯 woman👩, “Your💯 faith🙏 has💯 saved🛟 you🫵. Go🚶 in💯 shalom🇮🇱🕊️.”

50📖 Jesus🙏 heard👂 and✨ answered🗣️ Jairus👤, “Don’t🚫 be✨ afraid😨. Just💯 believe🙏, and✨ she👧 will💯 be✨ healed🩺.”

51📖 When⏱️ Jesus🙏 reached🚶 the💯 house🏠, He🙏 let🚫 only💯 Peter👤, John👤, James👤, the💯 girl’s👧 father👨, and✨ her💯 mother👩 come🚪 inside🏠 with💯 Him🙏.

52📖 Everyone💯 was✨ crying😭 and✨ mourning😢 for💯 her👧, but✨ Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Don’t🚫 cry😭. She👧 isn’t🚫 dead💀; she’s👧 sleeping😴.”

53📖 They🫂 laughed🤡 at💯 Him🙏, because✨ they🫂 knew🧠 she👧 was✨ dead💀.

54📖 Jesus🙏 sent📨 everyone💯 outside🚪. Then⏱️ He🙏 took🫴 her👧 hand🤲 and✨ called📣, “Child👧, arise⬆️!”

# Luke 9 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Luke 9. Draft only; native-language, rendering, red-letter, and independent human review remain pending.

1📖 Jesus🙏 called📣 the💯 twelve🔢 together🫂 and✨ gave🎁 them🫂 authority👑 and✨ power⚡ over💯 every💯 demon👿, plus✨ power💪 to✨ heal🩺 diseases🤒.

2📖 He🙏 sent📨 them🫂 out➡️ to✨ proclaim📣 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 and✨ heal🩺 the💯 sick🤒.

3📖 He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “Take🚫 nothing💯 for💯 the💯 journey🧳—no🚫 walking🦯 sticks🪵, no🚫 bag🎒, no🚫 Brot🇩🇪🍞, no🚫 money💰. Don’t🚫 pack🎒 two🔢 tunics👘 each💯.

4📖 Whenever⏱️ you🫵 enter🚪 a💯 gida🇳🇬🏠, stay🛏️ there📍, then⏱️ leave🚪 from💯 there📍.

5📖 If✨ people👥 won’t🚫 welcome👋 you🫵, when⏱️ you🫵 leave🚶 that💯 town🏘️, shake🫨 even💯 its💯 dust🌫️ from💯 your💯 feet🦶 as✨ testimony📜 against💯 them🫂.”

6📖 They🫂 left🚶 and✨ travelled🚶 through💯 the💯 villages🏘️, sharing📣 the💯 Good✨ News📣 and✨ healing🩺 people👥 everywhere🌍.

7📖 Herod👑 the💯 tetrarch👑 heard👂 about💭 everything💯 Jesus🙏 was✨ doing💪 and✨ felt😵 deeply💯 confused😟. Some💯 people👥 said🗣️ John👤 had💯 risen⬆️ from💯 the💯 dead💀,

8📖 others💯 said🗣️ Elijah👤 had💯 appeared✨, and✨ others💯 said🗣️ one🔢 of💯 the💯 old📜 prophets📣 had💯 risen⬆️ again🔁.

9📖 Herod👑 said🗣️, “I🙏 had💯 John👤 beheaded🗡️, so✨ who❓ is✨ this💯 person👤 I🙏 keep🔁 hearing👂 about💭?” He👑 kept🔎 trying🤲 to✨ see👀 Jesus🙏.

10📖 When⏱️ the💯 apostles📣 returned🚶, they🫂 told🗣️ Jesus🙏 what💭 they🫂 had💯 done✅. He🙏 took🫴 them🫂 away↩️ with💯 Him🙏 to✨ a💯 deserted🏜️ region📍 of💯 a💯 city🏙️ called🏷️ Bethsaida🏘️.

11📖 The💯 crowds🫂 noticed👀 and✨ followed🚶 Him🙏; dem🇯🇲 came🚶 from💯 all💯 over🌍. Jesus🙏 welcomed👋 them🫂, spoke🗣️ about💭 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑, and✨ healed🩺 those💯 who✨ needed🆘 it💯.

12📖 As⏱️ the💯 day📆 started🔄 fading🌇, the💯 twelve🔢 came🚶 and✨ said🗣️, “Send📨 the💯 crowd🫂 away↩️ so✨ they🫂 can💯 find🔎 food🍽️ and✨ lodging🛏️ in💯 nearby💯 villages🏘️ and✨ farms🌾. We🫂 are✨ out🏜️ here📍 in💯 a💯 deserted🏜️ place📍.”

13📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “You🫵 give🎁 them🫂 something🍽️ to✨ eat🍽️.” They🫂 replied🗣️, “We🫂 have💯 only💯 five🔢 loaves🍞 of💯 pan🇪🇸🍞 and✨ two🔢 fish🐟—unless✨ we🫂 go🚶 buy🛒 oúnjẹ🇳🇬🍲 for💯 this💯 whole💯 crowd🫂.”

14📖 There✨ were✨ about💯 five🔢 thousand🔢 men👨. Jesus🙏 told🗣️ His🙏 disciples👥, “Have💭 them🫂 sit🪑 in💯 groups🫂 of💯 about💯 fifty🔢 each💯.”

15📖 They🫂 did✅ that💯 and✨ got💯 everyone💯 seated🪑.

16📖 Jesus🙏 took🫴 the💯 five🔢 loaves🍞 and✨ two🔢 fish🐟, looked👀 up⬆️ to✨ the💯 sky🌌, blessed🙏 them🫂, broke🫓 them🫂, and✨ handed🤲 them🫂 to✨ the💯 disciples👥 to✨ set🫴 before💯 the💯 crowd🫂.

17📖 Everyone💯 ate🍽️ and✨ got✨ full🍽️. The💯 disciples👥 gathered🫴 twelve🔢 baskets🧺 of💯 broken🫓 pieces🍞 left💯 over💯.

18📖 One🔢 time⏱️, Jesus🙏 was✨ praying🙏 alone🤫, with💯 His🙏 disciples👥 nearby📍. He🙏 asked🗣️ them🫂, “Who❓ do✨ the💯 crowds🫂 say🗣️ I🙏 am💭?”

19📖 They🫂 answered🗣️, “Some💯 say🗣️ John👤 the💯 Baptiser💧; others💯 say🗣️ Elijah👤; and✨ others💯 say🗣️ one🔢 of💯 the💯 old📜 prophets📣 has💯 risen⬆️ again🔁.”

20📖 Jesus🙏 asked🗣️, “But✨ who❓ do✨ you🫵 say🗣️ I🙏 am💭?” Peter👤 answered🗣️, “You🙏 are✨ the💯 Mashiach🇮🇱👑 of💯 God🙏.”

21📖 But✨ Jesus🙏 warned⚠️ them🫂 and✨ ordered🗣️ them🫂 to✨ tell🗣️ no🚫 one👤.

22📖 He🙏 said🗣️, “The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 must💯 suffer😣 many💯 things💭, be✨ rejected🚫 by💯 the💯 elders👴, chief👑 priests🕍, and✨ scribes📜, be✨ killed💀, and✨ be✨ raised⬆️ on💯 day📆 three🔢.”

23📖 He🙏 told🗣️ everyone💯, “If✨ anyone👤 wants💭 to✨ come🚶 after💯 me🙏, let✨ them🫂 deny🚫 themselves🫵, take🫴 up⬆️ their💯 cross✝️, and✨ follow🚶 me🙏.

24📖 Whoever💯 tries🤲 to✨ save🛟 their💯 life💛 will💯 lose📉 it💯; whoever💯 loses📉 their💯 life💛 for💯 my🙏 sake💛 will💯 save🛟 it💯.

25📖 What💭 do💪 you🫵 gain🏆 by💯 getting🫴 the💯 whole💯 world🌍 if✨ you🫵 lose📉 or✨ forfeit🚫 yourself🫵?

26📖 Whoever💯 is✨ ashamed😳 of💯 me🙏 and✨ my💯 words📣, the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 will💯 be✨ ashamed😔 of💯 them🫂 when⏱️ He🙏 comes🚶 in💯 His🙏 glory✨, the💯 Father’s🙏 glory✨, and✨ the💯 glory✨ of💯 the💯 holy🕊️ angels👼.

27📖 I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵 truly💯: some💯 standing🧍 here📍 will💯 not🚫 taste😋 death💀 before⏱️ they🫂 see👀 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑.”

28📖 About💯 eight🔢 days📆 after💯 these💯 sayings🗣️, Jesus🙏 took🫴 Peter👤, John👤, and✨ James👤 up⬆️ a💯 mountain⛰️ to✨ pray🙏.

29📖 While⏱️ He🙏 prayed🙏, His🙏 face😌 changed🔄 in💯 appearance👀, and✨ His🙏 clothes👘 became✨ white⚪ and✨ dazzling✨ with💯 luz🇵🇹✨.

30📖 Suddenly⚡, two🔢 men👥—Moses👤 and✨ Elijah👤—appeared✨, talking🗣️ with💯 Him🙏.

31📖 They🫂 appeared✨ in💯 glory✨ and✨ spoke🗣️ about💭 His🙏 departure🚶, which💭 He🙏 was✨ about⏱️ to✨ complete✅ in💯 Jerusalem🏙️.

32📖 Peter👤 and✨ those💯 with💯 him👤 were✨ heavy😴 with💯 sleep😴. When⏱️ they🫂 fully💯 woke🔔 up⬆️, they🫂 saw👀 Jesus🙏 in💯 His🙏 glory✨, with💯 the💯 two🔢 men👥 standing🧍 beside💯 Him🙏.

33📖 As⏱️ Moses👤 and✨ Elijah👤 were✨ leaving🚶, Peter👤 said🗣️ to✨ Jesus🙏, “Master👑, it’s✨ good💯 for💯 us🫂 to✨ be✨ here📍! Let’s🫂 make🏗️ three🔢 tents⛺—one🔢 for💯 You🙏, one🔢 for💯 Moses👤, and✨ one🔢 for💯 Elijah👤.” He👤 didn’t🚫 know🧠 what💭 he👤 was✨ saying🗣️.

34📖 While⏱️ Peter👤 was✨ speaking🗣️, a💯 cloud☁️ came🚶 over💯 them🫂. They🫂 got✨ scared😨 as⏱️ they🫂 entered🚶 the💯 cloud☁️.

35📖 A💯 voice🗣️ came🚪 from💯 the💯 cloud☁️: “This💯 is✨ my🙏 beloved💛 Son👑. Listen👂 to✨ Him🙏!”

36📖 When⏱️ the💯 voice🗣️ came🚪, Jesus🙏 was✨ there📍 alone🤍. The💯 disciples👥 stayed🤫 silent🤫 and✨ told🗣️ no🚫 one👤 in💯 those💯 days📆 what💭 they🫂 had💯 seen👀.

37📖 The💯 next🔜 day📆, as⏱️ they🫂 came⬇️ down⬇️ the💯 mountain⛰️, a💯 huge💯 crowd🫂 met🚶 Jesus🙏.

38📖 A💯 man👨 in💯 the💯 crowd🫂 called📣 out🔊, “Teacher👨‍🏫, I🙏 beg🙏 You🙏, look👀 at💯 my💯 son👦. He’s✨ my💯 only💯 child👦.

39📖 A💯 spirit👿 seizes🫴 him👦 suddenly⚡, makes💥 him👦 cry📣 out🔊, and✨ convulses⚡ him👦 until⏱️ he👦 foams🤍 at💯 the💯 mouth🗣️. It💯 hardly💯 leaves🚪 him👦 without🚫 bruising💥 him👦 badly💥.

40📖 I🙏 begged🙏 Your🙏 disciples👥 to✨ cast🚪 it💯 out⬆️, but✨ they🫂 couldn’t🚫.”

41📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Faithless💔 and✨ twisted🌀 generation📆! How⏱️ long💯 must💭 I🙏 stay⏱️ with💯 you🫂 and✨ bear😣 with💯 you🫂? Bring🫴 your💯 son👦 here📍.”

42📖 As⏱️ the💯 boy👦 was✨ coming🚶 over💯, the💯 demon😈 threw🫴 him👦 down⬇️ and✨ convulsed⚡ him👦 violently💥. Jesus🙏 rebuked⚠️ the💯 unclean🕷️ spirit👿, healed🩺 the💯 boy👦, and✨ gave🫴 him👦 back↩️ to✨ his💯 father👨.

43📖 Everyone💯 was✨ amazed😲 by💯 God’s🙏 majesty👑. While⏱️ they🫂 were✨ still⏱️ marvelling😲 at💯 everything💯 Jesus🙏 did✅, He🙏 told🗣️ His🙏 disciples👥,

44📖 “Let✨ these💯 paroles🇫🇷📣 sink⬇️ into💯 your💯 ears👂: the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 is✨ about⏱️ to✨ be✨ handed🤲 over💯 into💯 human👥 hands🫴.”

45📖 They🫂 didn’t🚫 understand🧠 what💭 He🙏 meant💭. Its💯 meaning💭 was✨ hidden🫣 from💯 them🫂, and✨ they🫂 were✨ afraid😨 to✨ ask🗣️ Him🙏 about💭 it💯.

46📖 Then⏱️ the💯 disciples👥 started🔄 arguing🗣️ about💭 which👤 of💯 them🫂 was✨ the💯 greatest🏆.

47📖 Jesus🙏 knew🧠 what💭 they🫂 were✨ reasoning🧠 in💯 their💯 obi🇳🇬❤️. He🙏 took🫴 a💯 little💯 child🧒 and✨ stood🧍 the💯 child🧒 beside💯 Him🙏.

48📖 He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “Whoever💯 welcomes👋 this💯 child🧒 in💯 my💯 name🏷️ welcomes👋 me🙏. Whoever💯 welcomes👋 me🙏 welcomes👋 the💯 One🔢 who✨ sent📨 me🙏. The💯 least💯 among💯 all💯 of💯 you🫂—that💯 one🔢 will💯 be✨ great🏆.”

49📖 John👤 said🗣️, “Master👑, we🫂 saw👀 someone👤 driving🚪 demons👿 out⬆️ in💯 Your🙏 name🏷️, and✨ we🫂 tried🚫 to✨ stop🛑 him👤 because✨ he👤 doesn’t🚫 follow🚶 with💯 us🫂.”

50📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him👤, “Don’t🚫 stop🛑 him👤, because✨ whoever💯 isn’t🚫 against⚔️ us🫂 is✨ for🤝 us🫂.”

51📖 As⏱️ the💯 time⏱️ came🚶 near📍 for💯 Jesus🙏 to✨ be✨ taken⬆️ up⬆️, He🙏 firmly💪 set🎯 His🙏 face👀 toward💯 Jerusalem🏙️.

52📖 He🙏 sent📨 messengers📣 ahead🔜. They🫂 entered🚪 a💯 Samaritan🏘️ village🏘️ to✨ get💯 things💭 ready✅ for💯 Him🙏.

53📖 The💯 villagers👥 wouldn’t🚫 welcome👋 Him🙏, because✨ He🙏 was✨ travelling🚶 with💯 His🙏 face👀 set🎯 toward💯 Jerusalem🏙️.

54📖 James👤 and✨ John👤 saw👀 this💯 and✨ asked🗣️, “Lord🙏, do💭 You🙏 want💭 us🫂 to✨ call📣 fire🔥 down⬇️ from💯 the💯 sky🌌 and✨ destroy💥 them🫂, just💯 like💯 Elijah👤 did✅?”

55📖 Jesus🙏 turned🔄 and✨ rebuked⚠️ them🫂: “You🫵 don’t🚫 know🧠 what💭 kind💯 of💯 spirit👻 you🫂 are✨ showing👀.

56📖 The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 didn’t🚫 come🚶 to✨ destroy💥 people’s👥 lives💛, but✨ to✨ save🛟 them🫂.” Then⏱️ they🫂 went🚶 to✨ another💯 village🏘️.

57📖 As⏱️ they🫂 travelled🚶, someone👤 said🗣️ to✨ Jesus🙏, “I🙏 want💭 to✨ follow🚶 You🙏 wherever🌍 You🙏 go🚶, Lord🙏.”

58📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “Foxes🦊 have💯 holes🕳️, and✨ birds🐦 of💯 the💯 sky🌌 have💯 nests🪺, but✨ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 has🚫 nowhere💯 to✨ lay🛏️ His🙏 head🧑.”

59📖 He🙏 told🗣️ another💯 person👤, “Follow🚶 me🙏.” The💯 man👤 said🗣️, “Lord🙏, first⏱️ let✨ me👤 go🚶 bury⚰️ my💯 father👨.”

60📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Leave🚪 the💯 dead💀 to✨ bury⚰️ their💯 own💯 dead💀. You🫵 go🚶 announce📣 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑.”

61📖 Someone👤 else💯 said🗣️, “I🙏 want💭 to✨ follow🚶 You🙏, Lord🙏, but✨ first⏱️ let✨ me👤 say👋 goodbye👋 to✨ my💯 family👨‍👩‍👧 at💯 home🏠.”

62📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “No🚫 one👤 who✨ puts🫴 a💯 hand🫴 to✨ the💯 plough🚜 and✨ keeps🔄 looking👀 back↩️ is✨ fit✅ for💯 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑.”
Luke 10
# Luke 10 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Luke 10. Draft only; native-language, rendering, red-letter, and independent human review remain pending.

1📖 After⏱️ this💯, the💯 Lord🙏 chose🫴 seventy🔢 more💯 disciples👥 and✨ sent📨 them🫂 out➡️ in💯 pairs👥 ahead🔜 of💯 Him🙏 to✨ every💯 town🏘️ and✨ place📍 He🙏 planned🧭 to✨ visit🚶.

2📖 He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “The💯 Ernte🇩🇪 is✨ huge💯, but✨ the💯 workers🧑‍🌾 are✨ few💯. Ask🙏 the💯 Lord🙏 of💯 the💯 Ernte🇩🇪 to✨ send📨 more💯 workers🧑‍🌾 into💯 His🙏 harvest🌾.

3📖 Go🚶! I🙏’m🙏 sending📨 unu🇯🇲 out➡️ like💯 lambs🐑 walking🚶 among💯 wolves🐺.

4📖 Don’t🚫 carry🧳 a💯 purse👛, bag🎒, or✨ sandals👡. Don’t🚫 stop🛑 to✨ greet👋 people👥 on💯 the💯 road🛣️.

5📖 Whenever⏱️ you🫵 enter🚪 a💯 gida🇳🇬🏠, first🥇 say🗣️, ‘Shalom🇮🇱 to✨ this💯 house🏠.’

6📖 If✨ a💯 person👤 who✨ welcomes👋 peace🕊️ is✨ there📍, your💯 peace🕊️ will💯 rest🛏️ on💯 them🫂. If✨ not🚫, it💯 will💯 return↩️ to✨ you🫵.

7📖 Stay🛏️ in💯 that💯 same💯 house🏠. Eat🍽️ and✨ drink🥤 whatever💯 they🫂 give🎁 you🫵, because✨ workers🧑‍🌾 deserve💯 their💯 pay💰. Don’t🚫 keep🔁 hopping🚶 from💯 house🏠 to✨ house🏠.

8📖 When⏱️ you🫵 enter🚪 a💯 town🏘️ and✨ people👥 welcome👋 you🫵, eat🍽️ the💯 oúnjẹ🇳🇬🍲 they🫂 set🫴 in💯 front💯 of💯 you🫵.

9📖 Heal🩺 the💯 sick🤒 there📍 and✨ tell🗣️ them🫂, ‘God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 has💯 come🚶 near📍 to✨ you🫵.’

10📖 But✨ if✨ a💯 town🏘️ doesn’t🚫 welcome👋 you🫵, go🚶 out➡️ into💯 its💯 streets🛣️ and✨ say🗣️,

11📖 ‘Even💯 the💯 dust🌫️ from💯 your💯 town🏘️ clinging🦶 to✨ our💯 feet🦶, we🫂 wipe🧹 off➡️ against💯 you🫵. Still✨ know🧠 this💯: God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 has💯 come🚶 near📍.’

12📖 I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵, on💯 that💯 day📆 it💯 will💯 be✨ more💯 tolerable⚖️ for💯 Sodom🏙️ than✨ for💯 that💯 town🏘️.

13📖 “Woe⚠️ to✨ you🫵, Chorazin🏘️! Woe⚠️ to✨ you🫵, Bethsaida🏘️! If✨ the💯 mighty💥 works💪 done✅ in💯 you🫵 had💯 happened✅ in💯 Tyre🏙️ and✨ Sidon🏙️, they🫂 would💯 have✨ turned🔄 back↩️ to✨ God🙏 long⏳ ago⏱️, sitting🪑 in💯 sackcloth🧵 and✨ ashes⚱️.

14📖 So✨ Tyre🏙️ and✨ Sidon🏙️ will💯 have💯 an💯 easier💯 judgement⚖️ than✨ you🫵.

15📖 And✨ you🫵, Capernaum🏘️, exalted⬆️ to✨ heaven🌌, will💯 be✨ brought⬇️ down⬇️ to✨ Hades⚰️.

16📖 Whoever💯 listens👂 to✨ you🫵 listens👂 to✨ me🙏; whoever💯 rejects🚫 you🫵 rejects🚫 me🙏; and✨ whoever💯 rejects🚫 me🙏 rejects🚫 the💯 One🔢 who✨ sent📨 me🙏.”

17📖 The💯 seventy🔢 came🚶 back↩️ with💯 joie🇫🇷😄, saying🗣️, “Lord🙏, even💯 the💯 demons👿 obey🙏 us🫂 in💯 Your🙏 name🏷️!”

18📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “I🙏 saw👀 Satan😈 fall⬇️ from💯 heaven🌌 like💯 lightning⚡.

19📖 Look👀, I🙏 gave🎁 you🫵 authority👑 to✨ step👣 on💯 snakes🐍 and✨ scorpions🦂, and✨ power💪 over💯 all💯 the💯 enemy’s⚔️ strength💪. Nothing💯 will💯 harm💥 you🫵.

20📖 Still✨, don’t🚫 celebrate🎉 because✨ spirits👿 obey🙏 you🫵. Celebrate🎉 because✨ your💯 names📝 are✨ written✍️ in💯 heaven🌌.”

21📖 Right⏱️ then⏱️, Jesus🙏 rejoiced😄 in💯 the💯 Holy🕊️ Spirit🕊️ and✨ said🗣️, “Father🙏, Lord👑 of💯 heaven🌌 and✨ earth🌍, I🙏 thank🙏 You🙏 for✨ hiding🫣 these💯 things💭 from💯 the💯 wise🧠 and✨ understanding🧠, and✨ revealing🔦 them💯 to✨ little💯 children🧒. Yes✅, Father🙏, that💯 pleased💛 You🙏.”

22📖 Then⏱️ He🙏 told🗣️ the💯 disciples👥, “My🙏 Father🙏 has💯 handed🤲 everything💯 over💯 to✨ me🙏. No🚫 one👤 truly💯 knows🧠 who💭 the💯 Son👑 is✨ except💯 the💯 Father🙏, or✨ who💭 the💯 Father🙏 is✨ except💯 the💯 Son👑 and✨ anyone💯 the💯 Son👑 chooses🫴 to✨ reveal🔦 Him🙏 to✨.”

23📖 Later⏱️, when⏱️ they🫂 were✨ by💯 themselves🤫, Jesus🙏 told🗣️ His🙏 disciples👥, “Blessed✨ are✨ the💯 eyes👀 that✨ see👀 what💭 you🫵 see👀.

24📖 I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵, many💯 prophets📣 and✨ kings👑 wanted💭 to✨ see👀 what💭 you🫵 see👀 but✨ didn’t🚫 see👀 it💯, and✨ to✨ hear👂 what💭 you🫵 hear👂 but✨ didn’t🚫 hear👂 it💯.”

25📖 A💯 law⚖️ expert👨‍⚖️ stood⬆️ up⬆️ to✨ test🧪 Jesus🙏. “Teacher👨‍🏫, what❓ should💭 I🙏 do💪 to✨ inherit🎁 eternal⏳ life💛?”

26📖 Jesus🙏 asked🗣️ him👤, “What❓ does✨ the💯 law📜 say🗣️? How❓ do💭 you🫵 read🔎 it💯?”

27📖 He👤 answered🗣️, “Love💛 the💯 Lord🙏 your💯 God🙏 with💯 all💯 your💯 obi🇳🇬❤️, all💯 your💯 soul🫀, all💯 your💯 strength💪, and✨ all💯 your💯 mind🧠; and✨ love💛 your💯 neighbour👥 as✨ yourself🫵.”

28📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “That’s✨ right✅. Do💪 that💯, and✨ you🫵 will💯 live💛.”

29📖 Wanting💭 to✨ make💯 himself👤 look👀 righteous✅, the💯 expert👨‍⚖️ asked🗣️ Jesus🙏, “So✨ who❓ counts💯 as✨ my💯 neighbour👥?”

30📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “A💯 man👤 was✨ travelling🚶 from💯 Jerusalem🏙️ down⬇️ to✨ Jericho🏘️ when⏱️ he👤 fell⬇️ among💯 robbers🦹. They🫂 stripped👕 him👤, beat💥 him👤, and✨ left🚶 him👤 half💯 dead💀.

31📖 By💯 chance⚡ a💯 priest🕍 was✨ travelling🚶 that💯 way🛣️. He👤 saw👀 the💯 man👤, crossed🚶 to✨ the💯 other💯 side↔️, and✨ kept🔁 going🚶.

32📖 A💯 Levite🕍 came🚶 to✨ that💯 spot📍 too💯. He👤 saw👀 the💯 injured🤕 man👤 and✨ crossed🚶 over💯 to✨ the💯 other💯 side↔️.

33📖 But✨ a💯 Samaritan🧍 travelling🚶 through💯 there📍 saw👀 him👤 and✨ was✨ moved💛 with💯 compassion💛.

34📖 He👤 came🚶 close📍, wrapped🩹 the💯 man’s👤 wounds🩹, and✨ poured🫗 oil🫒 and✨ wine🍷 on💯 them🩹. Then⏱️ he👤 lifted🫴 him👤 onto💯 his💯 own💯 animal🐴, brought🫴 him👤 to✨ an💯 inn🏨, and✨ cared💛 for💯 him👤.

35📖 The💯 next🔜 day📆, as⏱️ he👤 left🚶, he👤 took🫴 out⬆️ two🔢 denarii💰, gave🎁 them🫂 to✨ the💯 host👤, and✨ said🗣️, ‘Take💛 care🤲 of💯 him👤. Whatever💭 you🫵 spend💸 beyond💯 this💯, I🙏 will💯 pay💸 you🫵 back↩️ when⏱️ I🙏 return🚶.’

36📖 So✨ which🔢 of💯 these💯 three🔢 acted💪 like💯 a💯 neighbour👥 to✨ the💯 man👤 who✨ fell⬇️ among💯 robbers🦹?”

37📖 The💯 expert👨‍⚖️ said🗣️, “The💯 one🔢 who✨ showed💛 him👤 mercy🤲.” Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him👤, “Go🚶 and✨ do💪 the💯 same💯.”

38📖 As⏱️ Jesus🙏 and✨ the💯 disciples👥 travelled🚶, they🫂 entered🚪 a💯 village🏘️. A💯 woman👩 named🏷️ Martha👩 welcomed👋 Him🙏 into💯 her💯 casa🇪🇸🏠.

39📖 Her💯 sister👩 Mary👩 sat🪑 at💯 Jesus🙏’ feet🦶 and✨ listened👂 to✨ His🙏 word📣.

40📖 Martha👩 was✨ distracted🌀 by💯 all💯 the💯 serving🍽️. She👩 came🚶 over💯 and✨ said🗣️, “Lord🙏, don’t🚫 You🙏 care💭 that✨ my💯 sister👩 left🚪 me👤 to✨ do💪 all💯 this💯 trabalho🇵🇹📋 alone🤷? Tell🗣️ her👩 to✨ help🤲 me👩.”

41📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Martha👩, Martha👩, you🫵 are✨ anxious😰 and✨ troubled😟 about💭 so💯 many💯 things💭.

42📖 But✨ one🔢 thing💯 is✨ needed💯. Mary👩 chose🫴 the💯 good✨ part💯, and✨ it💯 won’t🚫 be✨ taken🫴 away↩️ from💯 her👩.”
Luke 11

10📖 Everyone💯 who✨ asks🙏 receives🎁; whoever💯 seeks🔎 finds🔎; and✨ the💯 one🔢 who✨ knocks🚪 gets💯 the💯 door🚪 opened🔓.

14📖 Jesus🙏 was✨ driving🚪 out⬆️ a💯 demon👿 that✨ was✨ mute🤐. When⏱️ it💯 left🚪, the💯 mute🤐 man👤 spoke🗣️, and✨ the💯 crowd🫂 was✨ amazed😲.

19📖 If✨ I🙏 do💪 it💯 by💯 Beelzebul👿, then⏱️ by💯 whom❓ do💪 your💯 children🧒 drive🚪 them🫂 out⬆️? They🫂 will💯 judge⚖️ you🫵.

21📖 When⏱️ a💯 strong💪 man👤, fully💯 armed🛡️, guards🔒 his💯 own💯 home🏠, his💯 goods📦 stay💯 safe✅.

22📖 But✨ when⏱️ someone👤 stronger💪 attacks⚔️ and✨ beats💥 him👤, they🫂 take🫴 all💯 the💯 armour🛡️ he👤 trusted🙏 and✨ divide➗ the💯 plunder💰.

23📖 Whoever💯 isn’t🚫 with💯 me🙏 is✨ against⚔️ me🙏; whoever💯 doesn’t🚫 gather🫂 with💯 me🙏 scatters🌬️.

24📖 “When⏱️ an💯 unclean🕷️ spirit👿 leaves🚪 someone👤, it💯 roams🚶 through💯 dry🏜️ places🏜️, looking🔎 for💯 rest🛏️. Finding🚫 none🚫, it💯 says🗣️, ‘I🙏’ll🙏 go↩️ back↩️ to✨ my💯 house🏠, the💯 place📍 I🙏 left🚪.’

25📖 It💯 returns🚶 and✨ finds🔎 the💯 place🏠 swept🧹 and✨ put🫴 in💯 order✅.

26📖 Then⏱️ it💯 brings🫴 seven🔢 other💯 spirits👿 more💯 evil😈 than💯 itself💯. They🫂 move🚪 in🏠 and✨ stay💯 there📍; that💯 person’s👤 last🔚 state📉 ends🔚 up⬆️ worse💥 than💯 the💯 first🥇.”

27📖 As⏱️ Jesus🙏 said🗣️ that💯, a💯 woman👩 in💯 the💯 crowd🫂 called📣 out🔊, “Blessed✨ is✨ the💯 womb🤰 that✨ carried🤰 You🙏, and✨ the💯 breasts🍼 that✨ nursed🍼 You🙏!”

32📖 At💯 the💯 judgement⚖️, the💯 men👥 of💯 Nineveh🏘️ will💯 stand⬆️ with💯 this💯 generation📆 and✨ condemn⚠️ it💯, because✨ they🫂 turned🔄 back↩️ to✨ God🙏 when⏱️ Jonah👤 preached📣. And✨ look👀—someone👤 greater💯 than💯 Jonah👤 is✨ here📍.

33📖 “No🚫 one👤 lights🪔 a💯 lamp🪔 then⏱️ hides🫣 it💯 in💯 a💯 cellar🏚️ or✨ under💯 a💯 basket🧺. They🫂 put🪔 it💯 on💯 a💯 stand🪜 so✨ people👥 coming🚪 in💯 can💯 see👀 the💯 light💡.

35📖 So✨ check🔎 that💯 the💯 light💡 in💯 you🫵 isn’t🚫 actually💯 darkness🌑.

36📖 If✨ your💯 whole💯 body🧍 is✨ full💡 of💯 light💡, with💯 no🚫 dark🌑 part💯, it💯 will💯 shine🌟 all💯 over💯 you🫵, like💯 a💯 lamp🪔 lighting💡 a💯 room🏠 with💯 haske🇳🇬✨.”

37📖 While⏱️ Jesus🙏 was✨ speaking🗣️, a💯 Pharisee👤 invited📨 Him🙏 to✨ eat🍽️ with💯 him👤. Jesus🙏 went🚶 in🚪 and✨ sat🪑 at💯 the💯 table🍽️.

41📖 Give🎁 to✨ the💯 needy🫶 what💭 is✨ within💭 you🫵, and✨ then⏱️ everything💯 will💯 be✨ clean✨ for💯 you🫵.

42📖 But✨ woe⚠️ to✨ you🫵 Pharisees👥! You🫵 give💸 a💯 tenth🔟 of💯 mint🌿, rue🌿, and✨ every💯 herb🌱, yet✨ skip🚫 justice⚖️ and✨ God’s🙏 love💛. You🫵 should💭 do💪 these💯 things💯 without🚫 leaving🚫 the💯 others💯 undone🚫.

43📖 Woe⚠️ to✨ you🫵 Pharisees👥! You🫵 love💛 the💯 best🏆 seats🪑 in💯 the💯 synagogues🕍 and✨ the💯 greetings👋 in💯 the💯 marketplaces🏪.

44📖 Woe⚠️ to✨ you🫵, scribes📜 and✨ Pharisees👥, hypocrites🎭! You🫵 are✨ like💯 hidden🫣 graves⚰️ that✨ people👥 walk🚶 over💯 without🚫 knowing🧠.”

45📖 One🔢 law⚖️ expert👨‍⚖️ answered🗣️, “Teacher👨‍🏫, saying🗣️ that💯 insults⚠️ us🫂 too💯.”

46📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Woe⚠️ to✨ you🫵 law⚖️ experts👨‍⚖️ too💯! You🫵 load🪨 people👥 with💯 burdens🪨 too💯 heavy💯 to✨ carry🫴, but✨ you🫵 won’t🚫 lift💪 even💯 one🔢 finger☝️ to✨ help🤲 them🫂.

47📖 Woe⚠️ to✨ you🫵! You🫵 build🏗️ tombs⚰️ for💯 the💯 prophets📣 your💯 ancestors👨‍👩‍👧 killed💀.

48📖 So✨ you🫵 testify📣 and✨ agree✅ with💯 what💭 your💯 ancestors👨‍👩‍👧 did✅: they🫂 killed💀 the💯 prophets📣, and✨ you🫵 build🏗️ their💯 tombs⚰️.

49📖 That’s✨ why💭 God’s🙏 wisdom🧠 said🗣️, ‘I🙏 will💯 send📨 them🫂 prophets📣 and✨ apostles📣; they🫂 will💯 kill💀 and✨ persecute⚔️ some💯 of💯 them🫂,

50📖 so✨ this💯 generation📆 will💯 answer⚖️ for💯 the💯 blood🩸 of💯 all💯 the💯 prophets📣 shed🩸 since💯 the💯 world🌍 began🔛,

51📖 from💯 Abel👤 to✨ Zechariah👤, who✨ was✨ killed💀 between💯 the💯 altar🕍 and✨ the💯 sanctuary🕍.’ Yes✅, I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵, this💯 generation📆 will💯 answer⚖️ for💯 it💯.

52📖 Woe⚠️ to✨ you🫵 law⚖️ experts👨‍⚖️! You🫵 took🫴 away🚫 the💯 key🔑 to💯 knowledge🧠. You🫵 didn’t🚫 enter🚪 yourselves🫵, and✨ you🫵 blocked🚧 those💯 trying🤲 to✨ get🚪 in💯.”

53📖 When⏱️ Jesus🙏 left🚶, the💯 scribes📜 and✨ Pharisees👥 got😡 furious😡 and✨ began🔄 grilling🗣️ Him🙏 with💯 questions❓,

54📖 waiting⏳ to✨ catch🪤 Him🙏 in💯 something💭 He🙏 might💭 say🗣️, so✨ they🫂 could💯 accuse⚖️ Him🙏.”
Luke 12
# Luke 12 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Luke 12. Draft only; native-language, rendering, red-letter, and independent human review remain pending.

1📖 A💯 huge💯 crowd🫂—thousands🔢 deep💯—packed🫂 in🫂, trampling👣 each💯 other💯. Jesus🙏 first🥇 told🗣️ His🙏 disciples👥, “Watch👀 out⚠️ for💯 the💯 Pharisees👥’ yeast🍞: their💯 hypocrisy🎭.

2📖 Nothing💯 hidden🤫 stays💯 covered🫣 forever⏳; what💭 got💯 kept🔒 secret🤐 will💯 come🔦 out💯.

3📖 What💭 you🫵 whispered🤫 in💯 the💯 dark🌑 will💯 get👂 heard💯 in💯 the💯 light💡. What💭 you🫵 said🗣️ quietly🤫 indoors🏠 will💯 be✨ shouted📣 from💯 rooftops🏠.

4📖 “My💛 friends🤝, don’t🚫 let💭 Angst🇩🇪😰 about💯 people👥 take🫴 over💯. They🫂 can💯 kill💀 your💯 body🧍, then⏱️ that’s✨ it💯—they🫂 can’t🚫 do💪 anything💯 more💯.

5📖 I🙏’ll🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵 who💭 you🫵 should💭 fear😨: fear😨 the💯 One🔢 who✨ can💯 throw🫴 you🫵 into💯 Gehenna🔥 after⏱️ killing💀 you🫵. Yes✅, I🙏’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵: fear😨 Him🙏.

6📖 Five🔢 sparrows🐦 go💸 for💯 two🔢 assaria🪙, right❓? Still💯, God🙏 forgets🚫 not🚫 even💯 one🔢 of💯 them🫂.

7📖 Even💯 the💯 hairs💇 on💯 your💯 head🧑 are✨ all💯 counted🔢. So✨ don’t🚫 be✨ scared😨—you🫵 matter💛 way💯 more💯 than💯 many💯 sparrows🐦.”

8📖 “I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵, everyone💯 who✨ confesses🗣️ allegiance🤝 to💯 me🙏 in💯 front💯 of💯 people👥 will💯 be✨ acknowledged✅ by💯 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 in💯 front💯 of💯 God’s🙏 angels🕊️.

9📖 But✨ whoever💯 denies🚫 me🙏 in💯 front💯 of💯 people👥 will💯 be✨ denied🚫 in💯 front💯 of💯 God’s🙏 angels🕊️.

10📖 Anyone💯 who✨ speaks🗣️ against⚔️ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 can💯 be✨ forgiven🤲. But✨ the💯 one🔢 who✨ blasphemes⚠️ against💯 the💯 Holy🕊️ Ruach🇮🇱🙏 won’t🚫 be✨ forgiven🤲.

11📖 When⏱️ they🫂 haul🫴 you🫵 before💯 synagogues🕍, rulers👑, and✨ authorities🏛️, don’t🚫 get😰 anxious😣 about💯 what💭 to✨ answer🗣️ or✨ say🗣️.

12📖 The💯 Holy🕊️ Ruach🇮🇱🙏 will💯 teach📚 you🫵 right💯 then⏱️ what💭 you🫵 must💯 say🗣️.”

13📖 Someone👤 in💯 the💯 crowd🫂 called📣 out🔊, “Teacher👨‍🏫, tell🗣️ my💯 brother👨 to✨ split➗ the💯 inheritance💰 with💯 me👤.”

14📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Man👤, who❓ appointed🫴 me🙏 judge⚖️ or✨ referee⚖️ over💯 you🫵?”

15📖 Then⏱️ He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “Watch👀 out⚠️! Keep🛑 greed💰 at💯 a💯 distance🚫. A💯 person’s👤 life💛 isn’t🚫 measured⚖️ by💯 how💯 much💯 riqueza🇪🇸💰 they🫂 own🏦.”

16📖 Then⏱️ Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂 a💯 story📖: “A💯 rich💰 man’s👤 land🌾 produced🌱 a💯 massive💯 harvest🌾.

17📖 He👤 thought🧠 inside💯 his💯 obi🇳🇬❤️, ‘What❓ am💭 I🙏 gonna💭 do💪? I🙏 don’t🚫 have💯 room🏠 to✨ store📦 all💯 these💯 crops🌾.’

18📖 Then⏱️ he👤 said🗣️, ‘Here👀’s✨ the💯 move💯: I🙏’ll🙏 pull💥 down⬇️ my💯 barns🏚️, build🏗️ bigger💯 ones🏠, and✨ stack📦 all💯 my💯 grain🌾 and✨ goods📦 there📍.

19📖 Then⏱️ I🙏’ll🙏 tell🗣️ myself💭, “You’ve🫵 got💯 plenty💯 stored📦 for💯 years📆. Take😌 it💯 easy😎—eat🍽️, drink🥤, and✨ enjoy🎉 life💛.”’

20📖 But✨ God🙏 told🗣️ him👤, ‘You👤 fool🤦! Tonight🌙 your💯 soul💛 is✨ being💯 demanded⚖️. All💯 that💭 you🫵 stocked📦 up💯—who❓ gets💯 it💯 now⏱️?’

21📖 That💯’s✨ how💭 it💯 goes📉 for💯 anyone👤 who✨ piles📦 wealth💰 up💯 for💯 themselves🫵 but✨ stays💯 broke📉 toward💯 God🙏.”

22📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ His🙏 disciples👥, “So✨ don’t🚫 stress😣 about💯 your💯 life💛—what💭 oúnjẹ🇳🇬🍲 you🫵 will💯 eat🍽️—or✨ your💯 body🧍—what💭 you🫵 will💯 wear👕.

23📖 Life💛 is✨ bigger💯 than💯 food🍽️, and✨ your💯 body🧍 is✨ more💯 than💯 clothes👕.

24📖 Look👀 at💯 the💯 ravens🐦: dem🇯🇲 don’t🚫 plant🌱 or✨ harvest🌾; they🫂 have💯 no🚫 storeroom🏚️ or✨ barn🏚️, yet✨ God🙏 feeds🍽️ them🫂. You🫵 are✨ worth💛 far💯 more💯 than💯 birds🐦!

25📖 Can❓ any💯 of💯 you🫵 add📏 even💯 one🔢 cubit📏 to✨ your💯 height📐 by💯 stressing😣?

26📖 If✨ you🫵 can’t🚫 even💯 handle💪 that💯 little💯 thing💯, why❓ stress😣 about💯 the💯 rest💯?

27📖 Look👀 at💯 the💯 lilies🌸 growing🌱: they🫂 don’t🚫 work💪 or✨ spin🧵 cloth🧵. Still✨, even💯 Solomon👑 in💯 full💯 glory✨ wasn’t🚫 dressed👘 like💯 one🔢 of💯 them🫂.

28📖 If✨ God🙏 dresses👕 the💯 field🌾 grass🌱—here📍 today📆, tossed🫴 into💯 the💯 oven🔥 tomorrow📆—won’t🚫 He🙏 care🤲 for💯 you🫵 even💯 more💯? You🫵 have💯 little💯 faith🙏.

29📖 Don’t🚫 chase🏃 what💭 you🫵 will💯 eat🍽️ or✨ drink🥤, and✨ don’t🚫 spiral🌀 over💯 it💯.

30📖 The💯 nations🌍 of💯 the💯 world🌎 chase🏃 all💯 that💯 stuff💯, but✨ your💯 Father🙏 knows🧠 you🫵 need🆘 it💯.

31📖 Go🏃 after💯 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑; all💯 these💯 things💭 will💯 be✨ added🎁 to✨ you🫵 too💯.

32📖 “Don’t🚫 be✨ afraid😨, my💛 little💯 flock🐑. Your💯 Father🙏 is✨ happy😄 to✨ give🎁 you🫵 the💯 Kingdom👑.

33📖 Sell💸 what💭 you🫵 own🏠 and✨ give🎁 to✨ people👥 in💯 need🆘. Make🧵 purses👛 that✨ won’t🚫 wear⏳ out💥; store📦 treasure💎 in💯 heaven🌌 where📍 it💯 won’t🚫 run💨 out💥, no🚫 thief🦹 can💯 get🚪 close📍, and✨ no🚫 moth🦋 can💯 wreck💥 it💯.

34📖 Where📍 your💯 treasure💎 is✨, your💯 cœur🇫🇷❤️ will💯 be✨ there📍 too💯.

35📖 “Keep🧍 your💯 clothes👕 ready✅ for💯 action🏃 and✨ your💯 lamps🪔 burning🔥.

36📖 Be✨ like💯 servants🧑‍🍳 waiting⏳ for💯 their💯 master👑 to✨ return🚶 from💯 a💯 wedding💍, ready✅ to✨ open🔓 the💯 door🚪 the💯 moment⏱️ he👤 knocks🚪.

37📖 The💯 master👑 will💯 bless✨ those💯 servants🧑‍🍳 he👤 finds🔎 alert👀 when⏱️ he👤 arrives🚶. For💯 real💯, he’ll💯 dress👘 himself👤 to✨ serve🍽️ them🫂, seat🪑 them🫂 at💯 the💯 table🍽️, and✨ serve🍽️ them🫂 himself👤.

38📖 They’ll💯 be✨ blessed✨ if✨ he👤 comes🚶 in💯 the💯 second⏱️ or✨ even💯 third⏱️ watch🌙 and✨ finds🔎 them🫂 ready✅.

39📖 Get🧠 this💯: if✨ the💯 owner👤 of💯 a💯 gida🇳🇬🏠 knew🧠 when⏱️ a💯 thief🦹 was✨ coming🚶, they’d💯 stay👀 awake😴 and✨ stop🛑 the💯 break-in🦹.

40📖 So✨ stay🧍 ready✅ too💯, because✨ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 is✨ coming🚶 when⏱️ you🫵 don’t🚫 expect💭 Him🙏.”

41📖 Peter👤 asked🗣️, “Lord🙏, is✨ this💯 story📖 for💯 us🫂 or✨ for💯 everybody💯?”

42📖 The💯 Lord🙏 replied🗣️, “Who❓ is✨ the💯 faithful✅, wise🧠 manager📋 the💯 master👑 puts🫴 over💯 the💯 household🏠, to✨ hand🎁 everyone💯 their💯 food🍽️ at💯 the💯 right⏱️ time⏱️?

43📖 That💯 servant🧑‍🍳 is✨ blessed✨ if✨ the💯 master👑 arrives🚶 and✨ finds🔎 them🫂 doing💪 that💯 trabalho🇵🇹📋.

44📖 I🙏’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵 straight💯: the💯 master👑 will💯 put🫴 that💯 servant🧑‍🍳 over💯 everything💯 he👤 owns🏠.

45📖 But✨ if✨ that💯 servant🧑‍🍳 thinks🧠, ‘My💯 master👑 is✨ taking⏳ forever💯 to✨ come🚶,’ then⏱️ starts🔄 beating💥 the💯 men🧍 and✨ women👩 servants🧑‍🍳, eating🍽️ and✨ drinking🥤 until⏱️ drunk🍷,

46📖 the💯 master👑 will💯 arrive🚶 on💯 a💯 day📆 and✨ at💯 an💯 hour⏱️ the💯 servant🧑‍🍳 doesn’t🚫 expect💭; he👑 will💯 cut✂️ him👤 in💯 two🔪 and✨ assign📍 him👤 a💯 place📍 with💯 the💯 unfaithful💔.

47📖 The💯 servant🧑‍🍳 who✨ knew🧠 the💯 master’s👑 will💭 but✨ didn’t🚫 get✨ ready✅ or✨ do💪 it💯 will💯 be✨ beaten💥 with💯 many💯 lashes⚡.

48📖 The💯 one🔢 who✨ didn’t🚫 know🧠, but✨ did💪 things💭 deserving⚖️ lashes⚡, will💯 get💥 only💯 a💯 few💯. Whoever💯 receives🎁 a💯 lot💯 will💯 have💯 a💯 lot💯 required📋 from💯 them🫂; whoever💯 is✨ trusted🤝 with💯 much💯 will💯 be✨ asked🗣️ for💯 even💯 more💯.

49📖 “I🙏 came🚶 to✨ set🔥 the💯 earth🌍 on💯 fire🔥. I🙏 wish💭 it💯 were✨ already💯 lit🔥!

50📖 But✨ I🙏 have💯 a💯 baptism💧 I🙏 must💯 go🚶 through💯, and✨ I’m🙏 distressed😣 until⏳ it💯’s✨ completed✅.

51📖 Do❓ you🫵 think🧠 I🙏 came🚶 to✨ bring🕊️ peace🕊️ to💯 earth🌍? No🚫—I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵, division⚔️.

52📖 From💯 now⏱️ on💯, five🔢 people👥 in💯 one🔢 house🏠 will💯 split💔: three🔢 against⚔️ two🔢, and✨ two🔢 against⚔️ three🔢.

53📖 They’ll💯 be✨ split💔 apart💯: father👨 against⚔️ son👦, son👦 against⚔️ father👨; mother👩 against⚔️ daughter👧, daughter👧 against⚔️ mother👩; mother-in-law👵 against⚔️ daughter-in-law👩, and✨ daughter-in-law👩 against⚔️ mother-in-law👵.”

54📖 Jesus🙏 also➕ told🗣️ the💯 crowds🫂, “When⏱️ you🫵 see👀 a💯 cloud☁️ rising⬆️ in💯 the💯 west🌅, you🫵 instantly⏱️ say🗣️, ‘A💯 shower🌧️ is✨ coming🚶,’ and✨ it💯 does✅.

55📖 When⏱️ the💯 south🌴 wind💨 blows🌬️, you🫵 say🗣️, ‘It✨’s✨ gonna💭 scorch🥵,’ and✨ it💯 does✅.

56📖 Hypocrites🎭! You🫵 can💪 read🔎 the💯 look👀 of💯 earth🌍 and✨ sky🌌, so✨ how💭 come💭 you🫵 can’t🚫 read🔎 this💯 moment⏱️?

57📖 Why❓ not🚫 decide⚖️ for💯 yourselves🫵 what💭 is✨ right✅?

58📖 If✨ you🫵 are✨ heading🚶 to✨ court⚖️ with💯 someone👤 accusing⚠️ you🫵, work💪 hard💯 on💯 the💯 way🛣️ to✨ settle🤝 it💯. Otherwise💯, they🫂 could💭 drag🫴 you🫵 to✨ the💯 judge⚖️, the💯 judge⚖️ hand🤲 you🫵 to✨ an💯 officer👮, and✨ the💯 officer👮 throw🫴 you🫵 in💯 prison🔒.

59📖 I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵, you🫵 won’t🚫 get🚪 out🚪 until⏳ you’ve💯 paid💸 the💯 final🔚 coin🪙.”
Luke 13
# Luke 13 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Luke 13. Draft only; language, rendering, red-letter, and independent human review remain pending.

1📖 Some👥 folks🫂 who✨ were✨ there📍 told🗣️ Jesus🙏 how💭 Pilate👑 had💯 mixed🩸 Galileans🌍’ blood🩸 right💯 in💯 with💯 their💯 sacrifices🎁.

2📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “You🫵 think🧠 those💯 Galileans🌍 were✨ bigger💥 sinners💔 than💯 every💯 other💯 Galilean🌍 just💯 because✨ they🫂 went💨 through💯 that💯? For🧠 real💯?

3📖 No🚫. But✨ I🙏’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵 straight💯: unless⏳ you🫵 turn🔄 back↩️ to✨ God🙏, you🫵 all💯 will💯 perish💀 too💯.

4📖 And💭 those💯 eighteen🔢 the💯 tower🏗️ at💯 Siloam🏘️ fell⬇️ on💥 and✨ killed💀—you🫵 think🧠 they🫂 were✨ worse💥 offenders⚖️ than💯 everybody💯 living🏠 in💯 Jerusalem🏙️?

5📖 No🚫. But✨ unless⏳ you🫵 turn🔄 back↩️ to✨ God🙏, you🫵 all💯 will💯 perish💀 too💯.”

6📖 Then⏱️ Jesus🙏 told🗣️ this💯 story📖: “A💯 man👤 had💯 a💯 Feigenbaum🇩🇪🌳 planted🌱 in💯 his💯 vineyard🍇. He👤 came🚶 looking🔎 for💯 fruto🇪🇸 on💯 it💯, but✨ found🚫 none🚫.

7📖 He👤 told🗣️ the💯 gardener🧑‍🌾, ‘Look👀, three🔢 years📆 running🔁 I🙏’ve🙏 come🚶 for💯 fruit🍇 and✨ found🚫 nothing🚫. Cut✂️ it💯 down⬇️! Why❓ let💯 it💯 drain💧 the💯 soil🌱?’

8📖 The💯 gardener🧑‍🌾 said🗣️, ‘Sir🙏, give🎁 it💯 one🔢 more💯 year📆. I’ll🙏 loosen⛏️ the💯 ground🌱 around💯 it💯 and✨ feed🌿 it💯.

9📖 If✨ fruit🍇 comes🌱, bless✨. If✨ not🚫, then⏱️ cut✂️ it💯 down⬇️.’”

10📖 One🔢 repos🇫🇷 day📆, Jesus🙏 was✨ teaching📣 in💯 a💯 synagogue🕍.

11📖 A💯 woman👩 there📍 had💯 been✨ held⛓️ by💯 a💯 spirit👿 of💯 weakness😣 for💯 eighteen🔢 years📆. She👩 was✨ folded🙇 over💯 and✨ couldn’t🚫 straighten🧍 up⬆️ at💯 all💯.

12📖 Jesus🙏 noticed👀 her👩, called📣 her👩 over🚶, and✨ said🗣️, “Woman👩, you🫵 are✨ free🔓 from💯 this💯 weakness😣.”

13📖 He🙏 laid🫴 His🙏 hands👐 on💯 her👩; right💯 away⏱️ she👩 stood⬆️ straight🧍 and✨ gave🙌 God🙏 glory✨.

14📖 The💯 synagogue🕍 leader👑 got💯 heated😠 because✨ Jesus🙏 healed🩺 on💯 repos🇫🇷. He👑 told🗣️ the💯 crowd🫂, “There✨ are✨ six🔢 work💪 days📆. Come🚶 get💯 healed🩺 then⏱️, not🚫 on💯 the💯 Sabbath🕍!”

15📖 The💯 Lord🙏 shot🗣️ back↩️, “Hypocrites🎭! Don’t🚫 each💯 of💯 you🫵 untie🔓 your💯 ox🐂 or✨ donkey🫏 from💯 its💯 stall🏚️ on💯 Sabbath🕍 and✨ lead🧭 it💯 to✨ ruwa🇳🇬💧?

16📖 So💭 shouldn’t🚫 this💯 woman👩, Abraham’s👨 daughter👧—whom✨ Satan😈 kept🔒 chained⛓️ for💯 eighteen🔢 long💯 years📆—be✨ set🔓 free💯 from💯 that💯 bondage⛓️ on💯 Sabbath🕍?”

17📖 As⏱️ He🙏 said🗣️ that💯, His🙏 opponents⚔️ felt😳 embarrassed😳, while✨ dem🇯🇲 rejoiced🎉, their💯 obi🇳🇬❤️ full💯 of💯 joy😊 over✨ all💯 the💯 amazing✨ things💯 He🙏 had💯 done✅.

18📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “What❓ is✨ God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 like💭? What❓ can💭 I🙏 compare⚖️ it💯 with💯?

19📖 It✨’s✨ like💯 a💯 mustard🌱 seed🌱 that✨ someone👤 planted🌱 in💯 a💯 garden🌿. It💯 grew🌱 into💯 a💯 big💯 tree🌳, and✨ birds🐦 of💯 the💯 sky🌤️ made🏠 homes🪺 in💯 its💯 branches🌳.”

20📖 Again🔁 He🙏 asked🗣️, “What❓ can💭 I🙏 compare⚖️ God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 with💯?

21📖 It✨’s✨ like💯 yeast🍞 a💯 woman👩 mixed👐 into💯 three🔢 measures🪣 of💯 flour🌾 until⏳ the💯 whole💯 batch🍞 of💯 pão🇵🇹🌾 rose⬆️.”

22📖 Jesus🙏 kept🔁 travelling🚶 through💯 towns🏘️ and✨ villages🏡, teaching📣 as⏱️ He🙏 headed🧭 for💯 Jerusalem🏙️.

23📖 Someone👤 asked🗣️ Him🙏, “Lord🙏, will💭 only💯 a💯 few💯 people👥 be✨ saved🛟?” He🙏 answered🗣️,

24📖 “Strive💪 to✨ enter🚪 through💯 the💯 narrow🚪 door🚪. Many💯, I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵, will💯 try🤲 to✨ get🚪 in💯 but✨ won’t🚫 be💯 able💪.

25📖 When⏱️ the💯 house🏠 master👤 gets⬆️ up⬆️ and✨ shuts🔒 the💯 door🚪, you🫵 may💭 be✨ left🚪 outside🧍, knocking🚪 and✨ calling📣, ‘Lord🙏, Lord🙏, open🔓 up⬆️!’ But✨ he’ll💯 answer🗣️, ‘I🙏 don’t🚫 know🧠 you🫵 or✨ where📍 you🫵 came🚶 from💯.’

26📖 Then⏱️ you🫵 will💯 say🗣️, ‘We🫂 ate🍽️ and✨ drank🥤 with💯 you🙏, and✨ you🙏 taught📣 in💯 our💯 streets🛣️.’

27📖 He🙏 will💯 answer🗣️, ‘I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵, I🙏 don’t🚫 know🧠 where📍 you🫵 came🚶 from💯. Get🚪 away↩️ from💯 me🙏, all💯 you🫵 who✨ do💪 wrong😈!’

31📖 That💯 same💯 day📆, some👥 Pharisees👥 came🚶 to✨ Jesus🙏 and✨ warned⚠️ Him🙏, “Leave🚪 here📍! Herod👑 wants💭 to✨ kill💀 you🙏.”

33📖 But✨ I🙏 must💯 keep🔁 moving🚶 today📆, tomorrow📆, and✨ the💯 next🔜 day📆. A💯 prophet📣 can’t🚫 die💀 somewhere💯 outside🚪 Jerusalem🏙️.

34📖 “Jerusalem🏙️, Jerusalem🏙️—the💯 city🏙️ that✨ kills💀 the💯 prophets📣 and✨ stones🪨 God’s🙏 messengers📣! So💭 many💯 times⏱️ I🙏 wanted💭 to✨ gather🫂 your💯 ọmọ🇳🇬👧👦 close💞, like💯 a💯 hen🐔 pulls🫂 her💯 chicks🐣 under💯 her💯 wings🪽, but✨ you🫵 wouldn’t🚫.

35📖 Look👀, your💯 house🏠 is✨ left🏚️ empty💯. I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫵, you🫵 won’t🚫 see👀 me🙏 again🔁 until⏳ you🫵 say🗣️, ‘Blessed✨ is✨ the💯 one🔢 coming🚶 in💯 the💯 name🏷️ of💯 Adonai🇮🇱🙏!’”

13📖 A💯 few💯 days📆 later⏱️, the💯 younger👦 son👦 gathered🧳 everything💯 he👤 owned💰 and✨ headed🚶 for💯 a💯 faraway🌍 country🏞️. There📍 he👤 burned💸 through💯 it💯 all💯, living🎉 recklessly💥.

17📖 Then⏱️ he👤 came🧠 to✨ his💯 senses🧠: ‘All💯 my💯 dad’s👨 hired🧑‍🌾 workers👥 have💯 bread🍞 to✨ spare💰, while✨ I🙏 am✨ out🌾 here📍 starving🤤!

19📖 I’m🙏 not🚫 worthy💔 to✨ be✨ called📣 your💯 son👦 anymore💯. Treat💼 me👤 like💯 one🔢 of💯 your💯 hired🧑‍🌾 workers👥.”’

20📖 He👦 got⬆️ up⬆️ and✨ headed🚶 back↩️ to✨ his💯 father👨. While⏱️ he👦 was✨ still⏱️ far💯 off↔️, his💯 father👨 saw👀 him👦 and✨ was✨ moved💛 with💯 àánú🇳🇬. He👨 ran🏃 over💯, wrapped🫂 him👦 in💯 his💯 arms🤲, and✨ kissed💋 him👦.

21📖 His💯 son👦 said🗣️, ‘Dad👨, I’ve🙏 sinned💔 against⚔️ heaven🌌 and✨ in💯 your💯 sight👀. I’m🙏 not🚫 worthy💔 to✨ be✨ called📣 your💯 son👦 anymore💯.’

22📖 But✨ the💯 father👨 told🗣️ his💯 servants🧑, ‘Quick🏃—bring🫴 the💯 best🏆 robe👘 and✨ put👘 it💯 on💯 him👦. Put🫴 a💯 ring💍 on💯 his💯 hand🤲 and✨ sandals👡 on💯 his💯 feet🦶.

24📖 This💯 hijo🇪🇸 of💯 mine👨 was✨ dead💀 but✨ is✨ alive💛 again🔄; he👦 was✨ lost💔 but✨ is✨ found🔎!’ Then⏱️ the💯 celebration🎉 kicked🔛 off💥.

25📖 The💯 older👦 son👦 was✨ out🌾 in💯 the💯 field🌾. As⏱️ he👤 got🚶 close📍 to✨ home🏠, he👤 heard👂 music🎶 and✨ dancing💃.

26📖 He👦 called📣 one🔢 of💯 the💯 servants🧑 over💯 and✨ asked🗣️ what💭 was✨ going⏱️ on💭.

27📖 The💯 servant🧑 said🗣️, ‘Your💯 brother👦 came🚶 home🏠, and✨ your💯 father👨 prepared🍖 the💯 fattened🍖 calf🐄 because✨ he👨 got💯 him👦 back↩️ safe🛟 and✨ sound💪.’

28📖 The💯 older👦 brother👦 felt💢 his💯 obi🇳🇬 burn🔥 with💯 anger😡 and✨ refused🚫 to✨ go🚪 in💯. His💯 father👨 came🚶 out🚪 and✨ begged🙏 him👦 to✨ come🚪 inside🏠.

# Luke 16 — AdigunBible draft
