1📖 Jesus🙏 also💯 told🗣️ His🙏 disciples👥, “A💯 rich💰 man👤 had💯 a💯 manager📋, and✨ someone👤 reported🗣️ that✨ the💯 manager📋 was✨ wasting💸 his💯 Geld🇩🇪.

2📖 The💯 owner👤 called📣 him👤 in🚪: ‘What❓ is✨ this💯 I’m🙏 hearing👂 about💭 you🫵? Bring🫴 me👤 the💯 account📚 of💯 your💯 management📋. You’re🫵 done🚫 managing📋 my💯 property🏠.’

3📖 The💯 manager📋 thought🧠, ‘What❓ do💭 I🙏 do💪 now⏱️? My💯 boss👑 is✨ taking🫴 my💯 job🧰. I’m🙏 not🚫 strong💪 enough💯 to✨ dig⛏️, and✨ I’m🙏 too💯 ashamed😳 to✨ beg🙏.

4📖 I’ve🙏 got💭 a💯 plan🧠: when⏱️ I’m🙏 out🚪 of💯 this💯 job🧰, people👥 will💯 welcome👋 me👤 into💯 their💯 homes🏠.’

5📖 He👤 called📣 in💯 each💯 person👤 who✨ owed💰 his💯 boss👑: ‘How💰 much💯 do💭 you🫵 owe💰 him👑?’ The💯 first🥇 said🗣️, ‘A💯 hundred💯 batos🪣 of💯 olive🫒 oil🫒.’

6📖 The💯 manager📋 told🗣️ him👤, ‘Take🫴 your💯 bill📜, sit🪑 down⬇️ quick⚡, and✨ change✍️ it💯 to✨ fifty💯.’

7📖 Then⏱️ he👤 asked🗣️ another🔢, ‘How💰 much💯 do💭 you🫵 owe💰?’ The💯 person👤 said🗣️, ‘A💯 hundred💯 cors🪣 of💯 wheat🌾.’ ‘Take🫴 your💯 bill📜 and✨ write✍️ eighty💯,’ he👤 said🗣️.

8📖 “The💯 owner👤 praised👍 the💯 dishonest🕵️ manager📋 for💯 being✨ shrewd🧠. People👥 of💯 this💯 world🌍 can💭 be✨ sharper🧠 among💯 their💯 own💯 crowd🫂 than✨ the💯 people👥 of💯 the💯 lumière🇫🇷.

9📖 I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂, use💪 unrighteous💸 wealth💰 to✨ build🤝 friendships🤝, so✨ when⏱️ it💯 fails📉, you’ll💯 be✨ welcomed👋 into💯 eternal⏳ homes🏠.

10📖 Anyone💯 trusted🤝 with💯 a💯 little💰 can💯 be✨ trusted🤝 with💯 a💯 lot💰. Anyone💯 dishonest🕵️ with💯 a💯 little💰 will💯 be✨ dishonest🕵️ with💯 a💯 lot💰.

11📖 If✨ you🫂 haven’t🚫 been✨ faithful🤝 with💯 unrighteous🕵️ wealth💰, who❓ will💭 trust🤝 you🫂 with💯 true✅ riches💎?

12📖 If✨ you🫂 weren’t🚫 faithful🤝 with💯 what💭 belongs💯 to✨ someone👤 else💯, who❓ will💭 hand🎁 you🫂 what💭 is✨ yours🫵?

13📖 No🚫 servant🧑 can💯 serve🤝 two🔢 bosses👑. They’ll💯 hate😡 one🔢 and✨ love💛 the💯 other💯, or✨ stick🤝 with💯 one🔢 and✨ despise😒 the💯 other💯. You🫂 can’t🚫 serve🤝 Elohim🇮🇱 and✨ Mammon💰 at💯 the💯 same💯 time⏱️.”

14📖 The💯 Pharisees👥 loved💰 riqueza🇵🇹. They🫂 heard👂 all💯 this💯 and✨ started🤡 mocking🤡 Jesus🙏.

15📖 He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “You🫂 make💭 yourselves🫵 look👀 righteous🙏 in💯 front💯 of💯 people👥, but✨ God🙏 knows🧠 your💯 obi🇳🇬. What💭 people👥 hype📣 up⬆️ can💭 be✨ disgusting🤢 to✨ God🙏.

16📖 “The💯 Law📜 and✨ the💯 Prophets📣 were✨ until⏱️ John👤. Since💯 then⏱️, the💯 Good✨ News📣 of💯 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 is✨ being✨ preached📣, and✨ people👥 are✨ pushing💥 their💯 way🛣️ into💯 it💯.

17📖 Still✨, heaven🌌 and✨ earth🌍 could💭 pass💨 away↩️ sooner💯 than✨ one🔢 tiny💯 stroke🖊️ in💯 the💯 Law📜 could💭 fall🚫.

18📖 “Anyone💯 who✨ divorces📜 their💯 wife👩 and✨ marries💍 someone👤 else💯 commits💔 adultery💔. Anyone💯 who✨ marries💍 a💯 woman👩 divorced📜 from💯 her💯 husband👨 commits💔 adultery💔 too💯.

19📖 “There✨ was✨ a💯 rich💰 man👤 dressed👘 in💯 purple💜 and✨ fine💎 linen👔, living🏰 it💯 up⬆️ in💯 luxury💎 every💯 day📆.

20📖 A💯 beggar💸 named🏷️ Lazarus👤 had💯 been✨ laid🛏️ at💯 the💯 rich💰 man’s👤 gate🚪, covered🤕 in💯 sores🩹.

21📖 He👤 longed💭 for💯 even💯 the💯 crumbs🍞 falling⬇️ from💯 the💯 rich💰 man’s👤 table🍽️. Even✨ the💯 perros🇪🇸 came🚶 and✨ licked👅 his💯 sores🩹.

22📖 The💯 beggar💸 died💀, and✨ angels👼 carried🫴 him👤 to✨ Abraham’s👨 side📍. The💯 rich💰 man👤 died💀 too💯 and✨ was✨ buried⚰️.

23📖 In💯 Hades⚰️, the💯 rich💰 man👤 was✨ suffering🔥. He👤 looked👀 up⬆️ and✨ saw👀 Abraham👨 far💯 away↔️, with✨ Lazarus👤 close📍 beside💯 him👨.

24📖 He👤 called📣 out🔊, ‘Father👨 Abraham👨, please🙏 show💛 me👤 mercy🤲! Send📨 Lazarus👤 to✨ dip🤏 the💯 tip☝️ of💯 his💯 finger☝️ in💯 water💧 and✨ cool🧊 my💯 tongue👅. I’m🙏 in💯 agony🔥 in💯 these💯 flames🔥!’

25📖 Abraham👨 answered🗣️, ‘My💛 son👤, remember🧠: you🫵 had💯 good✨ things💎 in💯 your💯 life💛, while✨ Lazarus👤 had💯 hardship😣. Now⏱️ he👤 gets💯 ìtùnú🇳🇬 here📍, and✨ you🫵 are✨ in💯 agony🔥.

26📖 Besides✨, a💯 huge💯 chasm🕳️ is✨ fixed🔒 between💯 us🫂. Nobody💯 can💯 cross🚶 from💯 here📍 to✨ you🫵, and✨ nobody💯 can💯 cross🚶 from💯 there📍 to✨ us🫂.’

27📖 The💯 rich💰 man👤 said🗣️, ‘Then⏱️ please🙏 send📨 Lazarus👤 to✨ my💯 father’s👨 gida🇳🇬—

28📖 I🙏 have💯 five🔢 brothers👨‍👦 there📍. Let✨ Lazarus👤 warn⚠️ them🫂 so✨ they🫂 don’t🚫 end🔚 up⬆️ in💯 this💯 place📍 of💯 torment🔥 too💯.’

29📖 Abraham👨 said🗣️, ‘They🫂 have💯 Moses👤 and✨ the💯 prophets📣. Let✨ dem🇯🇲 listen👂 to✨ them🫂.’

30📖 The💯 rich💰 man👤 replied🗣️, ‘No🚫, father👨 Abraham👨! If✨ someone👤 came🚶 back↩️ from💯 the💯 dead💀, they🫂 would💭 repent🙏.’

31📖 Abraham👨 told🗣️ him👤, ‘If✨ they🫂 don’t🚫 listen👂 to✨ Moses👤 and✨ the💯 prophets📣, they🫂 won’t🚫 be✨ convinced🤔 even💯 if✨ someone👤 rises⬆️ from💯 the💯 dead💀.’”
Luke 17
# Luke 17 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Luke 17. Draft only; verse 36 is included from the WEBP textual note. Not validated for release.

1📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ His🙏 disciples👥, “People👥 will💯 stumble⚠️—that💯 part💯 is✨ unavoidable💯. But✨ judgment⚖️ is✨ coming⏳ for💯 the💯 one🔢 who✨ trips💥 them🫂 up⬆️.

2📖 That💯 person👤 would💭 be✨ better✅ off💯 with💯 a💯 Mühlstein🇩🇪 tied⛓️ around💯 their💯 neck🧣 and✨ thrown🫴 into💯 the💯 sea🌊 than✨ making💥 one🔢 of💯 these💯 little💯 ones🧒 stumble⚠️.

3📖 Watch👀 yourselves🫵. If✨ your💯 brother👤 sins💔 against⚔️ you🫵, call🗣️ it💯 out⚠️; if✨ he👤 turns🔄 back↩️, forgive🤲 him👤.

4📖 Even💯 if✨ he👤 sins💔 against⚔️ you🫵 seven🔢 times⏱️ in💯 one🔢 day📆 and✨ comes🚶 back↩️ seven🔢 times⏱️ saying🗣️, ‘I🙏 repent🙏,’ you🫵 give🎁 him👤 pardon🇫🇷 each💯 time⏱️.”

5📖 The💯 apostles📣 said🗣️ to✨ the💯 Lord🙏, “Give🎁 us🫂 more💯 faith🙏.”

6📖 The💯 Lord🙏 replied🗣️, “If✨ you🫂 had💯 faith🙏 even💯 like💯 a💯 mustard🌱 seed🌱, you🫂 could💭 tell🗣️ this💯 sycamore🌳, ‘Pull🫴 yourself🫵 up⬆️ and✨ plant🌱 yourself🫵 in💯 the💯 sea🌊,’ and✨ it💯 would💯 obey✅ you🫂.

7📖 “If✨ one🔢 of💯 you🫂 had💯 a💯 servant🧑 ploughing🚜 your💯 field🌾 or✨ tending🐑 sheep🐑, would💭 you🫵 tell🗣️ them🫂 to✨ sit🪑 at💯 the💯 table🍽️ the💯 second⏱️ they🫂 got🚶 back↩️ from💯 the💯 field🌾?

8📖 Wouldn’t❓ you🫵 say🗣️, ‘Get🍽️ supper🌙 ready✅, dress👕 for💯 work💪, and✨ serve🤲 me👤 while⏱️ I🙏 eat🍽️ and✨ drink🥤. Then⏱️ you🫂 eat🍽️ and✨ drink🥤.’

9📖 Does❓ the💯 master👑 thank🙏 the💯 servant🧑 just💯 for💯 doing✅ what💭 was✨ assigned📋? No🚫.

10📖 Same💯 with💯 you🫂: after⏱️ doing✅ everything💯 God🙏 asked🙏, say🗣️, ‘We🫂 are✨ unworthy💯 servants🧑. We🫂 only💯 did✅ our💯 duty📋.’”

11📖 On⏱️ His🙏 way🛣️ to✨ Jerusalem🏙️, Jesus🙏 travelled🚶 along💯 the💯 border🌍 between💯 Samaria🏘️ and✨ Galilee🌍.

12📖 As⏱️ He🙏 entered🚪 a💯 village🏘️, ten🔟 men👥 with💯 leprosy🤒 came🚶 to✨ meet🤝 Him🙏. They🫂 stopped🧍 far💯 away↔️,

13📖 then⏱️ called📣 out🔊, “Jesus🙏, Adonai🇮🇱—please🙏 show💛 us🫂 mercy🤲!”

14📖 Jesus🙏 saw👀 them🫂 and✨ said🗣️, “Go🚶 show👀 yourselves🫵 to✨ the💯 priests🕍.” As⏱️ they🫂 went🚶, they🫂 were✨ cleansed✨.

15📖 One🔢 of💯 them🫂 saw👀 he👤 was✨ healed🩺, turned🔄 back↩️, and✨ praised🙌 God🙏 with💯 a💯 loud🔊 voice🗣️.

16📖 He👤 fell⬇️ face-first🙇 at💯 the💯 feet🦶 of💯 Jesus🙏, thanking🙏 Him🙏. And✨ he👤 was✨ a💯 Samaritan🧍.

17📖 Jesus🙏 asked🗣️, “Weren’t❓ all💯 ten🔟 cleansed✨? Where📍 dem🇯🇲 other💯 nine🔢?

18📖 Was✨ this💯 outsider🌍 the💯 only💯 one🔢 who✨ came🚶 back↩️ to✨ give🎁 glory✨ to✨ God🙏?”

19📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him👤, “Get⬆️ up⬆️ and✨ go🚶. Your💯 faith🙏 has💯 healed🩺 you🫵.”

20📖 The💯 Pharisees👥 asked🗣️ Jesus🙏 when⏱️ the💯 Kingdom👑 of💯 God🙏 would💭 come🚶. He🙏 answered🗣️, “The💯 Kingdom👑 of💯 God🙏 does🚫 not🚫 show🚦 up⬆️ with💯 signs🔎 you🫂 can💯 track🔎; it✨ is✨ already💯 n'etiti🇳🇬 you🫂.

21📖 People👥 will🚫 not🚫 be✨ saying🗣️, ‘Look👀, it✨ is✨ over💯 here📍!’ or✨ ‘There📍 it💯 is✨!’ The💯 Kingdom👑 of💯 God🙏 is✨ already💯 among💯 you🫂.”

22📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ His🙏 disciples👥, “Days📆 are✨ coming⏳ when💭 you🫂 will💯 ache💔 to✨ see👀 even💯 one🔢 day📆 of💯 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤—and✨ you🫂 will🚫 not🚫 see👀 it💯.

23📖 People👥 will💯 tell🗣️ you🫂, ‘Look👀 over💯 here📍!’ or✨ ‘Look👀 over💯 there📍!’ Do🚫 not🚫 run🏃 after💯 them🫂 or✨ follow🚶 them🫂.

24📖 When⏱️ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 comes🚶, it💯 will💯 be✨ like💯 lightning⚡ flashing🌟 across💯 the💯 sky🌌, from💯 one🔢 side↔️ to✨ the💯 other💯—visible👀 across💯 the💯 whole💯 céu🇵🇹.

25📖 But✨ first🥇 He🙏 must💯 suffer😣 many💯 things💯 and✨ be✨ rejected🚫 by💯 this💯 generation📆.

26📖 “Just💯 as✨ it💯 was✨ in💯 Noah’s👤 days📆, that💯 is✨ how💭 it💯 will💯 be✨ in💯 the💯 days📆 of💯 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤.

27📖 People👥 ate🍽️, drank🥤, married💍, and✨ were✨ given💍 in💯 marriage💍 right💯 up⬆️ to✨ the💯 day📆 Noah👤 entered🚪 the💯 ark🛶. Then⏱️ the💯 flood🌊 came🚶 and✨ swept💥 them🫂 all💯 away💀.

28📖 It💯 was✨ much💯 the💯 same💯 in💯 Lot’s👤 days📆: people👥 ate🍽️, drank🥤, bought🛒, sold💸, planted🌱, and✨ built🏗️.

29📖 But✨ on💯 the💯 day📆 Lot👤 left🚪 Sodom🏙️, fuego🇪🇸 and✨ sulphur🟡 rained🌧️ from💯 the💯 sky🌌 and✨ destroyed💥 them🫂 all💯.

30📖 That💯 is✨ how💭 it💯 will💯 be✨ on💯 the💯 day📆 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 is✨ revealed🔦.

31📖 If✨ you🫂 are✨ on💯 the💯 rooftop🏠 that💯 day📆, do🚫 not🚫 go⬇️ back↩️ inside🏠 the💯 gida🇳🇬 for💯 your💯 stuff📦. If✨ you🫂 are✨ out🌾 in💯 the💯 field🌾, do🚫 not🚫 turn🔄 back↩️ home🏠 for💯 anything💭.

32📖 Remember🧠 Lot’s👤 wife👩.

33📖 Whoever💯 tries🤲 to✨ save🛟 their💯 ìyè🇳🇬 will💯 lose📉 it💯; whoever💯 loses📉 their💯 life💛 will💯 preserve🛟 it💯.

34📖 I🙏 am✨ telling🗣️ you🫂, that💯 night🌙 two🔢 people👥 will💯 be✨ in💯 one🔢 bed🛏️—one🔢 taken🫴, the💯 other💯 left🧍.

35📖 Two🔢 women👩 will💯 be✨ grinding🌾 grain🌾 together🫂—one🔢 taken🫴, the💯 other💯 left🧍.

36📖 Some💯 Greek🇬🇷 copies📜 add➕: two🔢 people👥 will💯 be✨ in💯 the💯 field🌾; one🔢 taken🫴, the💯 other💯 left🧍.

37📖 The💯 disciples👥 asked🗣️ Jesus🙏, “Where📍, Lord🙏?” He🙏 answered🗣️, “Wherever📍 there💯 is✨ a💯 body🧍, vultures🦅 gather🫂 there📍 too💯.”
Luke 18
# Luke 18 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Luke 18. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂 this💯 parable📖: keep🔁 praying🙏, and✨ don’t🚫 quit🚫.

2📖 “In💯 one🔢 city🏙️ there✨ was✨ a💯 judge⚖️ who✨ didn’t🚫 fear😨 God🙏 or✨ respect🤝 people👥.

3📖 A💯 widow👵 lived🏠 there📍 too💯. She👵 kept🔁 coming🚶 back↩️: ‘Give⚖️ me👵 justice⚖️ against⚔️ my💯 opponent⚔️!’

4📖 For💯 a💯 while⏳ he⚖️ refused🚫. Then⏱️ he👤 said🗣️ to✨ himself👤, ‘I🙏 don’t🚫 fear😨 God🙏 or✨ respect🤝 people👥,

5📖 but✨ this💯 widow👵 keeps🔁 wearing😣 me👤 down⬇️. I’ll🙏 give⚖️ her💯 justice⚖️ before⏱️ she👵 wears😩 me👤 out💥 by💯 showing🚶 up⬆️ again🔄!’”

6📖 The💯 Lord🙏 said🗣️, “Hear👂 what💭 this💯 unjust⚖️ judge⚖️ is✨ saying🗣️.

7📖 Won’t🚫 God🙏 bring⚖️ justice⚖️ for💯 His🙏 chosen🫴 people👥 who✨ cry📣 to✨ Him🙏 day🌞 and✨ night🌙? Will💭 He🙏 keep⏳ showing💛 Geduld🇩🇪 with💯 them🫂?

8📖 I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂, He🙏 will💯 bring⚖️ them🫂 justice⚖️ quickly⏱️. Still✨, when⏱️ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 comes🚶, will💭 He🙏 find🔎 faith🙏 on💯 earth🌍?”

9📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ this💯 parable📖 to✨ people👥 sure💯 their💯 obi🇳🇬 was✨ right✅, looking👀 down⬇️ on💯 everyone💯 else💯.

10📖 “Two🔢 men👥 went⬆️ up⬆️ to✨ the💯 Temple🕍 to✨ pray🙏. One🔢 was✨ a💯 Pharisee👤, the💯 other💯 a💯 tax💰 collector👤.

11📖 The💯 Pharisee👤 stood🧍 and✨ prayed🙏 on💯 his💯 own🫵: ‘God🙏, thank🙏 You🙏 I’m🙏 not🚫 like💯 everybody💯 else💯—extortionists💰, unrighteous😈 people👥, adulterers💔, or✨ this💯 tax💰 collector👤.

12📖 I🙏 fast🚫 twice🔢 a💯 week📆 and✨ give💸 You🙏 a💯 tenth🔟 of💯 everything💯 I🙏 receive🎁.’

13📖 But✨ the💯 tax💰 collector👤 stood🧍 way💯 back↩️ and✨ wouldn’t🚫 even💯 lift⬆️ his💯 eyes👀 to✨ heaven🌌. He👤 beat👊 his💯 chest🫀: ‘God🙏, please🙏 show💛 me🙏 miséricorde🇫🇷. I’m🙏 a💯 sinner💔.’

14📖 I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂, this💯 man👤 went🚶 home🏠 justificado🇪🇸 before💯 God🙏, unlike💯 the💯 other💯 one🔢. Everyone💯 who✨ lifts⬆️ themselves🫵 up🏆 will💯 be✨ brought⬇️ low⬇️, but✨ the💯 one🔢 who✨ humbles⬇️ themselves🫵 will💯 be✨ lifted⬆️ up🏆.”

15📖 People👥 were✨ bringing🫴 their💯 babies👶 to✨ Jesus🙏 so✨ He🙏 could💭 touch🤲 them🫂. When⏱️ the💯 disciples👥 saw👀 it💯, they🫂 rebuked⚠️ the💯 families👨‍👩‍👧.

16📖 But✨ Jesus🙏 called📣 the💯 little💯 ones👶 over💯: “Let✨ the💯 little💯 ọmọ🇳🇬 come🚶 to✨ Me🙏. Don’t🚫 block🚧 them🫂—God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 belongs💯 to✨ people👥 like💯 them🫂.

17📖 I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂 for💯 real✅: anyone💯 who✨ won’t🚫 receive🤲 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 like💯 a💯 child🧒 will💯 never🚫 enter🚪 it💯.”

18📖 A💯 ruler👑 asked🗣️ Jesus🙏, “Good✨ Teacher👨‍🏫, what❓ do💭 I🙏 need🆘 to✨ do💪 to✨ inherit🎁 eternal⏳ life💛?”

19📖 Jesus🙏 asked🗣️ him👤, “Why❓ call📣 Me🙏 good✨? No🚫 one👤 is✨ good✨ except💯 God🙏 alone🤍.

20📖 You🫵 know🧠 the💯 commandments📜: ‘Don’t🚫 commit💔 adultery💔. Don’t🚫 murder🔪. Don’t🚫 steal🦹. Don’t🚫 testify🗣️ falsely🤥. Honour🙌 your💯 father👨 and✨ mother👩.’”

21📖 The💯 ruler👑 replied🗣️, “I’ve🙏 kept✅ all💯 those💯 since⏱️ I🙏 was✨ young👦.”

22📖 When⏱️ Jesus🙏 heard👂 that💯, He🙏 said🗣️, “You🫵 still⏳ lack💭 one🔢 thing💯: sell💸 everything💯 you🫵 own🏠 and✨ distribute🫴 it💯 to✨ the💯 poor💸. Then⏱️ you’ll💯 have💯 treasure💎 in💯 heaven🌌. After⏱️ that💯, come🚶 follow🚶 Me🙏.”

23📖 When⏱️ the💯 ruler👑 heard👂 this💯, he👤 became✨ deeply💯 sad😔—he👤 was✨ very💯 rich💰.

24📖 Jesus🙏 saw👀 his💯 sadness😔 and✨ said🗣️, “It’s✨ hard💥 for💯 people👥 with💯 riqueza🇵🇹 to✨ enter🚪 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑.

25📖 It’s✨ easier💯 for💯 a💯 camel🐫 to✨ squeeze🪡 through💯 a💯 needle’s🪡 eye👁️ than✨ for💯 a💯 rich💰 person👤 to✨ enter🚪 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑.”

26📖 The💯 people👥 listening👂 asked🗣️, “Then⏱️ who❓ can💯 be✨ saved🛟?”

27📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “What💭 people👥 can’t🚫 do💪, God🙏 can💯 do✅.”

28📖 Peter👤 said🗣️, “Look👀, we🫂 left🚪 everything💯 and✨ followed🚶 You🙏.”

29📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂 for💯 certain✅: nobody💯 who✨ left🚪 a💯 gida🇳🇬, wife👩, brothers👥, parents👨‍👩‍👧, or✨ children🧒 for💯 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑

30📖 will💯 miss🚫 out💯—they’ll💯 receive🎁 so✨ much💯 more💯 in💯 this💯 life💛, and✨ eternal⏳ life💛 in💯 the💯 age📆 to✨ come🚶.”

31📖 Jesus🙏 took🫴 the💯 twelve🔢 aside↩️: “We🫂 are✨ heading🚶 up⬆️ to✨ Jerusalem🏙️. Everything💯 written✍️ through💯 the💯 prophets📣 about💭 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 is✨ about⏱️ to✨ be✨ completed✅.

32📖 He👤 will💯 be✨ handed🤲 over💯 to✨ the💯 Gentiles🌍. They’ll💯 mock🤡 Him🙏, treat😔 Him🙏 shamefully😳, and✨ spit💦 on💯 Him🙏.

33📖 They’ll💯 whip🪢 Him🙏 and✨ kill💀 Him🙏, but✨ on💯 day📆 three🔢 He🙏 will💯 rise⬆️ again🔄.”

34📖 The💯 disciples👥 understood🧠 none🚫 of💯 it💯. The💯 meaning🧠 stayed🔒 hidden🫣 from💯 them🫂, and✨ they🫂 couldn’t🚫 grasp🧠 what💭 He🙏 was✨ saying🗣️.

35📖 As⏱️ Jesus🙏 got🚶 close📍 to✨ Jericho🏘️, a💯 blind🙈 man👤 was✨ sitting🪑 beside💯 the💯 road🛣️, begging🙏.

36📖 He👤 heard👂 a💯 crowd🫂 moving🚶 past💨 and✨ asked🗣️ what💭 was✨ going⏱️ on💭.

37📖 People👥 told🗣️ him👤, “Jesus🙏 of💯 Nazareth🏘️ is✨ passing🚶 by💯.”

38📖 The💯 man👤 shouted📣, “Jesus🙏, Son👑 of💯 David👑, have💛 mercy🤲 on💯 me🙏!”

39📖 The💯 people👥 leading🚶 the💯 way🛣️ rebuked⚠️ him👤, telling🗣️ him👤 to✨ be✨ quiet🤫. But✨ he👤 yelled📣 even💯 louder🔊: “Son👑 of💯 David👑, have💛 mercy🤲 on💯 me🙏!”

40📖 Jesus🙏 stopped🛑 and✨ ordered📣 them🫂 to✨ bring🫴 the💯 man👤 over💯. When⏱️ he👤 came🚶 close📍, Jesus🙏 asked🗣️,

41📖 “What❓ do💭 you🫵 want💭 Me🙏 to✨ do💪 for💯 you🫵?” The💯 man👤 answered🗣️, “Adonai🇮🇱, I🙏 want💭 to✨ see👀 again🔄.”

42📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Receive🤲 your💯 sight👀. Your💯 faith🙏 has💯 healed🩺 you🫵.”

43📖 Right⏱️ away✅ the💯 man👤 could💯 see👀 and✨ followed🚶 Jesus🙏, praising🙌 God🙏. Dem🇯🇲 all💯 praised🙌 God🙏 too💯.”

19📖 The💯 nobleman👑 told🗣️ him👤, ‘You🫵 take🫴 charge👑 of💯 five🔢 cities🏙️.’

21📖 I🙏 was✨ scared😨 of💯 you🫵—you’re🫵 strict⚖️, taking🫴 what💭 you🫵 didn’t🚫 put🫴 down⬇️ and✨ harvesting🌾 what💭 you🫵 didn’t🚫 plant🌱.’

48📖 but✨ couldn’t🚫 find🔎 a💯 way🛣️, because✨ all💯 the💯 people👥 were✨ locked👂 in💯, listening👂 to✨ every💯 word📣 He🙏 said🗣️.

1📖 On⏱️ one🔢 of💯 those💯 days📆, Jesus🙏 was✨ teaching📣 the💯 people👥 in💯 the💯 Temple🕍 and✨ preaching📣 the💯 Good✨ News📣 when⏱️ the💯 priests🕍, scribes📜, and✨ elders👴 walked🚶 up⬆️ to✨ Him🙏.

2📖 They🫂 pressed🗣️ Him🙏: “Tell🗣️ us🫂 straight💯—who❓ gave🎁 You🙏 the💯 authority👑 to✨ do💪 all💯 this💯?”

3📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “I’ll🙏 put💬 one🔢 question❓ to✨ you🫂 too💯. Tell🗣️ Me🙏:

4📖 John’s👤 baptism💧—did❓ it💯 come🚶 from💯 heaven🌌, or✨ from💯 people👥?”

5📖 They🫂 started🔄 reasoning🧠 it💯 out💭: “If✨ we🫂 say🗣️, ‘From💯 heaven🌌,’ He🙏 will💯 ask🗣️ why❓ we🫂 didn’t🚫 believe🙏 John👤.

6📖 But✨ if✨ we🫂 say🗣️, ‘From💯 people👥,’ the💯 crowd🫂 could💭 stone🪨 us🫂—they🫂 are✨ convinced✅ John👤 was✨ a💯 prophet📣.”

7📖 So✨ they🫂 answered🗣️, “We🫂 don’t🚫 know🧠 where📍 it💯 came🚶 from💯.”

8📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “Then⏱️ I🙏 won’t🚫 tell🗣️ you🫂 what💭 authority👑 I🙏 have💯 to✨ do💪 this💯 either💯.”

9📖 Then⏱️ Jesus🙏 told🗣️ the💯 people👥 this💯 parable📖: “A💯 man👤 planted🌱 a💯 Weinberg🇩🇪, rented🧾 it💯 out➡️ to✨ farmers🧑‍🌾, and✨ left🚶 the💯 country🌍 for💯 a💯 long💯 stretch⏳.

10📖 When⏱️ harvest🌾 time⏱️ came🚶, he👤 sent📨 a💯 servant🧑 to✨ collect🧺 his💯 share💰 of💯 the💯 fruit🍇. The💯 farmers🧑‍🌾 beat💥 him👤 and✨ sent📨 him👤 back↩️ with💯 nothing🫙.

11📖 The💯 owner👤 sent📨 another🔢 servant🧑. They🫂 beat💥 him👤 too💯, treated💥 him👤 shamefully😔, and✨ sent📨 him👤 away↩️ empty-handed🫙.

12📖 He👤 sent📨 a💯 third🔢 servant🧑. They🫂 wounded🤕 him👤 and✨ threw🫴 him👤 out🚪.

13📖 The💯 vineyard🍇 owner👤 said🗣️ to✨ himself👤, ‘What❓ do💭 I🙏 do💪 now⏱️? I’ll🙏 send📨 my💯 beloved💛 son👦. Maybe💭 they’ll💯 respect🙌 him👦.’

14📖 But✨ when⏱️ dem🇯🇲 saw👀 the💯 son👦, they🫂 huddled🫂 up⬆️: ‘That’s💯 the💯 heir👑! Let’s🫂 kill💀 him👦 and✨ make💰 the💯 inheritance💰 ours🫂.’

15📖 They🫂 threw🫴 him👦 out🚪 of💯 the💯 vineyard🍇 and✨ killed💀 him👦. So✨ what❓ will💭 the💯 owner👤 do💪 to✨ them🫂?

16📖 He👤 will💯 come🚶, destroy💥 those💯 farmers🧑‍🌾, and✨ hand🤲 the💯 vineyard🍇 to✨ others👥.” The💯 people👥 heard👂 that💯 and✨ cried📣, “May🚫 that💯 never💯 happen💥!”

17📖 Jesus🙏 looked👀 straight💯 at💯 them🫂: “Then⏱️ what💭 does✨ this💯 Scripture📜 mean💭: ‘The💯 stone🪨 the💯 builders🧑‍🏗️ rejected🚫 became🔄 the💯 chief🏛️ cornerstone🪨’?

18📖 Everyone💯 who✨ falls⬇️ on💯 that💯 stone🪨 will💯 break💥 apart🧩; if✨ it💯 falls⬇️ on💯 someone👤, it’ll💯 crush💥 them🫂 to✨ dust🌫️.”

19📖 The💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ scribes📜 wanted💭 to✨ grab🫴 Jesus🙏 right💯 then⏱️. They🫂 knew🧠 the💯 parable📖 was✨ aimed🎯 at💯 them🫂, but✨ feared😨 the💯 people👥.

20📖 They🫂 kept👀 watching👀 Jesus🙏 and✨ sent📨 spies🕵️ posing🎭 as✨ righteous🙏, hoping💭 to✨ trap🪤 Him🙏 in💯 His🙏 own💯 words🗣️ and✨ hand🤲 Him🙏 over💯 to✨ the💯 governor’s👑 authority👑.

21📖 The💯 spies🕵️ said🗣️, “Teacher👨‍🏫, we🫂 know🧠 You🙏 speak🗣️ and✨ teach📣 what’s💭 right✅. You🙏 don’t🚫 play🎭 favourites👥; Your🙏 parole🇫🇷 teaches📣 God’s🙏 way🛣️ truthfully✅.

23📖 Jesus🙏 saw👀 through💯 their💯 obi🇳🇬 and✨ caught🕵️ the💯 setup🪤. He🙏 said🗣️, “Why❓ are✨ you🫂 testing🧪 Me🙏?

25📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Then⏱️ give🎁 Caesar👑 what💭 belongs💯 to✨ Caesar👑, and✨ give🎁 God🙏 what💭 belongs💯 to✨ God🙏.”

26📖 They🫂 couldn’t🚫 catch🪤 Him🙏 saying🗣️ anything💯 wrong💔 in💯 front💯 of💯 the💯 people👥. His🙏 answer🗣️ amazed😲 them🫂, and✨ they🫂 went🤐 quiet🤫.

27📖 Then⏱️ some👥 Sadducees👥 came🚶 over💯—the💯 group👥 that✨ says🗣️ there🚫 is✨ no🚫 resurrection⬆️.

28📖 They🫂 asked🗣️, “Teacher👨‍🏫, Moses👤 wrote✍️ that✨ if✨ a💯 married💍 man👤 dies💀 childless🚫, his💯 brother👨 should💭 marry💍 the💯 widow👩 and✨ raise👶 children🧒 for💯 his💯 brother👨.

29📖 There✨ were✨ seven🔢 brothers👨‍👦. The💯 first🥇 married💍 a💯 woman👩, then⏱️ died💀 without🚫 children🧒.

30📖 The💯 second🔢 married💍 her👩 too💯, then⏱️ died💀 childless🚫.

31📖 Brother👨 number🔢 three🔢 did💪 the💯 same💯. All💯 seven🔢 took💍 her👩 as✨ wife👩 and✨ died💀 without🚫 children🧒.

32📖 After⏱️ all💯 of💯 them🫂, the💯 woman👩 died💀 too💯.

33📖 So✨ in💯 the💯 resurrection⬆️, whose❓ wife👩 will💭 she👩 be✨? All💯 seven🔢 had💍 her👩 as✨ their💯 wife👩.”

35📖 But✨ those👥 considered💭 worthy✅ of💯 the💯 coming🔜 age📆 and✨ resurrection⬆️ from💯 the💯 dead💀 don’t🚫 marry💍 or✨ get💍 married💍.

36📖 They🫂 can’t🚫 die💀 anymore💯. They’ll💯 be✨ like💯 angels👼 and✨ be✨ God’s🙏 ọmọ🇳🇬—children🧒 of💯 the💯 resurrection⬆️.

37📖 Moses👤 showed📜 that✨ the💯 dead💀 are✨ raised⬆️ at💯 the💯 bush🌿, when⏱️ he👤 called📣 the💯 Lord🙏 ‘the💯 God🙏 of💯 Abraham👨, Isaac👨, and✨ Jacob👨.’

38📖 He🙏 isn’t🚫 God🙏 of💯 the💯 dead💀, but✨ of💯 the💯 living💛—everyone💯 is✨ alive💛 to✨ Him🙏.”

39📖 Some👥 scribes📜 answered🗣️, “Teacher👨‍🏫, that💯 was✨ well✅ said🗣️.”

40📖 After⏱️ that💯, nobody💯 dared😨 to✨ ask🗣️ Him🙏 anything💯 else💯.

41📖 Then⏱️ Jesus🙏 asked🗣️ them🫂, “Why❓ do✨ people👥 say🗣️ the💯 Mashiach🇮🇱 is✨ David’s👑 son👦?

42📖 David👑 himself👤 says🗣️ in💯 Psalms📜: ‘The💯 Lord🙏 said🗣️ to✨ my💯 Lord👑,

43📖 “Sit🪑 at💯 My💯 right➡️ hand🤲 until⏱️ I🙏 put💪 Your💯 enemies⚔️ under💯 Your💯 feet🦶.”’

44📖 So✨ David👑 calls📣 the💯 Mashiach🇮🇱 Lord👑. How❓ can✨ He🙏 be✨ only💯 David’s👑 son👦?”

45📖 With💯 everyone👥 listening👂, Jesus🙏 told🗣️ His🙏 disciples👥,

46📖 “Watch👀 those💯 scribes📜 who✨ love💛 strolling🚶 in💯 long👘 robes👘, soaking🫶 up⬆️ greetings👋 in💯 the💯 marketplaces🏪, and✨ claiming🪑 the💯 best🏆 seats🪑 in💯 synagogues🕍 and✨ feasts🍽️.

47📖 They🫂 devour🫴 the💯 gida🇳🇬 of💯 widows👵 and✨ put🎭 on💯 a💯 show🎭 with💯 long⏳ prayers🙏. They’ll💯 face⚖️ a💯 heavier💥 judgment⚖️.”

# Luke 22 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Luke 22. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 Pesach🇮🇱 is✨ getting⏱️ close📍—the💯 Festival🎉 of💯 Unleavened🍞 Bread🍞 is✨ nearly💯 here📍.

2📖 The💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ scribes📜 were✨ plotting🧠 how💭 to✨ take🛑 Jesus🙏 out💀, because✨ they🫂 were✨ scared😨 of💯 what💭 the💯 people👥 might💭 do💥.

3📖 Then⏱️ Satan😈 entered🚪 Judas👤, the💯 one🔢 also✨ known🏷️ as✨ Iscariot👤, who🫂 was✨ counted🔢 with💯 the💯 twelve🔢.

4📖 Judas👤 went🚶 to✨ the💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ captains🛡️, talking🗣️ through💭 how💭 he👤 could💭 hand🤲 Jesus🙏 over💯 to✨ them🫂.

5📖 They🫂 were✨ pleased😄 and✨ agreed🤝 to✨ pay💰 him👤.

6📖 Judas👤 agreed✅ and✨ started🔎 hunting👀 for💯 a💯 chance⏱️ to✨ betray⚠️ Jesus🙏 when💭 the💯 crowd🫂 wasn’t🚫 around💯.

7📖 The💯 day📆 of💯 Unleavened🍞 Bread🍞 arrived🚶—the💯 Passover🕍 had💯 to✨ be✨ sacrificed🩸 that💯 day📆.

8📖 Jesus🙏 sent📨 Peter👤 and✨ John👤 with💯 a💯 clear✅ instruction🗣️: “Go🚶 get🍽️ the💯 Passover🕍 meal🍽️ ready✅ for💯 us🫂.”

9📖 They🫂 asked🗣️ Him🙏, “Where📍 do💭 You🙏 want💭 us🫂 to✨ set🛠️ it💯 up✅?”

10📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “When⏱️ you🫂 walk🚶 into💯 the💯 city🏙️, a💯 man👤 carrying🫴 a💯 pitcher🏺 of💯 ruwa🇳🇬 will💯 cross🤝 your💯 path📍. Follow🚶 him👤 into💯 the💯 house🏠 he👤 enters🚪.

11📖 Tell🗣️ the💯 homeowner👤, ‘The💯 Teacher👨‍🏫 asks🙏, “Where📍 is✨ the💯 guest💯 room🏠 where📍 I🙏 can💯 share🍽️ the💯 Passover🕍 meal🍽️ with💯 my💯 disciples👥?”’

12📖 He👤 will💯 point👀 you🫂 to✨ a💯 big💯 furnished🪑 upstairs⬆️ room🏠. Get💪 things💯 ready✅ there📍.”

13📖 Peter👤 and✨ John👤 went🚶, found🔎 it💯 just💯 as✨ Jesus🙏 had💯 said🗣️, and✨ got🍽️ the💯 Passover🕍 ready✅.

14📖 When⏱️ the💯 moment⏱️ came🚶, Jesus🙏 sat🪑 down⬇️ with💯 the💯 twelve🔢 apostles📣.

15📖 He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “I’ve🙏 been💯 looking💭 forward💯 to✨ eating🍽️ this💯 Passover🕍 with💯 you🫂 before⏱️ I🙏 suffer😣.

16📖 I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂, I🙏 won’t🚫 eat🍽️ it💯 again🔄 until⏱️ it💯 reaches✅ its💯 fulfilment💯 in💯 God’s🙏 Kingdom👑.”

17📖 Jesus🙏 took🫴 a💯 cup🍷, gave🙏 thanks🙏, and✨ said🗣️, “Take🫴 this💯 and✨ share🤲 it💯 around💯 among💯 yourselves🫵.

18📖 I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂, I🙏 won’t🚫 drink🥤 vinho🇵🇹 from💯 the💯 vine🌿 again🔄 until⏱️ God’s🙏 Kingdom👑 comes🚶.”

19📖 He🙏 took🫴 el🇪🇸 pan🇪🇸, gave🙏 thanks🙏, broke🫓 it💯, and✨ passed🫴 it💯 to✨ them🫂: “This💯 is✨ My🙏 body🧍, given🎁 for💯 you🫂. Do💪 this💯 to✨ remember🧠 Me🙏.”

21📖 But✨ look👀—the💯 hand🤲 of💯 the💯 one🔢 betraying⚠️ Me🙏 is✨ right💯 here📍 at💯 the💯 table🍽️ with💯 Mine🙏.

22📖 The💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 is✨ going🚶 the💯 way💯 God🙏 has💯 set🧭, but✨ heavy⚠️ consequences💥 await⏳ the💯 one🔢 who✨ betrays⚠️ Him🙏.”

23📖 They🫂 started🔄 asking🗣️ one🔢 another👥 which💭 of💯 them🫂 would💭 do💪 it💯.

24📖 Then⏱️ an💯 argument🗣️ broke💥 out💥 over💭 which👤 of💯 them🫂 counted🏆 as💯 the💯 greatest💯.

25📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Kings👑 of💯 other💯 nations🌍 throw👑 their💯 weight💯 around💥, and✨ rulers👑 love💛 being✨ tagged🏷️ ‘benefactors🎁.’

26📖 But✨ that’s💯 not🚫 the💯 way💯 for💯 you🫂. Let💭 the💯 greatest🏆 among💯 you🫂 become✨ like💯 the💯 youngest👶, and✨ let💭 the💯 leader👑 serve🤲.

27📖 Who❓ outranks💭 whom👤: the💯 person👤 at💯 the💯 table🍽️, or✨ the💯 one🔢 serving🍽️? Isn’t✨ it💯 the💯 one👤 seated🪑? But✨ I🙏 am💯 among💯 you🫂 as✨ the💯 one🔢 who✨ serves🤲.

28📖 You🫂 are✨ the💯 ones🔢 who✨ stayed🤝 with💯 Me🙏 through💯 all💯 My🙏 trials⚔️.

29📖 So✨ just💯 as✨ My🙏 Father🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏 a💯 Kingdom👑, I🙏 give🎁 one🔢 to✨ you🫂,

30📖 so✨ you🫂 can💯 eat🍽️ and✨ drink🥤 at💯 My🙏 table🍽️ in💯 My🙏 Kingdom👑 and✨ sit🪑 on💯 thrones👑 to✨ judge⚖️ the💯 twelve🔢 tribes👥 of💯 Israel✡️.”

31📖 The💯 Lord🙏 said🗣️, “Simon👤, Simon👤, listen👂—Satan😈 asked🙏 to✨ have💯 all💯 of💯 you🫂, so✨ he👿 could💭 sift🌾 you🫂 like💯 wheat🌾.

32📖 But✨ I🙏 prayed🙏 for💯 you🫵 so✨ your💯 faith🙏 wouldn’t🚫 give💥 out💔. And✨ when⏱️ you🫵 have💯 turned🔄 back↩️, strengthen💪 your💯 siblings👥; keep💪 their💯 obi🇳🇬 steady🪨.”

33📖 Peter👤 answered🗣️, “Lord🙏, I’m🙏 ready✅ to✨ go🚶 with💯 You🙏 to✨ prison🔒—even💯 to✨ death💀!”

34📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Peter👤, I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂, the💯 rooster🐓 won’t🚫 crow📣 today📆 before⏱️ you🫵 deny🚫 three🔢 times⏱️ that✨ you🫂 know🧠 Me🙏.”

35📖 Jesus🙏 asked🗣️ them🫂, “When⏱️ I🙏 sent📨 you🫂 out➡️ without🚫 a💯 wallet👛, bag🎒, or✨ sandals👡, did❓ you🫂 go🚫 without💯 anything💭?” They🫂 answered🗣️, “Nothing🚫.”

36📖 He🙏 said🗣️, “But✨ now⏱️, if✨ you🫂 have💯 a💯 wallet👛, take🫴 it💯; same💯 with💯 a💯 bag🎒. If✨ you🫂 don’t🚫 have💯 a💯 sword⚔️, sell💸 your💯 cloak👘 and✨ buy🛒 one🔢.

37📖 I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂, this💯 Scripture📜 has💯 to✨ be✨ fulfilled✅ in💯 Me🙏: ‘He👤 was✨ counted🔢 among💯 criminals🦹.’ What💭 was✨ written✍️ about💯 Me🙏 is✨ coming✅ true💯.”

38📖 They🫂 said🗣️, “Lord🙏, look👀—we🫂 have💯 two🔢 swords⚔️.” He🙏 replied🗣️, “That’s💯 enough💯.”

39📖 Jesus🙏 left🚪 and✨ went🚶, as💯 usual💯, to✨ the💯 Mount⛰️ of💯 Olives🫒. His🙏 disciples👥 went🚶 with💯 Him🙏.

40📖 When⏱️ He🙏 reached🚶 the💯 place📍, He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “Priez🇫🇷 so✨ you🫂 don’t🚫 get💥 pulled💥 into💯 temptation⚠️.”

41📖 Jesus🙏 stepped🚶 about💯 a💯 stone’s🪨 throw🤾 away↔️, knelt🧎 down⬇️, and✨ prayed🙏,

42📖 “Father🙏, if✨ You🙏 are✨ willing💭, take🫴 this💯 cup🍷 from💯 Me🙏. Still✨, let✨ Your🙏 will💭 happen✅—not🚫 Mine🙏.”

43📖 An💯 angel👼 from💯 heaven🌌 appeared✨ and✨ strengthened💪 Him🙏.

44📖 Jesus🙏 was✨ in💯 deep💯 agony😣, and✨ prayed🙏 even💯 more💯 intensely💯. Sweat💦 fell⬇️ from💯 Him🙏 onto💯 the💯 ground🌍 like💯 huge💯 drops💧 of💯 blood🩸.

45📖 When⏱️ He🙏 got⬆️ up⬆️ from💯 praying🙏, He🙏 found🔎 the💯 disciples👥 asleep😴, weighed💯 down⬇️ by💯 grief😢.

46📖 He🙏 said🗣️, “Why❓ are✨ you🫂 asleep😴? Get⬆️ up⬆️ and✨ pray🙏 so✨ you🫂 don’t🚫 get💥 pulled💥 into💯 temptation⚠️.”

47📖 While⏱️ Jesus🙏 was✨ still⏱️ speaking🗣️, a💯 crowd🫂 came🚶 into💯 view👀. Judas👤, one🔢 of💯 the💯 twelve🔢, was✨ leading👑 them🫂. He👤 came🚶 close📍 to✨ Jesus🙏 to✨ kiss💋 Him🙏.

48📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️ to✨ him👤, “Judas👤, are✨ you🫵 betraying⚠️ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 with💯 a💯 kiss💋?”

49📖 The💯 disciples👥 around💯 Jesus🙏 saw👀 what💭 was✨ about⏱️ to✨ happen💥 and✨ asked🗣️, “Lord🙏, should💭 we🫂 strike⚔️ with💯 our💯 swords⚔️?”

50📖 One🔢 of💯 them🫂 struck⚔️ the💯 servant🧑 of💯 the💯 high👑 priest🕍 and✨ cut✂️ off➡️ his💯 right➡️ ear👂.

51📖 But✨ Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Let🙏 Me🙏 do💪 this💯 much💯.” He🙏 touched🤲 the💯 servant’s🧑 ear👂 and✨ healed🩺 him👤.

52📖 Then⏱️ Jesus🙏 said🗣️ to✨ the💯 chief👑 priests🕍, Temple🕍 captains🛡️, and✨ elders👴 who✨ had💯 come🚶 for💯 Him🙏, “Did❓ you🫂 come🚶 out➡️ against⚔️ Me🙏 like💯 I🙏 was✨ a💯 robber🦹, carrying🫴 swords⚔️ and✨ clubs🪵?

53📖 I🙏 was✨ with💯 you🫂 in💯 the💯 Temple🕍 every💯 day📆, but✨ you🫂 never🚫 laid🫴 hands🤲 on💯 Me🙏. Now⏱️ this💯 is✨ your💯 hour⏱️, and✨ darkness🌑 has💯 its💯 power💪.”

54📖 They🫂 seized🫴 Jesus🙏, led🚶 Him🙏 away↩️, and✨ brought🫴 Him🙏 to✨ the💯 high👑 priest’s🕍 house🏠. Peter👤 followed🚶 from💯 far💯 behind↩️.

55📖 People👥 lit🔥 a💯 fire🔥 in💯 the💯 middle🎯 of💯 the💯 courtyard🏛️ and✨ sat🪑 around💯 it💯. Peter👤 sat🪑 with💯 dem🇯🇲 too💯.

56📖 A💯 servant👩 girl👧 spotted👀 Peter👤 sitting🪑 by💯 the💯 fire🔥. She👩 stared👀 at💯 him👤 and✨ said🗣️, “This💯 man👤 was✨ with💯 Jesus🙏 too💯.”

57📖 Peter👤 denied🚫 it💯: “Woman👩, I🙏 don’t🚫 know🧠 Him🙏.”

58📖 A💯 little💯 later⏱️, someone👤 else💯 saw👀 him👤 and✨ said🗣️, “You’re🫵 one🔢 of💯 them🫂 too💯!” Peter👤 answered🗣️, “Man👤, I’m🙏 not🚫!”

59📖 About💯 an💯 hour⏱️ later⏱️, another🔢 person👤 insisted🗣️, “For💯 real✅, this💯 man👤 was✨ with💯 Jesus🙏—he’s✨ a💯 Galilean🌍.”

60📖 Peter👤 said🗣️, “Man👤, I🙏 don’t🚫 know🧠 what💭 you🫵 are✨ talking🗣️ about💭!” Right⏱️ then⏱️, while⏱️ he👤 was✨ still⏱️ speaking🗣️, a💯 rooster🐓 crowed📣.

61📖 The💯 Lord🙏 turned🔄 and✨ looked👀 at💯 Peter👤. Peter👤 remembered🧠 the💯 Lord’s🙏 words📣: “Before⏱️ the💯 rooster🐓 crows📣, you’ll💯 deny🚫 Me🙏 three🔢 times⏱️.”

62📖 Peter👤 went🚶 outside🚪 and✨ wept😭 bitterlich🇩🇪.

63📖 The💯 men👥 guarding🛡️ Jesus🙏 mocked🤡 Him🙏 and✨ beat💥 Him🙏.

64📖 They🫂 blindfolded🫣 Him🙏, struck👊 His🙏 face😣, and✨ demanded🗣️, “Prophesy🔮! Who❓ hit👊 You🙏?”

65📖 They🫂 kept🔁 throwing🗣️ more💯 insults🤬 at💯 Him🙏.

66📖 At💯 dawn🌅, the💯 people’s👥 elders👴 gathered🫂 with💯 the💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ scribes📜, then⏱️ brought🫴 Jesus🙏 before💯 their💯 council🏛️.

67📖 They🫂 said🗣️, “If✨ You🙏 are✨ the💯 Mashiach🇮🇱, say🗣️ so💯.” Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “If✨ I🙏 tell🗣️ you🫂, you🫂 won’t🚫 believe🙏 Me🙏.

68📖 And✨ if✨ I🙏 ask🗣️ you🫂, you🫂 won’t🚫 answer🗣️ or✨ let💭 Me🙏 go🚪.

69📖 From💯 now⏱️ on➡️, the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 will💯 be✨ seated🪑 at💯 the💯 right➡️ hand🤲 of💯 God’s🙏 power💪.”

70📖 They🫂 all💯 asked🗣️, “So✨ You🙏 are✨ the💯 Son👑 of💯 God🙏?” Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “You🫂 say🗣️ it💯, because✨ I🙏 am💯.”

71📖 They🫂 said🗣️, “Why❓ do✨ we🫂 need🆘 more💯 witnesses👀? We🫂 heard👂 it💯 ourselves👥, straight💯 from💯 His🙏 own💯 mouth🗣️!”
Luke 23
# Luke 23 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Luke 23. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 The💯 whole💯 council🏛️ got⬆️ to✨ its💯 feet🧍 and✨ brought🫴 Jesus🙏 straight💯 to✨ Pilate👑.

2📖 They🫂 came🚶 at💯 Him🙏 with💯 charges⚠️: “We🫂 caught🔎 this💯 man👤 turning🔄 our💯 nación🇪🇸 upside🙃 down⬇️, telling🗣️ people👥 not🚫 to✨ pay💸 Caesar👑 taxes💰, and✨ claiming🗣️ He🙏 is✨ Mashiach🇮🇱—a💯 king👑.”

3📖 Pilate👑 asked🗣️ Jesus🙏, “Are✨ You🙏 the💯 King👑 of💯 the💯 Jews✡️?” Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “That’s💯 what💭 you🫵 say🗣️.”

4📖 Pilate👑 told🗣️ the💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ the💯 crowd🫂, “I🙏 see👀 no🚫 basis⚖️ to✨ charge⚠️ this💯 man👤.”

5📖 But✨ they🫂 kept🔁 pressing🗣️ it💯: “He👤 keeps🔁 stirring🌀 up⬆️ the💯 people👥 with💯 His🙏 teaching📣 all💯 across💯 Judea🌍, from💯 Galilee🌍 right💯 here📍.”

6📖 When⏱️ Pilate👑 heard👂 “Galilee🌍,” he👑 asked🗣️ whether❓ Jesus🙏 was✨ Galilean🌍.

7📖 Once⏱️ he👑 learned🧠 Jesus🙏 fell💯 under💯 Herod’s👑 jurisdiction⚖️, he👑 sent📨 Him🙏 to✨ Herod👑, who✨ was✨ also💯 in💯 Jerusalem🏙️ then⏱️.

8📖 Herod👑 was✨ thrilled😄 to✨ see👀 Jesus🙏. He👑 had💯 wanted💭 to✨ meet🤝 Him🙏 for💯 a💯 long💯 time⏳, had💯 heard👂 all💯 kinds💯 of💯 stories📣 about💯 Him🙏, and✨ hoped💭 to✨ see👀 a💯 miracle✨.

9📖 Herod👑 fired📣 question❓ after💯 question❓ at💯 Jesus🙏, but✨ Jesus🙏 gave🚫 him👑 no💯 answer🗣️.

10📖 The💯 chief👑 priests🕍 and✨ scribes📜 stood🧍 there📍, fiercely💥 laying🗣️ charges⚠️ against💯 Him🙏.

11📖 Herod👑 and✨ his💯 soldiers🪖 mocked🤡 and✨ humiliated😔 Jesus🙏. They🫂 dressed👘 Him🙏 in💯 a💯 fancy✨ robe👘, then⏱️ sent📨 Him🙏 back↩️ to✨ Pilate👑.

12📖 That💯 same💯 day📆, Herod👑 and✨ Pilate👑 became🤝 allies🤝. Before⏮️ that💯, they🫂 had💯 been✨ enemies⚔️.

13📖 Pilate👑 called📣 the💯 chief👑 priests🕍, rulers👑, and✨ people👥 together🫂.

14📖 He👑 told🗣️ them🫂, “You🫂 brought🫴 this💯 man👤 to✨ me👑 saying🗣️ He🙏 was✨ turning🔄 the💯 people👥 against⚔️ you🫂. I🙏 questioned🗣️ Him🙏 right💯 here📍 in💯 front💯 of💯 you🫂, and✨ found🔎 no🚫 basis⚖️ for💯 your💯 charges⚠️.

15📖 Herod👑 didn’t🚫 find🔎 anything💯 either💯—that’s💯 why💭 he👑 sent📨 Him🙏 back↩️ to✨ us🫂. Jesus🙏 hasn’t🚫 done💪 anything💯 deserving⚖️ death💀.

16📖 So✨ I👑 will💯 have💯 Him🙏 disciplined⚖️, then⏱️ release🔓 Him🙏.”

17📖 At💯 the💯 feast🎉, Pilate👑 had💯 to✨ release🔓 one🔢 prisoner🔒 to✨ them🫂.

18📖 But✨ the💯 whole💯 crowd🫂 shouted📣 together🫂, “Get🚪 this💯 man👤 out🚫 of💯 here📍! Release🔓 Barabbas👤 to✨ us🫂!”

19📖 Barabbas👤 had💯 been✨ locked🔒 up⬆️ for💯 starting⚔️ a💯 revolt⚔️ in💯 the💯 city🏙️ and✨ committing🔪 murder💀.

20📖 Pilate👑 spoke🗣️ to✨ the💯 crowd🫂 again🔄, hoping💭 to✨ release🔓 Jesus🙏.

21📖 But✨ they🫂 yelled📣 back🔊, “Crucify✝️ Him🙏! Crucify✝️ Him🙏!”

22📖 Pilate👑 asked🗣️ them🫂 for💯 a💯 third🔢 time⏱️, “Why❓? What💭 evil⚠️ has✨ He🙏 done💪? I🙏 found🔎 no🚫 capital⚖️ crime⚠️ in💯 Him🙏. I👑 will💯 have💯 Him🙏 disciplined⚖️, then⏱️ release🔓 Him🙏.”

23📖 But✨ they🫂 kept🔁 demanding📣 it💯, voices🗣️ loud🔊, pushing🫸 for💯 Jesus🙏 to✨ be✨ crucified✝️. The💯 crowd🫂 kept💯 shouting📣, and✨ so✨ did💯 the💯 chief👑 priests🕍—their💯 combined🫂 voices🗣️ won⚖️ out💥.

24📖 Pilate👑 ruled⚖️ that✨ their💯 demand🗣️ had💯 to✨ be✨ carried✅ out💯.

25📖 He👑 released🔓 the💯 man👤 they🫂 wanted💭, jailed🔒 for💯 revolt⚔️ and✨ murder💀, and✨ handed🤲 Jesus🙏 over💯 to✨ their💯 will💭.

26📖 As⏱️ they🫂 led🚶 Jesus🙏 away↩️, soldiers🪖 grabbed🫴 Simon👤 of💯 Cyrene🌍, who✨ was✨ coming🚶 in💯 from💯 the💯 countryside🌾. They🫂 laid🫴 the💯 Kreuz🇩🇪 on💯 him👤 to✨ carry🫴 behind🔙 Jesus🙏.

27📖 A💯 huge💯 crowd🫂 followed🚶 Jesus🙏, including💯 women👩 mourning😢 and✨ lamenting😭 Him🙏.

28📖 Jesus🙏 turned🔄 to✨ them🫂 and✨ said🗣️, “Daughters👩 of💯 Jerusalem🏙️, don’t🚫 cry😭 for💯 Me🙏. Cry😭 for💯 yourselves🫵 and✨ your💯 children🧒.

29📖 The💯 days📆 are✨ coming⏳ when💭 people👥 will💯 say🗣️, ‘Blessed✨ are✨ the💯 women👩 who✨ never🚫 had👶 children👶, the💯 wombs🤰 that✨ never🚫 carried🤰 babies👶, and✨ the💯 breasts🍼 that✨ never🚫 nursed🍼.’

30📖 Then⏱️ they🫂 will💯 beg🙏 the💯 mountains⛰️, ‘Fall⬇️ on💯 us🫂!’ and✨ call📣 to✨ the💯 hills⛰️, ‘Cover🫣 us🫂!’

31📖 If✨ people👥 do💪 this💯 while⏱️ the💯 tree🌳 is✨ green🌿, what❓ will💭 happen💥 when💭 it💯 dries🍂 up⬇️?”

32📖 Two🔢 other💯 criminals🔒 were✨ being✨ led🚶 out➡️ to✨ be✨ executed⚖️ alongside💯 Jesus🙏.

33📖 When⏱️ they🫂 reached🚶 the💯 place📍 called🏷️ “The💯 Skull💀,” they🫂 crucified✝️ Jesus🙏 there📍, with💯 one🔢 criminal🔒 on💯 His🙏 right➡️ and✨ the💯 other💯 on💯 His🙏 left⬅️.

34📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Father🙏, pardonne🇫🇷 dem🇯🇲—for✨ they🫂 don’t🚫 know🧠 what💭 they’re✨ doing💪.” The💯 soldiers🪖 split➗ His🙏 roupas🇵🇹 among💯 themselves🫂 by💯 casting🎲 lots🎲.

35📖 The💯 people👥 stood🧍 watching👀. Their💯 leaders👑 sneered🤡, “He👤 saved🛟 others👥. Let✨ Him👤 save🛟 Himself👤, if✨ He🙏 is✨ God’s🙏 Mashiach🇮🇱, God’s🙏 chosen✅ One🔢!”

36📖 The💯 soldiers🪖 mocked🤡 Jesus🙏 too💯. They🫂 came🚶 close📍 and✨ offered🫴 Him🙏 vinegar🥤.

37📖 They🫂 said🗣️, “If✨ You’re🙏 the💯 King👑 of💯 the💯 Jews✡️, save🛟 Yourself🫵!”

38📖 A💯 sign🪧 was✨ written✍️ above⬆️ Him🙏 in💯 Greek🇬🇷, Latin🏛️, and✨ Hebrew✡️: “THIS💯 IS✨ THE💯 KING👑 OF💯 THE💯 JEWS✡️.”

39📖 One🔢 criminal🔒 hanging✝️ there📍 insulted🤬 Jesus🙏: “Aren’t❓ You🙏 the💯 Christ👑? Save🛟 Yourself🫵 and✨ us🫂!”

40📖 But✨ the💯 other💯 criminal🔒 checked🛑 him👤: “Don’t🚫 you🫵 even💯 fear😨 God🙏? We🫂 are✨ under💯 the💯 same💯 sentence⚖️.

41📖 We🫂 deserve⚖️ what💭 we’re🫂 getting💯 for💯 what💭 we🫂 did💪. But✨ this💯 man👤 did🚫 nothing💯 wrong💔.”

42📖 Then⏱️ he👤 said🗣️ to✨ Jesus🙏, “Lord🙏, remember🧠 me👤 when⏱️ You🙏 come🚶 into💯 Your🙏 Kingdom👑.”

43📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “I’m🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵 for💯 certain✅: today📆 you’ll💯 be✨ with💯 Me🙏 in💯 Paradise🌿.”

44📖 It💯 was✨ around💯 noon🌞, and✨ darkness🌑 covered🫣 the💯 whole💯 land🌍 until⏱️ about💯 three🔢 in💯 the💯 afternoon🌆.

45📖 The💯 sun☀️ went🌑 dark🌑, and✨ the💯 Temple🕍 curtain🧵 tore💥 right💯 down⬇️ the💯 tsakiya🇳🇬.

John 1
# John 1 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, John 1. Draft only; not validated for release.

1📖 From⏱️ jump🏃, the💯 Verbe🇫🇷 was✨ already💯 there📍—with💯 God🙏, and✨ that💯 Verbe🇫🇷 was✨ God🙏.

2📖 That💯 same💯 Verbe🇫🇷 was✨ with💯 God🙏 from⏱️ jump🏃.

3📖 Everything💯 came🚶 through💯 Him🙏; without🚫 Him🙏, nothing💯 got✅ made🏗️. Zero💯.

4📖 Life💛 was✨ in💯 Him🙏, and✨ that💯 life💛 was✨ the💯 Licht🇩🇪 for💯 everyone💯.

5📖 The💯 Licht🇩🇪 keeps🔆 shining🌟 in💯 the💯 darkness🌑, and✨ the💯 darkness🌑 couldn't🚫 overcome💥 it💯.

6📖 God🙏 sent📨 a💯 man👤 named🏷️ John👤.

7📖 John👤 came🚶 to✨ speak🗣️ as💯 a💯 witness👀 about💯 the💯 light💡, so✨ everyone💯 could💯 believe🙏 through💯 him👤.

8📖 John👤 wasn't🚫 the💯 light💡 himself👤. He🙏 came🚶 to✨ point👉 people👥 toward💯 the💯 light💡.

9📖 The💯 true✅ light💡, the💯 luz🇵🇹 that✨ shines🌟 on💯 everyone💯, was✨ coming🚶 into💯 the💯 world🌍.

10📖 He🙏 was✨ in💯 the💯 world🌍, the💯 world🌍 was✨ made🏗️ through💯 Him🙏, but✨ the💯 world🌍 didn't🚫 recognise👀 Him🙏.

11📖 He🙏 came🚶 to✨ His🙏 own💯 people👥, but✨ dem🇯🇲 didn't🚫 welcome👋 Him🙏.

18📖 No🚫 one👤 has💯 ever💯 seen👀 God🙏. The💯 only💯 Son👑, close💛 to✨ the💯 Father's🙏 heart💛, has💯 made🔦 Him🙏 known🧠.

27📖 He's✨ the💯 One🔢 coming🚶 after💯 me🙏, ranking🏆 ahead💯 of💯 me🙏. I'm🙏 not🚫 worthy✨ to✨ untie🔓 even💯 His🙏 sandal👡 strap🪢.”

29📖 The💯 next🔜 day📆, John👤 saw👀 Jesus🙏 coming🚶 toward💯 him👤 and✨ said🗣️, “Look👀! The💯 Lamb🐑 of💯 God🙏 who✨ takes🫴 away🚪 the💯 sin💔 of💯 the💯 world🌍!

30📖 This💯 is✨ the💯 One🔢 I🙏 meant💭 when⏱️ I🙏 said🗣️, ‘Someone👤 is✨ coming🚶 after💯 me🙏 who✨ is✨ greater💯 than💯 me🙏, because✨ He🙏 existed✨ before💯 me🙏.’

31📖 I🙏 didn't🚫 know🧠 who💭 He🙏 was✨, but✨ I🙏 came🚶 baptising💧 with💯 water💧 so✨ He🙏 could💯 be✨ revealed🔦 to✨ Israel✡️.”

32📖 John👤 testified🗣️, “I🙏 saw👀 the💯 Spirit🕊️ come⬇️ down⬇️ from💯 heaven🌌 like💯 a💯 dove🕊️ and✨ stay🛬 on💯 Him🙏.

33📖 I🙏 didn't🚫 know🧠 Him🙏 before⏱️, but✨ the💯 One🔢 who✨ sent📨 me👤 to✨ baptise💧 with💯 water💧 told🗣️ me👤, ‘When⏱️ you🫵 see👀 the💯 Mmụọ🇳🇬 come⬇️ down⬇️ and✨ stay🛬 on💯 someone👤, that's💯 the💯 One🔢 who✨ baptises💧 with💯 the💯 Holy🕊️ Spirit🕊️.’

34📖 I've🙏 seen👀 this💯 and✨ testified🗣️: He🙏 is✨ God's🙏 Son👑.”

35📖 The💯 next🔜 day📆, John👤 was✨ standing🧍 there📍 again🔄 with💯 two🔢 of💯 his💯 disciples👥.

36📖 He👤 looked👀 at💯 Jesus🙏 walking🚶 by💯 and✨ said🗣️, “Look👀! The💯 Lamb🐑 of💯 God🙏!”

37📖 The💯 two🔢 disciples👥 heard👂 him👤 and✨ started🔄 following🚶 Jesus🙏.

38📖 Jesus🙏 turned🔄 around💯, saw👀 them🫂 following🚶, and✨ asked🗣️, “What❓ are✨ you🫂 looking🔎 for💭?” They🫂 said🗣️, “Rabbi👨‍🏫—which✨ means💭 Teacher👨‍🏫—where📍 are✨ You🙏 staying🏠?”

39📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Come🚶 and✨ see👀.” They🫂 went🚶 with💯 Him🙏, saw👀 where📍 He🙏 stayed🏠, and✨ spent⏱️ that💯 day📆 with💯 Him🙏. It💯 was✨ about💯 four🔢 in💯 the💯 afternoon🌇.

40📖 Andrew👤 was✨ one🔢 of💯 the💯 two🔢 who✨ heard👂 John👤 and✨ followed🚶 Jesus🙏. He👤 was✨ brother👨 to✨ Simon👤 Peter👤.

41📖 Andrew👤 first🥇 found🔎 his💯 brother👨 Simon👤 and✨ told🗣️ him👤, “We🫂 found🔎 the💯 Mashiach🇮🇱!”—the💯 Christ👑.

42📖 Andrew👤 brought🫴 Simon👤 to✨ Jesus🙏. Jesus🙏 looked👀 at💯 him👤 and✨ said🗣️, “You're🫵 Simon👤, son👦 of💯 Jonah👤. You'll💯 be✨ called🏷️ Cephas👤”—which💭 means💭 Peter👤, “Rock🪨.”

43📖 The💯 next🔜 day📆, Jesus🙏 decided✅ to✨ go🚶 to✨ Galilee🌍. He🙏 found🔎 Philip👤 and✨ said🗣️, “Follow🚶 me🙏.”

44📖 Philip👤 was✨ from💯 Bethsaida🏘️, the💯 same💯 town🏙️ as💯 Andrew👤 and✨ Peter👤.

45📖 Philip👤 found🔎 Nathanael👤 and✨ said🗣️, “We🫂 found🔎 the💯 One🔢 Moses👤 wrote✍️ about💭 in💯 the💯 Law📜, the💯 One🔢 the💯 prophets📣 wrote✍️ about💭 too💯—Jesus🙏 of💯 Nazareth🏘️, son👦 of💯 Joseph👤.”

46📖 Nathanael👤 said🗣️, “Can❓ anything💯 good✨ come🚶 from💯 Nazareth🏘️?” Philip👤 replied🗣️, “Come🚶 and✨ see👀.”

47📖 Jesus🙏 saw👀 Nathanael👤 coming🚶 and✨ said🗣️, “Here📍 is✨ a💯 real✅ Israelite✡️—no🚫 deception🎭 in💯 him👤!”

48📖 Nathanael👤 asked🗣️, “How❓ do✨ You🙏 know🧠 me👤?” Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Before⏱️ Philip👤 called📣 you🫵, I🙏 saw👀 you🫵 under💯 the💯 fig🌳 tree🌳.”

49📖 Nathanael👤 replied🗣️, “Rabbi👨‍🏫, You🙏 are✨ God's🙏 Son👑! You🙏 are✨ Israel's✡️ King👑!”

50📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “You🫵 believe🙏 just💯 because✨ I🙏 told🗣️ you🫵, ‘I🙏 saw👀 you🫵 under💯 the💯 fig🌳 tree🌳’❓ You'll💯 see👀 things💯 even💯 greater💯 than💯 that💯!”

51📖 He🙏 told🗣️ him👤, “I'm🙏 telling🗣️ you🫵 the💯 truth✅: you'll💯 see👀 heaven🌌 opened🔓 and✨ God's🙏 angels👼 going⬆️ up⬆️ and✨ coming⬇️ down⬇️ on💯 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤.”

1📖 A💯 Pharisee✡️ named🏷️ Nicodemus👤 was✨ there💯, one🔢 of💯 the💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👑.

2📖 He👤 came🚶 to💯 Jesus🙏 at🌙 night🌃 and✨ said🗣️, “Rabbi👨‍🏫, we🫂 know🧠 You🙏 came🚶 from💯 God🙏 as💯 a💯 teacher📚. Nobody🫂 can✨ pull✨ off💯 these💯 signs✨ You🙏 do✅ unless🚫 God🙏 is✨ with💯 Him🙏. For💯 real💯.”

4📖 Nicodemus👤 said🗣️, “How❓ can✨ an💯 older👴 person🫂 be✨ born🌱? They🫂 can't🚫 go🚶 back🔙 into💯 their💯 mom's👩 womb🤰 and✨ be✨ born🌱 again🔄, right❓?”

1📖 Jesus🙏 found🔎 out💨 the💯 Pharisees✡️ had✨ heard👂 He🙏 was✨ making✨ and✨ baptising💧 more📈 disciples🫂 than💯 John👤.

3📖 So✨ He🙏 left🚶 Judea📍 and✨ headed🚶 back🔙 toward💯 Galilee📍.

4📖 Samaria📍 was✨ on💯 the💯 route🛣️, so✨ He🙏 had✨ to✨ pass🚶 through💯 it💯.

5📖 He🙏 reached🚶 a💯 Samaritan👥 town🏘️ called🏷️ Sychar📍, close📍 to💯 the💯 land🌍 Jacob👤 had✨ given🎁 his💯 son👤 Joseph👤.

6📖 Jacob's👤 well💧 was✨ there📍. Jesus🙏 was✨ worn😮‍💨 out💨 from💯 the💯 trip🛣️, so✨ He🙏 sat🪑 down⬇️ beside💯 it💯. It✨ was✨ about💯 ọ̀sán🇳🇬.

7📖 A💯 Samaritan👩 woman👩 came🚶 to💯 draw🥣 mmiri🇳🇬. Jesus🙏 told🗣️ her👩, “Give🎁 me🙏 a💯 drink💧.”

8📖 His🙏 disciples🫂 had✨ gone🚶 into💯 town🏘️ to✨ grab🛍️ food🍞.

9📖 The💯 Samaritan👩 woman👩 said🗣️, “Hold🛑 up⏸️—You're🙏 Jewish✡️, and✨ You're🙏 asking🗣️ me👩, a💯 Samaritan👩, for💯 a💯 drink💧?” Jewish✡️ people🫂 and✨ Samaritans👥 dem🇯🇲 didn't🚫 mix🔀 like💯 that💯.

10📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “If💯 you🫵 knew🧠 God's🙏 gift🎁, and✨ who❓ is✨ asking🗣️ you🫵 for💯 a💯 drink💧, you'd🫵 ask🗣️ Him🙏 instead💯. He🙏 would✨ give🎁 you🫵 living💛 água🇵🇹.”

11📖 She👩 said🗣️, “Sir🙏, You🙏 don't🚫 even💯 have✨ a💯 bucket🪣, and✨ this💯 well💧 is✨ deep⬇️. Where📍 are✨ You🙏 getting💯 this💯 living💛 water💧 from💯?”

12📖 “You🙏 saying🗣️ You🙏 are✨ greater📈 than💯 our🫂 ancestor👴 Jacob👤? He👤 gave🎁 us🫂 this💯 well💧 and✨ drank🥤 from💯 it💯 himself👤, same💯 as✨ his💯 kids🫂 and✨ livestock🐄.”

13📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Everyone🫂 who✨ drinks🥤 this💯 water💧 will✨ be✨ thirsty🥵 again🔄.”

14📖 “But✨ anyone🫂 who✨ drinks🥤 the💯 ruwa🇳🇬 I🙏 give🎁 will✨ never🚫 thirst🥵 again🔄. My🙏 water💧 becomes🔄 a💯 spring💦 inside💯 them🫂, bubbling💦 up⬆️ into💯 eternal♾️ vida🇪🇸.”

15📖 The💯 woman👩 said🗣️, “Sir🙏, give🎁 me👩 that💯 water💧! Then⏱️ I👩 won't🚫 get🥵 thirsty💧 or✨ have✨ to✨ keep🔄 trekking🚶 all💯 the💯 way💯 here📍 to✨ draw🥣 it💯.”

16📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ her👩, “Go🚶 call📣 your🫵 husband👨 and✨ come🚶 back🔙.”

17📖 The💯 woman👩 replied🗣️, “I👩 don't🚫 have💯 a💯 husband👨.”

18📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “You're🫵 right✅ to✨ say🗣️ you🫵 don't🚫 have💯 a💯 husband👨. You've🫵 had✨ five🔢 husbands👨, and✨ the💯 man👤 you're🫵 with💯 now💯 isn't🚫 your🫵 husband👨. You🫵 told🗣️ it💯 straight💯.”

19📖 She👩 said🗣️, “Sir🙏, I👩 can✨ tell👀 You're🙏 a💯 prophet📣.”

20📖 “Our🫂 ancestors👴 worshipped🙏 on💯 this💯 mountain⛰️, but✨ you🫵 Jewish✡️ people🫂 say🗣️ Jerusalem📍 is✨ where📍 everyone🫂 should💯 worship🙏.”

21📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ her👩, “Believe💯 Me🙏, the💯 time⏱️ is✨ coming🚶 when⏱️ worship🙏 of💯 the💯 Father🙏 won't🚫 be✨ locked🔒 to✨ this💯 mountain⛰️ or✨ Jerusalem📍.”

22📖 “You🫵 worship🙏 what💯 you🫵 don't🚫 know🧠. We🫂 worship🙏 what💯 we🫂 know🧠, because✨ salvation🛟 comes🚶 through💯 the💯 Jewish✡️ people🫂.”

23📖 “The💯 time⏱️ is✨ here📍 now💯 when⏱️ real💯 worshippers🙏 will✨ worship🙏 the💯 Father🙏 in💯 spirit🕊️ and✨ Wahrheit🇩🇪 truth✅. The💯 Father🙏 is✨ looking🔎 for💯 people🫂 who✨ worship🙏 like💯 that💯.”

24📖 “God🙏 is✨ Ruach🇮🇱, so✨ His🙏 worshippers🙏 must💯 worship🙏 in💯 spirit🕊️ and✨ vérité🇫🇷.”

25📖 The💯 woman👩 said🗣️, “I👩 know🧠 Mashiach🇮🇱 is✨ coming🚶—the💯 One👤 called🏷️ Christ👑. When⏱️ He🙏 comes🚶, He🙏 will✨ explain🗣️ everything💯 to💯 us🫂.”

26📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ her👩, “I🙏 am✨ He🙏—the💯 One👤 talking🗣️ with💯 you🫵 right💯 now💯.”

27📖 Just⏱️ then💯, His🙏 disciples🫂 came🚶 back🔙. They🫂 were✨ shocked😲 He🙏 was✨ talking🗣️ with💯 a💯 woman👩, but✨ nobody🫂 asked🗣️, “What❓ do💯 You🙏 want💯?” or✨ “Why❓ are✨ You🙏 talking🗣️ with💯 her👩?”

28📖 The💯 woman👩 left🚶 her💯 water💧 jar🏺, went🚶 into💯 town🏘️, and✨ told🗣️ the💯 people🫂,

29📖 “Come🚶 see👀 a💯 man👤 who✨ told🗣️ me👩 everything💯 I've👩 ever💯 done✅. Could❓ He🙏 be✨ the💯 Christ👑?”

30📖 So✨ they🫂 left🚶 town🏘️ and✨ headed🚶 toward💯 Jesus🙏.

31📖 Meanwhile⏱️, His🙏 disciples🫂 kept🔁 urging🗣️ Him🙏, “Rabbi👨‍🏫, please🙏 eat🍽️ something💯.”

32📖 But✨ He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “I🙏 have✨ food🍞 to✨ eat🍽️ that💯 you🫂 don't🚫 know🧠 about💯.”

33📖 The💯 disciples🫂 wondered🤔 to💯 each💯 other🫂, “Did❓ somebody🫂 bring🚶 Him🙏 food🍞 already💯?”

34📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “My🙏 food🍞 is✨ doing✅ the💯 will💯 of💯 the💯 One👤 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏 and✨ finishing🏁 His🙏 work🛠️.”

35📖 “You🫵 say🗣️, ‘Four🔢 months📅 till💯 harvest🌾.’ But✨ look👀 up⬆️ and✨ check👀 the💯 fields🌾—they🫂 are✨ already💯 white🤍 and✨ ready✅ to✨ harvest🌾!”

36📖 “The💯 reaper🌾 gets💯 paid💰 and✨ gathers🧺 fruit🍎 for💯 eternal♾️ life🌱, so✨ the💯 planter🌱 and✨ reaper🌾 can✨ celebrate🎉 together🫂.”

37📖 “That💯 saying🗣️ hits🎯: one🔢 person🫂 plants🌱, another🔢 reaps🌾.”

38📖 “I🙏 sent📨 you🫂 to✨ reap🌾 what💯 you🫂 didn't🚫 work🛠️ for💯. Other👥 people🫂 did✨ the💯 hard💪 work🛠️, and✨ you🫂 stepped🚶 into💯 what💯 they🫂 built🏗️.”

39📖 Many💯 Samaritans👥 from💯 that💯 town🏘️ trusted💯 Jesus🙏 because✨ the💯 woman👩 told🗣️ them🫂, “He🙏 told🗣️ me👩 everything💯 I've👩 done✅.”

40📖 When⏱️ the💯 Samaritans👥 reached🚶 Him🙏, they🫂 begged🙏 Him🙏 to✨ stay🏠 with💯 them🫂. He🙏 stayed🏠 there💯 two🔢 days📅.

41📖 Even💯 more📈 people🫂 believed💯 because✨ of💯 His🙏 words🗣️.

42📖 They🫂 told🗣️ the💯 woman👩, “We🫂 believe💯 now💯, not🚫 just💯 because✨ you🫵 told🗣️ us🫂. We🫂 heard👂 Him🙏 ourselves🫂 and✨ know🧠 He🙏 really💯 is✨ the💯 Christ👑, the💯 Saviour🛟 of💯 the💯 world🌍.”

43📖 After⏱️ two🔢 days📅, Jesus🙏 left🚶 and✨ continued🚶 into💯 Galilee📍.

44📖 Jesus🙏 Himself🙏 had✨ said🗣️ a💯 prophet📣 doesn't🚫 get💯 honour🏅 in💯 his💯 own🏠 country🌍.

45📖 When⏱️ He🙏 arrived🚶 in💯 Galilee📍, people🫂 welcomed👋 Him🙏. They🫂 had✨ seen👀 everything💯 He🙏 did✅ in💯 Jerusalem📍 during💯 the💯 feast🎉, and✨ they'd🫂 gone🚶 there💯 too💯.

46📖 Jesus🙏 returned🔄 to💯 Cana📍 in💯 Galilee📍, where📍 He🙏 had✨ turned🔄 water💧 into💯 wine🍷. A💯 royal👑 official🧑‍💼 had✨ a💯 sick🤒 son👦 back🏠 in💯 Capernaum📍.

47📖 When⏱️ he👨‍💼 heard👂 Jesus🙏 had✨ come🚶 from💯 Judea📍 to💯 Galilee📍, he👨‍💼 went🚶 to✨ Him🙏 and✨ begged🙏 Him🙏 to✨ come🚶 heal🩺 his💯 son👦, who✨ was✨ close📉 to💯 dying💀.

48📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Unless🚫 you🫂 see👀 signs✨ and✨ miracles🔥, you🫂 won't🚫 believe💯.”

49📖 The💯 official👨‍💼 pleaded🙏, “Sir🙏, please🙏 come🚶 down⬇️ before⏱️ my👨‍💼 child👦 dies💀.”

50📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him👨‍💼, “Go🚶—your🫵 son👦 is✨ alive💛.” The💯 man👨‍💼 trusted💯 the💯 word🗣️ Jesus🙏 spoke🗣️ and✨ headed🚶 home🏠.

51📖 While⏱️ he👨‍💼 was✨ on💯 the💯 way🛣️ down⬇️, his💯 servants🧑‍🍳 met🤝 him👨‍💼 and✨ said🗣️, “Your🫵 child👦 is✨ alive💛!”

52📖 He👨‍💼 asked🗣️ what⏱️ time📅 his💯 son👦 started🔛 getting📈 better💪. They🫂 said🗣️, “Yesterday📅 at💯 one🕐 in💯 the💯 afternoon☀️, the💯 fever🤒 left🚪 him👦.”

53📖 The💯 father👨‍💼 realised🧠 that💯 was✨ exactly💯 when⏱️ Jesus🙏 had✨ told🗣️ him👨‍💼, “Your🫵 son👦 is✨ alive💛.” Then⏱️ he👨‍💼 and✨ his💯 whole💯 household🏠 believed💯.

54📖 This💯 was✨ the💯 second🥈 sign✨ Jesus🙏 did✅ after⏱️ coming🚶 from💯 Judea📍 into💯 Galilee📍.
John 5
# John 5 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, John 5. The verse 4 pool tradition is included as printed, with its source note that NU omits John 5:3b–4. WEBP page was temporarily inaccessible; text and count checked against its British Edition counterpart. Draft only; not release-validated.

1📖 Later⏱️, Jesus🙏 went🚶 up⬆️ to💯 Jerusalem📍 for💯 a💯 Jewish✡️ festival🎉.

2📖 Near📍 the💯 Sheep🐑 Gate🚪 in💯 Jerusalem📍 was✨ a💯 pool💧 called🏷️ Bethesda🇮🇱 in💯 Hebrew🕎, with💯 five🔢 covered🏛️ walkways🏛️.

3📖 A💯 huge📈 crowd🫂 of💯 sick🤒, blind👀, disabled🦽, and✨ paralysed🛏️ people🫂 lay🛏️ there💯. Dem🇯🇲 waited⏳ for💯 the💯 pool💧 omi🇳🇬 to✨ move🌊.

4📖 Sometimes⏱️ an💯 angel👼 went🚶 down⬇️ into💯 the💯 pool💧 and✨ stirred🌀 the💯 Wasser🇩🇪. Whoever🫂 stepped👣 in💧 first🥇 after💯 it✨ moved🌊 got✨ healed🩺 of💯 whatever💯 illness🤒 they🫂 had💯.

5📖 One🔢 man👤 there💯 had✨ been✨ sick🤒 for💯 thirty-eight🔢 years📅.

6📖 Jesus🙏 saw👀 him👤 lying🛏️ there💯 and✨ knew🧠 he'd👤 been✨ ill🤒 a💯 long⏳ time📅. He🙏 asked🗣️, “Do❓ you🫵 want💭 to✨ get💪 well🩺?”

7📖 The💯 sick🤒 man👤 answered🗣️, “Sir🙏, I👤 have✨ nobody🫂 to✨ help🫶 me👤 into💯 the💯 pool💧 when⏱️ the💯 ruwa🇳🇬 moves🌊. While⏱️ I👤 try🏃 to✨ get💧 there💯, someone🫂 else🔄 gets💧 in💯 ahead💯 of💯 me👤.”

8📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him👤, “Get⬆️ up🚶, pick🛏️ up⬆️ your🫵 mat🛏️, and✨ walk🚶.”

9📖 Instantly⚡, the💯 man👤 was✨ healed🩺. He👤 picked🛏️ up⬆️ his💯 mat🛏️ and✨ walked🚶. That💯 day📅 was✨ Sabbath🕎.

10📖 So✨ the💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👥 told🗣️ the💯 healed🩺 man👤, “It's💯 Sabbath🕎! Carrying🛏️ that💯 mat🛏️ isn't🚫 allowed🚫.”

11📖 He👤 replied🗣️, “The💯 One👤 who✨ healed🩺 me👤 told🗣️ me👤, ‘Pick🛏️ up⬆️ your🫵 mat🛏️ and✨ walk🚶.’”

12📖 They🫂 asked🗣️ him👤, “Who❓ told🗣️ you🫵, ‘Pick🛏️ up⬆️ your🫵 mat🛏️ and✨ walk🚶’?”

13📖 The💯 healed🩺 man👤 didn't🚫 know🧠 who👤 it✨ was✨. Jesus🙏 had✨ slipped🚶 away↩️ into💯 the💯 crowd🫂 there💯.

14📖 Later⏱️, Jesus🙏 found🔎 him👤 in💯 the💯 temple⛪ and✨ said🗣️, “Look👀 at💯 you🫵—you're🫵 well🩺 now💯. Stop🛑 sinning💔, so✨ nothing💯 worse📉 hits💥 you🫵.”

15📖 The💯 man👤 went🚶 off➡️ and✨ told🗣️ the💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👥 Jesus🙏 had✨ healed🩺 him👤.

16📖 That's💯 why💭 they🫂 persecuted💢 Jesus🙏 and✨ wanted💭 Him🙏 dead💀: He🙏 was✨ doing✅ this💯 on💯 Sabbath🕎.

17📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️ them🫂, “My🙏 Father🙏 is✨ still💯 at💯 work🛠️, and✨ so✨ am✨ I🙏.”

18📖 That💯 made💥 the💯 leaders👥 even💯 more📈 determined💯 to✨ kill💀 Him🙏. He🙏 had✨ not🚫 only💯 broken💔 Sabbath🕎 rules📜; He🙏 called🗣️ God🙏 His🙏 own💯 Father🙏, making✨ Himself🙏 equal⚖️ with💯 God🙏.

19📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “I'm💯 telling🗣️ you🫵 the💯 truth💯: the💯 Son👑 can't🚫 act✅ on💯 His🙏 own💯. He🙏 does✅ what💯 He🙏 sees👀 the💯 Father🙏 doing✅. Whatever💯 the💯 Father🙏 does✅, the💯 Son👑 does✅ too💯.”

20📖 “The💯 Father🙏 loves💛 the💯 Son👑 and✨ shows👀 Him🙏 everything💯 He🙏 does✅. He🙏 will✨ show👀 Him🙏 even💯 greater📈 works🛠️, and✨ you'll🫵 be✨ amazed😲.”

21📖 “Just💯 as✨ the💯 Father🙏 raises⬆️ the💯 dead⚰️ and✨ gives🎁 them🫂 ndụ🇳🇬, the💯 Son👑 gives🎁 life🌱 to✨ whoever🫂 He🙏 chooses✅.”

22📖 “The💯 Father🙏 judges⚖️ nobody🫂; He🙏 has✨ entrusted📥 all💯 judgement⚖️ to✨ the💯 Son👑.”

23📖 “That💯 way🛣️ everyone🫂 can✨ honour🏅 the💯 Son👑 just💯 as✨ they🫂 honour🏅 the💯 Father🙏. Anyone🫂 who✨ won't🚫 honour🏅 the💯 Son👑 won't🚫 honour🏅 the💯 Father🙏 who✨ sent📨 Him🙏.”

24📖 “Listen👂 close🔎: whoever🫂 hears👂 My🙏 word🗣️ and✨ trusts💯 the💯 One👤 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏 has✨ eternal♾️ vida🇪🇸. They🫂 won't🚫 face⚖️ judgement⚖️; they've✨ crossed🚶 from💯 death💀 into💯 life🌱.”

25📖 “The💯 time⏱️ is✨ coming🚶—actually💯, it's✨ here📍—when⏱️ the💯 dead⚰️ will✨ hear👂 the💯 Son👑 of💯 God's🙏 voice🗣️. Those🫂 who✨ hear👂 will✨ live🌱.”

26📖 “The💯 Father🙏 has✨ life🌱 in💯 Himself🙏, and✨ He🙏 gave🎁 the💯 Son👑 life🌱 in💯 Himself🙏 too💯.”

27📖 “He🙏 also💯 gave🎁 Him🙏 authority⚖️ to✨ judge⚖️, because✨ He🙏 is✨ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤.”

28📖 “Don't🚫 be✨ shocked😲: the💯 time⏱️ is✨ coming🚶 when⏱️ everyone🫂 in💯 the💯 tombs⚰️ will✨ hear👂 His🙏 voice🗣️.”

29📖 “They🫂 will✨ come🚶 out💨: those🫂 who✨ did✅ good💛 to✨ a💯 resurrection🌅 of💯 life🌱, and✨ those🫂 who✨ did✅ evil💔 to✨ a💯 resurrection🌅 of💯 judgement⚖️.”

30📖 “I🙏 can't🚫 do✅ anything💯 independently💯. I🙏 judge⚖️ as💯 I🙏 hear👂, and✨ My🙏 judgement⚖️ is✨ right✅. I🙏 don't🚫 chase🏃 My🙏 own💯 will💭; I🙏 follow🚶 the💯 will💭 of💯 My🙏 Father🙏 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏.”

31📖 “If💯 I🙏 testify🗣️ about💯 Myself🙏, My🙏 testimony🗣️ doesn't🚫 stand✅ on💯 its💯 own💯.”

32📖 “Someone🫂 else🔄 testifies🗣️ about💯 Me🙏, and✨ I🙏 know🧠 their💯 testimony🗣️ is✨ true💯.”

33📖 “You🫵 sent📨 people🫂 to💯 John👤, and✨ he👤 testified🗣️ to💯 the💯 truth💯.”

34📖 “I🙏 don't🚫 need💯 human🫂 testimony🗣️, but✨ I'm💯 saying🗣️ this💯 so✨ you🫵 can✨ be✨ saved🛟.”

35📖 “John👤 was✨ a💯 lamp🪔 burning🔥 bright✨, and✨ for💯 a💯 little💯 while⏱️ you🫵 were✨ happy😊 in💯 his💯 lumière🇫🇷.”

36📖 “But✨ I🙏 have✨ testimony🗣️ bigger📈 than💯 John's👤: the💯 work🛠️ the💯 Father🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏 to✨ finish🏁—the💯 very💯 trabalho🇵🇹 I'm💯 doing✅—proves✅ the💯 Father🙏 sent📨 Me🙏.”

37📖 “The💯 Father🙏 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏 has✨ testified🗣️ about💯 Me🙏 Himself🙏. You've🫵 never🚫 heard👂 His🙏 voice🗣️ or✨ seen👀 His🙏 form👤.”

38📖 “His🙏 word🗣️ isn't🚫 living🌱 in💯 you🫵, because✨ you🫵 don't🚫 trust💯 the💯 One👤 He🙏 sent📨.”

39📖 “You🫵 search🔎 the💯 Scriptures📜, thinking🧠 they🫂 hold🔐 eternal♾️ life🌱. But✨ those💯 very💯 writings🖋️ point👉 to💯 Me🙏.”

40📖 “Still💯, you🫵 refuse🚫 to✨ come🚶 to💯 Me🙏 for💯 life🌱.”

41📖 “I🙏 don't🚫 chase🏃 praise🏅 from💯 people🫂.”

42📖 “But✨ I🙏 know🧠 you🫂: God's🙏 love💛 isn't🚫 living🌱 in💯 your🫵 hearts🫀.”

43📖 “I🙏 came🚶 in💯 My🙏 Father's🙏 name📛, and✨ you🫵 don't🚫 welcome👋 Me🙏. If💯 someone🫂 else🔄 comes🚶 using💯 their💯 own💯 name📛, you'll🫵 welcome👋 them🫂.”

44📖 “How❓ can✨ you🫵 believe💯 while✨ collecting🏅 applause👏 from💯 each💯 other🫂, but✨ not🚫 seeking🔎 the💯 honour🏅 that✨ comes🚶 from💯 the💯 only💯 God🙏?”

45📖 “Don't🚫 think🧠 I'm💯 about💯 to✨ accuse⚖️ you🫵 to💯 the💯 Father🙏. Moses📜 is✨ the💯 one👤 accusing⚖️ you🫵—the💯 one👤 you🫵 put💯 your💯 hope💛 in💯.”

46📖 “If💯 you🫵 really💯 believed💯 Moses📜, you'd🫵 believe💯 Me🙏, because✨ he👤 wrote🖋️ about💯 Me🙏.”

47📖 “But✨ if💯 you🫵 don't🚫 believe💯 his💯 writings🖋️, how❓ will✨ you🫵 believe💯 My🙏 words🗣️?”

66📖 After⏱️ this💯, many💯 of💯 His🙏 disciples🫂 turned🔄 back🔙 and✨ stopped🚫 following🚶 Him🙏.

69📖 “We🫂 believe💯 and✨ know🧠 You🙏 are✨ the💯 Christ👑, the💯 Son👑 of💯 the💯 living💛 God🙏.”

71📖 He🙏 was✨ talking🗣️ about💯 Judas👤, Simon👤 Iscariot's👤 son👦, who❓ would✨ betray💔 Him🙏, even💯 though✨ he👤 was✨ one🔢 of💯 the💯 twelve🔢.

# John 7 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, John 7. WEBP page was unavailable; text and count checked against its British Edition counterpart. Draft only; not release-validated.

1📖 After⏱️ that💯, Jesus🙏 kept✨ moving🚶 around💯 Galilee📍. He🙏 wasn't🚫 walking🚶 through💯 Judea📍 because✨ the💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👥 wanted💭 Him🙏 dead💀.

2📖 The💯 Jewish✡️ Feast🎉 of💯 Booths🏕️ was✨ getting⏳ close📍.

3📖 His🙏 brothers👨 told🗣️ Him🙏, “Leave🚶 here📍 and✨ go🚶 into💯 Judea📍, so✨ Your🙏 disciples🫂 can✨ see👀 what💯 You're🙏 doing✅.”

4📖 “Nobody🫂 does✅ things💯 in💯 secret🤫 when⏱️ they🫂 want💭 the💯 world🌍 to✨ know🧠 them🫂. If💯 You🙏 do✅ all💯 this💯, show👀 Yourself🙏 to✨ el🇪🇸 mundo🇪🇸.”

5📖 Even💯 His🙏 own💯 brothers👨 didn't🚫 believe💯 in💯 Him🙏.

6📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “My🙏 àkókò🇳🇬 hasn't🚫 come💨 yet⏳, but✨ your🫂 time⏱️ is✨ always💯 ready✅.”

7📖 “The💯 world🌍 can't🚫 hate💢 you🫂, but✨ it✨ hates💢 Me🙏 because✨ I🙏 tell🗣️ the💯 truth💯 about💯 its💯 evil💔 moves👐.”

8📖 “You🫂 go🚶 up⬆️ to💯 the💯 feast🎉. I'm🙏 not🚫 going🚶 to✨ this💯 festival🎉 yet⏳—My🙏 time⏱️ hasn't🚫 reached💯 its💯 moment⏱️.”

9📖 After⏱️ saying🗣️ that💯, He🙏 stayed🏠 in💯 Galilee📍.

10📖 But✨ after⏱️ His🙏 brothers👨 left🚶 for💯 the💯 feast🎉, He🙏 went🚶 too💯—secretamente🇵🇹.

11📖 The💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👥 were✨ looking🔎 for💯 Him🙏 at💯 the💯 festival🎉, asking🗣️, “Where📍 is✨ He🙏?”

12📖 The💯 crowd🫂 kept🔁 whispering🤫 about💯 Him🙏. Some💯 said🗣️, “He's🙏 a💯 good💛 man👤.” Others🫂 said🗣️, “Nah🚫, He's🙏 misleading🌀 the💯 crowd🫂.”

13📖 Still💯, nobody🫂 spoke🗣️ openly📣 about💯 Him🙏 because✨ dem🇯🇲 were✨ scared😨 of💯 the💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👥.

14📖 Halfway⏳ through💯 the💯 festival🎉, Jesus🙏 went🚶 into💯 the💯 temple⛪ and✨ started🔛 teaching📚.

15📖 The💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👥 were✨ stunned😲: “How❓ does✨ He🙏 know🧠 Scripture📜 when⏱️ He🙏 never🚫 went🎓 through💯 formal🎓 schooling📚?”

16📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “My🙏 teaching📚 isn't🚫 mine👤; it✨ comes🚶 from💯 the💯 One👤 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏.”

17📖 “Anyone🫂 who✨ truly💛 wants💭 to✨ do✅ God's🙏 will💭 will✨ know🧠 whether❓ My🙏 teaching📚 comes🚶 from💯 God🙏 or✨ whether❓ I'm🙏 speaking🗣️ on💯 My🙏 own💭.”

18📖 “People🫂 speaking🗣️ for💯 themselves🫂 chase🏃 their💯 own💯 glory🏆. But✨ someone🫂 seeking🔎 the💯 glory🏆 of💯 the💯 One👤 who✨ sent📨 them🫂 is✨ eziokwu🇳🇬; there's🚫 nothing💯 fake🚫 in💯 them🫂.”

19📖 “Didn't❓ Moses📜 give🎁 you🫂 the💯 law📜? Yet✨ none🚫 of💯 you🫂 keeps🔒 it💯. Why❓ are✨ you🫂 trying🏃 to✨ kill💀 Me🙏?”

20📖 The💯 crowd🫂 answered🗣️, “You're🙏 possessed😈! Who❓ is✨ trying🏃 to✨ kill💀 You🙏?”

21📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “I🙏 did✅ one🔢 work🛠️, and✨ you're🫂 all💯 shocked😲.”

22📖 “Moses📜 gave🎁 you🫂 circumcision✂️—not🚫 that✨ it✨ started🔛 with💯 Moses📜; it✨ came🚶 from💯 the💯 ancestors👴. So✨ you🫂 circumcise✂️ a💯 boy👦 even💯 on💯 Sabbath🕎.”

23📖 “If💯 a💯 boy👦 can✨ be✨ circumcised✂️ on💯 Sabbath🕎 to✨ keep🔒 the💯 law📜 Moses📜 gave🎁, why❓ are✨ you🫂 angry😡 with💯 Me🙏 for💯 making✨ a💯 whole💯 man👤 well🩺 on💯 Sabbath🕎?”

24📖 “Stop🛑 judging⚖️ by💯 the💯 surface👀. Urteilt🇩🇪 gerecht🇩🇪.”

25📖 Some💯 people🫂 from💯 Jerusalem📍 said🗣️, “Isn't❓ this💯 the💯 man👤 they🫂 want💭 to✨ kill💀?”

26📖 “Look👀, He's🙏 speaking🗣️ in💯 public📣 and✨ nobody🫂 is✨ stopping🛑 Him🙏. Did❓ the💯 leaders👥 figure🧠 out💯 He🙏 really💯 is✨ the💯 Christ👑?”

27📖 “But✨ we🫂 know🧠 where📍 this💯 man👤 comes🚶 from💯. When⏱️ the💯 Christ👑 shows👀 up⬆️, nobody🫂 will✨ know🧠 where📍 He🙏 came🚶 from💯.”

28📖 Jesus🙏 called📣 out💨 while✨ teaching📚 in💯 the💯 temple⛪, “You🫂 know🧠 Me🙏 and✨ where📍 I'm🙏 from💯? I🙏 didn't🚫 come🚶 on💯 My🙏 own💭. The💯 One👤 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏 is✨ true✅—and✨ you🫂 don't🚫 know🧠 Him🙏.”

29📖 “I🙏 know🧠 Him🙏 because✨ I🙏 come🚶 from💯 Him🙏, and✨ He🙏 sent📨 Me🙏.”

30📖 They🫂 tried🏃 to✨ grab🫳 Him🙏, but✨ nobody🫂 laid🫳 a💯 hand✋ on💯 Him🙏 because✨ His🙏 time⏱️ hadn't🚫 arrived⏳.

31📖 Still💯, many💯 in💯 the💯 crowd🫂 believed💯 in💯 Him🙏. They🫂 said🗣️, “When⏱️ the💯 Christ👑 comes🚶, will✨ He🙏 do✅ more📈 signes🇫🇷 than💯 this💯 man👤?”

32📖 The💯 Pharisees✡️ heard👂 the💯 crowd🫂 whispering🤫 this💯 about💯 Jesus🙏, so✨ the💯 chief👑 priests⛪ and✨ Pharisees✡️ sent📨 officers👮 to✨ arrest🔒 Him🙏.

33📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “I'll🙏 be✨ with💯 you🫂 a💯 little💯 longer⏳, then⏱️ I'll🙏 go🚶 to💯 the💯 One👤 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏.”

34📖 “You'll🫂 look🔎 for💯 Me🙏 but✨ won't🚫 find🔎 Me🙏. You🫂 can't🚫 come🚶 where📍 I🙏 am✨.”

35📖 The💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👥 wondered🤔 among💯 themselves🫂, “Where📍 is✨ He🙏 planning📋 to✨ go🚶 that✨ we🫂 can't🚫 find🔎 Him🙏? Is✨ He🙏 going🚶 to💯 the💯 Jewish✡️ communities🌍 among💯 the💯 Greeks🇬🇷 to✨ teach📚 Greek🇬🇷 people🫂?”

36📖 “What❓ does💯 He🙏 mean💭 by💯 ‘You'll🫂 look🔎 for💯 Me🙏 but✨ won't🚫 find🔎 Me🙏’ and✨ ‘You🫂 can't🚫 come🚶 where📍 I🙏 am✨’?”

37📖 On💯 the💯 feast's🎉 biggest📈, final🏁 day📅, Jesus🙏 stood🧍 and✨ called📣 out💨, “Anyone🫂 who's💯 thirsty🥵, come🚶 to💯 Me🙏 and✨ drink🥤!”

38📖 “As💯 Scripture📜 says🗣️, rivers🌊 of💯 living💛 ruwa🇳🇬 will✨ flow🌊 from💯 inside💯 whoever🫂 believes💯 in💯 Me🙏.”

39📖 He🙏 was✨ talking🗣️ about💯 the💯 Ruach🇮🇱, whom✨ believers🫂 in💯 Him🙏 would✨ receive📥. The💯 Spirit🕊️ had✨ not🚫 yet⏳ been✨ given🎁 because✨ Jesus🙏 had✨ not🚫 yet⏳ been✨ glorified🌟.

40📖 When⏱️ people🫂 in💯 the💯 crowd🫂 heard👂 Him🙏, some💯 said🗣️, “This💯 is✨ de🇵🇹 fato🇵🇹 the💯 Prophet📣.”

41📖 Others🫂 said🗣️, “He's🙏 the💯 Christ👑.” But✨ some💯 asked🗣️, “What❓, does✨ the💯 Christ👑 come🚶 from💯 Galilee📍?”

42📖 “Doesn't❓ Scripture📜 say🗣️ the💯 Christ👑 comes🚶 from💯 David's👑 family👨‍👩‍👧‍👦, from💯 Bethlehem📍, David's👑 hometown🏘️?”

43📖 So✨ the💯 crowd🫂 split🔀 over💯 Him🙏.

44📖 Some💯 wanted💭 to✨ arrest🔒 Him🙏, but✨ nobody🫂 touched🤚 Him🙏.

45📖 The💯 officers👮 returned🔙 to💯 the💯 chief👑 priests⛪ and✨ Pharisees✡️, who✨ asked🗣️, “Why❓ didn't🚫 you🫂 bring🚶 Him🙏 in💯?”

46📖 The💯 officers👮 answered🗣️, “Nobody🫂 has✨ ever💯 spoken🗣️ like💯 this💯 man👤!”

47📖 The💯 Pharisees✡️ snapped🗣️, “What❓, did💯 He🙏 fool🌀 you🫂 too💯?”

48📖 “Has✨ any💯 leader👑 or✨ Pharisee✡️ believed💯 in💯 Him🙏?”

49📖 “This💯 crowd🫂 doesn't🚫 know🧠 the💯 law📜—they🫂 are✨ cursed💢!”

50📖 Nicodemus👤, the💯 one👤 who✨ had✨ visited🚶 Jesus🙏 at🌙 night🌃 and✨ was✨ one🔢 of💯 them🫂, spoke🗣️ up⬆️.

51📖 He👤 asked🗣️, “Does💯 our🫂 law📜 judge⚖️ someone🫂 before⏱️ hearing👂 them🫂 directly💯 and✨ checking🔎 what💯 they🫂 did✅?”

52📖 They🫂 replied🗣️, “Are❓ you🫵 from💯 Galilee📍 too💯? Look🔎 it💯 up📚: no🚫 prophet📣 comes🚶 from💯 Galilee📍.”

53📖 Then⏱️ everybody🫂 went🚶 home🏠.
John 8
# John 8 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, John 8. The source prints John 8:1–11 while noting textual uncertainty; these verses are retained in sequence as the source presents them. Draft only; not release-validated.

1📖 But✨ Jesus🙏 went🚶 to💯 the💯 Mount⛰️ of💯 Olives🫒.

2📖 Early🌅 next⏱️ morning🌅, He🙏 returned🚶 to💯 the💯 temple⛪. Everyone🫂 came🚶 to💯 Him🙏; He🙏 sat🪑 down⬇️ and✨ began🔛 teaching📚.

3📖 The💯 law📜 teachers📚 and✨ Pharisees✡️ brought🚶 in💨 a💯 woman👩 caught👀 in💯 adultery💔. They🫂 stood🧍 her👩 right✅ in💯 the💯 middle💯.

4📖 They🫂 told🗣️ Him🙏, “Teacher👨‍🏫, this💯 woman👩 was✨ caught👀 in💯 the💯 act💔 of💯 adultery💔.”

5📖 “Our🫂 law📜 says🗣️ Moses📜 commanded🗣️ us🫂 to✨ stone🪨 women👩 in💯 this💯 situation💯. So💭 what❓ do💯 You🙏 say🗣️?”

6📖 They🫂 were✨ baiting🪤 Him🙏, hoping💭 to✨ get📝 something💯 they🫂 could✨ use⚖️ against💯 Him🙏. Jesus🙏 bent⬇️ down⬇️ and✨ wrote🖋️ on💯 the💯 ground🌍 with💯 His🙏 finger☝️.

7📖 They🫂 kept🔁 pressing💢 Him🙏. He🙏 stood⬆️ up⬆️ and✨ said🗣️, “Whoever🫂 among💯 you🫂 has💯 no🚫 sin💔, you🫵 be✨ the💯 first🥇 to✨ throw🪨 a💯 stone🪨 at💯 her👩.”

8📖 Then⏱️ He🙏 bent⬇️ down⬇️ again🔄 and✨ wrote🖋️ on💯 the💯 ground🌍.

9📖 When⏱️ they🫂 heard👂 Him🙏, their💯 conscience🫀 called📣 them🫂 out💯. Dem🇯🇲 walked🚶 away↩️ one🔢 by💯 one🔢, starting🔛 with💯 the💯 oldest👴. Jesus🙏 was✨ left🚶 alone👤 with💯 the💯 woman👩 still💯 there💯.

10📖 Jesus🙏 stood⬆️ and✨ asked🗣️ her👩, “Woman👩, where📍 did💯 your🫵 accusers⚖️ go🚶? Did❓ nobody🫂 condemn⚖️ you🫵?”

11📖 She👩 replied🗣️, “Nobody🫂, Lord🙏.” Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “I🙏 don't🚫 condemn⚖️ you🫵 either💯. Go🚶 your🫵 way🛣️. From💯 now⏱️ on💯, sin💔 no🚫 more💯.”

12📖 Jesus🙏 spoke🗣️ to💯 them🫂 again🔄: “I🙏 am✨ the💯 Licht🇩🇪 of💯 the💯 mundo🇵🇹. Whoever🫂 follows🚶 Me🙏 won't🚫 walk🚶 in💯 duhu🇳🇬; they'll🫂 have✨ the💯 light💡 of💯 life🌱. Whoever🫂 follows🚶 Me🙏 won't🚫 walk🚶 in💯 darkness🌑; they'll🫂 have✨ the💯 light💡 that✨ gives🎁 life🌱.”

13📖 The💯 Pharisees✡️ said🗣️ to💯 Him🙏, “You're🫵 testifying🗣️ about💯 Yourself🙏. That💯 testimony🗣️ doesn't🚫 count✅.”

14📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “Even💯 if💯 I🙏 testify🗣️ about💯 Myself🙏, My🙏 testimony🗣️ is✨ vérité🇫🇷. I🙏 know🧠 where📍 I🙏 came🚶 from💯 and✨ where📍 I'm🙏 going🚶. You🫂 don't🚫 know🧠 either💯.”

15📖 “You🫂 judge⚖️ by💯 human🫀 standards📏. I🙏 don't🚫 judge⚖️ anyone🫂 like💯 that💯.”

16📖 “And✨ if💯 I🙏 do✅ judge⚖️, My🙏 judgement⚖️ is✨ true✅ because✨ I'm🙏 not🚫 alone👤—the💯 Father🙏 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏 is✨ with💯 Me🙏.”

17📖 “Your🫂 own💯 law📜 says🗣️ the💯 testimony🗣️ of💯 two🔢 people🫂 is✨ valid✅.”

18📖 “I'm🙏 one🔢 witness👀 for💯 Myself🙏, and✨ the💯 Father🙏 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏 is✨ the💯 other💯 witness👀.”

19📖 They🫂 asked🗣️, “Where📍 is✨ Your🙏 Father🙏?” Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “You🫂 don't🚫 know🧠 Me🙏 or✨ My🙏 Father🙏. If💯 you🫂 knew🧠 Me🙏, you'd🫂 know🧠 My🙏 Father🙏 too💯.”

20📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️ these💯 things💯 while⏱️ teaching📚 in💯 the💯 temple⛪ treasury💰. Nobody🫂 arrested🔒 Him🙏, because✨ His🙏 time⏱️ hadn't🚫 come⏳ yet💯.

21📖 He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂 again🔄, “I'm🙏 going🚶 away↩️. You'll🫂 look🔎 for💯 Me🙏, but✨ you'll🫂 die💀 in💯 your🫂 sins💔. You🫂 can't🚫 come🚶 where📍 I'm🙏 going🚶.”

22📖 The💯 Jewish✡️ people🫂 said🗣️, “Is✨ He🙏 going🚶 to✨ kill💀 Himself🙏? He🙏 said🗣️, ‘You🫂 can't🚫 come🚶 where📍 I'm🙏 going🚶.’”

23📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “You🫂 are✨ from💯 below⬇️; I'm🙏 from💯 above⬆️. You're🫂 from💯 this💯 world🌍; I'm🙏 not🚫 from💯 this💯 world🌍.”

24📖 “I🙏 told🗣️ you🫂, you'll🫂 die💀 in💯 your🫂 sins💔 unless🚫 you🫂 believe💯 that✨ Ani🇮🇱 hu🇮🇱. If💯 you🫂 don't🚫 believe💯, you'll🫂 die💀 in💯 your🫂 sins💔.”

25📖 They🫂 asked🗣️, “Who❓ are✨ You🙏?” Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “I've🙏 been💯 telling🗣️ you🫂 from💯 the💯 start🔛.”

26📖 “I🙏 have✨ plenty💯 to✨ say🗣️ and✨ judge⚖️ about💯 you🫂. The💯 One👤 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏 is✨ true✅, and✨ I🙏 tell🗣️ the💯 world🌍 what💯 I🙏 heard👂 from💯 Him🙏.”

27📖 They🫂 didn't🚫 realise🧠 He🙏 was✨ talking🗣️ about💯 the💯 Father🙏.

28📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “When⏱️ you🫂 lift⬆️ up⬆️ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤, then⏱️ you'll🫂 know🧠 Ani🇮🇱 hu🇮🇱. I🙏 do✅ nothing💯 on💯 My🙏 own💭; I🙏 speak🗣️ what💯 My🙏 Father🙏 taught📚 Me🙏.”

29📖 “The💯 One👤 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏 is✨ with💯 Me🙏. The💯 Father🙏 hasn't🚫 left🚫 Me🙏 alone👤, because✨ I🙏 always💯 do✅ what💯 pleases💛 Him🙏.”

30📖 While⏱️ He🙏 was✨ saying🗣️ this💯, many💯 people🫂 believed💯 in💯 Him🙏.

31📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ the💯 Jewish✡️ people🫂 who✨ believed💯 Him🙏, “If💯 you🫂 stay🏠 in💯 My🙏 word🗣️, then⏱️ you're🫂 truly💯 My🙏 disciples🫂.”

32📖 “You'll🫂 know🧠 the💯 vérité🇫🇷, and✨ the💯 truth💯 will✨ set🔓 you🫂 free🕊️.”

33📖 They🫂 answered🗣️, “We're🫂 descendants👨‍👩‍👧‍👦 of💯 Abraham👴. We've🫂 never🚫 been✨ enslaved🔗 to💯 anybody🫂. How❓ can✨ You🙏 say🗣️ we'll🫂 be✨ set🔓 free🕊️?”

34📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “I'm🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂 the💯 truth💯: everyone🫂 who✨ sins💔 becomes✨ enslaved⛓️ to💯 sin💔.”

35📖 “An💯 ohu🇳🇬 doesn't🚫 stay🏠 in💯 the💯 household🏠 forever♾️, but✨ an💯 ọmọ🇳🇬 belongs🏠 there💯 forever♾️.”

36📖 “So✨ if💯 the💯 Son👑 sets🔓 you🫂 free🕊️, you're🫂 really💯 free🕊️.”

37📖 “I🙏 know🧠 you're🫂 descendants👨‍👩‍👧‍👦 of💯 Abraham👴, but✨ you're🫂 trying🏃 to✨ kill💀 Me🙏 because✨ My🙏 word🗣️ has✨ no🚫 room🏠 in💯 you🫂.”

38📖 “I🙏 say🗣️ what💯 I🙏 saw👀 with💯 My🙏 Father🙏, and✨ you🫂 do✅ what💯 you🫂 heard👂 from💯 your🫂 father👤.”

39📖 They🫂 replied🗣️, “Abraham👴 is✨ our🫂 father👴.” Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “If💯 you🫂 were✨ children🫂 of💯 Abraham👴, you'd🫂 do✅ the💯 kind💯 of💯 things💯 Abraham👴 did✅.”

40📖 “But✨ now💯 you're🫂 trying🏃 to✨ kill💀 Me🙏, a💯 man👤 who✨ told🗣️ you🫂 the💯 verdad🇪🇸 I🙏 heard👂 from💯 God🙏. Abraham👴 never🚫 did✅ that💯.”

41📖 “You're🫂 doing✅ the💯 kind💯 of💯 things💯 your🫂 father👤 does✅.” They🫂 said🗣️, “We🫂 weren't🚫 born🌱 from💯 sexual💋 sin💔. We🫂 have✨ one🔢 Father🙏—God🙏.”

42📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “If💯 God🙏 were✨ your🫂 Father🙏, you'd🫂 love💛 Me🙏. I🙏 came🚶 from💯 God🙏 and✨ am✨ here📍 now💯. I🙏 didn't🚫 come🚶 on💯 My🙏 own💭; He🙏 sent📨 Me🙏.”

43📖 “Why❓ can't🚫 you🫂 understand🧠 what💯 I'm🙏 saying🗣️? Because✨ you🫂 can't🚫 hear👂 My🙏 word🗣️.”

44📖 “Your🫂 father👤 is✨ the💯 devil😈, and✨ you🫂 want💭 to✨ do✅ what💯 he👤 wants💭. He👤 was✨ a💯 murderer🔪 from💯 the💯 start🔛; he👤 never🚫 stood🧍 in💯 truth💯 because✨ there's🚫 no🚫 truth💯 in💯 him👤. When⏱️ he👤 lies🤥, he👤 speaks🗣️ from💯 his💯 own💭 nature🫀: he's✨ a💯 liar🤥 and✨ the💯 source🌱 of💯 lies🤥.”

45📖 “But✨ I🙏 tell🗣️ the💯 truth💯, and✨ you🫂 still🚫 don't🚫 believe💯 Me🙏.”

46📖 “Who❓ here💯 can✨ prove🧾 I🙏 sinned💔? If💯 I🙏 tell🗣️ the💯 truth💯, why❓ don't🚫 you🫂 believe💯 Me🙏?”

47📖 “Whoever🫂 belongs💯 to💯 God🙏 hears👂 God's🙏 words🗣️. You🫂 don't🚫 hear👂 because✨ you🫂 don't🚫 belong💯 to💯 God🙏.”

48📖 The💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👥 answered🗣️ Him🙏, “We🫂 were✨ right💯 to✨ call🏷️ You🙏 a💯 Samaritan👤 with💯 a💯 demon😈!”

49📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “I🙏 don't🚫 have💯 a💯 demon😈. I🙏 honour🏅 My🙏 Father🙏, and✨ you🫂 disrespect💢 Me🙏.”

50📖 “I'm🙏 not🚫 chasing🏃 My🙏 own💭 glory🏆. There💯 is✨ One👤 who✨ seeks🔎 it💯 and✨ judges⚖️.”

John 9
# John 9 — AdigunBible draft

# John 10 — AdigunBible draft

John 11
# John 11 — AdigunBible draft

1📖 A💯 man👤 named🏷️ Lazarus👤 was✨ sick🤒 in💯 Bethany📍, the💯 village🏘️ where📍 Mary👩 and✨ her💯 sister👩 Martha👩 lived🏠.

# John 12 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition with Deuterocanon (WEBBE), John 12. Draft only; not release-validated.

1📖 Six🔢 days📅 before⏱️ Passover🍞, Jesus🙏 came🚶 to💯 Bethany📍, where📍 Lazarus👤 lived🏠—the💯 man👤 Jesus🙏 had✨ raised⬆️ from💯 the💯 dead⚰️.

28📖 “Father🙏, bring✨ honour🏅 to💯 Your🙏 name📛!” Then⏱️ a💯 voice🗣️ came🚶 from💯 the💯 sky☁️: “I🙏 have✨ honoured🏅 it💯, and✨ I'll🙏 honour🏅 it💯 again🔄.”

29📖 The💯 crowd🫂 standing🧍 nearby📍 heard👂 it💯 and✨ thought🧠 thunder🌩️ had✨ hit💥. Others🫂 said🗣️, “An👼 angel👼 spoke🗣️ to💯 Him🙏.”

30📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “That💯 voice🗣️ came🚶 for💯 your🫂 sake💛, not🚫 Mine🙏.”

31📖 “Now⏱️ is✨ the💯 world's🌍 judgement⚖️. The💯 sarki🇳🇬 of💯 this💯 ụwa🇳🇬 will✨ be✨ cast🚪 out💨.”

32📖 “When⏱️ I🙏 am✨ lifted⬆️ up⬆️ from💯 the💯 earth🌍, I'll🙏 draw🧲 everyone🫂 to💯 Myself🙏.”

33📖 He🙏 said🗣️ this💯 to✨ show👀 what💯 kind💯 of💯 death💀 He🙏 would✨ face💯.

34📖 The💯 crowd🫂 replied🗣️, “Our🫂 law📜 says🗣️ the💯 Mashiach🇮🇱 remains♾️ forever♾️. So💭 how❓ can✨ You🙏 say🗣️ the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 has✨ to✨ be✨ lifted⬆️ up⬆️? Who❓ is✨ this💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤?”

35📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “The💯 light💡 will✨ be✨ with💯 you🫂 a💯 little💯 longer⏳. Walk🚶 while⏱️ you🫂 have✨ the💯 light💡, so✨ darkness🌑 doesn't🚫 overtake🏃 you🫂. Someone🫂 walking🚶 in💯 darkness🌑 can't🚫 see👀 where📍 they're🫂 going🚶.”

36📖 “While⏱️ you🫂 have✨ the💯 light💡, trust💯 the💯 light💡 and✨ become🔄 children🫂 of💯 light💡.” After⏱️ saying🗣️ this💯, Jesus🙏 left🚶 and✨ hid🙈 from💯 them🫂.

37📖 Even💯 after⏱️ all💯 the💯 signs✨ He🙏 had✨ done✅ in💯 front👀 of💯 them🫂, many💯 still🚫 didn't🚫 believe💯 in💯 Him🙏.

38📖 This💯 fulfilled✅ the💯 words🗣️ spoken📣 by💯 prophet📣 Isaiah📜:

“Lord🙏, who❓ believed💯 our🫂 report📣? To💯 whom❓ was✨ the💯 arm💪 of💯 the💯 Lord🙏 revealed🔦?”

39📖 That💯 was✨ why💭 they🫂 couldn't🚫 believe💯, just💯 as✨ Isaiah📜 had✨ said🗣️ again🔄:

40📖 “He🙏 blinded🙈 their💯 eyes👀 and✨ hardened🪨 their💯 hearts🫀, so✨ they🫂 wouldn't🚫 see👀 or✨ understand🧠 or✨ turn🔄 back🔙 and✨ be✨ healed🩺.”

41📖 Isaiah📜 said🗣️ this💯 when⏱️ he👤 saw👀 His🙏 glory🌟 and✨ spoke🗣️ about💯 Him🙏.

42📖 Still💯, even💯 many💯 leaders👑 believed💯 in💯 Jesus🙏, but✨ they🫂 wouldn't🚫 admit🗣️ it💯 because✨ they🫂 feared😨 the💯 Pharisees✡️ might✨ kick🚪 them🫂 out💨 of💯 the💯 synagogue⛪.

43📖 They🫂 cared💛 more📈 about💯 applause👏 from💯 people🫂 than✨ praise🙏 from💯 God🙏.

44📖 Jesus🙏 called📣 out💨, “Whoever🫂 believes💯 in💯 Me🙏 believes💯 not🚫 in💯 Me🙏, but✨ in💯 the💯 One👤 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏.”

45📖 “Whoever🫂 sees👀 Me🙏 sees👀 the💯 One👤 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏.”

46📖 “I🙏 came🚶 into💯 the💯 world🌍 as💯 its💯 light💡, so✨ whoever🫂 believes💯 in💯 Me🙏 doesn't🚫 have✨ to✨ stay🏠 in💯 darkness🌑.”

47📖 “If💯 anyone🫂 hears👂 My🙏 words🗣️ but✨ doesn't🚫 believe💯, I🙏 don't🚫 judge⚖️ them🫂. I🙏 came🚶 to✨ save🛟 the💯 world🌍, not🚫 condemn⚖️ it💯.”

48📖 “Whoever🫂 rejects🚫 Me🙏 and✨ won't🚫 receive📥 My🙏 words🗣️ already💯 has✨ a💯 judge⚖️. The💯 word🗣️ I🙏 spoke🗣️ will✨ judge⚖️ them🫂 on💯 the💯 last⏳ day📅.”

49📖 “I🙏 didn't🚫 speak🗣️ from💯 Myself🙏. The💯 Father🙏 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏 the💯 command📜 for💯 what💯 to✨ say🗣️ and✨ speak🗣️.”

50📖 “I🙏 know🧠 His🙏 command📜 is✨ eternal♾️ life🌱. So✨ what💯 I🙏 say🗣️ is✨ exactly💯 what💯 the💯 Father🙏 told🗣️ Me🙏 to✨ say🗣️.”
John 13
# John 13 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, John 13. Draft only; not release-validated.

1📖 Before⏱️ the💯 Pesach🇮🇱 feast🎉, Jesus🙏 knew🧠 His🙏 time⏱️ had✨ come💨 to✨ leave🚶 this💯 world🌍 and✨ return🔙 to💯 the💯 Father🙏. He🙏 loved💛 His🙏 own💯 people🫂 in💯 the💯 world🌍 all💯 the💯 way💯 to💯 the💯 end🏁.

2📖 Dinner🍽️ was✨ underway💯. The💯 devil😈 had✨ already💯 planted🌱 betrayal💔 in💯 Judas👤 Iscariot👤, son👦 of💯 Simon👤.

3📖 Jesus🙏 knew🧠 the💯 Father🙏 had✨ put📥 everything💯 in💯 His🙏 hands🤲, that✨ He🙏 came🚶 from💯 God🙏, and✨ that✨ He🙏 was✨ going🚶 back🔙 to💯 God🙏.

4📖 He🙏 stood⬆️ up⬆️ from💯 dinner🍽️, took👐 off👕 His🙏 outer👘 clothes👕, grabbed👐 a💯 Handtuch🇩🇪, and✨ wrapped🧻 it💯 around🔄 His🙏 waist🧍.

5📖 He🙏 poured💧 omi🇳🇬 into💯 a💯 bowl🥣 and✨ began🔛 to✨ laver🇫🇷 the💯 ụkwụ🇳🇬 of💯 His🙏 disciples🫂, then⏱️ dried🧻 them🦶 with💯 the💯 Handtuch🇩🇪 around💯 Him🙏.

6📖 When⏱️ He🙏 reached🚶 Simon👤 Peter👤, Peter👤 asked🗣️, “Lord🙏, You're🙏 washing🫧 my👤 feet🦶?”

7📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “You🫵 don't🚫 understand🧠 what💯 I'm🙏 doing✅ right💯 now⏱️, but✨ later⏱️ it'll✨ click💡.”

8📖 Peter👤 said🗣️, “No🚫 way💯—You🙏 will✨ never🚫 wash🫧 my👤 feet🦶!” Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “If💯 I🙏 don't🚫 wash🫧 you🫵, you🫵 have✨ no🚫 share💯 with💯 Me🙏.”

9📖 Simon👤 Peter👤 said🗣️, “Lord🙏, then💯 wash🫧 my👤 hands🤲 and✨ head🧠 too💯—not🚫 just💯 my👤 feet🦶!”

10📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Someone🫂 who's💯 bathed🛁 only💯 needs💭 their💯 feet🦶 washed🫧; they're✨ already💯 clean🫧 all💯 over💯. You🫂 are✨ clean🫧, though💭 not🚫 every💯 one🔢 of💯 you🫂.”

11📖 He🙏 knew🧠 who❓ would✨ betray💔 Him🙏, so✨ He🙏 added🗣️, “Not🚫 every💯 one🔢 of💯 you🫂 is✨ clean🫧.”

12📖 After⏱️ washing🫧 their💯 feet🦶, putting👕 His🙏 outer👘 clothes👕 back🔙 on💯, and✨ sitting🪑 down⬇️ again🔄, He🙏 asked🗣️, “Do❓ you🫂 understand🧠 what💭 I🙏 did✅ to✨ you🫂?”

13📖 “You🫂 call📣 Me🙏 ‘Teacher📚’ and✨ ‘Lord🙏,’ and✨ you're🫂 right✅—that's💯 who✨ I🙏 am✨.”

14📖 “So✨ if💯 I🙏, your🫂 Lord🙏 and✨ Teacher📚, washed🫧 your🫂 feet🦶, you🫂 should💯 wash🫧 the💯 feet🦶 of💯 others🫂 too💯.”

15📖 “I🙏 gave🎁 you🫂 this💯 example🪞 so✨ you🫂 can✨ do✅ what💯 I🙏 did✅ for💯 you🫂.”

16📖 “I'm💯 telling🗣️ you🫂 the💯 truth💯: a💯 servant🫶 isn't🚫 greater📈 than💯 their💯 lord👑, and✨ a💯 messenger📨 isn't🚫 above⬆️ the💯 one👤 who✨ sent📨 them🫂.”

17📖 “You🫂 know🧠 this💯 now💯, so✨ you're🫂 blessed🙏 if💯 you🫂 live✅ it💯.”

18📖 “I'm🙏 not🚫 talking🗣️ about💯 all💯 of💯 you🫂. I🙏 know🧠 those🫂 I🙏 chose✅. But✨ Scripture📜 must💯 be✨ fulfilled✅: ‘The💯 one👤 who✨ eats🍞 bread🍞 with💯 Me🙏 has✨ turned🔄 against💔 Me🙏.’”

19📖 “I'm🙏 telling🗣️ you🫂 before⏱️ it✨ happens💨, so✨ when⏱️ it✨ does✅, you'll🫂 believe💯 I🙏 am✨ He🙏.”

20📖 “I'm💯 telling🗣️ you🫂 the💯 truth💯: whoever🫂 welcomes👋 someone🫂 I🙏 send📨 welcomes👋 Me🙏, and✨ whoever🫂 welcomes👋 Me🙏 welcomes👋 the💯 One👤 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏.”

21📖 After⏱️ saying🗣️ this💯, His🙏 spirit🕊️ felt😔 deeply💯 troubled😔. He🙏 said🗣️, “I'm💯 telling🗣️ you🫂 the💯 truth💯: one🔢 of💯 you🫂 will✨ betray💔 Me🙏.”

22📖 Dem🇯🇲 looked👀 at💯 one🔢 another🫂, confused😵 about💯 who❓ He🙏 meant💭.

23📖 One🔢 disciple🫂 Jesus🙏 loved💛 was✨ leaning🛋️ close📍 against💯 His🙏 chest🫀 at💯 the💯 table🍽️.

24📖 Simon👤 Peter👤 signalled👋 to💯 him👤: “Ask🗣️ who❓ Jesus🙏 means💭.”

25📖 So✨ the💯 disciple🫂 leaned🛋️ back🔙 against💯 Jesus🙏 and✨ asked🗣️, “Lord🙏, who❓ is✨ it💯?”

26📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “It's✨ the💯 one👤 I🙏 give🎁 this💯 dipped🥣 piece🍞 of💯 bread🍞 to💯.” He🙏 dipped🥣 it💯 and✨ handed🤲 it💯 to💯 Judas👤, son👦 of💯 Simon👤 Iscariot👤.

27📖 As⏱️ soon💯 as💯 Judas👤 took👐 the💯 bread🍞, Satan😈 entered🚪 him👤. Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him👤, “Do✅ what💯 you're🫵 going🚶 to✨ do✅—vite🇫🇷.”

28📖 Nobody🫂 at💯 the💯 table🍽️ understood🧠 why💭 Jesus🙏 said🗣️ that💯.

29📖 Some💯 thought🧠 that💭, since💯 Judas👤 had🫴 the💯 money💰 box📦, Jesus🙏 meant💭, “Go🚶 buy🛍️ what💯 we🫂 need💭 for💯 the💯 feast🎉,” or✨ give🎁 something💯 to💯 poor🫶 people🫂.

30📖 Judas👤 took👐 the💯 bread🍞 and✨ left🚶 immediately⚡. It✨ was✨ dare🇳🇬.

31📖 After⏱️ he👤 left🚶, Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “Agora🇵🇹 the💯 Son👑 of💯 Man👤 is✨ glorified🌟, and✨ God🙏 is✨ glorified🌟 through💯 Him🙏.”

32📖 “If💯 God🙏 is✨ glorified🌟 through💯 Him🙏, God🙏 will✨ glorify🌟 Him🙏 in💯 Himself🙏—right💯 away⚡.”

33📖 “My🙏 dear💛 children🫂, I'll🙏 be✨ with💯 you🫂 only💯 a💯 little💯 longer⏳. You'll🫂 look🔎 for💯 Me🙏. Like💯 I🙏 told🗣️ the💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👥, ‘You🫂 can't🚫 come🚶 where📍 I'm🙏 going🚶,’ I🙏 say🗣️ that💯 to💯 you🫂 now💯 too💯.”

34📖 “Here's💯 a💯 new🆕 command📜: amem🇵🇹 one🔢 another🫂 the💯 way💯 I've🙏 loved💛 you🫂. That's💯 how💯 you🫂 should💯 show💛 amor🇪🇸 to💯 one🔢 another🫂.”

35📖 “Everyone🫂 will✨ know🧠 you're🫂 My🙏 disciples🫂 by💯 your🫂 love💛 for💯 one🔢 another🫂.”

36📖 Simon👤 Peter👤 asked🗣️ Him🙏, “Lord🙏, where📍 are✨ You🙏 going🚶?” Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “You🫵 can't🚫 follow🚶 Me🙏 there💯 right💯 now⏱️, but✨ later⏱️ you🫵 will✨.”

37📖 Peter👤 said🗣️, “Lord🙏, why❓ can't🚫 I👤 follow🚶 You🙏 now💯? I'd👤 lay⬇️ down💯 my👤 life🌱 for💯 You🙏!”

38📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “You🫵 would✨ lay⬇️ down💯 your🫵 life🌱 for💯 Me🙏? I'm💯 telling🗣️ you🫵 the💯 truth💯: the💯 rooster🐓 won't🚫 crow📣 until⏱️ you🫵 have✨ denied🚫 Me🙏 three🔢 times🔁.”
John 14
# John 14 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, John 14. Draft only; not release-validated.

1📖 “Don't🚫 let🚪 your🫵 ọkàn🇳🇬 feel😔 troubled😟. Trust💯 God🙏; trust💯 Me🙏 too💯.”

2📖 “My🙏 Father's🙏 home🏠 has✨ plenty💯 of💯 rooms🏘️. If💯 that💯 weren't🚫 true✅, I🙏 would've✨ told🗣️ you🫂. I'm🙏 going🚶 to✨ get🏗️ a💯 place🏠 ready✅ for💯 you🫂.”

3📖 “When⏱️ I🙏 go🚶 and✨ get🏗️ your🫂 place🏠 ready✅, I'll🙏 come🔙 back🔙 and✨ bring🚶 you🫂 to💯 Myself🙏, so✨ you'll🫂 be✨ where📍 I🙏 am✨ too💯.”

4📖 “You🫂 know🧠 where📍 I'm🙏 going🚶, and✨ you🫂 know🧠 the💯 route🛣️.”

5📖 Thomas👤 said🗣️, “Lord🙏, we🫂 don't🚫 know🧠 where📍 You're🙏 going🚶. How❓ can✨ we🫂 know🧠 the💯 way🛣️?”

6📖 Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him👤, “I🙏 am✨ the💯 Weg🇩🇪, the💯 vérité🇫🇷, and✨ the💯 vida🇵🇹. Nobody🫂 comes🚶 to💯 the💯 Father🙏 except💯 through💯 Me🙏.”

7📖 “If💯 you🫂 had✨ really💯 known🧠 Me🙏, you'd🫂 have✨ known🧠 My🙏 Father🙏 too💯. From💯 now⏱️ on💯, you🫂 do✅ know🧠 Him🙏 and✨ have✨ seen👀 Him🙏.”

8📖 Philip👤 said🗣️, “Lord🙏, show👀 us🫂 the💯 Father🙏, and✨ we're🫂 good✅.”

9📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Philip👤, I've🙏 been✨ with💯 you🫂 this💯 long⏳, and✨ you🫵 still🚫 don't🚫 know🧠 Me🙏? Whoever🫂 has✨ seen👀 Me🙏 has✨ seen👀 the💯 Father🙏. Why❓ ask🗣️, ‘Show👀 us🫂 the💯 Father🙏’?”

10📖 “Don't🚫 you🫂 believe💯 I'm🙏 in💯 the💯 Father🙏 and✨ the💯 Father🙏 is✨ in💯 Me🙏? The💯 words🗣️ I🙏 speak🗣️ aren't🚫 from💯 Me🙏 alone👤. The💯 Father🙏 living🌱 in💯 Me🙏 is✨ doing✅ His🙏 work🛠️.”

11📖 “Trust💯 Me🙏: I'm🙏 in💯 the💯 Father🙏, and✨ the💯 Father🙏 is✨ in💯 Me🙏. Or💭 let✨ the💯 works🛠️ themselves💯 show👀 you🫂.”

12📖 “I'm💯 telling🗣️ you🫂 the💯 truth💯: whoever🫂 believes💯 in💯 Me🙏 will✨ do✅ the💯 works🛠️ I'm🙏 doing✅ too💯—and✨ even💯 greater📈 things💯, because✨ I'm🙏 going🚶 to💯 the💯 Father🙏.”

13📖 “Whatever💯 you🫂 ask🗣️ in💯 My🙏 name📛, I'll🙏 do✅ it💯, so✨ the💯 Father🙏 may✨ be✨ honoured🏅 through💯 the💯 Son👑.”

14📖 “If💯 you🫂 ask🗣️ Me🙏 for💯 something💯 in💯 My🙏 name📛, I'll🙏 do✅ it💯.”

15📖 “If💯 you🫂 love💛 Me🙏, keep🔒 My🙏 commands📜.”

16📖 “I'll🙏 ask🗣️ the💯 Father🙏, and✨ He🙏 will✨ give🎁 you🫂 another🔄 Helper🫶 to✨ stay🏠 with💯 you🫂 forever♾️.”

17📖 “He's🙏 the💯 Spirit🕊️ of💯 truth✅. The💯 world🌍 can't🚫 receive📥 Him🙏 because✨ dem🇯🇲 don't🚫 see👀 or✨ know🧠 Him🙏. You🫂 know🧠 Him🙏—He🙏 lives🏠 with💯 you🫂 and✨ will✨ be✨ in💯 you🫂.”

18📖 “I🙏 won't🚫 leave🚪 you🫂 as💯 orphans🧒. I'll🙏 come🔙 back🔙 to💯 you🫂.”

19📖 “Soon⏳ the💯 world🌍 won't🚫 see👀 Me🙏 anymore💯, but✨ you🫂 will✨ see👀 Me🙏. Because✨ I🙏 live🌱, you'll🫂 live🌱 too💯.”

20📖 “When⏱️ that💯 day📅 comes💨, you'll🫂 know🧠 I'm🙏 in💯 My🙏 Father🙏, you're🫂 in💯 Me🙏, and✨ I'm🙏 in💯 you🫂.”

21📖 “Whoever🫂 holds📥 My🙏 commands📜 and✨ lives✅ them💯 is✨ the💯 one🫂 who✨ loves💛 Me🙏. My🙏 Father🙏 will✨ love💛 them🫂, and✨ I'll🙏 love💛 them🫂 and✨ show👀 Myself🙏 to💯 them🫂.”

22📖 Judas👤 (not🚫 Iscariot👤) asked🗣️ Him🙏, “Lord🙏, why❓ will✨ You🙏 show👀 Yourself🙏 to💯 us🫂 but✨ not🚫 to💯 the💯 mundo🇪🇸?”

23📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Anyone🫂 who✨ loves💛 Me🙏 will✨ live✅ by💯 My🙏 word🗣️. My🙏 Father🙏 will✨ love💛 them🫂, and✨ We'll🫂 come🚶 and✨ make🏠 Our🙏 gida🇳🇬 with💯 them🫂.”

24📖 “If✨ you🫂 don't🚫 love💛 Me🙏, you're🫂 not🚫 keeping🔒 My🙏 words🗣️. What💯 you🫂 hear👂 from💯 Me🙏 is✨ from💯 the💯 Father🙏 who✨ sent📨 Me🙏.”

25📖 “I've🙏 said🗣️ all💯 this💯 while⏱️ I'm🙏 still💯 here📍 with💯 you🫂.”

26📖 “But✨ the💯 Helper🫶—the💯 Holy🙏 Spirit🕊️—whom✨ the💯 Father🙏 will✨ send📨 in💯 My🙏 name📛 will✨ teach📚 you🫂 everything💯 and✨ remind🔔 you🫂 of💯 everything💯 I🙏 told🗣️ you🫂.”

4📖 “I🙏 brought🙌 You🫵 glory✨ here🌍 on💯 earth🌍 by💯 finishing✅ the💯 work🛠️ You🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏 to💯 do✅.”

5📖 “Now💯, Father🙏, bring🙌 Me🙏 into💯 glory✨ beside💯 You🫵—the💯 same💯 glory✨ I🙏 shared🫶 with💯 You🙏 before⏮️ the💯 world🌍 even💯 existed🌌.”

6📖 “I🙏 showed💡 Your🙏 name🗣️ to💯 the💯 people🫂 from💯 this💯 world🌍 You🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏. Dem🇯🇲 were💯 Yours🫵; You🙏 gave🎁 them🫂 to💯 Me🙏, and✨ they🫵 held🫳 on💯 to💯 Your🙏 word📖.”

7📖 “Now💯 they🫵 know🧠 everything💯 You🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏 came🚶 from💯 You🙏.”

9📖 “I🙏 am💯 praying🙏 for💯 them🫵. I🙏 am💯 not🚫 praying🙏 for💯 the💯 world🌍, but💯 for💯 the💯 people🫂 You🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏, because💯 they🫵 belong🏠 to💯 You🙏.”

10📖 “Everything💯 that💯 is💯 Mine🫶 is💯 Yours🙏, and✨ everything💯 Yours🙏 is💯 Mine🫶. I🙏 am💯 glorified✨ through💯 them🫂.”

11📖 “I🙏 am💯 leaving🚶 this💯 world🌍, but💯 they🫵 are💯 staying🏠 here🌍 while💯 I🙏 come🚶 to💯 You🙏. Holy✨ Father🙏, protect🛡️ them🫂 by💯 Your🙏 name🗣️—the💯 name🗣️ You🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏—so✨ they🫵 can💯 be💯 otu🇳🇬🤝, just💯 as💯 We🫶 are💯 one🫶.”

12📖 “While💯 I🙏 was💯 with💯 them🫵 here🌍, I🙏 guarded🛡️ them🫂 by💯 Your🙏 name🗣️. I🙏 kept✅ safe🛡️ everyone💯 You🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏; only💯 the💯 son👦 of💯 destruction💥 was💯 lost💔, so✨ Scripture📜 could💯 come✅ true💯.”

13📖 “Now💯 I🙏 am💯 coming🚶 to💯 You🙏, and✨ I🙏 am💯 saying🗣️ this💯 while💯 still💯 in💯 the💯 world🌍, so✨ My🙏 Freude🇩🇪😄 can💯 fill🫗 them🫂 completely💯.”

14📖 “I🙏 gave🎁 them🫂 Your🙏 word📖. Le🇫🇷 monde🇫🇷🌍 hated😠 them🫵 because💯 they🫵 don’t🚫 belong🏠 to💯 its💯 system🌐, just💯 as💯 I🙏 don’t🚫 belong🏠 to💯 it🌍.”

15📖 “I🙏 am💯 not🚫 asking🙏 You🙏 to💯 pull🫳 them🫂 out💨 of💯 the💯 world🌍. I🙏 am💯 asking🙏 You🫵 to💯 protect🛡️ them🫂 from💯 the💯 evil😈 one👤.”

16📖 “They🫵 don’t🚫 belong🏠 to💯 this💯 world🌍, just💯 as💯 I🙏 don’t🚫 belong🏠 to💯 it🌍.”

17📖 “Set🫵 them🫂 apart✨ through💯 Your🙏 gaskiya🇳🇬. Your🙏 word📖 is💯 truth✅.”

18📖 “Just💯 as💯 You🙏 sent📨 Me🙏 into💯 the💯 world🌍, I🙏 sent📨 them🫂 into💯 the💯 world🌍 too💯.”

19📖 “For💯 their🫵 sake🫶, I🙏 set✨ Myself🫵 apart🙏 so✨ they🫂 can💯 also💯 be💯 set✨ apart💯 through💯 truth✅.”

20📖 “I🙏 am💯 not🚫 praying🙏 for💯 these💯 people🫂 alone💯. I🙏 am💯 also💯 praying🙏 for💯 everyone💯 who💯 will💯 believe💭 in💯 Me🙏 through💯 their🫵 message📣.”

21📖 “Let🙏 them🫂 all💯 be💯 echad🇮🇱🤝, just💯 as💯 You🙏, Father🙏, are💯 in💯 Me🙏 and✨ I🙏 am💯 in💯 You🙏. Let🙏 them🫵 also💯 be💯 one🤝 with💯 Us🫶, so✨ the💯 world🌍 can💯 believe💭 You🙏 sent📨 Me🙏.”

22📖 “I🙏 gave🎁 them🫂 the💯 glory✨ You🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏, so✨ they🫵 can💯 be💯 one🤝 just💯 as💯 We🫶 are💯 one🤝.”

23📖 “I🙏 am💯 in💯 them🫂, and✨ You🙏 are💯 in💯 Me🙏, assim🇵🇹✨ they🫵 can💯 become💯 fully💯 one🤝. Then✨ the💯 world🌍 will💯 know🧠 You🙏 sent📨 Me🙏 and✨ loved💛 them🫂 just💯 as💯 You🙏 loved💛 Me🙏.”

24📖 “Father🙏, I🙏 want💭 the💯 people🫂 You🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏 to💯 be💯 with💯 Me🙏 where💯 I🙏 am💯, so✨ they🫵 can💯 see👀 My🙏 glory✨—the💯 glory✨ You🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏 because💯 You🙏 loved💛 Me🙏 before⏮️ the💯 world's🌍 foundation🏛️.”

25📖 “Righteous⚖️ Father🙏, the💯 world🌍 hasn’t🚫 known🧠 You🙏, but💯 I🙏 know🧠 You🙏, and✨ these💯 people🫂 know🧠 You🙏 sent📨 Me🙏.”

26📖 “I🙏 made💡 Your🙏 name🗣️ known📣 to💯 them🫂 and✨ will💯 keep🔁 making💡 it💯 known📣, so✨ the💯 ìfẹ́🇳🇬❤️ You🙏 have✨ for💯 Me🙏 can💯 live🏠 in💯 them🫂, and✨ I🙏 can💯 live🏠 in💯 them🫂 too💯.”
John 18
# John 18 — AdigunBible draft
