1📖 After💯 Jesus🙏 said🗣️ all💯 that💯, He🙏 crossed🚶 the💯 Kidron🌊 stream🌊 with💯 His🙏 disciples🫂 and✨ entered🚪 a💯 garden🌿 with💯 them🫂.

2📖 Judas🫵, the💯 one👤 who💯 betrayed💔 Him🙏, knew🧠 that💯 place📍 too💯, because💯 Jesus🙏 often🔁 met🤝 there👀 with💯 His🙏 disciples🫂.

3📖 So✨ Judas🫵 brought🚶 a💯 squad🪖 of💯 soldiers🪖 and✨ officers🫡 from💯 the💯 chief👑 priests🙏 and✨ Pharisees📜, rolling🚶 up💯 with💯 lanterns🏮, torches🔥, and✨ weapons⚔️.

4📖 Jesus🙏 knew🧠 everything💯 that💯 was💯 about💯 to💯 happen⚡ to💯 Him🙏, so✨ He🙏 stepped🚶 forward⏩ and✨ asked🗣️ them🫵, “Who👀 are💯 you🫵 looking🔎 for💭?”

5📖 They👥 answered🗣️, “Jesus🙏 from💯 Nazareth📍.” Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “That’s💯 Me🙏.” Judas🫵, the💯 betrayer💔, was💯 standing🧍 right💯 there👀 with💯 them🫂 too💯.

6📖 When💯 Jesus🙏 said🗣️, “That’s💯 Me🙏,” they👥 stumbled😵 backward↩️ and✨ hit🫳 the💯 ground🌍.

7📖 Jesus🙏 asked🗣️ them🫵 again🔁, “Who👀 are💯 you🫵 looking🔎 for💭?” They👥 said🗣️, “Jesus🙏 from💯 Nazareth📍.”

8📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “I🙏 already✅ told🗣️ you🫵, that’s💯 Me🙏. So💯 if💯 I🙏 am💯 who💯 you🫵 want🔎, let🙏 these🫂 others👥 go🚶.”

9📖 This💯 happened✅ so✨ His🙏 words📜 could💯 come✅ true💯: “I🙏 didn’t🚫 lose💔 even💯 one👤 of💯 the💯 people🫂 You🙏 gave🎁 Me🙏.”

10📖 Simon🙏 Peter🫵 had💯 a💯 sword⚔️; he🫵 pulled🫳 it💯 out💨, swung⚔️ at💯 the💯 high👑 priest’s🙏 servant🧑🏽‍🌾, and✨ cut✂️ off💨 his🫵 right➡️ ear👂. The💯 servant’s🧑🏽‍🌾 name🗣️ was💯 Malchus👤.

12📖 Then✨ the💯 soldiers🪖, their🫵 commander🫡, and✨ the💯 Jewish✡️ officers🫡 grabbed🫳 Jesus🙏 and✨ tied🔗 Him🙏 up💯.

13📖 They👥 took🚶 Him🙏 first🥇 to💯 Annas👤, Caiaphas’s👤 father-in-law👨‍👧‍👦. Caiaphas👤 was💯 high👑 priest🙏 that💯 year📅.

14📖 Caiaphas👤 was💯 the💯 one👤 who💯 had💯 told🗣️ the💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👑 that💯 one👤 man🙏 should💯 die💀 for💯 the💯 people🫂.

15📖 Simon🙏 Peter🫵 followed🚶 Jesus🙏, and✨ another💯 disciple🫂 did💯 too💯. That💯 disciple🫂 was💯 known🤝 to💯 the💯 high👑 priest🙏, so✨ he🫵 entered🚪 the💯 high👑 priest’s🙏 courtyard🏛️ with💯 Jesus🙏.

16📖 Peter🫵 stayed💯 outside🚪 by💯 the💯 door🚪. The💯 other💯 disciple🫂, who💯 knew🤝 the💯 high👑 priest🙏, went🚶 out💨, spoke🗣️ with💯 the💯 woman👩🏽 guarding🛡️ the💯 door🚪, and✨ brought🚶 Peter🫵 in🚪.

18📖 It💯 was💯 cold🥶, so✨ the💯 servants🧑🏽‍🌾 and✨ officers🫡 stood🧍 around💯 a💯 charcoal🔥 fire🔥, warming🧤 themselves🫂. Peter🫵 stood🧍 there👀 warming🔥 up💯 with💯 them🫂 too💯.

19📖 The💯 high👑 priest🙏 questioned❓ Jesus🙏 about💯 His🙏 disciples🫂 and✨ what💯 He🙏 taught📣.

20📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “I🙏 spoke📣 out💨 in💯 the💯 open🌤️. I🙏 always🔁 taught📖 in💯 synagogues🕍 and✨ the💯 temple🏛️, where💯 the💯 Jewish✡️ people🫂 gathered🤝. I🙏 never🚫 kept🤫 My🙏 teaching📖 secret🔒.”

21📖 “Why❓ are💯 you🫵 questioning❓ Me🙏? Ask❓ the💯 people🫂 who💯 heard👂 Me🙏 what💯 I🙏 said🗣️. They🫵 know🧠 what💯 I🙏 taught📣.”

22📖 When💯 Jesus🙏 said🗣️ that💯, one💯 officer🫡 beside💯 Him🙏 slapped🫳 Him🙏 and✨ snapped🗣️, “Is💯 that💯 how💯 You🙏 answer🗣️ the💯 high👑 priest🙏?”

23📖 Jesus🙏 replied🗣️, “If✨ I🙏 spoke🗣️ evil💔, bring🫴 testimony🧾 of💯 the💯 evil💔; but💯 if✨ I🙏 spoke🗣️ well✅, why❓ are💯 you🫵 hitting👋 Me🙏?”

24📖 Annas👤 then✨ sent📨 Jesus🙏, still💯 tied🔗 up💯, to💯 Caiaphas👤 the💯 high👑 priest🙏.

25📖 Meanwhile💯 Simon🙏 Peter🫵 was💯 standing🧍 by💯 the💯 fire🔥, warming🧤 up💯. They👥 asked🗣️ him🫵, “You🫵 aren’t🚫 one💯 of💯 His🙏 disciples🫂 too💭, are💯 you🫵?” Peter🫵 denied🚫 it💯: “I’m🚫 not🚫.”

26📖 One💯 servant🧑🏽‍🌾 of💯 the💯 high👑 priest🙏—a💯 relative👨‍👧‍👦 of💯 the💯 man👤 whose🫵 ear👂 Peter🫵 had💯 cut✂️ off💨—asked🗣️, “Didn’t💭 I🙏 see👀 you🫵 in💯 the💯 garden🌿 with💯 Him🙏?”

27📖 Peter🫵 denied🚫 it💯 again🔁, and✨ right💯 then⏱️ a💯 rooster🐓 crowed🗣️.

28📖 They👥 took🚶 Jesus🙏 from💯 Caiaphas👤 to💯 the💯 governor’s🏛️ headquarters🏛️. It💯 was💯 early🌅. They👥 stayed🚫 outside🚪 so✨ they🫵 wouldn’t🚫 become💯 ceremonially🫧 unclean🚫 and✨ miss🚫 the💯 Passover🍞 meal🍽️.

29📖 Pilate👑 came🚶 out💨 to💯 them🫂 and✨ asked🗣️, “What❓ charge⚖️ are💯 you🫵 bringing🫳 against💯 this💯 man👤?”

30📖 They👥 answered🗣️, “If💯 He🙏 wasn’t🚫 doing💯 evil😈, we🫵 wouldn’t🚫 have💯 handed🫳 Him🙏 over🔗 to💯 you🫵.”

31📖 Pilate👑 told🗣️ them🫂, “Take🚶 Him🙏 yourselves🫵 and✨ judge⚖️ Him🙏 by💯 your🫵 own💯 law📜.” The💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👑 replied🗣️, “We🫵 aren’t🚫 legally⚖️ allowed✅ to💯 execute⚔️ anyone👤.”

32📖 This💯 happened✅ to💯 fulfil📜 Jesus’s🙏 words📣 about💯 the💯 kind💯 of💯 death💀 He🙏 was💯 going🚶 to💯 face⚖️.

33📖 Pilate👑 went🚶 back↩️ inside🏛️, called🗣️ Jesus🙏 over💯, and✨ asked❓, “Are💯 You🙏 the💯 ọba🇳🇬 of💯 the💯 Jewish✡️ people🫂?”

34📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “Is💭 that💯 your🫵 own💯 question❓, or💯 did💯 somebody👥 tell🗣️ you🫵 about💯 Me🙏?”

35📖 Pilate👑 said🗣️, “Do💯 I🙏 look👀 Jewish✡️ to💯 you🫵? Your🫵 own💯 people🫂 and✨ chief👑 priests🙏 handed🫳 You🙏 over💯 to💯 me🫵. What❓ did💯 You🙏 do✅?”

36📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “My🙏 Kingdom👑 doesn’t🚫 come🚶 from💯 this💯 world🌍. If💯 it💯 did💯, My🙏 servants🫂 would💯 fight⚔️ so✨ I🙏 wouldn’t🚫 be💯 handed🫳 over💯 to💯 the💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👑. But💯 My🙏 Kingdom👑 is💯 from💯 somewhere🌌 else💯.”

37📖 Pilate👑 asked🗣️, “So💭 You🙏 are💯 an💯 eze🇳🇬, then💯?” Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “You🫵 say🗣️ I🙏 am💯 a💯 König🇩🇪👑. I🙏 was💯 born👶 and✨ came🚶 into💯 the💯 world🌍 for💯 this💯: to💯 testify🗣️ to💯 vérité🇫🇷. Everyone💯 who💯 belongs🏠 to💯 the💯 vérité🇫🇷 listens👂 to💯 My🙏 voz🇵🇹.”

38📖 Pilate👑 asked🗣️, “What💭 is💯 verdad🇪🇸?” Then✨ he🫵 went🚶 back💨 outside🚪 to💯 the💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👑 and✨ said🗣️, “I🙏 find🔎 no🚫 reason⚖️ to💯 charge⚖️ Him🙏.”

39📖 “But💯 you🫵 have💯 a💯 Passover🍞 al'ada🇳🇬: I🙏 release🕊️ one💯 prisoner🔒 for💯 you🫵. Want❓ me🫵 to💯 release🕊️ the💯 מלך🇮🇱 of💯 the💯 Jewish✡️ people🫂?”

40📖 Dem🇯🇲👥 shouted📣 back🔊, “Not🚫 Him🙏! Give🎁 us🫵 Barabbas👤!” Barabbas👤 was💯 a💯 robber🦹.”
John 19
# John 19 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, John 19. Draft only; not release-validated.

1📖 Pilate👑 had💯 Jesus🙏 taken🚶 and✨ whipped🪢.

2📖 The💯 soldiers🪖 twisted🌀 thorns🌵 into💯 une🇫🇷 couronne🇫🇷👑—pressed🫳 it💯 onto💯 His🙏 head🧠, and✨ dressed👘 Him🙏 in💯 a💯 purple🟣 robe👘.

3📖 They👥 kept🔁 mocking😏 Him🙏: “Hail🙌, King👑 of💯 the💯 Jews✡️!” Then✨ they👥 kept🔁 slapping👋 Him🙏.

4📖 Pilate👑 came🚶 out💨 again🔁 and✨ told🗣️ them🫂, “Look👀, I’m💯 bringing🚶 Him🙏 out💨 so✨ you🫵 know🧠 I🙏 find🔎 no🚫 charge⚖️ against💯 Him🙏.”

5📖 Jesus🙏 came🚶 out💨 wearing👘 the💯 thorn🌵 crown👑 and✨ purple🟣 robe👘. Pilate👑 said🗣️, “Here👀 He🙏 is💯—the💯 man🧍.”

6📖 When💯 the💯 chief👑 priests🙏 and✨ officers🫡 saw👀 Him🙏, Dem🇯🇲👥 yelled📣, “Crucify⚔️ Him🙏! Crucify⚔️ Him🙏!” Pilate👑 told🗣️ them🫂, “Take🚶 Him🙏 yourselves🫵 and✨ crucify⚔️ Him🙏. I🙏 find🔎 no🚫 charge⚖️ against💯 Him🙏.”

7📖 The💯 Jewish✡️ leaders👑 replied🗣️, “We🫵 have💯 a💯 law📜, and✨ under💯 that💯 law📜 He🙏 should💯 die💀, because💯 He🙏 claimed🗣️ to💯 be💯 God’s🙏 Son👑.”

8📖 When💯 Pilate👑 heard👂 that💯, he🫵 got💯 even✨ more💯 scared😰.

9📖 He🫵 went🚶 back↩️ into💯 his🫵 headquarters🏛️ and✨ asked🗣️ Jesus🙏, “Where🧭 are💯 You🙏 from💭?” But💯 Jesus🙏 gave💯 him🫵 no🚫 answer🗣️.

10📖 Pilate👑 said🗣️, “You🫵 won’t🚫 talk🗣️ to💯 me🫵? Don’t🚫 You🙏 know🧠 I🙏 have💯 power⚡ to💯 release🕊️ You🙏 or💯 crucify⚔️ You🙏?”

11📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “You🫵 would💯 have💯 no🚫 authority⚡ over💯 Me🙏 unless💯 it💯 had💯 been🎁 given🎁 to💯 you🫵 from💯 above⬆️. That’s💯 why💯 the💯 one👤 who💯 handed🫳 Me🙏 over🔗 to💯 you🫵 carries💯 the💯 greater📈 sin🖤.”

12📖 After💯 that💯, Pilate👑 kept🔁 trying💭 to💯 release🕊️ Jesus🙏. But💯 the💯 leaders👑 shouted📣, “If💯 you🫵 release🕊️ this💯 man👤, you🫵 aren’t🚫 Caesar’s👑 friend🫶! Anyone💯 who💯 claims👑 to💯 be💯 a💯 king👑 is💯 speaking🗣️ against⚔️ Caesar👑!”

13📖 When💯 Pilate👑 heard👂 them🫂, he🫵 brought🚶 Jesus🙏 outside🚪 and✨ sat🪑 on💯 the💯 judge’s⚖️ seat🪑 at💯 a💯 place📍 called🗣️ The💯 Pavement🪨—Gabbatha🇮🇱📍 in💯 Hebrew🕎.

14📖 It💯 was💯 Passover🍞 Preparation🗓️ Day📅, around💯 the💯 sixth⏰ hour🕕. Pilate👑 said🗣️ to💯 the💯 people🫂, “Here👀 is💯 your🫵 Rey🇪🇸👑!”

15📖 They👥 yelled📣, “Get💨 rid🚪 of💯 Him🙏! Get💨 rid🚪 of💯 Him🙏! Crucify⚔️ Him🙏!” Pilate👑 asked🗣️, “Should🤔 I🙏 crucify⚔️ your🫵 Melekh🇮🇱👑?” The💯 chief👑 priests🙏 answered🗣️, “We🫵 have💯 no🚫 king👑 except💯 Caesar👑!”

16📖 So✨ Pilate👑 handed🫳 Jesus🙏 over🔗 to💯 them🫂 for💯 crucifixion⚔️. They👥 took🚶 Him🙏 away🚶.

17📖 Jesus🙏 carried🫳 His🙏 Kreuz🇩🇪⚔️ and✨ went🚶 to💯 the💯 place📍 called🗣️ The💯 Skull💀 Place📍, called🗣️ Golgotha🇮🇱📍 in💯 Hebrew🕎.

18📖 There👀 they👥 crucified⚔️ Him🙏, with💯 two👥 others👥—one👤 on💯 each💯 side↔️, Jesus🙏 in💯 the💯 middle🎯.

19📖 Pilate👑 also💯 wrote✍️ a💯 sign🪧 and✨ put🫳 it💯 on💯 the💯 cross⚔️. It💯 said🗣️, “JESUS🙏 OF💯 NAZARETH📍, THE💯 REI🇵🇹👑 OF💯 THE💯 JEWS✡️.”

17📖 Jesus🙏 asked🗣️ him🫵 a💯 third🔢 time⏰, “Simon🙏, son👦 of💯 Jonah👤, do💭 you🫵 care🫶 about💯 Me🙏?” Peter🫵 felt😞 hurt💔 that💯 Jesus🙏 had💯 asked❓ three🔢 times⏰, “Do💭 you🫵 care🫶 about💯 Me🙏?” He🫵 said🗣️, “Lord🙏, You🙏 know🧠 everything💯; You🙏 know🧠 I🙏 care🫶 about💯 You🙏.” Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him🫵, “Feed🍼 My🙏 tson🇮🇱.”

18📖 “For💯 real💯, when💯 you🫵 were💯 younger🧒, you🫵 got👘 yourself🫂 dressed👕 and✨ went🚶 wherever💯 you🫵 wanted💭. But💯 when💯 you🫵 grow👴 old⏳, you🫵 will💯 stretch👐 out💨 your🫵 hands👐, and✨ someone👤 else💯 will💯 dress👘 you🫵 and✨ take🚶 you🫵 somewhere📍 you🚫 don’t🚫 want💭 to💯 go🚶.”

19📖 Jesus🙏 said🗣️ this💯 to💯 show💡 the💯 kind💯 of💯 death💀 Peter🫵 would💯 face⚡ to💯 bring🙌 glory✨ to💯 God🙏. Then✨ Jesus🙏 told🗣️ him🫵, “Follow🚶 Me🙏.”

20📖 Peter🫵 turned↩️ around💯 and✨ saw👀 the💯 disciple🫂 Jesus🙏 loved💛 following🚶 them🫂. This💯 was💯 the💯 one💯 who💯 leaned🫂 against💯 Jesus🙏 at💯 supper🍽️ and✨ asked🗣️, “Lord🙏, who👀 is💯 going🚶 to💯 betray💔 You🙏?”

21📖 When💯 Peter🫵 saw👀 him🫂, he🫵 asked🗣️ Jesus🙏, “Lord🙏, what💭 about💯 him🫂?”

22📖 Jesus🙏 answered🗣️, “If💯 I🙏 want💭 him🫂 to💯 stay🏠 until💯 I🙏 come🚶, how💭 is💯 that💯 your🫵 business💼? You🫵 follow🚶 Me🙏.”

23📖 So✨ word🗣️ spread💨 among💯 the💯 believers🫂 that💯 this💯 disciple🫂 wouldn’t🚫 die💀. But💯 Jesus🙏 never🚫 said🗣️ he🫵 wouldn’t🚫 die💀; He🙏 said🗣️, “If💯 I🙏 want💭 him🫂 to💯 stay🏠 until💯 I🙏 come🚶, how💭 is💯 that💯 your🫵 business💼?”

24📖 This💯 is💯 the💯 disciple🫂 who💯 testifies🗣️ about💯 these💯 things💯 and✨ wrote✍️ them📝 down⬇️. We🫵 know🧠 his🫵 testimony🧾 is💯 true✅.

25📖 Jesus🙏 did✅ plenty💯 more💯 things🛠️ too💯. If💯 every💯 one💯 got💯 written✍️ down⬇️, I🙏 reckon🧠 the💯 whole🌍 world🌍 couldn’t🚫 fit📚 all💯 those💯 books📚.”

# Acts 1 — AdigunBible draft

Original AdigunBible wording for editorial review. Source meaning and verse count checked against the public-domain World English Bible British Edition, Acts 1. Draft only; not release-validated.

1📖 Theophilus👤, in💯 my🙏 first🥇 write-up📜 I🙏 laid🧾 out💯 everything💯 Jesus🙏 kicked🚀 off🔛 doing✅ and✨ teaching🗣️—that’s🫵 where📍 it🫵 all💯 started🚦,

2📖 right➡️ up💯 to💯 the💯 day📅 He🙏 was💯 lifted⬆️ up☁️. Before⏮️ that💯, through💯 the💯 Holy✨ Spirit🕊️, He🙏 gave🎁 the💯 chosen✅ apôtres🇫🇷 their🫂 instructions📋.

3📖 After💯 He🙏 suffered😣, Jesus🙏 showed👀 up⬆️ alive🌱 with💯 plenty💯 of💯 proof🧾, appearing👀 to💯 them🫂 over💯 forty🔢 days📆 and✨ talking🗣️ about💯 God’s🙏 ìjọba🇳🇬.

4📖 While💯 they👥 were💯 together🤝, Jesus🙏 told🗣️ them🫵, “Don’t🚫 bounce🚪 from💨 Jerusalem📍 yet⏳. Wartet🇩🇪 auf💯 the💯 promessa🇵🇹 from💯 the💯 Father🙏—the💯 one👆 you🫵 heard👂 Me🙏 tell🗣️ you🫂 about💭.”

5📖 “John🙏 baptised💧 y’all🫵 in💯 water💧, but💯 in💯 just💯 a💯 few🔢 days📆 you🫵 will💯 be💯 baptised💧 in💯 the💯 Holy✨ Ruach🇮🇱.”

6📖 So✨ when💯 the💯 crew👥 linked🤝 up🔗, they👥 asked🗣️ Him🙏, “Lord🙏, are💭 You🫵 restoring🔄 Israel’s🇮🇱 kingdom👑 right💯 now⏰?”

7📖 He🙏 told🗣️ them🫂, “That💯 schedule🗓️ isn’t🚫 yours🫵 to💯 know🧠. The💯 Father🙏 set📌 those💯 times⏰ and✨ seasons🍂 under💯 His🙏 own💯 authority⚡.”

8📖 “But💯 you🫵 will💯 receive🙌 ƙarfi🇳🇬 when💯 the💯 Holy✨ Spirit🕊️ comes🚶 upon⬇️ you🫂. Then✨ you🫵 will💯 be💯 My🙏 testigos🇪🇸 in💯 Jerusalem📍, across🌍 Judea📍 and✨ Samaria📍, all💯 the💯 way🌍 to💯 earth’s🌍 farthest🚀 edges📍.”

9📖 After💯 Jesus🙏 said🗣️ that💯, while💯 they👥 watched👀, He🙏 was💯 lifted⬆️ up💨, and✨ a💯 cloud☁️ carried🫴 Him🙏 out💨 of💯 their🫵 sight👀.

10📖 dem🇯🇲 stayed👀 locked🔒 on👀 the💯 sky🌌 as💯 He🙏 went⬆️. Then✨ suddenly💨 two👥 men🧍🧍 in💯 white🤍 clothes👘 stood🧍 right➡️ beside💯 them🫂.

11📖 They🧍🧍 said🗣️, “Galilee📍 people🫂, why❓ you🫵 still⏳ watching👀 the💯 sky🌌 like💭 that💯? This💯 same💯 Jesus🙏, taken⬆️ from💨 you🫂 into💯 heaven☁️, will💯 come🚶 back🔁 the💯 same💯 way🌌 you🫵 saw👀 Him🙏 go⬆️.”

12📖 Then✨ they👥 headed🚶 back↩️ to💯 Jerusalem📍 from💯 the💯 hill⛰️ called🗣️ Olivet📍, which💯 was💯 about💯 a💯 Sabbath🕯️ walk🚶 away💨.

13📖 When💯 they👥 got🚶 inside🏠, they👥 went⬆️ to💯 the💯 upstairs🔝 room🏠 where💯 they🫵 were💯 staying🏠: Peter👤, John👤, James👤, Andrew👤, Philip👤, Thomas👤, Bartholomew👤, Matthew👤, James👤 son👦 of💯 Alphaeus👤, Simon👤 the💯 Zealot🔥, and✨ Judas👤 son👦 of💯 James👤.

14📖 Dem🇯🇲 stayed🤝 on💯 one💯 accord🫶, keeping🔁 prayer🙏 and✨ supplication🙏 going🔁 with💯 the💯 women👩🏽, Mary👩🏽 Jesus’s🙏 nne🇳🇬, and✨ His🙏 brothers🫂.

15📖 Around💯 that💯 time⏰, Peter👤 got🆙 up⬆️ right💯 in💯 the💯 middle🎯 of💯 the💯 disciples🫂—about💯 one💯 hundred🔢 twenty🔢 people🫂 all💯 in💯—and✨ said🗣️,

16📖 “Fam🫂, Scripture📜 had💯 to💯 land🎯 just💯 so💯. The💯 Holy✨ Spirit🕊️ spoke🗣️ way⏮️ back💨 through💯 David👑 about💯 Judas🫵, the💯 guide🧭 for💯 the💯 people👥 who💯 arrested🔗 Jesus🙏.”

17📖 “Judas🫵 had💯 his🫵 place📍 with💯 us🫂 and✨ received🎁 his🫵 share🎁 in💯 this💯 ministry🛠️.”

18📖 “That💯 field🏞️ was💯 obtained🫴 with💯 the💯 payoff💰 for💯 Judas’s🫵 wickedness🖤. He🫵 fell⬇️ headfirst🤕; his🫵 body🫀 burst💥 open💨, and✨ his🫵 insides🫀 spilled🌊 out💨.”

19📖 “Everybody💯 in💯 Jerusalem📍 heard👂 about💯 it💯. In💯 their🫂 own💯 language🗣️, that💯 field🏞️ got🗣️ called📛 Akeldama📍—‘Field🏞️ of💯 Blood🩸.’”

20📖 “It’s💯 written✍️ in💯 Psalms📜: ‘Let🙏 his🫵 home🏠 become💯 abandoned🏚️; let🙏 nobody🚫 live🏠 there👀,’ and✨ ‘Let🙏 someone👤 else💯 take🫳 his🫵 role📋.’”

21📖 “So💯 we🫂 need💯 one💯 of💯 the💯 men🧍 who💯 rolled🚶 with💯 us🫂 the💯 whole💯 time⏳ the💯 Lord🙏 Jesus🙏 was💯 here📍 among💯 us🫂.”

22📖 “That💯 means💭 someone👤 who💯 stayed🤝 with💯 us🫂 from💯 John’s🙏 baptism💧 through➡️ the💯 day📅 Jesus🙏 was💯 taken⬆️ up💨. That💯 person👤 has💯 to💯 join🤝 us🫂 as💯 a💯 witness🗣️ that💭 He🙏 rose🌅 again🔁.”

23📖 They👥 put🫳 two👥 names📋 forward➡️: Joseph👤, called🗣️ Barsabbas👤—also💭 known🗣️ as💯 Justus👤—and✨ Matthias👤.

24📖 They👥 prayed🙏, “Lord🙏, You🫵 know🧠 everybody’s💯 heart🫀. Show👀 us🫂 which💭 one👤 of💯 these🫵 two👥 You🙏 chose✅

25📖 to💯 take🫳 up⬆️ this💯 ministry🛠️ and✨ apostleship📣 spot📍. Judas🫵 stepped🚶 away💨 from💯 it💯 and✨ went🚶 where📍 he🫵 belonged🏠.”

26📖 They👥 cast🎲 lots🎲, and✨ the💯 lot🎲 landed⬇️ on💯 Matthias👤. He🫵 was💯 counted🧾 in💯 with💯 the💯 eleven🔢 apostles🫂.
